# V7 Lot 74 — Arena I Implementation Plan (Part 1: L74-01 … L74-13)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the first half of the V7 arena behind `?ui=next`: the shell and its six regions, the read-only card and kit sheets, the top bar, opponent seats with their sheet, threat arrows and the central plate, the hand, card actions through one commit helper, the self strip, tap-tap targeting, Assassin multi-attack, drag, and the dock.

**Architecture:**
- `screens/arena/` components render the Lot 73 view-model (`seats`, `threats`, `cardActions`, `dropTarget`, `selfStrip`) and the L72 `ui/` layer (`Sheet`, `Card`, `KitPortrait`, `Button`, …).
- `ArenaScreen` computes the models once per render and passes them down as props. Every intent leaves through `createArenaCommit` (L74-08).
- Open read-only sheets and targeting live in one pure reducer (`arena-sheets.ts`). The `Sheet` primitive owns stacking, Esc and focus.
- The old `TableScreen` stays the default and the tutorial path. Nothing changes for a player who did not opt in.

**Tech Stack:** React 19 · TypeScript (strict) · Vitest 4 · @testing-library/react + user-event on happy-dom (`renderUi`, `apps/client/src/test/render.tsx`) · motion · the L72 `ui/` design system. No new dependency.

**Spec:** `docs/technical_spec_v7.md` (normative; §4.2–§4.4, §5.4, §5.6, §6.1–§6.6, §6.12, §11, §12) with Appendix A `docs/technical_spec_v7_parity.md`. Tracker: `docs/backlog_v7.md` Lot 74. **Part 2** (L74-14 … L74-26) is still in brief form below the Part 1 tasks; it is expanded into TDD steps once Part 1 is merged into this branch, on the real interfaces. One branch (`claude/v7-lot-74`), one PR into `dev` at the end of Part 2 (L74-26).

> Generated 2026-10-28. The controller mapped the code (`ui/`, the L73 view-model, the V2 table, the connection and the FX anchors), fixed one interface contract (Appendix A), and had one planning agent write each domain's tasks from the contract, the spec, the briefs and the code. The controller then ran a cross-task consistency pass and a critic review. Each task ends with **Decisions**: choices the executor cannot derive from the spec. They are binding unless the designer overrides them in review (Appendix B).

## Global Constraints

- **Client-only (§1, §15 row 74).** No server, engine, protocol or `packages/shared` change; `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays 45; no Classic rule or value changes. A task that finds a real need stops and asks.
- **Opt-in only (§4.4).** The arena renders only for `?ui=next` in a Classic room. **No Part 1 task edits `screens/table.tsx`, `screens/table/**` or `design/**`**, so the old table and its source-text tests stay green unchanged. No What's new item in Part 1 (§10.4; only L74-17 adds one).
- **Import rule (L74-01 brief).**
  - `screens/arena/` imports only from `ui/`, `view-model/`, `fx/`, `net/` (types), `help/`, `feedback/` and the shared screen modules (`status-labels`, `illegal-action-copy`, `how-to-play-dialog`).
  - It never imports from `screens/table/` or `design/`.
  - A pure helper still living there moves, with its tests, in the task that first needs it.
  - It never imports `TableScreen`, except `TableScreenProps` as the `ArenaScreenProps` alias (Appendix A.2).
- **No rule logic in components (§4.3, golden rule 8).**
  - Legality, targets, disabled reasons and drop zones come only from the L73 view-model.
  - Components never re-derive a tier, a target set or a price.
  - Disabled reasons render `DISABLED_REASON_COPY[reason]` (`view-model/action-reasons.ts`).
- **Commit rule (§6.6.4).**
  - An intent leaves only on a target tap, an explicit button, or a drop on a valid zone. Every `ArenaIntent` leaves through `createArenaCommit` (L74-08).
  - Leave, forfeit and chat are App-level actions, not `ArenaIntent`s: the `TopBar` (and later the chat sheet) calls `onLeave` / `onForfeit` / `onSendChat` directly from an explicit confirm or send button, outside the helper. L75 handles the tutorial Skip separately.
  - Selecting, lifting, opening or closing a sheet, hovering or long-pressing sends nothing.
  - Every component test that opens or selects asserts that no intent fired.
- **No hidden information (§6.3).** Render only what the view-model exposes. A hidden value shows `?`, a hidden kit `KitPortrait kitId={null}`, and a hidden card `Card face="hidden"`. No DOM node, attribute or `aria-label` carries an unspied value.
- **Never a summed attack total** (golden rule 1, designer 2026-09-29): one label per pending effect.
- **Tests (§12.2, AGENTS.md §6, `docs/agent/testing.md`).**
  - Component tests are `*.test.tsx` that open with `// @vitest-environment happy-dom` and render through `renderUi`. They query by role, name and visible text.
  - Pure logic is tested in node `*.test.ts`.
  - Never a new source-text (`readFileSync`) test. `vitest` imports are explicit. Tests never call `Date.now()`; use fake timers with `advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime`.
  - No test is skipped, disabled or weakened.
  - Drag gestures are checked in the browser (L74-12); drag logic is tested through `dropTarget` / `dispatchDrop`.
- **Strict TypeScript.**
  - `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes` (spread optional props conditionally), `noPropertyAccessFromIndexSignature`.
  - Never widen a type or cast to silence them, and no `eslint-disable` on a type-safety rule.
  - Unions come from `as const` arrays; named exports only.
- **Design (DESIGN.md, map-ui.md §13).**
  - Colors via `bg-v7-*` / `text-v7-*` classes or hex from `ui/palette.ts` inline (never `var()` / `color-mix()` inline).
  - Seat hue via `v7SeatColor(index)`.
  - Tap targets ≥ 44 px; no functional text under 11 px; own lives ≥ 20 px; incoming badge ≥ 22 px.
  - No emoji or ✕ characters (use `Icon`).
  - Chrome composed from `ui/primitives/chrome-classes.ts`.
  - Overlays use `Sheet` / `Dialog` / `Toast` only, never a custom `fixed` layer. The one exception is the L74-12 hand-drag ghost and Sell zone (Appendix A.8 ruling 10): a `pointer-events: none` portal mounted only during a drag, below the sheet layer, on `Z_INDEX.dragGhost` (`ui/layers.ts`).
  - Reduced motion via `usePrefersReducedMotion()`, with `data-motion` on animated roots.
  - A task that adds or changes a `ui/` file or a token updates `DESIGN.md` in the same commit (§5.8).
- **Copy:** English only.
  - Reuse V2 strings verbatim from `view-model/table-copy.ts`, `view-model/chrome-labels.ts`, `view-model/table-banner.ts`, `screens/status-labels.ts` and `ui/ui-copy.ts`.
  - Every new player-facing string is listed in the task's Decisions and in Appendix B "New strings (§10.6)".
- **Docs per task (§12.5, AGENTS.md §9, §12).**
  - L74-01 opens an `## Arena (V7, ?ui=next)` section in `docs/agent/frontend.md`. Each later task appends its bullet there in task order and fixes any `frontend.md` line it supersedes.
  - `decisions.md` entries are appended at the end in the `## YYYY-MM-DD · [P] …` format, each closing with its own `---`. A past entry is never edited.
  - `docs/backlog_v7.md`: the task row `To do` → `Done`. After task L74-NN the Progress row reads `| 74 · Arena I | 26 | NN |` and the Total row `| **Total** | **134** | **34 + NN** |` (L74-01 → 35, L74-13 → 47). If a parallel lot (76, 80) moved the Total first, add one to whatever it holds; the Lot 74 cell is exact.
- **Commits:**
  - Exactly one Conventional Commit per task, subject ≤ 50 chars (hard cap 72), with the task id, for example `feat(arena): add arena shell regions (L74-01)`.
  - Files staged by name, never `git add -A`.
  - No AI attribution and no Co-Authored-By trailer (AGENTS.md §10).
  - `pnpm verify` green before each commit.
- **Tooling:** run every command through `pnpm` (Node is pinned via `devEngines`). Single test file: `pnpm vitest run <path>`.

## Conventions (shared by every task)

- **Files:** kebab-case names under `apps/client/src/screens/arena/` (Appendix A.1). `.tsx` exports components only; constants, types and pure helpers sit in sibling `.ts` files.
- **Inputs:**
  - Build every test view with `makeArenaView` / `makeArenaProps` (`screens/arena/test-view.ts`, L74-01) or the `view-model/test-fixtures.ts` builders it wraps. Tests override only what they assert on.
  - A component test asserts intents on the `makeArenaProps` spies (`onPlayCard`, `onSellCard`, …) or, below `ArenaScreen`, on a `vi.fn()` passed as `commit`.
- **Finished board:** `props.readOnly === true`, `view` = `finished.finalTable`. A walk-in is `view.isSpectator === true`. An eliminated POV is the `isYou` player with `isEliminated`.
- **Sheets:**
  - Card, kit and seat sheets are `Sheet mode="read-only"` with `placement="bottom"`; desktop centering is L75.
  - Titles: card sheet = the card name (`getCard(cardId).name`, with the existing upgraded suffix if V2 has one); kit sheet = kit name; seat sheet = nickname, or `'{nick} (eliminated)'`.
  - Each `<Sheet>` gets `dataAttributes={{ 'data-arena-sheet': arenaSheetKey(sheet) }}`.
- **Order** is meaningful and comes from the model (seat order, badge order, action order); components never sort.
- **Docs bullets** in the frontend.md Arena section start with the file and the task id, for example `` - `screens/arena/hand-fan.tsx` (L74-07, spec v7 §6.1 item 4): … ``.

## Review Focus

The five input classes the spec implies, no brief Acceptance tests, and a player is most likely to meet. Each one has a test in the owning task.

1. **A card leaves the hand while its sheet is open.** A state update played, stole, sold or transformed it. The sheet closes, or shows only the read-only face with no enabled action, and no intent can fire for the stale instance. Owner: **L74-08** (test: rerender without the instance; no Use button is enabled, and clicking where it was sends nothing).
2. **A sub-choice opens, or the turn passes, during targeting.** Targeting ends, the bar disappears, and a later seat tap sends nothing. Owner: **L74-10** (tests: rerender with a `mirror` subChoice; rerender with `currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1'`).
3. **A double tap on a target seat or on Use.** Exactly one intent fires. Owner: **L74-10** (two clicks on a valid seat → `onPlayCard` called once) and **L74-08** (two clicks on Use for an untargeted card → once).
4. **A drag ends after the card left the hand or the turn passed mid-drag.** The drop resolves against the latest view and sends nothing. Owner: **L74-12** (pure test: `dispatchDrop` with a model whose `canDrag` is false → no commit).
5. **8 seats with long nicknames on a 360 px phone.** The visible name truncates (`truncate` + `title`), while the accessible name and the seat sheet title keep the full nickname. Owner: **L74-04** (test: a 24-character nickname is the full accessible name of the seat button).

## Execution order

Sequential, in id order: L74-01 → L74-13. Each task starts from the previous task's commit.

- L74-01 lands first (shell, test helpers).
- L74-02 before L74-04, L74-05, L74-06, L74-07, L74-09, because it creates the sheets reducer and the card sheet.
- L74-08 before L74-09, L74-10, L74-11 and L74-13, because it creates the commit helper.
- L74-10 before L74-12.

After L74-13, Part 2 is planned on the real code; nothing ships until L74-26.

```ts
// arena-regions.ts
export const ARENA_REGIONS = ['top-bar', 'ticker', 'arena', 'self-strip', 'hand', 'dock'] as const;
export type ArenaRegion = (typeof ARENA_REGIONS)[number];
/** Spread on a region root: a misspelt region fails typecheck. */
export function arenaRegionAttributes(region: ArenaRegion): { readonly 'data-arena-region': ArenaRegion };

// arena-screen.tsx (Appendix A.2, unchanged alias)
export type ArenaScreenProps = TableScreenProps;
export function ArenaScreen(props: ArenaScreenProps): ReactElement;

// test-view.ts (Appendix A.4; imported by tests only)
export type ArenaSeatTier = 'unspied' | 'base-spy' | 'spy-plus' | 'eliminated';
export type ArenaPov = 'seated' | 'walk-in' | 'eliminated' | 'reanimating';
export interface ArenaViewOptions {
  /** Total seats including the POV (2–8). Seat ids: 'me', then 'p1'…'p7'. Default 3. */
  seatCount?: number;
  /** Tier per opponent id; unlisted opponents are unspied. */
  tiers?: Readonly<Record<string, ArenaSeatTier>>;
  /** Default 'seated'. 'walk-in' makes every seat a non-POV seat (you = 'watcher'). */
  pov?: ArenaPov;
  /** Spread last (after the tiers and the POV are applied). */
  overrides?: Partial<PlayingStateView>;
}
/** Live resources every Spy relation sends (§6.3): base Spy and Spy+ seats carry exactly these. */
export const ARENA_SPIED_RESOURCES: { readonly lives: 7; readonly points: 12; readonly upgradePoints: 1; readonly shield: 2 };
/** The Spy+ kit (Appendix A.4). */
export const ARENA_SPY_PLUS_KIT: KitId; // 'tactician'
/** The intent callbacks `arenaSpies` reads, in this order. */
export const ARENA_INTENT_PROPS: readonly [
  'onDraw', 'onPlayCard', 'onPlayMultipleAttacks', 'onResolveSubChoice', 'onBuyCard', 'onSellCard',
  'onUpgradeCard', 'onBuyUpgradePoint', 'onBuySpecialCard', 'onBuyPoolCard', 'onClearSpy',
  'onSellUpgradePoint', 'onLeave', 'onForfeit', 'onSendChat', 'onDeactivatePersistent', 'onActivateDuplication',
]; // declared `as const satisfies readonly (keyof ArenaScreenProps)[]`
export function makeArenaView(options?: ArenaViewOptions): PlayingStateView;
/** Every `ArenaScreenProps` callback is a `vi.fn()`; `view` defaults to `makeArenaView()`. */
export function makeArenaProps(overrides?: Partial<ArenaScreenProps>): ArenaScreenProps;
/** The `vi.fn()` behind each `ARENA_INTENT_PROPS` callback of `props` (commit-rule checks, §6.6.4). */
export function arenaSpies(props: ArenaScreenProps): readonly MockInstance[];
/** The commit rule (§6.6.4): asserts that every intent spy of `props` has zero calls. The only "nothing sent" helper (Appendix A.4). */
export function expectNoArenaIntent(props: ArenaScreenProps): void;
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/test-view.test.ts` (node):

```ts
/**
 * Arena test views — Lot 74 plan Appendix A.4, technical spec v7 §6.3 (one tier per wire shape).
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { makeEliminationReveal } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ARENA_SPIED_RESOURCES, arenaSpies, expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

describe('arena test views (Appendix A.4)', () => {
  it('seats me then p1…p7 in turn order with me as the POV, 3 seats by default', () => {
    expect(makeArenaView().players.map((player) => player.id)).toEqual(['me', 'p1', 'p2']);
    const eight = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8 });
    expect(eight.turnOrder).toEqual(['me', 'p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4', 'p5', 'p6', 'p7']);
    expect(eight.players.filter((player) => player.isYou).map((player) => player.id)).toEqual(['me']);
    expect([eight.you, eight.isSpectator]).toEqual(['me', undefined]);
    expect(() => makeArenaView({ seatCount: 1 })).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() => makeArenaView({ seatCount: 9 })).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it('builds every §6.3 tier from the wire fields only', () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 5, tiers: { p1: 'base-spy', p2: 'spy-plus', p3: 'eliminated' } });
    const [, p1, p2, p3, p4] = view.players;
    expect(ARENA_SPIED_RESOURCES).toEqual({ lives: 7, points: 12, upgradePoints: 1, shield: 2 });
    expect(p1?.spied).toEqual({ lives: 7, points: 12, upgradePoints: 1, shield: 2 });
    expect(p2?.spied).toEqual({
      lives: 7, points: 12, upgradePoints: 1, shield: 2, kitId: 'tactician',
      hand: [
        { instanceId: 'p2-basic-attack', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false },
        { instanceId: 'p2-spy', cardId: 'spy', isUpgraded: false },
      ],
      specialCards: [],
    });
    expect([p3?.isEliminated, p3?.eliminationReveal, p3?.spied]).toEqual([true, makeEliminationReveal(), undefined]);
    expect([p4?.isEliminated, p4?.spied, p4?.eliminationReveal]).toEqual([false, undefined, undefined]);
  });

  it('makes a walk-in watcher with no seated POV', () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({ pov: 'walk-in', tiers: { me: 'spy-plus' } });
    expect([view.you, view.isSpectator]).toEqual(['watcher', true]);
    expect(view.players.some((player) => player.isYou)).toBe(false);
    expect(view.players[0]?.spied?.kitId).toBe('tactician');
  });

  it('marks an eliminated POV, and a Reanimating one with its pending Reanimation', () => {
    const dead = makeArenaView({ pov: 'eliminated' }).players[0];
    expect([dead?.isYou, dead?.isEliminated, dead?.eliminationReveal, dead?.pendingReanimation])
      .toEqual([true, true, makeEliminationReveal(), null]);
    expect(makeArenaView({ pov: 'reanimating' }).players[0]?.pendingReanimation).toEqual({ isUpgraded: false });
  });

  it('spreads overrides last', () => {
    expect(makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1', gameCode: 'ABCDEF' } }))
      .toMatchObject({ currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1', gameCode: 'ABCDEF', you: 'me' });
  });

  it('gives every callback a spy and lists the 17 intent spies, none called', () => {
    const props = makeArenaProps();
    expect(props).toMatchObject({
      actionReject: null, statusLabel: 'Connected', nowMs: 0, deadlineMs: null, lastActionResolved: null, subChoice: null,
    });
    expect(props.view.players.map((player) => player.id)).toEqual(['me', 'p1', 'p2']);
    expect([props.readOnly, props.youWon, props.onShowStats, props.onOpenFeedback]).toEqual([undefined, undefined, undefined, undefined]);
    const spies = arenaSpies(props);
    expect(spies).toHaveLength(17);
    for (const spy of spies) {
      expect(spy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    }
    expect(arenaSpies(makeArenaProps({ readOnly: true })).length).toBe(17);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    props.onDraw();
    expect(() => {
      expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    }).toThrow();
  });
});
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-screen.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * The arena shell — technical spec v7 §4.4, §6.1, §6.4; Appendix A Q60 (every region always
 * renders); L74-01. It replaces the L72-20 pass-through test `arena-screen.test.ts`
 * (decisions.md 2026-10-28).
 */
import type { SubChoiceRequiredPayload } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { ARENA_REGIONS } from './arena-regions';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const MIRROR_OPEN: SubChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 1 };

function arenaRoot(container: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {
  const root = container.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-arena="next"]');
  if (root === null) {
    throw new Error('no arena root');
  }
  return root;
}

function region(container: HTMLElement, name: string): HTMLElement {
  const found = container.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
  if (found === null) {
    throw new Error(`no ${name} region`);
  }
  return found;
}

const has = (root: HTMLElement, selector: string): boolean => root.querySelector(selector) !== null;

describe('ArenaScreen shell (spec v7 §6.1, L74-01)', () => {
  it.each([2, 8])('renders the six regions top to bottom with their anchors for a %i-seat view', (seatCount) => {
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: makeArenaView({ seatCount }) });
    const { container } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    const root = arenaRoot(container);
    expect(root.getAttribute('data-zone')).toBe('table');
    expect(Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('[data-arena-region]'), (node) => node.getAttribute('data-arena-region')))
      .toEqual([...ARENA_REGIONS]);
    expect(has(region(container, 'top-bar'), '[data-zone="timers"][data-hint-anchor="your-turn"]')).toBe(true);
    expect(has(region(container, 'ticker'), '[data-zone="log-collapsed"]')).toBe(true);
    const arena = region(container, 'arena');
    expect([
      arena.getAttribute('data-zone'),
      has(arena, '[data-zone="opponents"]'),
      has(arena, '[data-zone="pending"][data-hint-anchor="incoming"]'),
    ]).toEqual(['felt', true, true]);
    const self = region(container, 'self-strip');
    expect([self.getAttribute('data-zone'), self.getAttribute('data-player-id')]).toEqual(['private-zone', 'me']);
    expect(has(self, '[data-zone="resources"][data-hint-anchor="resources"]')).toBe(true);
    const hand = region(container, 'hand');
    expect([
      hand.getAttribute('data-zone'),
      has(hand, '[data-zone="hand"][data-hint-anchor="hand"]'),
      has(hand, '[data-zone="specials"][data-hint-anchor="specials"]'),
    ]).toEqual(['card-band', true, true]);
    expect(region(container, 'dock').getAttribute('data-zone')).toBe('dock');
    expect(container.querySelector('[data-card-row]')).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('shows the empty states: an empty hand and no specials, in V2 words (Q60)', () => {
    const { container } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...makeArenaProps()} />);
    const band = region(container, 'hand');
    const hand = band.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="hand"][data-hint-anchor="hand"]');
    const specials = band.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="specials"][data-hint-anchor="specials"]');
    if (hand === null || specials === null) {
      throw new Error('missing hand or specials zone');
    }
    expect(within(hand).getByText('Hand')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(hand).getByText('Empty')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(specials).getByText('Specials')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(specials).getByText('None')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('keeps every region for a walk-in, its private zone keyed to the watcher (A.3)', () => {
    const { container } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...makeArenaProps({ view: makeArenaView({ pov: 'walk-in' }) })} />);
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-arena-region]')).toHaveLength(ARENA_REGIONS.length);
    expect(region(container, 'self-strip').getAttribute('data-player-id')).toBe('watcher');
  });

  it('fills #root on the V7 ground with no viewport unit and no page scroll (L53-07, §6.4)', () => {
    const root = arenaRoot(renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...makeArenaProps()} />).container);
    for (const name of ['v7-ground', 'h-full', 'w-full', 'overflow-hidden']) {
      expect(root.classList.contains(name)).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(root.className).not.toMatch(/vh|w-screen|h-screen/);
  });

  it.each<{ label: string; overrides: Partial<ArenaScreenProps>; lock: string }>([
    { label: 'your turn', overrides: {}, lock: 'none' },
    { label: 'another seat’s turn', overrides: { view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' } }) }, lock: 'not-your-turn' },
    { label: 'the finished board', overrides: { readOnly: true }, lock: 'actions-locked' },
    { label: 'an open sub-choice', overrides: { subChoice: MIRROR_OPEN }, lock: 'actions-locked' },
    { label: 'an eliminated POV', overrides: { view: makeArenaView({ pov: 'eliminated', overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' } }) }, lock: 'actions-locked' },
    { label: 'a walk-in', overrides: { view: makeArenaView({ pov: 'walk-in' }) }, lock: 'actions-locked' },
  ])('computes one action lock for $label', ({ overrides, lock }) => {
    const { container } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...makeArenaProps(overrides)} />);
    expect(arenaRoot(container).getAttribute('data-action-lock')).toBe(lock);
  });
});
```

That is 6 node tests plus 11 component tests: the region `it.each` counts as 2 and the lock `it.each` as 6.

```tsx
export type ArenaScreenProps = TableScreenProps;

export function ArenaScreen(props: ArenaScreenProps): ReactElement {
  const { view } = props;
  const readOnly = props.readOnly ?? false;
  const ctx: ActionContext = { subChoice: props.subChoice, readOnly };
  const lock = actionLock(view, ctx);
  return (
    <main
      data-zone="table"
      data-arena="next"
      data-action-lock={lock ?? 'none'}
      className="v7-ground relative flex h-full w-full min-w-0 flex-col overflow-hidden font-v7-body text-v7-text"
    >
      <div {...arenaRegionAttributes('top-bar')} className="shrink-0 px-2 pt-2">
        {/* Clock slot: L74-21 renders the turn clock here (spec v7 §6.10). */}
        <div data-zone="timers" data-hint-anchor="your-turn" />
      </div>
      <div {...arenaRegionAttributes('ticker')} className="shrink-0 px-2">
        <p data-zone="log-collapsed" className="truncate text-v7-caption text-v7-text-2">
          {view.actionLog.length === 0 ? 'No actions yet' : null}
        </p>
      </div>
      {/* Measured by L74-04: ref-able, relative, overflow-hidden, no padding (Appendix A.3). */}
      <div {...arenaRegionAttributes('arena')} data-zone="felt" className="relative min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
        <div data-zone="opponents" />
        <div data-zone="pending" data-hint-anchor="incoming" />
      </div>
      <div {...arenaRegionAttributes('self-strip')} data-zone="private-zone" data-player-id={view.you} className="shrink-0 px-2">
        <div data-zone="resources" data-hint-anchor="resources" />
      </div>
      <div {...arenaRegionAttributes('hand')} data-zone="card-band" className="min-w-0 shrink-0">
        <div data-zone="hand" data-hint-anchor="hand">
          {view.self.hand.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="flex flex-col font-v7-body text-v7-caption font-bold text-v7-text-2"><span>Hand</span><span>Empty</span></p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        <div data-zone="specials" data-hint-anchor="specials">
          {view.self.specialCards.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="flex flex-col font-v7-body text-v7-caption font-bold text-v7-text-2"><span>Specials</span><span>None</span></p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div {...arenaRegionAttributes('dock')} data-zone="dock" className="shrink-0 px-2 pb-2" />
    </main>
  );
}
```

  - The empty-state words are V2 copy: the log panel's `No actions yet`, and the card band's `Hand` / `Empty` and `Specials` / `None` (the caption and text L74-07's `HandFan` keeps). Later tasks render inside each region element and keep its attributes and anchors (Appendix A.3).

```
## Arena (V7, ?ui=next)

Technical spec v7 §6 and §4.4; the Lot 74 plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-21-v7-lot-74-arena-1.md`,
Appendix A). Each Lot 74 task appends its bullet here, in task order.

- `screens/arena/arena-screen.tsx` (L74-01, spec v7 §4.4): the switch. `App.tsx` (playing) and
  `EndScreen` (finished board, `tableUi`) mount `ArenaScreen` when `resolveTableUi(...)` is
  `'next'`: `?ui=next` was set and the room is Classic. The default and tutorial rooms keep
  `TableScreen`. `ArenaScreenProps` is the `TableScreenProps` alias, so App passes one prop set
  to either board.
- Regions (L74-01, §6.1, Q60): `ARENA_REGIONS` (`arena-regions.ts`) top to bottom: `top-bar`,
  `ticker`, `arena`, `self-strip`, `hand`, `dock`. Each is a `data-arena-region` element that
  always renders, with its empty state. The root is `<main data-zone="table" data-arena="next">`,
  `.v7-ground`, `h-full w-full overflow-hidden` of `#root`. It has no `vh`, `dvh` or `w-screen` and
  never page-scrolls (L53-07); only the hand scrolls, inside itself.
- Anchors (L74-01, §6.12): regions keep the V2 `data-zone` names that `fx/`, hints and the tutorial
  query.
  - `top-bar`: `timers` with `data-hint-anchor="your-turn"` (the clock slot).
  - `ticker`: `log-collapsed`.
  - `arena`: `felt`, holding `opponents` and `pending` with `incoming`.
  - `self-strip`: `private-zone` with `data-player-id={view.you}` (a walk-in too), holding
    `resources` with `resources`.
  - `hand`: `card-band`, holding `hand` with `hand` and `specials` with `specials`.
  - `dock`: `dock`.
  Never use `data-card-row`: `index.css` forces nowrap on it.
- Action lock (L74-01, §4.3, Appendix A.2): `ArenaScreen` computes `ctx = { subChoice,
  readOnly }` and `actionLock(view, ctx)` once per render and passes models down as props.
  There is no React context for game data. The root exposes the lock as `data-action-lock`
  (`none` | `not-your-turn` | `actions-locked`).
- Import rule (L74-01): `screens/arena/` imports only `ui/`, `view-model/`, `fx/`, `net/`
  (types), `help/`, `feedback/` and `screens/status-labels`, `screens/illegal-action-copy` and
  `screens/how-to-play-dialog`. It never imports `screens/table/` or `design/`; the one exception
  is the `TableScreenProps` type behind the alias. A pure helper still living there moves, with its
  tests, in the task that first needs it.
```

```
## Arena fixtures (V7 · L74-01)

`apps/client/src/screens/arena/test-view.ts` builds what every arena component test renders;
tests import it and shipped code never does.

- `makeArenaView({ seatCount, tiers, pov, overrides })` is a `makeTableView` table of 2–8
  seats: `me`, then `p1`…`p7`, in turn order.
- `tiers` gives each opponent a spec v7 §6.3 tier, built from the wire fields only:
  - `base-spy`: `spied` with `ARENA_SPIED_RESOURCES` (7 lives, 12 points, 1 upgrade point,
    2 shield) and no kit.
  - `spy-plus`: the same, plus kit `tactician`, a hand of `<id>-basic-attack` and `<id>-spy`,
    and no specials.
  - `eliminated`: `isEliminated` plus `makeEliminationReveal()`.
- `pov`:
  - `seated`.
  - `eliminated`.
  - `reanimating`: eliminated, plus `pendingReanimation`.
  - `walk-in`: `you: 'watcher'` and `isSpectator`; every seat is a non-POV seat.
- `makeArenaProps(overrides?)` gives every `ArenaScreenProps` callback a `vi.fn()`. It sets no
  `readOnly`, `youWon`, `onShowStats` or `onOpenFeedback`.
- `arenaSpies(props)` returns the 17 intent spies (`ARENA_INTENT_PROPS`). A test that renders
  `ArenaScreen` and opens, selects or lifts something calls `expectNoArenaIntent(props)`, which
  asserts that none of them ran (commit rule, spec v7 §6.6.4). It is the only "nothing sent"
  helper; below `ArenaScreen`, tests assert on the `commit` or callback `vi.fn()` they pass.
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Routing (switch on with Classic gives the arena; switch off or a tutorial room gives the old table): `ui-switch.test.ts` › `tutorial rooms always get the classic table`. It checks `preference: 'next'` + Classic → `'next'`, `'classic'` → `'classic'`, and tutorial → `'classic'` for both preferences. App maps `'next'` to `ArenaScreen` in a single ternary; App is not tested through its source text.
- Six regions with their empty states for a 2-seat and an 8-seat view: `renders the six regions top to bottom with their anchors for a %i-seat view` (2 and 8) and `shows the empty states: an empty hand and no specials, in V2 words (Q60)`.
- Old table and its source-text tests untouched: Step 6 item 1 (empty diff); every `screens/table` test still passes in `pnpm verify`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **App.tsx is not edited** although the brief lists it. L72-20 already routes `phase: 'playing'` and the finished board through `resolveTableUi`; repeating it would add nothing.
- **No new routing test.** `ui-switch.test.ts` already covers all three Acceptance cases through `resolveTableUi`. The only remaining step is App's `=== 'next' ? ArenaScreen : TableScreen`, and pinning it would need a source-text test, which is forbidden (§12.1).
- **`data-action-lock` on the root.** Appendix A.2 requires the lock to be computed once per render. Until a control reads it, exposing it as an attribute makes the computation observable (and lint-clean) and gives later tests and the browser gate one place to read it.
- **Regions are plain `<div>`s** with `data-arena-region`, not named landmarks. Six region names would be new strings, and `<main>` already names the board. Tests find regions through the contract attribute.
- **Empty states reuse V2 copy:** `No actions yet` (log panel) in the ticker, `Hand` / `Empty` and `Specials` / `None` (card band) in the hand. The top bar, arena, self strip and dock render only their anchors until their tasks land; nothing ships before L74-26.
- **Region tests pin anchors, never a placeholder a later task replaces** (Appendix A.3). The card-band words stay true after L74-07, because `HandFan` renders the same V2 caption and text; the ticker's `No actions yet` is not asserted, because L74-15 rebuilds the ticker.
- **Ticker anchor is a `<p>` for now.** `data-zone="log-collapsed"` sits on a non-interactive `<p>`. L74-15 turns it into the ticker button, so no unnamed button ships.
- **Test helper semantics left open by Appendix A.4:**
  - Spied resource values 7 / 12 / 1 / 2: distinct, so each value can be found in the DOM.
  - Spy+ instance ids `<id>-basic-attack` and `<id>-spy`.
  - `RangeError` outside 2–8 seats.
  - In a walk-in view the `me` seat keeps id `me` but is not the POV.
  - `expectNoArenaIntent(props)` is the one "nothing sent" helper for every later arena test (Appendix A.4).
- **New strings:** none.

```ts
// card-sheet-source.ts
export type CardSheetSource =
  | { kind: 'hand'; instance: CardInstance }
  | { kind: 'special'; instance: CardInstance }
  /** An own or an opponent in-play card: Shield, active persistent, ticking Sentence (L73-07). */
  | { kind: 'in-play'; thumb: InPlayThumb }
  /** A Spy+ or death-frozen hand / special of another seat (§6.3). */
  | { kind: 'spy-reveal'; instance: CardInstance; ownerPlayerId: string }
  /** A plate badge or arrow label: catalog-only face, no footer (L56-05). */
  | { kind: 'queue'; effectId: string; cardId: CardId; isUpgraded: boolean }
  /** A log card: catalog-only face, no footer (L56-05). Opened by the plate's landing card (L74-06) and the log (L74-15). */
  | { kind: 'log'; cardId: CardId; isUpgraded: boolean };
// Part 2 adds 'pool', 'shop' and 'reward'. Every switch on `kind` ends in a `never` default.

export const ACTIVE_LABEL = 'Active';
export const CARD_LIVES_LABEL = 'Card lives';
export const TURNS_LEFT_LABEL = 'Turns left';
export const SPY_REVEAL_LABEL = 'Spy reveal — inspect only';
export interface CardSheetCount {
  readonly label: typeof CARD_LIVES_LABEL | typeof TURNS_LEFT_LABEL;
  readonly value: number;
}
export type CardSheetContext =
  | { readonly kind: 'active'; readonly count: CardSheetCount | null }
  | { readonly kind: 'spy-reveal' }
  | null;
/** hand → zone 'hand', special → 'special', in-play → thumb.ref, others → 'read-only'. */
export function cardRefFor(source: CardSheetSource): CardRef;
/** queue: `{ instanceId: `queue-${effectId}`, cardId, isUpgraded }`; log: `log-${cardId}-${'up'|'base'}`. */
export function sheetInstanceFor(source: CardSheetSource): CardInstance;
/** `getCard(cardId)?.name ?? 'Card'` (the V2 dialog title, no upgraded suffix). */
export function cardSheetTitle(source: CardSheetSource): string;
/** Activated look = counter glow, only for a persistent's card lives or Invisibility turns (A.8 ruling 1). */
export function cardSheetCounter(source: CardSheetSource): CardCounter | null;
export function cardSheetContext(source: CardSheetSource): CardSheetContext;
/** An in-play source re-read from `inPlayThumbs(view, owner)` by instance id; anything else (or a thumb that left play) unchanged. */
export function liveCardSheetSource(source: CardSheetSource, view: PlayingStateView): CardSheetSource;

// arena-sheets.ts
export type ArenaSheet =
  | { kind: 'card'; source: CardSheetSource }
  | { kind: 'kit'; kitId: KitId };
// L74-05 adds { kind: 'seat'; playerId: string }.
export interface ArenaSheetsState {
  /** Open read-only sheets, oldest first. The `Sheet` primitive paints the newest two (MAX_READ_ONLY_DEPTH). */
  readonly open: readonly ArenaSheet[];
  /** The last closed sheets, mounted with `open={false}` through their exit (press shield). */
  readonly closing: readonly ArenaSheet[];
}
// L74-10 adds `readonly targeting: TargetingState | null`.
export const EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS: ArenaSheetsState; // { open: [], closing: [] }
export const MAX_CLOSING_SHEETS = 2;
/** `card:<sheetInstanceFor(source).instanceId>`, `kit:<kitId>` (L74-05: `seat:<playerId>`). */
export function arenaSheetKey(sheet: ArenaSheet): string;
export function openArenaSheet(state: ArenaSheetsState, sheet: ArenaSheet): ArenaSheetsState;
export function closeArenaSheet(state: ArenaSheetsState, sheet: ArenaSheet): ArenaSheetsState;
export interface ArenaSheetEntry { readonly sheet: ArenaSheet; readonly key: string; readonly open: boolean }
/** `closing` entries (open: false), then `open` entries (open: true): render one Sheet per entry, keyed by `key`. */
export function arenaSheetEntries(state: ArenaSheetsState): readonly ArenaSheetEntry[];

// card-sheet.tsx
export interface CardSheetProps {
  /** False while the sheet plays its exit from `closing` (arena-sheets.ts). */
  open: boolean;
  source: CardSheetSource;
  /** The live view: in-play counters follow it; L74-08 reads card actions from it. */
  view: PlayingStateView;
  /** The arena's action context (Appendix A.2); read from L74-08 on. */
  ctx: ActionContext;
  onClose: () => void;
}
export function CardSheet(props: CardSheetProps): ReactElement;

// kit-sheet.tsx
export interface KitSheetProps { open: boolean; kitId: KitId; onClose: () => void }
export function KitSheet(props: KitSheetProps): ReactElement;

// ui/motion/use-long-press.ts
export const LONG_PRESS_MS = 450;
export const LONG_PRESS_MOVE_PX = 8;
export interface UseLongPressOptions { onLongPress: () => void; disabled?: boolean }
export interface LongPressHandlers {
  onPointerDown: (event: PointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => void;
  onPointerMove: (event: PointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => void;
  onPointerUp: () => void;
  onPointerCancel: () => void;
  onPointerLeave: () => void;
  /** Swallows the click that ends a long press, so a tap action never follows it. */
  onClickCapture: (event: MouseEvent<HTMLElement>) => void;
}
export function useLongPress(options: UseLongPressOptions): LongPressHandlers;
```

```ts
/**
 * Card sheet sources — Lot 74 plan Appendix A.5 and ruling 1; technical spec v7 §6.6.1, §5.4;
 * L56-05 (log and queue inspect: catalog face, no footer).
 */
import type { CardId, PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { inPlayThumbs, type InPlayThumb } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import { makeCard, makePersistent, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import {
  ACTIVE_LABEL, CARD_LIVES_LABEL, SPY_REVEAL_LABEL, TURNS_LEFT_LABEL,
  cardRefFor, cardSheetContext, cardSheetCounter, cardSheetTitle, liveCardSheetSource, sheetInstanceFor,
  type CardSheetSource,
} from './card-sheet-source';
import { makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const POISON = makePersistent({ id: 'poison-e', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 });
const INVISIBILITY = makePersistent({ id: 'invisibility-e', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 4 });
const CURSE = makePersistent({ id: 'curse-e', cardId: 'curse' });
const SENTENCE = { id: 'sentence-s', sourcePlayerId: 'me', remainingOwnerTurns: 2, isUpgraded: false };

function boardWith(poisonCounter: number | null): PlayingStateView {
  const persistents = poisonCounter === null ? [INVISIBILITY, CURSE] : [{ ...POISON, counter: poisonCounter }, INVISIBILITY, CURSE];
  return makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ shield: 4, activePersistentEffects: persistents }), pendingSentences: [SENTENCE] } });
}
const BOARD = boardWith(3);

function thumb(cardId: CardId, view: PlayingStateView = BOARD): InPlayThumb {
  const found = inPlayThumbs(view, 'me').find((entry) => entry.instance.cardId === cardId);
  if (found === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`no ${cardId} thumb`);
  }
  return found;
}
const inPlay = (cardId: CardId): CardSheetSource => ({ kind: 'in-play', thumb: thumb(cardId) });
const REGEN = makeCard('regeneration');
const HAND: CardSheetSource = { kind: 'hand', instance: REGEN };
const SPECIAL: CardSheetSource = { kind: 'special', instance: makeCard('poison') };
const SPY: CardSheetSource = { kind: 'spy-reveal', instance: makeCard('strong-attack', { instanceId: 'p1-strong' }), ownerPlayerId: 'p1' };
const QUEUE: CardSheetSource = { kind: 'queue', effectId: 'e9', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false };
const LOG: CardSheetSource = { kind: 'log', cardId: 'spy', isUpgraded: true };

describe('card sheet sources (Appendix A.5, spec v7 §6.6.1)', () => {
  it('maps each source to the cardActions ref it reads', () => {
    expect(cardRefFor(HAND)).toEqual({ zone: 'hand', instance: makeCard('regeneration') });
    expect(cardRefFor(SPECIAL)).toEqual({ zone: 'special', instance: makeCard('poison') });
    expect(cardRefFor(inPlay('poison'))).toEqual({ zone: 'active', ownerPlayerId: 'me', effect: POISON });
    expect(cardRefFor(SPY)).toEqual({ zone: 'read-only', instance: makeCard('strong-attack', { instanceId: 'p1-strong' }) });
    expect(cardRefFor(QUEUE)).toEqual({ zone: 'read-only', instance: { instanceId: 'queue-e9', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false } });
    expect(cardRefFor(LOG)).toEqual({ zone: 'read-only', instance: { instanceId: 'log-spy-up', cardId: 'spy', isUpgraded: true } });
  });

  it('shows the instance face, or a synthetic catalog face for the queue and the log (L56-05)', () => {
    expect(sheetInstanceFor(HAND)).toBe(REGEN);
    expect(sheetInstanceFor(inPlay('shield'))).toEqual({ instanceId: 'active-shield', cardId: 'shield', isUpgraded: false });
    expect(sheetInstanceFor(QUEUE)).toEqual({ instanceId: 'queue-e9', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false });
    expect(sheetInstanceFor({ kind: 'log', cardId: 'spy', isUpgraded: false }).instanceId).toBe('log-spy-base');
  });

  it('titles the sheet with the card name', () => {
    expect([HAND, inPlay('shield'), QUEUE, LOG].map(cardSheetTitle)).toEqual(['Regeneration', 'Shield', 'Basic attack', 'Spy']);
  });

  it('gives the activated glow only to a persistent counter, never to Shield or a Sentence (A.8 ruling 1)', () => {
    expect(cardSheetCounter(inPlay('poison'))).toEqual({ kind: 'card-lives', amount: 3 });
    expect(cardSheetCounter(inPlay('invisibility'))).toEqual({ kind: 'turns', amount: 4 });
    expect([inPlay('shield'), inPlay('sentence'), inPlay('curse'), HAND, SPY, QUEUE].map(cardSheetCounter))
      .toEqual([null, null, null, null, null, null]);
  });

  it('names the V2 context: Active with its count, the Spy reveal, nothing for held, queue or log cards', () => {
    expect([ACTIVE_LABEL, CARD_LIVES_LABEL, TURNS_LEFT_LABEL, SPY_REVEAL_LABEL])
      .toEqual(['Active', 'Card lives', 'Turns left', 'Spy reveal — inspect only']);
    expect(cardSheetContext(inPlay('poison'))).toEqual({ kind: 'active', count: { label: 'Card lives', value: 3 } });
    expect(cardSheetContext(inPlay('invisibility'))).toEqual({ kind: 'active', count: { label: 'Turns left', value: 4 } });
    expect(cardSheetContext(inPlay('sentence'))).toEqual({ kind: 'active', count: { label: 'Turns left', value: 2 } });
    expect(cardSheetContext(inPlay('shield'))).toEqual({ kind: 'active', count: null });
    expect(cardSheetContext(SPY)).toEqual({ kind: 'spy-reveal' });
    expect([HAND, SPECIAL, QUEUE, LOG].map(cardSheetContext)).toEqual([null, null, null, null]);
  });

  it('refreshes an in-play card from the next view and keeps the last face once it is gone', () => {
    const open = inPlay('poison');
    const ticked = liveCardSheetSource(open, boardWith(2));
    expect(ticked.kind === 'in-play' ? ticked.thumb.badge : null).toEqual({ kind: 'card-lives', value: 2 });
    expect(liveCardSheetSource(open, boardWith(null))).toBe(open);
    expect(liveCardSheetSource(HAND, boardWith(2))).toBe(HAND);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-sheets.test.ts` (node):

```ts
/**
 * Arena sheets reducer — Lot 74 plan Appendix A.5; technical spec v7 §6.7 (read-only sheets
 * stack, Esc closes only the top one, Q49) and §6.6.4 (closing sends nothing).
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { inPlayThumbs } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import { makeCard, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import {
  arenaSheetEntries, arenaSheetKey, closeArenaSheet, EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, MAX_CLOSING_SHEETS, openArenaSheet,
  type ArenaSheet,
} from './arena-sheets';
import { makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const SPY: ArenaSheet = { kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy') } };
/** A fresh object with the same key as SPY: the reducer matches by key, not by reference. */
const SPY_AGAIN: ArenaSheet = { kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy') } };
const QUEUE: ArenaSheet = { kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'queue', effectId: 'e9', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false } };
const KIT: ArenaSheet = { kind: 'kit', kitId: 'tactician' };

describe('arena sheets (Appendix A.5, spec v7 §6.7)', () => {
  it('starts with no open and no closing sheet', () => {
    expect(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS).toEqual({ open: [], closing: [] });
    expect(MAX_CLOSING_SHEETS).toBe(2);
  });

  it('keys a card sheet by its face id and a kit sheet by its kit', () => {
    const shield = inPlayThumbs(makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ shield: 4 }) } }), 'me')[0];
    if (shield === undefined) {
      throw new Error('no shield thumb');
    }
    expect([
      arenaSheetKey(SPY),
      arenaSheetKey(QUEUE),
      arenaSheetKey({ kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'log', cardId: 'spy', isUpgraded: false } }),
      arenaSheetKey({ kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'in-play', thumb: shield } }),
      arenaSheetKey(KIT),
    ]).toEqual(['card:spy-1', 'card:queue-e9', 'card:log-spy-base', 'card:active-shield', 'kit:tactician']);
  });

  it('opens sheets oldest first and moves a sheet that is already open to the top', () => {
    const state = [SPY, KIT, SPY_AGAIN].reduce(openArenaSheet, EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS);
    expect(state.open.map(arenaSheetKey)).toEqual(['kit:tactician', 'card:spy-1']);
  });

  it('returns the same state when the top sheet opens again', () => {
    const once = openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, KIT);
    expect(openArenaSheet(once, { kind: 'kit', kitId: 'tactician' })).toBe(once);
  });

  it('moves a closed sheet to closing for its exit, keeping the last two', () => {
    let state = [SPY, QUEUE, KIT].reduce(openArenaSheet, EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS);
    state = closeArenaSheet(state, KIT);
    expect([state.open.map(arenaSheetKey), state.closing.map(arenaSheetKey)])
      .toEqual([['card:spy-1', 'card:queue-e9'], ['kit:tactician']]);
    state = closeArenaSheet(closeArenaSheet(state, QUEUE), SPY);
    expect([state.open, state.closing.map(arenaSheetKey)]).toEqual([[], ['card:queue-e9', 'card:spy-1']]);
  });

  it('takes a reopened sheet out of closing', () => {
    const reopened = openArenaSheet(closeArenaSheet(openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, KIT), KIT), KIT);
    expect([reopened.open.map(arenaSheetKey), reopened.closing]).toEqual([['kit:tactician'], []]);
  });

  it('ignores closing a sheet that is not open', () => {
    const state = openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, SPY);
    expect(closeArenaSheet(state, KIT)).toBe(state);
  });

  it('lists closing sheets, then open sheets, with their keys and open flags', () => {
    const state = closeArenaSheet([SPY, KIT].reduce(openArenaSheet, EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS), SPY);
    expect(arenaSheetEntries(state)).toEqual([
      { sheet: SPY, key: 'card:spy-1', open: false },
      { sheet: KIT, key: 'kit:tactician', open: true },
    ]);
  });
});
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Read-only card sheet — technical spec v7 §6.6.1 (face at sheet size, Cost row, effect and
 * Upgrade block), §5.4, Lot 74 plan ruling 1 (activated look = counter glow; Shield and
 * Sentence never), L56-05 (queue / log: no footer). It is also the behavior replacement that
 * the Owner lot 74 `card-effect-copy` classification row asks for (L51-12).
 */
import type { CardId, PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { glyphRoots, spokenTexts, visibleText } from '../../test/glyph-queries';
import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import type { ActionContext } from '../../view-model/action-lock';
import { inPlayThumbs } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import { makeCard, makePersistent, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { CardSheet } from './card-sheet';
import type { CardSheetSource } from './card-sheet-source';
import { makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const LIVE: ActionContext = { subChoice: null, readOnly: false };
const SENTENCE = { id: 'sentence-s', sourcePlayerId: 'me', remainingOwnerTurns: 2, isUpgraded: false };

function boardWith(poisonCounter: number): PlayingStateView {
  return makeArenaView({
    overrides: {
      self: makeSelf({
        shield: 4,
        activePersistentEffects: [
          makePersistent({ id: 'poison-e', cardId: 'poison', counter: poisonCounter }),
          makePersistent({ id: 'invisibility-e', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 4 }),
        ],
      }),
      pendingSentences: [SENTENCE],
    },
  });
}
const BOARD = boardWith(3);

function inPlay(cardId: CardId): CardSheetSource {
  const thumb = inPlayThumbs(BOARD, 'me').find((entry) => entry.instance.cardId === cardId);
  if (thumb === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`no ${cardId} thumb`);
  }
  return { kind: 'in-play', thumb };
}

function renderSheet(source: CardSheetSource, view: PlayingStateView = BOARD): ReturnType<typeof renderUi> & { onClose: () => void } {
  const onClose = vi.fn();
  return { ...renderUi(<CardSheet open source={source} view={view} ctx={LIVE} onClose={onClose} />), onClose };
}

function part(root: HTMLElement, name: string): HTMLElement {
  const found = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-effect-part="${name}"]`);
  if (found === null) {
    throw new Error(`missing ${name} part`);
  }
  return found;
}

function overlayOf(dialog: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {
  const overlay = dialog.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="sheet-overlay"]');
  if (overlay === null) {
    throw new Error('no sheet overlay');
  }
  return overlay;
}

describe('CardSheet, read-only (spec v7 §6.6.1, L74-02)', () => {
  it('shows a base card at sheet size with Cost, the effect and the Upgrade block', () => {
    renderSheet({ kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('regeneration', { instanceId: 'regen-1' }) });
    const sheet = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Regeneration' });
    expect(sheet.getAttribute('data-arena-sheet')).toBe('card:regen-1');
    expect(within(sheet).getByRole('article', { name: 'Regeneration' }).getAttribute('data-card-size')).toBe('sheet');
    expect(within(part(sheet, 'cost')).getByText('Cost')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(spokenTexts(part(sheet, 'cost'))).toEqual(['3 pts/life']);
    expect(glyphRoots(part(sheet, 'effect'))).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(spokenTexts(part(sheet, 'effect'))).toEqual(['4 lives', '3 pts']);
    expect(visibleText(part(sheet, 'upgrade'))).toBe('Upgrade — Lives cost 2 each instead of 3.');
    expect(within(sheet).queryByText(/Cost:/)).toBeNull();
    expect(within(sheet).queryByText('Choose Use, Upgrade, or Sell.')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('shows upgradeEffect alone for an upgraded card', () => {
    renderSheet({ kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('basic-attack', { isUpgraded: true }) });
    const sheet = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Basic attack' });
    expect(within(sheet).getByRole('article', { name: 'Basic attack upgraded, 3 damage' })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(part(sheet, 'effect').textContent).toBe('Deal 3 damage to an opponent.');
    expect(sheet.querySelector('[data-effect-part="upgrade"]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each([
    { cardId: 'shield', name: 'Shield', count: null },
    { cardId: 'sentence', name: 'Sentence', count: 'Turns left2' },
  ] as const)('never gives $name the activated look; its turns stay a separate badge (A.8 ruling 1)', ({ cardId, name, count }) => {
    renderSheet(inPlay(cardId));
    const sheet = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name });
    const face = within(sheet).getByRole('article', { name });
    expect([face.querySelector('[data-card-layer="glow"]'), face.querySelector('[data-card-counter]')]).toEqual([null, null]);
    expect(within(sheet).getByText('Active')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(sheet).queryByText('Turns left')?.parentElement?.textContent ?? null).toBe(count);
    expect(within(sheet).queryByText('Card lives')).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each([
    { cardId: 'poison', face: 'Poison, 3 card lives', counter: 'card-lives', label: 'Card lives', badge: 'Card lives3' },
    { cardId: 'invisibility', face: 'Invisibility, 4 turns remaining', counter: 'turns', label: 'Turns left', badge: 'Turns left4' },
  ] as const)('shows an active $cardId with its counter glow and its count', ({ cardId, face, counter, label, badge }) => {
    renderSheet(inPlay(cardId));
    const sheet = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: cardId === 'poison' ? 'Poison' : 'Invisibility' });
    const article = within(sheet).getByRole('article', { name: face });
    expect(article.querySelector('[data-card-counter]')?.getAttribute('data-card-counter')).toBe(counter);
    expect(within(sheet).getByText('Active')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(sheet).getByText(label).parentElement?.textContent).toBe(badge);
  });

  it('follows the live counter while it is open', () => {
    const source = inPlay('poison');
    const { rerender } = renderSheet(source);
    rerender(<CardSheet open source={source} view={boardWith(2)} ctx={LIVE} onClose={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('article', { name: 'Poison, 2 card lives' })).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('labels a Spy reveal as inspect only', () => {
    renderSheet({ kind: 'spy-reveal', instance: makeCard('strong-attack', { instanceId: 'p1-strong' }), ownerPlayerId: 'p1' });
    const sheet = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Strong attack' });
    expect(within(sheet).getByText('Spy reveal — inspect only')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(sheet).queryByText('Active')).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each<{ label: string; source: CardSheetSource; title: string; face: string; key: string }>([
    { label: 'queue', source: { kind: 'queue', effectId: 'e9', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false }, title: 'Basic attack', face: 'Basic attack, 1 damage', key: 'card:queue-e9' },
    { label: 'log', source: { kind: 'log', cardId: 'spy', isUpgraded: true }, title: 'Spy', face: 'Spy upgraded', key: 'card:log-spy-up' },
  ])('renders a $label source as the catalog card with no footer (L56-05)', ({ source, title, face, key }) => {
    renderSheet(source);
    const sheet = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: title });
    expect(sheet.getAttribute('data-arena-sheet')).toBe(key);
    expect(within(sheet).getByRole('article', { name: face })).toBeTruthy();
    expect([
      within(sheet).queryByText('Active'),
      within(sheet).queryByText('Spy reveal — inspect only'),
      sheet.querySelector('[data-card-sheet-context]'),
    ]).toEqual([null, null, null]);
    expect(within(sheet).queryAllByRole('button')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('closes through onClose on Esc and on a backdrop press', async () => {
    const { user, onClose } = renderSheet({ kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy') });
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await user.click(overlayOf(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })));
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
});
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Kit sheet — technical spec v7 §6.6.5 (all current sections), Q46; L30-05 (every `KitTraits`
 * field has a rendered section), moved here from the V2 kit inspect onto the arena sheet.
 */
import { getKit, KIT_IDS } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';

import { KIT_TRAIT_SECTION_KEYS } from '../../view-model/kit-inspect-traits';

describe('KitSheet (spec v7 §6.6.5, L74-02)', () => {

    renderUi(<KitSheet open kitId={kitId} onClose={vi.fn()} />);

});
```

Create `apps/client/src/ui/motion/use-long-press.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Long-press — technical spec v7 §6.6.5: about 450 ms, cancelled by more than 8 px of movement,
 * outside the hand only. It opens a read-only sheet and never sends anything.
 */
import { act, fireEvent, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { LONG_PRESS_MOVE_PX, LONG_PRESS_MS, useLongPress } from './use-long-press';

function Harness({ onLongPress, onClick, disabled }: { onLongPress: () => void; onClick: () => void; disabled?: boolean }): ReactElement {
  const handlers = useLongPress({ onLongPress, ...(disabled !== undefined ? { disabled } : {}) });
  return <button type="button" onClick={onClick} {...handlers}>Spy</button>;
}

function setup(disabled?: boolean): { onLongPress: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>; onClick: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>; button: HTMLElement } {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  const onLongPress = vi.fn();
  const onClick = vi.fn();
  renderUi(<Harness onLongPress={onLongPress} onClick={onClick} {...(disabled !== undefined ? { disabled } : {})} />, {
    advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime,
  });
  return { onLongPress, onClick, button: screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }) };
}

function advance(ms: number): void {
  act(() => {
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(ms);
  });
}

function press(button: HTMLElement): void {
  fireEvent.pointerDown(button, { button: 0, pointerId: 1, clientX: 10, clientY: 10 });
}

describe('useLongPress (spec v7 §6.6.5)', () => {
  it('pins 450 ms and 8 px', () => {
    expect([LONG_PRESS_MS, LONG_PRESS_MOVE_PX]).toEqual([450, 8]);
  });

  it('fires once after 450 ms of a still press, not before', () => {
    const { onLongPress, button } = setup();
    press(button);
    advance(449);
    expect(onLongPress).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    advance(1);
    expect(onLongPress).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    fireEvent.pointerUp(button);
    advance(1000);
    expect(onLongPress).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('keeps the press through 8 px of travel and cancels it past 8 px', () => {
    const { onLongPress, button } = setup();
    press(button);
    fireEvent.pointerMove(button, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 18, clientY: 10 });
    advance(450);
    expect(onLongPress).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    fireEvent.pointerUp(button);
    press(button);
    fireEvent.pointerMove(button, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 10, clientY: 19 });
    advance(450);
    expect(onLongPress).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('lets a short tap through as a click and never fires', () => {
    const { onLongPress, onClick, button } = setup();
    press(button);
    advance(200);
    fireEvent.pointerUp(button);
    fireEvent.click(button);
    advance(500);
    expect([onLongPress.mock.calls.length, onClick.mock.calls.length]).toEqual([0, 1]);
  });

  it('swallows the click that ends a long press, and only that one', () => {
    const { onLongPress, onClick, button } = setup();
    press(button);
    advance(450);
    fireEvent.pointerUp(button);
    fireEvent.click(button);
    expect([onLongPress.mock.calls.length, onClick.mock.calls.length]).toEqual([1, 0]);
    fireEvent.click(button);
    expect(onClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('cancels on pointercancel and ignores a secondary button', () => {
    const { onLongPress, button } = setup();
    press(button);
    fireEvent.pointerCancel(button);
    advance(450);
    fireEvent.pointerDown(button, { button: 2, pointerId: 1, clientX: 10, clientY: 10 });
    advance(450);
    expect(onLongPress).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('does nothing while disabled', () => {
    const { onLongPress, button } = setup(true);
    press(button);
    advance(1000);
    expect(onLongPress).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```
- **Long-press** (`ui/motion/use-long-press.ts`, L74-02, spec v7 §6.6.5): `useLongPress({
  onLongPress, disabled? })` returns pointer handlers for the face it opens. A primary-button
  press held for `LONG_PRESS_MS` (450 ms) fires once. Moving the pointer more than
  `LONG_PRESS_MOVE_PX` (8 px), releasing, cancelling or leaving cancels it. `onClickCapture`
  swallows the click that ends a long press, so a tap action never follows it. It applies
  outside the hand only (the hand reads a vertical move as a drag), opens the same read-only
  sheet that Enter or a click opens on the face, and never sends an intent. Motion preference
  does not change it.
```

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] Arena sheets: exit-mounted stack, early log source, kit sections (L74-02)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.6.1, §6.6.4, §6.6.5, §6.7; the L74-02 brief and Watch point; Lot 74
plan Appendix A.5, A.7 and ruling 1; frontend.md "L74 carry-overs".

- **Closed sheets stay mounted for their exit.** `ArenaSheetsState` keeps a `closing` list:
  the last two closed sheets, rendered with `open={false}`. The `Sheet` press shield then
  covers a second tap during the exit. Unmounting at once would let a double tap on a
  backdrop or a sheet button land on the arena: a seat, or Draw. This is the Lot 74 plan
  Appendix A.5 shape: `CardSheet` and `KitSheet` take `open`.
- **The `log` source lands with the card sheet.** The L74-02 Acceptance names log and queue
  sources. Its shape is `{ kind: 'log'; cardId; isUpgraded }`, with the synthetic face
  `log-<cardId>-<up|base>`. The plate's landing card (L74-06) and the log (L74-15) open it.
- **Card sheet keys use the face id:** `card:<sheetInstanceFor(source).instanceId>`. A queue
  effect keys as `card:queue-<effectId>`, so it cannot collide with an in-play persistent
  whose instance id is an effect id.
- **Kit details sections are headed groups.** `ui/kit/kit-details.tsx` drops `aria-labelledby`
  from its `<section>`s, so a kit sheet no longer exposes up to nine region landmarks. Each
  group keeps its `<h3>` for heading navigation. No test asserted the landmark role;
  `kit-details.test.tsx` gains a test that none renders.
- **No `onOpenKit` on the card sheet.** No Part 1 card source carries a kit, and V2's inspect
  never linked one. The kit sheet opens from `KitPortrait` buttons instead: the L74-05 seat
  sheet and the L74-09 self strip.

---
```

```
Closed sheets stay mounted through their exit so the Sheet press shield
covers a double tap; the log source lands early for the Acceptance.
Kit detail groups lose their region landmarks (watch point).

```

**Acceptance map:**
- Base card shows effect plus the Upgrade block; an upgraded card shows `upgradeEffect` only: `shows a base card at sheet size with Cost, the effect and the Upgrade block` and `shows upgradeEffect alone for an upgraded card`.
- Sentence and Shield never request activated art: `never gives $name the activated look; its turns stay a separate badge (A.8 ruling 1)` (Shield and Sentence) and `gives the activated glow only to a persistent counter, never to Shield or a Sentence (A.8 ruling 1)`.
- Active Poison shows its card lives and Invisibility shows turns: `shows an active $cardId with its counter glow and its count` (poison and invisibility).
- Log and queue sources render no footer: `renders a $label source as the catalog card with no footer (L56-05)` (queue and log).
- Long-press fires at 450 ms and is cancelled by 8 px of movement: `fires once after 450 ms of a still press, not before` and `keeps the press through 8 px of travel and cancels it past 8 px`.
- The kit sheet renders a section for every `KitTraits` field (the moved L30-05 assertion): `renders a section for every KitTraits field for %s (L30-05)`, one per kit. The L30-05 guard first lived in `screens/table/kit-inspect-dialog.test.ts` (keys equal `KitTraits`; kept as is). L72-15 moved it onto `ui/kit/kit-details.test.tsx`, and L74-02 re-proves it on the arena sheet, so it holds in the V7 surface the player opens.
- Classification row `card-effect-copy` (Owner lot 74): the first test above. It checks the Cost label with spoken cost, the effect through inline glyphs, no `Cost:`, no 'Choose Use, Upgrade, or Sell.', and the Upgrade block / upgraded cases.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Watch point resolution:** keep the `<h3>` and drop the label. `role="group"` would keep a name association but adds nothing for headings-first navigation in a read-only body with no controls.
- **Long-press threshold:** the press is cancelled only when it moves *more than* 8 px. Spec v7 §6.6.5 says "> 8 px" and is normative over the Acceptance wording "cancelled by 8 px". The test pins 8 px as kept and 9 px as cancelled.
- **The long-press swallows the trailing click** (`onClickCapture`), so a face whose tap selects (shop tile, Part 2) does not also select after a long-press.
- **The in-play card sheet follows the live view** (`liveCardSheetSource`). A Poison counter that ticks while the sheet is open shows the new value. A thumb that left play keeps its last face (read-only).
- **Sentence remaining turns use `Turns left`,** the V2 Invisibility label. V2's Sentence inspect showed only `Active`, but ruling 1 requires the turns as a separate line, and `Turns left` is existing copy.
- **Badge icons:** `life` for card lives and `clock` for turns, the same glyphs as the Card counter badge.
- **`CardSheet` takes `view` and `ctx` from L74-02 on**, so the ArenaScreen call site never changes. `ctx` is unused until L74-08; it is not destructured, so no unused-binding error.
- **The sheet title is the card name only** (`getCard(cardId)?.name ?? 'Card'`). V2 had no upgraded suffix in the title; the face's accessible name says "upgraded".
- **`MAX_CLOSING_SHEETS = 2`**: one close per tap, and two quick closes are the worst case before an exit (220 ms) ends.
- **Re-opening a covered sheet** reorders only the reducer's `open` list. The Sheet stack keeps its own paint order. No Part 1 flow can reopen a covered sheet, because everything under the top sheet is inert.
- **ArenaScreen is not wired in L74-02.** L74-05 adds the sheet host (the state, `openSheet` / `closeSheet` and the `arenaSheetEntries` render switch; Appendix A.5) together with the seat sheet, the first surface that opens a sheet.
- **New strings:** none. `Active`, `Card lives`, `Turns left`, `Spy reveal — inspect only` and `Card` are V2 `card-actions.tsx` copy.

  - From `@card-battle/shared`: `tableRound`, `chatRoundLabel`, `FORFEIT`, `type PlayingStateView`.
  - Tests: `makeArenaProps`, `makeArenaView`, `expectNoArenaIntent` (L74-01); `v7SeatColor` (`ui/palette`).
- Produces:

```ts
// top-bar-model.ts
export const MENU_LABEL = 'Menu';                      // new string (§10.6)
export const NO_TURN_OWNER_LABEL = '—';                // V2 Timers fallback nickname
export const COPY_CODE_LABEL = 'Copy';                 // V2 lobby
export const CODE_COPIED_LABEL = 'Code copied';        // V2 lobby
export const CODE_COPY_FAILED_LABEL = 'Copy failed';   // V2 lobby
/** `chatRoundLabel(tableRound(view.turnSequence, view.players.length))`: 'Round N' (A.8 ruling 2). */
export function topBarRoundLabel(view: PlayingStateView): string;
export type TurnBanner =
  | { readonly kind: 'you' }
  | { readonly kind: 'seat'; readonly playerId: string; readonly nickname: string }
  | { readonly kind: 'none' };
export function turnBannerOf(view: PlayingStateView): TurnBanner;
/** 'Your turn' (TABLE_BANNER_COPY.turn) | "{nick}'s turn" | '—'. */
export function turnBannerText(banner: TurnBanner): string;
/** The active seat's V2 Block line, or null. */
export function activeBlockLabel(view: PlayingStateView): string | null;
/** V2 Timers meta line: `Code ${gameCode} · ${statusLabel}`. */
export function gameCodeLine(view: PlayingStateView, statusLabel: string): string;
export type LeaveConfirmIntent = Exclude<TableFlagIntent, 'hidden'>;
export interface LeaveConfirmChrome { readonly title: string; readonly body: string | null; readonly confirm: string }
export const LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME: Readonly<Record<LeaveConfirmIntent, LeaveConfirmChrome>>;
export interface ArenaFlag { readonly intent: LeaveConfirmIntent; readonly ariaLabel: string }
/** `tableFlagIntent({ readOnly, selfEliminated, playKind, isSpectator })` (A.8 ruling 3); null only for 'hidden'. */
export function arenaFlag(view: PlayingStateView, readOnly: boolean): ArenaFlag | null;

// leave-confirm-sheet.tsx
export interface LeaveConfirmSheetProps {
  open: boolean;
  intent: LeaveConfirmIntent;
  onStay: () => void;
  onConfirm: () => void;
}
export function LeaveConfirmSheet(props: LeaveConfirmSheetProps): ReactElement | null;

// top-bar.tsx
export interface TopBarProps {
  view: PlayingStateView;
  readOnly: boolean;
  /** `STATUS_LABELS[status]` from App: the POV's connection label (Appendix A `global-status-labels`). */
  statusLabel: string;
  onLeave: () => void;
  /** Alive Classic flag confirm: FORFEIT, the socket stays (L43-06). */
  onForfeit: () => void;
  /** Finished board: Game over owns Feedback (L47-03). The live arena omits it and owns its dialog. */
  onOpenFeedback?: () => void;
}
export function TopBar(props: TopBarProps): ReactElement;
```
