```ts
/**
 * Top bar model — technical spec v7 §6.1 item 1, §6.12; Lot 74 plan rulings 2 and 3; Appendix A
 * `turn-strip-timers`, `table-leave-forfeit`, `global-status-labels`.
 */
import type { PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { makeArenaView } from './test-view';
import {
  activeBlockLabel, arenaFlag, CODE_COPIED_LABEL, CODE_COPY_FAILED_LABEL, COPY_CODE_LABEL, gameCodeLine,
  LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME, MENU_LABEL, topBarRoundLabel, turnBannerOf, turnBannerText, type TurnBanner,
} from './top-bar-model';

function withActive(patch: { blockTurnsRemaining: number; blockAttacksForbidden: boolean }): PlayingStateView {
  const base = makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' } });
  return { ...base, players: base.players.map((player) => (player.id === 'p1' ? { ...player, ...patch } : player)) };
}

describe('top bar model (spec v7 §6.1)', () => {
  it('counts the round like the log and the chat (ruling 2)', () => {
    expect(topBarRoundLabel(makeArenaView({ overrides: { turnSequence: 0 } }))).toBe('Round 1');
    expect(topBarRoundLabel(makeArenaView({ overrides: { turnSequence: 7 } }))).toBe('Round 3');
    expect(topBarRoundLabel(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8, overrides: { turnSequence: 15 } }))).toBe('Round 2');
  });

  it('names the turn owner: you, another seat, or nobody', () => {
    expect(turnBannerOf(makeArenaView())).toEqual({ kind: 'you' });
    expect(turnBannerOf(makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p2' } })))
      .toEqual({ kind: 'seat', playerId: 'p2', nickname: 'p2' });
    expect(turnBannerOf(makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: null } }))).toEqual({ kind: 'none' });
    expect(turnBannerOf(makeArenaView({ pov: 'walk-in' }))).toEqual({ kind: 'seat', playerId: 'me', nickname: 'me' });
    const banners: readonly TurnBanner[] = [{ kind: 'you' }, { kind: 'seat', playerId: 'p2', nickname: 'p2' }, { kind: 'none' }];
    expect(banners.map(turnBannerText)).toEqual(['Your turn', "p2's turn", '—']);
  });

  it.each([
    { turns: 2, ban: false, label: 'Block chain · 2 turns left' },
    { turns: 1, ban: false, label: 'Block chain · 1 turn left' },
    { turns: 0, ban: true, label: 'Block · attacks banned this turn' },
    { turns: 2, ban: true, label: 'Block chain · 2 turns left' },
    { turns: 0, ban: false, label: null },
  ])('gives the active seat the Block line $label ($turns turns, ban $ban)', ({ turns, ban, label }) => {
    expect(activeBlockLabel(withActive({ blockTurnsRemaining: turns, blockAttacksForbidden: ban }))).toBe(label);
  });

  it('derives the flag from tableFlagIntent with isSpectator (ruling 3)', () => {
    expect(arenaFlag(makeArenaView(), false)).toEqual({ intent: 'forfeit', ariaLabel: 'Forfeit' });
    for (const pov of ['walk-in', 'eliminated', 'reanimating'] as const) {
      expect(arenaFlag(makeArenaView({ pov }), false)).toEqual({ intent: 'leaveTable', ariaLabel: 'Leave table' });
    }
    expect(arenaFlag(makeArenaView(), true)).toEqual({ intent: 'returnHome', ariaLabel: 'Return home' });
  });

  it('keeps the V2 confirm copy for every flag intent', () => {
    expect(LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME).toEqual({
      forfeit: { title: 'Leave the game?', body: 'That counts as a forfeit.', confirm: 'Forfeit' },
      leaveTable: { title: 'Leave the table?', body: null, confirm: 'Leave' },
      returnHome: { title: 'Return home?', body: 'Leave this finished game and go back to the home screen.', confirm: 'Return home' },
      skipTutorial: { title: 'Skip the tutorial?', body: 'You will return to the hub. This is not a forfeit.', confirm: 'Skip tutorial' },
    });
  });

  it('prints the game code with the connection label, and the menu copy', () => {
    expect(gameCodeLine(makeArenaView(), 'Reconnecting…')).toBe('Code TEST · Reconnecting…');
    expect([MENU_LABEL, COPY_CODE_LABEL, CODE_COPIED_LABEL, CODE_COPY_FAILED_LABEL])
      .toEqual(['Menu', 'Copy', 'Code copied', 'Copy failed']);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/top-bar.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Top bar — technical spec v7 §6.1 item 1, §6.12 (leave / forfeit confirm, finished board),
 * §5.6 (icon-only controls keep aria-labels); Lot 74 plan rulings 2 and 3; L43-05 (the first
 * flag tap never leaves; Esc / overlay = Stay), L43-06 (Forfeit keeps the socket), L57-01 (`!`,
 * not the word), L47-03 (finished-board Feedback belongs to Game over). Every test that opens
 * something asserts that no intent fired (§6.6.4).
 */
import { fireEvent, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi, type RenderUiResult } from '../../test/render';
import { HOW_TO_PLAY_SEEN_KEY } from '../../ui/help-storage';
import { v7SeatColor } from '../../ui/palette';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

function renderArena(overrides: Partial<ArenaScreenProps> = {}): RenderUiResult & { props: ArenaScreenProps } {
  const props = makeArenaProps(overrides);
  return { ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />), props };
}

function topBar(container: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {
  const found = container.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-arena-region="top-bar"]');
  if (found === null) {
    throw new Error('no top bar');
  }
  return found;
}

function overlayOf(dialog: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {
  const overlay = dialog.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="sheet-overlay"]');
  if (overlay === null) {
    throw new Error('no sheet overlay');
  }
  return overlay;
}

/** A hex as happy-dom reads it back (PlayerName test pattern). */
function cssColor(hex: string): string {
  const probe = document.createElement('span');
  probe.style.color = hex;
  return probe.style.color;
}

beforeEach(() => {
  localStorage.clear();
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('TopBar banner (spec v7 §6.1 item 1)', () => {
  it('shows the round and Your turn on the POV turn, with no Block line', () => {
    const { container } = renderArena({ view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { turnSequence: 7 } }) });
    const bar = topBar(container);
    expect(within(bar).getByText('Round 3')).toBeTruthy();
    const banner = within(bar).getByRole('heading', { level: 2 });
    expect([banner.textContent, banner.querySelector('[data-seat-index]')]).toEqual(['Your turn', null]);
    expect(within(bar).queryByText(/Block/)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows {Nick}'s turn with the nickname in its seat hue", () => {
    const { container } = renderArena({ view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p2' } }) });
    const banner = within(topBar(container)).getByRole('heading', { level: 2 });
    expect(banner.textContent).toBe("p2's turn");
    const nick = banner.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-seat-index]');
    expect([nick?.textContent, nick?.getAttribute('data-seat-index'), nick?.style.color]).toEqual(['p2', '2', cssColor(v7SeatColor(2))]);
  });

  it('shows — when no seat is active', () => {
    const { container } = renderArena({ view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: null } }) });
    expect(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('heading', { level: 2 }).textContent).toBe('—');
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'a Block chain', patch: { blockTurnsRemaining: 2, blockAttacksForbidden: false }, text: 'Block chain · 2 turns left' },
    { label: 'an attack ban', patch: { blockTurnsRemaining: 0, blockAttacksForbidden: true }, text: 'Block · attacks banned this turn' },
  ])('shows the active seat’s Block line for $label', ({ patch, text }) => {
    const base = makeArenaView({ overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' } });
    const view = { ...base, players: base.players.map((player) => (player.id === 'p1' ? { ...player, ...patch } : player)) };
    const { container } = renderArena({ view });
    expect(within(topBar(container)).getByText(text)).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe('TopBar flag (spec v7 §6.12, L43-05, L43-06)', () => {
  it('opens the forfeit confirm on the first flag tap, with Stay focused, and sends nothing', async () => {
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena();
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Forfeit' }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Leave the game?' });
    expect(within(confirm).getByText('That counts as a forfeit.')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(within(confirm).getByRole('button', { name: 'Stay' }));
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it.each(['Stay', 'Esc', 'the backdrop'] as const)('closes the confirm on %s and sends nothing', async (way) => {
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena();
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Forfeit' }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Leave the game?' });
    if (way === 'Stay') {
      await user.click(within(confirm).getByRole('button', { name: 'Stay' }));
    } else if (way === 'Esc') {
      await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    } else {
      await user.click(overlayOf(confirm));
    }
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Leave the game?' })).toBeNull();
    });
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('forfeits once on Forfeit, even on a double tap, and never leaves', async () => {
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena();
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Forfeit' }));
    const forfeit = within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Leave the game?' })).getByRole('button', { name: 'Forfeit' });
    fireEvent.click(forfeit);
    fireEvent.click(forfeit);
    expect(props.onForfeit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(props.onLeave).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it.each(['walk-in', 'eliminated'] as const)('gives a %s POV Leave table, which leaves without forfeiting', async (pov) => {
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena({ view: makeArenaView({ pov, overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' } }) });
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Leave table' }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Leave the table?' });
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(within(confirm).getByRole('button', { name: 'Leave' }));
    expect(props.onLeave).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(props.onForfeit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('turns the finished-board flag into Return home, which leaves', async () => {
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena({ readOnly: true, onOpenFeedback: vi.fn(), onShowStats: vi.fn() });
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Return home' }));
    const confirm = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Return home?' });
    expect(within(confirm).getByText('Leave this finished game and go back to the home screen.')).toBeTruthy();
    await user.click(within(confirm).getByRole('button', { name: 'Return home' }));
    expect(props.onLeave).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(props.onForfeit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('TopBar Feedback and menu (spec v7 §6.1, §6.12)', () => {
  it('names the Feedback control Feedback with no visible word, and opens the live Feedback dialog', async () => {
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena();
    const feedback = within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Feedback' });
    expect(feedback.textContent).toBe('');
    await user.click(feedback);
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Feedback' })).toBeTruthy();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('hands Feedback to the finished-board parent and opens no dialog of its own', async () => {
    const onOpenFeedback = vi.fn();
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena({ readOnly: true, onOpenFeedback });
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Feedback' }));
    expect(onOpenFeedback).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Feedback' })).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it.each([
    { way: 'Skip', seen: '1' },
    { way: 'Got it', seen: '1' },
    { way: 'Esc', seen: null },
  ] as const)('marks How to play seen from the menu: $way → $seen', async ({ way, seen }) => {
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena();
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Menu' }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Menu' })).getByRole('button', { name: 'How to play' }));
    const howTo = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'How to play' });
    if (way === 'Esc') {
      await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    } else {
      await user.click(within(howTo).getByRole('button', { name: way }));
    }
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'How to play' })).toBeNull();
    });
    expect(localStorage.getItem(HOW_TO_PLAY_SEEN_KEY)).toBe(seen);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('shows the game code with the connection label and copies the code', async () => {
    const { container, user, props } = renderArena();
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Menu' }));
    const menu = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Menu' });
    expect(menu.getAttribute('data-arena-sheet')).toBe('menu');
    expect(within(menu).getByText('Code TEST · Connected')).toBeTruthy();
    await user.click(within(menu).getByRole('button', { name: 'Copy' }));
    expect(await within(menu).findByText('Code copied')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(await navigator.clipboard.readText()).toBe('TEST');
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('reports a failed copy', async () => {
    const { container, user } = renderArena();
    vi.spyOn(navigator.clipboard, 'writeText').mockRejectedValue(new Error('denied'));
    await user.click(within(topBar(container)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Menu' }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Menu' })).getByRole('button', { name: 'Copy' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Copy failed')).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`renderUi`'s `userEvent.setup()` installs user-event's clipboard stub on `navigator.clipboard` (`attachClipboardStubToView`), so `readText` returns what the arena wrote. `RenderUiResult` is exported from `test/render.tsx`.

Create `apps/client/src/net/use-room-connection.forfeit.behavior.test.ts`:

```ts
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * FORFEIT intent behavior — L43-06 / technical spec v6 §6.3; spec v7 §12.1. This is the
 * replacement (1) of the Owner lot 74 `forfeit` classification row. The source-text test
 * `use-room-connection.forfeit.test.ts` stays, because the hook does not change.
 */
import { CLIENT_READY, FORFEIT } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import '../test/render'; // act environment and per-test cleanup (L72-06)
import { useRoomConnection } from './use-room-connection';

const fake = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  send: vi.fn<(type: string, payload?: unknown) => void>(),
  leave: vi.fn<(consented?: boolean) => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.resolve()),
  onDrop: vi.fn<(callback: () => void) => void>(),
}));

vi.mock('@colyseus/sdk', () => ({
  Client: class {
    create(): Promise<unknown> {
      return Promise.resolve({
        roomId: 'ABCDEF',
        reconnectionToken: 'token',
        reconnection: { enabled: false, maxRetries: 0, maxDelay: 0, minDelay: 0 },
        send: fake.send,
        leave: fake.leave,
        onDrop: fake.onDrop,
        onMessage: () => undefined,
        onError: () => undefined,
        onReconnect: () => undefined,
        onLeave: () => undefined,
      });
    }
  },
}));

describe('forfeit intent behavior (L43-06)', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks(); // keeps `leave`'s resolved implementation, clears the recorded calls
  });

  it('sends FORFEIT once and keeps the room and the connection', async () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useRoomConnection());
    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.createGame('Ada');
    });
    expect(result.current.status).toBe('connected');
    expect(fake.send).toHaveBeenCalledWith(CLIENT_READY);
    act(() => {
      result.current.forfeit();
    });
    expect(fake.send.mock.calls.filter(([type]) => type === FORFEIT)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(fake.leave).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(result.current.status).toBe('connected');
  });

  it('still reconnects on a drop after a forfeit (intentionalLeave stays unset)', async () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useRoomConnection());
    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.createGame('Ada');
    });
    act(() => {
      result.current.forfeit();
    });
    const drop = fake.onDrop.mock.calls.at(-1)?.[0];
    if (drop === undefined) {
      throw new Error('onDrop was not registered');
    }
    act(() => {
      drop();
    });
    expect(result.current.status).toBe('reconnecting');
  });
});
```

Test counts:
- `top-bar-model.test.ts`: 10 (the Block `it.each` counts as 5).
- `top-bar.test.tsx`: 20. The Block, close and How to play `it.each` blocks count as 2, 3 and 3; the Leave table one as 2.
- `use-room-connection.forfeit.behavior.test.ts`: 2.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and see them fail.** `pnpm vitest run apps/client/src/screens/arena/top-bar-model.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/top-bar.test.tsx apps/client/src/net/use-room-connection.forfeit.behavior.test.ts`. Expected:
  - `top-bar-model.test.ts` errors on `Failed to resolve import "./top-bar-model"`.
  - `top-bar.test.tsx` fails 20 of 20: no `Round 3`, no heading, no `Forfeit` / `Feedback` / `Menu` buttons in the top-bar region.
  - The hook test **passes 2 of 2 on this first run.** It pins existing hook behavior (classification replacement), so it has no red step. Say so in the commit body.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**
  - **`top-bar-model.ts`** (pure; the header cites spec v7 §6.1 item 1, §6.12, rulings 2 and 3, and the V2 sources of each string):
    - `topBarRoundLabel`: `chatRoundLabel(tableRound(view.turnSequence, view.players.length))`.
    - `turnBannerOf`:
      - `currentTurnPlayerId === view.you` gives `you`.
      - `null`, or an id with no player, gives `none`.
      - Otherwise `seat` with that player's id and nickname.
    - `turnBannerText`: `you` gives `TABLE_BANNER_COPY.turn`; `seat` gives `` `${nickname}'s turn` ``; `none` gives `NO_TURN_OWNER_LABEL`.
    - `activeBlockLabel`: copied verbatim from V2 `screens/table.tsx` `blockStatusLabel`, which the import rule bars:
      - `blockTurnsRemaining > 0` gives `` `Block chain · ${n} turn${n === 1 ? '' : 's'} left` ``.
      - Else `blockAttacksForbidden` gives `'Block · attacks banned this turn'`.
      - Else `null`.
      - It reads the player whose id is `view.currentTurnPlayerId`.
    - `gameCodeLine`: `` `Code ${view.gameCode} · ${statusLabel}` ``.
    - `LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME` maps the four intents to the `table-copy.ts` constants (the values the model test pins). `leaveTable` has `body: null`.
    - `arenaFlag`:
      - `selfEliminated = view.players.some((player) => player.isYou && player.isEliminated)`.
      - `intent = tableFlagIntent({ readOnly, selfEliminated, playKind: view.playKind, ...(view.isSpectator === true ? { isSpectator: true } : {}) })`.
      - Return `null` for `'hidden'`.
      - Otherwise return `{ intent, ariaLabel }`, with `ariaLabel = tableFlagAriaLabel(intent, { forfeit: FORFEIT_ARIA_LABEL, leaveTable: LEAVE_TABLE_ARIA_LABEL, returnHome: RETURN_HOME_ARIA_LABEL, skipTutorial: SKIP_TUTORIAL_ARIA_LABEL })`. It is never null here; narrow it with a guard, not a cast.
    - Never call `tableFlagLeaveAction` (ruling 3).
  - **`leave-confirm-sheet.tsx`**: a V7 `Dialog` (read-only Sheet, placement `auto`; spec v7 §6.12 "confirm sheet"). The body has no control, so Stay, the first action, takes the initial focus (L43-05).

```tsx
const chrome = LEAVE_CONFIRM_CHROME[intent];
return (
  <Dialog
    open={open}
    title={chrome.title}
    onClose={onStay}
    dataAttributes={{ 'data-arena-sheet': 'leave-confirm', 'data-flag-intent': intent }}
    actions={
      <>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={onStay}>{STAY_LABEL}</Button>
        <Button variant="danger" onClick={onConfirm}>{chrome.confirm}</Button>
      </>
    }
  >
    {chrome.body !== null ? <p className="font-v7-body text-v7-body text-v7-text">{chrome.body}</p> : null}
  </Dialog>
);
```

  - **`top-bar.tsx`**:
    - Local state: `menuOpen`, `howToPlayOpen`, `feedbackOpen`, `confirmOpen`, and `copy: 'idle' | 'copied' | 'failed'`; plus the `confirmed` ref latch (Confirm, below).
    - Derived on every render: `banner`, `block`, `flag = arenaFlag(view, readOnly)`. The confirm always follows the live intent.
    - Returns a fragment. The sheets and dialogs are siblings of the bar, never inside a clickable element (portal bubbling).
    - Bar:
      1. Left: a `data-top-bar="turn"` panel, `min-w-0 flex-1 bg-v7-panel px-3 py-1.5 ${CHROME_SHAPE_SM_CLASS}`. Seat names stand on `--panel`, never on the ground. Inside it:
         - `<p data-top-bar="round">` with the round label.
         - `<h2 data-top-bar="banner" title={turnBannerText(banner)} className="truncate font-v7-display text-v7-title leading-tight">`. Its content is `Your turn`; or `<PlayerName nickname playerId view possessive /> turn` (with a literal space before `turn`); or `—`.
         - `<p data-top-bar="block">` when `block !== null`.
         - Last, the clock slot `<div data-zone="timers" data-hint-anchor="your-turn" />` with the comment `{/* Clock slot: L74-21 (spec v7 §6.10). */}`.
      2. Right: a `flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1` row with three 44 px `IconButton`s, each `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, in §6.1 order. L74-16 inserts Chat before Feedback.
         - `aria-label={FEEDBACK_ARIA_LABEL} icon="feedback"`: calls `onOpenFeedback()` when defined, else `setFeedbackOpen(true)`.
         - The flag, when `flag !== null`: `aria-label={flag.ariaLabel} icon="flag"`, `onClick` runs `confirmed.current = false; setConfirmOpen(true);` (it re-arms the latch below).
         - `aria-label={MENU_LABEL} icon="menu"`: sets `setCopy('idle')` and `setMenuOpen(true)`.
    - Menu: `<Sheet open={menuOpen} mode="read-only" placement="bottom" title={MENU_LABEL} onClose={closeMenu} dataAttributes={{ 'data-arena-sheet': 'menu' }}>`, whose body is a `flex flex-col gap-3` list:
      - `<Button variant="quiet" icon="help" fullWidth>` labelled `HOW_TO_PLAY_ARIA_LABEL`. On click: `setMenuOpen(false); setHowToPlayOpen(true);`.
      - A row: `<p className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{gameCodeLine(view, statusLabel)}</p>` and `<Button variant="quiet" icon="copy">{COPY_CODE_LABEL}</Button>`. The button runs `void (async () => { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(view.gameCode); setCopy('copied'); } catch { setCopy('failed'); } })()`.
      - `<p role="status">` holding `CODE_COPIED_LABEL`, `CODE_COPY_FAILED_LABEL` or nothing.
      - L74-15 (Log) and L74-22 (Table rules) append their entries to this list; coach (L75) and Sound / Vibration (L79) are not added.
    - Confirm:
      - `{flag !== null ? <LeaveConfirmSheet open={confirmOpen} intent={flag.intent} onStay={() => { setConfirmOpen(false); }} onConfirm={confirmLeave} /> : null}`.
      - A ref latch: `const confirmed = useRef(false);`. A state guard (`if (!confirmOpen) return;`) is not enough: while the `Dialog` exits, `AnimatePresence` keeps rendering its last element with its stale props (`ui/primitives/presence-layer.tsx`), so a second synchronous click runs the old closure in which `confirmOpen` is still `true`. A ref is read at call time, as Sheet's own `live` ref and L74-08's `committedFor` are.
      - `confirmLeave`: `if (confirmed.current || flag === null) return;` then `confirmed.current = true;` (a second tap, even on the exiting confirm's stale handler, sends nothing), then `setConfirmOpen(false)`, then `flag.intent === 'forfeit' ? onForfeit() : onLeave()`, written as an `if` with braces.
    - `<HowToPlayDialog open={howToPlayOpen} onClose={(reason) => { if (reason === 'skip' || reason === 'got-it') { markHowToPlaySeen(); } setHowToPlayOpen(false); }} />`.
    - `{onOpenFeedback === undefined ? <FeedbackDialog open={feedbackOpen} mode="manual" screen="table" gameCode={view.gameCode} playKind={view.playKind} actionLog={view.actionLog} {...nickname} onDismiss={() => { setFeedbackOpen(false); }} /> : null}`, where `nickname` is spread only when `view.players.find((player) => player.isYou)` exists. A walk-in sends no nickname (Appendix A `global-feedback-entry-points`).
  - **`arena-screen.tsx`**:
    - Replace the top-bar region's content with `<TopBar view={view} readOnly={readOnly} statusLabel={props.statusLabel} onLeave={props.onLeave} onForfeit={props.onForfeit} {...(props.onOpenFeedback !== undefined ? { onOpenFeedback: props.onOpenFeedback } : {})} />`.
    - Remove the bare `data-zone="timers"` div; `TopBar` renders it.
    - `onLeave` and `onForfeit` are not `ArenaIntent`s (Appendix A.6), so `TopBar` calls them directly from the explicit confirm button.

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] Arena flag confirm follows the live flag intent (L74-03)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.12 ("Leave / forfeit: confirm sheet"); Lot 74 plan ruling 3;
Appendix A `table-leave-forfeit`; L43-05, L43-06.

- V2 stored the flag intent when the flag was tapped (`setLeaveConfirm(flagIntent)`). The arena
  re-derives it on every render from `tableFlagIntent(...)` with `isSpectator`. If the POV is
  eliminated while the forfeit confirm is open, the same confirm becomes "Leave the table?",
  and its button leaves instead of sending a FORFEIT the server would reject.
- The confirm button latches on its first press: a ref, reset when the flag opens the confirm.
  A double tap therefore sends one FORFEIT even when the second press reaches the stale handler
  the exiting confirm keeps through its exit (AnimatePresence renders its last props); a state
  flag would not hold, because that handler closes over the old state.
- With no active seat, the banner reads `—` alone. V2 printed "—'s turn"; the brief names `—`.

---
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Banner copy and seat color match each state: `shows the round and Your turn on the POV turn, with no Block line`, `shows {Nick}'s turn with the nickname in its seat hue`, `shows — when no seat is active`, and the model test `names the turn owner: you, another seat, or nobody`.
- Both Block variants render: `shows the active seat’s Block line for $label` (chain and ban) and the model `gives the active seat the Block line …` (5 cases).
- The first flag tap opens the confirm and sends nothing: `opens the forfeit confirm on the first flag tap, with Stay focused, and sends nothing`.
- Stay and Esc send nothing: `closes the confirm on %s and sends nothing` (Stay, Esc, the backdrop).
- Forfeit calls `onForfeit` once and never `onLeave`: `forfeits once on Forfeit, even on a double tap, and never leaves`. The hook-level FORFEIT, socket kept, is `sends FORFEIT once and keeps the room and the connection`.
- The spectator variant calls `onLeave`: `gives a %s POV Leave table, which leaves without forfeiting` (walk-in and eliminated).
- `!` has aria-label 'Feedback' and no visible word: `names the Feedback control Feedback with no visible word, and opens the live Feedback dialog`.
- How to play Skip marks it seen: `marks How to play seen from the menu: $way → $seen` (Skip and Got it mark it; Esc does not).
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **The flag intent is derived live, not stored at tap** (decisions.md entry above).
- **How to play lives in the menu,** per spec v7 §6.1. V2's visible `?` is gone; the visible icons are Chat (L74-16), `!`, the flag and Menu.
- **Copy feedback is an inline polite status** (`Code copied` / `Copy failed`, the V2 lobby words), not a second dialog over the menu.
- **Stay is `primary` (`--cta`) and the confirm is `danger`.** V2 had Stay green and the confirm red; V7's safe default is the primary fill.
- **The finished board keeps Return home** through `onLeave` (EndScreen's `requestLeave`, ask-once) and hands Feedback to `onOpenFeedback`. The arena then renders no `FeedbackDialog`.
- **Pure derivations live in `top-bar-model.ts`.** The V2 Block and code-line strings live inline in `screens/table.tsx` / `timers.tsx`, which the import rule bars, so they are copied verbatim there (reuse, not new copy).
- **The banner `<h2>` truncates as one line, with the full text in `title`.** A 24-character nickname keeps its full text content and accessible name.
- **The forfeit hook behavior test is in scope here.** The Owner lot 74 classification row assigns the replacement to the lot that rebuilds the flag caller.
- **It sets the `net/` hook-test pattern** (controller ruling): `renderHook` from `@testing-library/react` in a happy-dom `.test.ts`, with a `vi.hoisted` fake room inside `vi.mock('@colyseus/sdk')`, named `use-room-connection.forfeit.behavior.test.ts` so it shares no base name with an existing `.ts` / `.tsx` test. L74-13's `use-room-connection.clear-spy.behavior.test.ts` follows it with its own inline fake.
- **New strings:** `Menu`, used as the menu `IconButton` aria-label and the menu sheet title. Everything else is V2 copy: `Round N` (`chatRoundLabel`), `Your turn`, `{Nick}'s turn`, `—`, the Block lines, `Code {code} · {status}`, `Copy`, `Code copied`, `Copy failed`, `How to play`, `Feedback` and every flag and confirm string in `table-copy.ts`.
- **Designer question:** whether the menu control should be named `Menu` (default) or `More`.

```ts
// use-arena-layout-box.ts
/** The visible viewport (it picks the mode) and the arena region's own box (it sizes the seats), spec v7 §6.4. 0×0 until the region is measured. */
export function useArenaLayoutBox(ref: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>): LayoutBox;

// seat-geometry.ts
/** The drawn seat: the portrait anatomy in every mode until L75 (L74-04 brief). */
export const SEAT_RENDER_BOX: Size; // = SEAT_BOX.portrait
/** Gutter between grid seats and above the first grid row: the §6.4 minimum gap. */
export const SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX: number; // = SEAT_MIN_GAP_PX
/** Seat centres in arena pixels, in drawing order: the arc placements as given, or the grid rows laid out. */
export function seatCenters(layout: SeatLayout, arena: Size): readonly SeatPlacement[];
/** The seat box around a centre (`SEAT_RENDER_BOX`). */
export function seatRect(center: SeatPlacement): Rect;

// seat-badges.ts
export const SEAT_MORE_STATUSES_LABEL = 'more statuses';
export const ABSORB_WINDOW_LABEL = 'Absorber window';
export interface SeatBadgeView {
  icon: IconName;
  /** Spoken and `title` words on the seat; the visible label in the seat sheet. */
  label: string;
  /** The short value printed beside the icon on the seat, else null. */
  value: string | null;
}
export function seatBadgeView(badge: SeatBadge): SeatBadgeView;

// opponent-seat.tsx
export interface OpponentSeatProps {
  model: SeatModel;
  /** The seat centre in arena pixels (`seatCenters`). */
  center: SeatPlacement;
  /** This seat's sheet is open (L74-05): a `--cta` ring; L74-06 lights its arrows. */
  tapped: boolean;
  onOpen: (playerId: string) => void;
}
export function OpponentSeat(props: OpponentSeatProps): ReactElement;

// arena-seats.tsx (Appendix A.7)
export interface ArenaSeatsProps {
  model: SeatsModel;
  /** The measured arena box `model` was computed for. */
  arena: Size;
  /** The seat whose sheet is open, else null. */
  tappedSeatId: string | null;
  /** A seat tap. Optional until L74-05 wires it to the seat sheet; without it a tap does nothing. */
  onOpenSeat?: (playerId: string) => void;
  // L74-10 adds `targeting?: SeatTargeting`; L74-12 adds `dropHighlight?`. No placeholder type here.
}
export function ArenaSeats(props: ArenaSeatsProps): ReactElement;
```

```ts
/** Seat geometry: spec v7 §6.4 (arc placements, grid rows `ceil(n/2)` over `floor(n/2)`, 8 px gap), L74-04. */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { SEAT_BOX, type SeatLayout } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX, SEAT_RENDER_BOX, seatCenters, seatRect } from './seat-geometry';

const ARENA = { width: 360, height: 320 };

describe('seat geometry (§6.4)', () => {
  it('draws the portrait seat box with the §6.4 gap', () => {
    expect(SEAT_RENDER_BOX).toEqual(SEAT_BOX.portrait);
    expect(SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX).toBe(8);
  });

  it('keeps the arc placements of the model', () => {
    const layout: SeatLayout = {
      kind: 'arc',
      mode: 'portrait',
      placements: [{ playerId: 'p1', centerX: 59, centerY: 179 }, { playerId: 'p2', centerX: 195, centerY: 46 }],
    };
    expect(seatCenters(layout, ARENA)).toEqual(layout.placements);
  });

  it('lays a 4 + 3 grid out centred, row by row, in seat order', () => {
    const layout: SeatLayout = { kind: 'grid', mode: 'portrait', rows: [['p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4'], ['p5', 'p6', 'p7']] };
    expect(seatCenters(layout, ARENA)).toEqual([
      { playerId: 'p1', centerX: 54, centerY: 54 },
      { playerId: 'p2', centerX: 138, centerY: 54 },
      { playerId: 'p3', centerX: 222, centerY: 54 },
      { playerId: 'p4', centerX: 306, centerY: 54 },
      { playerId: 'p5', centerX: 96, centerY: 154 },
      { playerId: 'p6', centerX: 180, centerY: 154 },
      { playerId: 'p7', centerX: 264, centerY: 154 },
    ]);
  });

  it('boxes a seat around its centre; a 4-seat row keeps 16 px from both 360 px edges', () => {
    expect(seatRect({ playerId: 'p1', centerX: 54, centerY: 54 })).toEqual({ left: 16, top: 8, width: 76, height: 92 });
    expect(seatRect({ playerId: 'p4', centerX: 306, centerY: 54 }).left + 76).toBe(344);
  });
});
```

```ts
/** Seat badge words: spec v7 §6.2, §11 (Q47); V2 flow-status and Draw words reused (L74-04). */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { SeatBadge } from '../../view-model/seats';
import { makePersistent } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { seatBadgeView, type SeatBadgeView } from './seat-badges';

const SENTENCE = { id: 's1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', remainingOwnerTurns: 2, isUpgraded: false };

describe('seatBadgeView (§6.2)', () => {
  it.each<[string, SeatBadge, SeatBadgeView]>([
    ['the eye', { kind: 'spying-on-you' }, { icon: 'spying-on-you', label: 'Spying on you', value: null }],
    ['a Block chain', { kind: 'block', turnsRemaining: 2 }, { icon: 'block', label: 'Block · 2 left', value: '2' }],
    ['the last Block turn', { kind: 'block', turnsRemaining: 0 }, { icon: 'block', label: 'Block · attacks banned', value: null }],
    ['Invisibility', { kind: 'invisibility', effect: makePersistent({ id: 'i1', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 3 }) },
      { icon: 'invisibility', label: 'Invisibility, 3 turns remaining', value: '3' }],
    ['a ticking Sentence', { kind: 'sentence', sentence: SENTENCE }, { icon: 'sentence-ticking', label: 'Sentence, 2 turns remaining', value: '2' }],
    ['an upgraded Shield', { kind: 'shield', isUpgraded: true }, { icon: 'shield-active', label: 'Shield upgraded', value: null }],
    ['Poison', { kind: 'persistent', effect: makePersistent({ id: 'x1', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 }) },
      { icon: 'persistent', label: 'Poison, 3 card lives', value: '3' }],
    ['a Curse (no counter)', { kind: 'persistent', effect: makePersistent({ id: 'x2', cardId: 'curse' }) },
      { icon: 'persistent', label: 'Curse', value: null }],
    ['Duplication', { kind: 'duplication' }, { icon: 'duplication-active', label: 'Duplication active', value: null }],
    ['a Gambler draw gain (Q47)', { kind: 'draw-gain', amount: 37 }, { icon: 'draw', label: 'Draw, plus 37 pts', value: '+37' }],
    ['a pending Reanimation', { kind: 'reanimation', isUpgraded: false }, { icon: 'reanimation-armed', label: 'Reanimating', value: null }],
    ['the Absorber window', { kind: 'absorb-window' }, { icon: 'absorb-window', label: 'Absorber window', value: null }],
  ])('%s', (_name, badge, expected) => {
    expect(seatBadgeView(badge)).toEqual(expected);
  });
});
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/** useArenaLayoutBox: spec v7 §6.4 (the visible box picks the mode; the arena sizes the seats), L74-04. */
import { act, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { useRef } from 'react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { useArenaLayoutBox } from './use-arena-layout-box';

/** happy-dom's ResizeObserver never fires, so the test fires it by hand (as `ui/chat/chat-log.test.tsx` does). */
class FakeResizeObserver {
  static instances: FakeResizeObserver[] = [];
  readonly observe = vi.fn<(target: Element) => void>();
  readonly unobserve = vi.fn<(target: Element) => void>();
  readonly disconnect = vi.fn<() => void>();
  constructor(private readonly callback: ResizeObserverCallback) {
    FakeResizeObserver.instances.push(this);
  }
  resize(): void {
    this.callback([], this);
  }
}

const originalViewport = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window, 'visualViewport');
let rect = { width: 0, height: 0 };

beforeEach(() => {
  const fake = Object.assign(new EventTarget(), { width: 390, height: 844, offsetTop: 0, offsetLeft: 0 });
  Object.defineProperty(window, 'visualViewport', { configurable: true, value: fake });
  vi.stubGlobal('ResizeObserver', FakeResizeObserver);
  vi.spyOn(Element.prototype, 'getBoundingClientRect').mockImplementation(() => new DOMRect(0, 0, rect.width, rect.height));
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  FakeResizeObserver.instances = [];
  rect = { width: 0, height: 0 };
  if (originalViewport === undefined) Reflect.deleteProperty(window, 'visualViewport');
  else Object.defineProperty(window, 'visualViewport', originalViewport);
});

function Probe() {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const { viewport, arena } = useArenaLayoutBox(ref);
  return (
    <div ref={ref}>
      <output data-testid="box">{`${viewport.width}x${viewport.height} ${arena.width}x${arena.height}`}</output>
    </div>
  );
}

function onlyObserver(): FakeResizeObserver {
  const [observer] = FakeResizeObserver.instances;
  if (observer === undefined) throw new Error('the hook observed nothing');
  return observer;
}

describe('useArenaLayoutBox (§6.4)', () => {
  it('reads the visible viewport, and a 0×0 arena before the region has a size', () => {
    renderUi(<Probe />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('box').textContent).toBe('390x844 0x0');
  });

  it('follows the arena region when its ResizeObserver fires', () => {
    renderUi(<Probe />);
    expect(onlyObserver().observe).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    rect = { width: 360, height: 320 };
    act(() => {
      onlyObserver().resize();
    });
    expect(screen.getByTestId('box').textContent).toBe('390x844 360x320');
  });

  it('disconnects its observer on unmount', () => {
    const { unmount } = renderUi(<Probe />);
    unmount();
    expect(onlyObserver().disconnect).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`apps/client/src/screens/arena/opponent-seat.test.tsx` (15 tests). The components take the computed `SeatsModel`, so each test picks arc or grid through the box it passes to `seats()`; expected numbers are read from that model (the tier logic is L73's, tested there):

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Opponent seats: technical spec v7 §6.2 (anatomy), §6.3 (one test per tier), §6.4 (arc and grid),
 * §11 (Q36, Q47, Q66); Appendix A `opponent-seat`; plan Review Focus 5 (L74-04).
 */
import { LOG_RESOURCE_KINDS, getKit, type PlayingStateView, type PublicPlayerView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { visibleText } from '../../test/glyph-queries';
import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { SEAT_BOX, type LayoutBox } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { seats, type SeatModel, type SeatsModel } from '../../view-model/seats';
import { makePersistent, makePlayer } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaSeats } from './arena-seats';
import { makeArenaView } from './test-view';

/** 3 opponents sit on the arc here; 7 opponents at 360 px fall back to the grid (L73 seat-layout tests). */
const ARC_BOX: LayoutBox = { viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 }, arena: { width: 390, height: 360 } };
const PHONE_360: LayoutBox = { viewport: { width: 360, height: 740 }, arena: { width: 360, height: 320 } };
/** 24 characters: the server's maximum (parity `home-nickname`). */
const LONG_NICK = 'Wolfeschlegelsteinhausen';

function renderSeats(view: PlayingStateView, box: LayoutBox = ARC_BOX) {
  const onOpenSeat = vi.fn<(playerId: string) => void>();
  const model = seats(view, box);
  const result = renderUi(<ArenaSeats model={model} arena={box.arena} tappedSeatId={null} onOpenSeat={onOpenSeat} />);
  return { ...result, model, onOpenSeat };
}

function seatEl(playerId: string): HTMLElement {
  const el = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="${playerId}"]`);
  if (el === null) throw new Error(`no seat ${playerId}`);
  return el;
}

function seatModelOf(model: SeatsModel, playerId: string): SeatModel {
  const seat = model.seats.find((entry) => entry.playerId === playerId);
  if (seat === undefined) throw new Error(`no model for ${playerId}`);
  return seat;
}

/** What a sighted player reads for each resource, in `LOG_RESOURCE_KINDS` order. */
function resourceTexts(seat: HTMLElement): string[] {
  return LOG_RESOURCE_KINDS.map((kind) => {
    const el = seat.querySelector(`[data-resource-kind="${kind}"]`);
    if (el === null) throw new Error(`no ${kind} value`);
    return visibleText(el);
  });
}

function replacePlayer(view: PlayingStateView, player: PublicPlayerView): PlayingStateView {
  return { ...view, players: view.players.map((entry) => (entry.id === player.id ? player : entry)) };
}

describe('opponent seat tiers (§6.3, one test per tier)', () => {
  it('unspied: lives and resources read ?, no kit shows, and the seat anchors the hidden-kit hint', () => {
    renderSeats(makeArenaView());
    const seat = seatEl('p1');
    expect(seat.getAttribute('data-tier')).toBe('unspied');
    expect(resourceTexts(seat)).toEqual(['?', '?', '?', '?']);
    expect(within(seat).getByText('Lives unknown')).toBeDefined();
    expect(seat.querySelector('[data-zone="kit-portrait"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(seat.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('hidden-kit');
  });

  it('never prints an unspied total: no digit in any value, title or spoken text of the seat', () => {
    renderSeats(makeArenaView());
    for (const el of seatEl('p1').querySelectorAll('[data-resource-kind]')) {
      expect(el.textContent).not.toMatch(/\d/);
      expect(el.getAttribute('title') ?? '').not.toMatch(/\d/);
    }
  });

  it('base Spy: live numbers show, but no kit and no hand; the kit still counts as hidden (Q66)', () => {
    const { model } = renderSeats(makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'base-spy' } }));
    const seat = seatEl('p1');
    const values = seatModelOf(model, 'p1').resources.map((resource) => resource.value);
    expect(values.every((value) => typeof value === 'number')).toBe(true);
    expect(resourceTexts(seat)).toEqual(values.map(String));
    expect(seat.querySelector('[data-zone="kit-portrait"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(seat.querySelector('[data-card-face]')).toBeNull();
    expect(seat.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('hidden-kit');
  });

  it('Spy+: the kit portrait shows, named by the kit (§11), with numbers and no hand on the seat', () => {
    const { model } = renderSeats(makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'spy-plus' } }));
    const seat = seatEl('p1');
    expect(within(seat).getByRole('img', { name: getKit('tactician').name })).toBeDefined();
    expect(seat.querySelector('[data-zone="opponent-portrait"] [data-zone="kit-portrait"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(resourceTexts(seat)).toEqual(seatModelOf(model, 'p1').resources.map((resource) => String(resource.value)));
    expect(seat.querySelector('[data-card-face]')).toBeNull();
    expect(seat.hasAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('eliminated: frozen values, the greyscale portrait with its Eliminated cross, and no hint anchor', () => {
    const { model } = renderSeats(makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'eliminated' } }));
    const seat = seatEl('p1');
    expect(seat.getAttribute('data-eliminated')).toBe('true');
    expect(seat.querySelector('[data-zone="kit-portrait"]')?.getAttribute('data-eliminated')).toBe('true');
    expect(within(seat).getByRole('img', { name: 'Eliminated' })).toBeDefined();
    expect(resourceTexts(seat)).toEqual(seatModelOf(model, 'p1').resources.map((resource) => String(resource.value)));
    expect(seat.hasAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe(false);
    expect(seat.querySelector('[data-seat-bot], [data-seat-connection], [data-spy-eye]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'a walk-in who stayed', view: makeArenaView({ pov: 'walk-in', seatCount: 4, tiers: { me: 'spy-plus', p1: 'spy-plus', p2: 'spy-plus', p3: 'spy-plus' } }), ids: ['me', 'p1', 'p2', 'p3'] },
    { label: 'an eliminated POV', view: makeArenaView({ pov: 'eliminated', seatCount: 3, tiers: { p1: 'spy-plus', p2: 'spy-plus' } }), ids: ['p1', 'p2'] },
  ])('spectator overlay ($label): every seat shows its kit and numbers', ({ view, ids }) => {
    renderSeats(view);
    const drawn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-zone="opponent-seat"]'), (el) => el.getAttribute('data-player-id'));
    expect(drawn).toHaveLength(ids.length);
    expect(drawn).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(ids));
    for (const id of ids) {
      const seat = seatEl(id);
      expect(seat.querySelector('[data-zone="kit-portrait"]')?.getAttribute('data-kit-face')).toBe('art');
      expect(resourceTexts(seat)).not.toContain('?');
    }
  });
});

describe('opponent seat layout (§6.4)', () => {
  it('renders the grid in seat order when seats() returns grid mode (7 opponents at 360 px)', () => {
    const { model } = renderSeats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8 }), PHONE_360);
    expect(model.layout).toEqual({ kind: 'grid', mode: 'portrait', rows: [['p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4'], ['p5', 'p6', 'p7']] });
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="opponents"]')?.getAttribute('data-seat-layout')).toBe('grid');
    const drawn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="opponent-seat"]'));
    expect(drawn.map((el) => el.getAttribute('data-player-id'))).toEqual(['p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4', 'p5', 'p6', 'p7']);
    const tops = drawn.map((el) => el.style.top);
    expect(new Set(tops.slice(0, 4)).size).toBe(1);
    expect(new Set(tops.slice(4)).size).toBe(1);
    expect(parseFloat(tops[4] ?? '')).toBeGreaterThan(parseFloat(tops[0] ?? ''));
  });

  it('places each arc seat at its model placement, in drawing order', () => {
    const { model } = renderSeats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 4 }));
    if (model.layout.kind !== 'arc') throw new Error('expected an arc');
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="opponents"]')?.getAttribute('data-seat-layout')).toBe('arc');
    for (const placement of model.layout.placements) {
      const seat = seatEl(placement.playerId);
      expect(seat.style.left).toBe(`${placement.centerX - SEAT_BOX.portrait.width / 2}px`);
      expect(seat.style.top).toBe(`${placement.centerY - SEAT_BOX.portrait.height / 2}px`);
    }
  });

  it('marks only the active seat with data-active-seat', () => {
    renderSeats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 4, overrides: { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p2' } }));
    expect(seatEl('p2').getAttribute('data-active-seat')).toBe('true');
    expect(seatEl('p1').hasAttribute('data-active-seat')).toBe(false);
    expect(seatEl('p3').hasAttribute('data-active-seat')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('opponent seat anatomy (§6.2)', () => {
  it('a 24-character nickname is the full accessible name of the seat button, truncated on screen with a title (Review Focus 5)', () => {
    renderSeats(replacePlayer(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8 }), makePlayer('p1', { nickname: LONG_NICK })), PHONE_360);
    const seat = seatEl('p1');
    expect(within(seat).getByRole('button', { name: LONG_NICK })).toBeDefined();
    const name = seat.querySelector('[data-seat-name]');
    expect(name?.getAttribute('title')).toBe(LONG_NICK);
    expect(name?.textContent).toContain(LONG_NICK);
  });

  it('shows two status badges then +n; the eye keeps data-spy-eye', () => {
    const p1 = makePlayer('p1', {
      spyingOnYou: true,
      blockTurnsRemaining: 2,
      activeShield: { isUpgraded: false },
      activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'poi', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 })],
    });
    renderSeats(replacePlayer(makeArenaView(), p1));
    const seat = seatEl('p1');
    expect(Array.from(seat.querySelectorAll('[data-seat-badge]'), (el) => el.getAttribute('data-seat-badge'))).toEqual(['spying-on-you', 'block']);
    expect(seat.querySelector('[data-seat-badge="spying-on-you"]')?.hasAttribute('data-spy-eye')).toBe(true);
    const more = seat.querySelector('[data-seat-badge-overflow]');
    expect(more === null ? '' : visibleText(more)).toBe('+2');
    expect(more?.textContent).toBe('+2 more statuses');
  });

  it('shows a bot, a connection state, or neither, with the words for screen readers and as a title', () => {
    let view = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 4 });
    view = replacePlayer(view, makePlayer('p1', { isBot: true, botDifficulty: 'hard' }));
    view = replacePlayer(view, makePlayer('p2', { connection: { status: 'disconnected', disconnectedAt: 0, automaticTurnsTaken: 0, consecutiveTimeouts: 0 } }));
    renderSeats(view);
    expect(seatEl('p1').querySelector('[data-seat-bot]')?.textContent).toBe('Bot·Hard');
    expect(seatEl('p2').querySelector('[data-seat-connection]')?.getAttribute('title')).toBe('disconnected — grace');
    expect(seatEl('p2').querySelector('[data-seat-connection]')?.textContent).toBe('disconnected — grace');
    expect(seatEl('p3').querySelector('[data-seat-bot], [data-seat-connection]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('shows a Gambler draw gain the POV receives (Q47)', () => {
    const base = makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'base-spy' } });
    const spied = base.players.find((player) => player.id === 'p1')?.spied;
    if (spied === undefined) throw new Error('p1 is not spied');
    renderSeats(replacePlayer(base, makePlayer('p1', { spied, drawGain: 37 })));
    const badge = seatEl('p1').querySelector('[data-seat-badge="draw-gain"]');
    expect(badge === null ? '' : visibleText(badge)).toBe('+37');
    expect(badge?.getAttribute('title')).toBe('Draw, plus 37 pts');
  });

  it('a tap on the seat calls onOpenSeat once with its id', async () => {
    const { user, onOpenSeat } = renderSeats(makeArenaView());
    await user.click(within(seatEl('p1')).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1' }));
    expect(onOpenSeat).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onOpenSeat).toHaveBeenCalledWith('p1');
  });
});
```

Add to `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-screen.test.tsx` (1 test). It already imports `within`, `renderUi`, `ArenaScreen`, `makeArenaProps`, `makeArenaView` and `expectNoArenaIntent` (L74-01):

```tsx
it('renders one seat per opponent in the opponents zone; a seat tap sends nothing (L74-04)', async () => {
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view: makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8 }) });
  const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
  const zone = document.querySelector('[data-arena-region="arena"] [data-zone="opponents"]');
  if (zone === null) throw new Error('no opponents zone');
  expect(zone.querySelectorAll('[data-zone="opponent-seat"]')).toHaveLength(7);
  const seat = zone.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-player-id="p1"]');
  if (seat === null) throw new Error('no p1 seat');
  await user.click(within(seat).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1' }));
  expectNoArenaIntent(props);
});
```

That is 15 + 3 + 4 + 12 + 1 = 35 new tests.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and see them fail.** `pnpm vitest run apps/client/src/screens/arena/seat-geometry.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/seat-badges.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/use-arena-layout-box.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/opponent-seat.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-screen.test.tsx`. Expected:
  - The four new files fail to resolve `./seat-geometry`, `./seat-badges`, `./use-arena-layout-box` and `./arena-seats`.
  - The new `arena-screen` test fails (no `[data-zone="opponent-seat"]`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**
  - **`seat-geometry.ts`** (pure, no React): `SEAT_RENDER_BOX = SEAT_BOX.portrait`, `SEAT_GRID_GAP_PX = SEAT_MIN_GAP_PX`. `seatCenters`: an arc returns `layout.placements` unchanged. A grid lays each row out centred: row `r` holding `k` seats, column `c` → `centerX = arena.width / 2 + (c - (k - 1) / 2) * (W + GAP)`, `centerY = GAP + r * (H + GAP) + H / 2` (`W`, `H` = `SEAT_RENDER_BOX`). Rows and seats keep model order. `seatRect(center)` = `{ left: centerX - W / 2, top: centerY - H / 2, width: W, height: H }`. Doc comment: grid seats are placed by this pure helper, not measured, so the threat arrows (L74-06) and the drop zones (L74-12) read the same numbers.
  - **`seat-badges.ts`** (pure): the table in Step 1 is the copy. Implementation notes:
    - Card words go through `cardFaceLabel({ name: getCard(id)?.name ?? id, isUpgraded, damage: null, counter? })`: Invisibility and Sentence pass `{ kind: 'turns', amount }`, a persistent with a counter passes `{ kind: 'card-lives', amount }`, Shield and a counterless persistent pass none.
    - `value` is `String(n)` for Block turns (> 0), Invisibility turns, Sentence turns and persistent card lives; `+${amount}` for draw gain; else null.
    - Draw gain: `` `${DRAW_ACTION_LABEL}, ${spokenGlyphAmount({ kind: 'points', amount }, { signed: 'gain' })}` `` (ruling A.8.4).
    - V2 words verbatim: `'Spying on you'` (`opponent-zone.tsx`), `` `Block · ${n} left` `` / `'Block · attacks banned'` / `'Reanimating'` (`flow-status-badges.tsx`), `'Duplication active'` (`private-zone.tsx`).
    - Exhaustive `switch (badge.kind)` with a `never` default.
  - **`use-arena-layout-box.ts`**: `const box = useVisualViewportBox()`; arena `useState<Size>({ width: 0, height: 0 })`. In `useLayoutEffect([ref])`: read `ref.current`; when null, return. `measure()` reads `element.getBoundingClientRect()` and sets state only when width or height changed (same object otherwise, so no render loop). Call it once, then `new ResizeObserver(measure).observe(element)`; the cleanup disconnects. Return `useMemo(() => ({ viewport: { width: box.width, height: box.height }, arena }), [box.width, box.height, arena])`.
  - **`opponent-seat.tsx`**: the seat is one control. A stretched `<button>` covers it, and the anatomy sits beside it as readable, non-interactive content, so no control nests inside another (frontend.md › Kit, Nesting).
    - Root `<div>` attributes:
      - always: `data-zone="opponent-seat"`, `data-player-id`, `data-seat={playerId}`, `data-seat-index={String(model.seatColorIndex)}`, `data-tier={model.tier}`, `data-eliminated="true|false"`;
      - when they apply: `data-active-seat="true"` only when `model.isActive`, `data-tapped-seat="true"` only when `tapped`, `data-hint-anchor="hidden-kit"` only when `model.kitHidden`.
      - Style: `seatRect(center)` as `left/top/width/height` px. The active seat adds an inline `outline: 3px solid ${v7SeatColor(model.seatColorIndex)}` with `outlineOffset: 2` (hex from the palette, never `var()`).
      - Classes: `pointer-events-auto absolute flex flex-col bg-v7-panel text-v7-text select-none [-webkit-touch-callout:none]`, plus `CHROME_SHAPE_SM_CLASS`; an eliminated seat adds `border-dashed` (the cracked frame), a tapped one `ring-3 ring-v7-cta`.
    - Button: `<button type="button" aria-label={model.nickname} aria-describedby={detailsId} onClick={() => onOpen(model.playerId)} className={`absolute inset-0 rounded-v7-chrome-sm ${CHROME_FOCUS_CLASS}`} />`, with `detailsId = useId()`.
    - Details `<div id={detailsId} className="pointer-events-none relative flex h-full min-w-0 flex-col">`, three rows inside the 70 × 86 px box inside the 3 px outline:
      1. **Name row (14 px):** `<div data-seat-name title={nickname} className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-0.5">` holding:
         - `<PlayerName nickname seatIndex={seatColorIndex} className="min-w-0 truncate text-v7-caption leading-[14px]" />`;
         - when `connectionLabel !== null`: `<span data-seat-connection title={label} className="inline-flex shrink-0"><Icon name="disconnected" size={12} /><span className="sr-only">{label}</span></span>`;
         - when `botDifficulty !== null`: `<span data-seat-bot className="inline-flex shrink-0"><Icon name="bot" size={12} /><span className="sr-only"><BotSeatLabel difficulty={botDifficulty} /></span></span>`.
      2. **Body row (53 px):**
         - Portrait slot: `<div data-zone="opponent-portrait" className="flex h-[53px] w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center">`. Kit avatar: `<KitPortrait kitId size="thumb" isEliminated={model.isEliminated} />`. Initial avatar: `<Avatar name={nickname} seatIndex={seatColorIndex} size="sm" />`. Read only `model.avatar`; never re-derive the tier.
         - Then `<ul data-zone="opponent-resources" className="flex w-7 flex-col leading-none">` with one `<li>` per `model.resources` entry, in model order. Each `<li>`:
           - carries `data-resource-kind={kind}` and `title={value === '?' ? `${caption} unknown` : caption}`;
           - holds `<Icon name={RESOURCE_ICON[kind]} />` and an `aria-hidden` Lilita value (`'?'` or the number);
           - and `<span className="sr-only">{`${caption} ${value === '?' ? 'unknown' : String(value)}`}</span>`, where `caption = RESOURCE_CAPTIONS[kind]`. These are Pill's words, but Pill's 28 px chip does not fit.
           - Life is the **lives pill**: 16 px tall, `rounded-v7-chrome-sm bg-v7-panel-2 ring-2 ring-inset ring-v7-life`, a 12 px icon and a `text-v7-caption` value.
           - Point, upgrade point and shield rows are 12 px tall, with a 10 px icon and a `text-v7-micro` value.
      3. **Badge row (16 px):** `<ul data-seat-badges className="flex items-center gap-0.5">`.
         - `model.badges.slice(0, SEAT_VISIBLE_BADGE_COUNT)`, each `<li data-seat-badge={kind} title={view.label}>` (plus `data-spy-eye=""` for `spying-on-you`), holding `<Icon name={view.icon} size={12} />`, the `aria-hidden` value when not null, and `<span className="sr-only">{view.label}</span>`. The chip is a flat `bg-v7-panel-2 rounded-v7-chrome-sm` with no padding beyond 1 px.
         - When `model.badgeOverflow > 0`: `<li data-seat-badge-overflow>` holding `+{n}` and `<span className="sr-only">{` ${SEAT_MORE_STATUSES_LABEL}`}</span>`.
    - Values may overflow the 28 px column by a pixel at three-digit points; the frame never clips them (no `overflow-hidden`). The browser gate checks this.
  - **`arena-seats.tsx`**:
    - Root `<div data-zone="opponents" data-seat-layout={model.layout.kind} data-opponent-count={String(model.seats.length)} className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0">`. The container never takes a press, so the plate below it stays tappable (L74-06); seats re-enable pointer events.
    - Children: `seatCenters(model.layout, arena)` in drawing order. Each looks its `SeatModel` up by `playerId` (skip a missing one) and renders `<OpponentSeat key={playerId} model center tapped={playerId === tappedSeatId} onOpen={(id) => { onOpenSeat?.(id); }} />`. `OpponentSeat.onOpen` stays required; only `ArenaSeats.onOpenSeat` is optional until L74-05 wires it.
    - No empty-state copy: a Classic view always has at least one other seat.
  - **`arena-screen.tsx`**:
    - `const arenaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)`, using the arena region's element type, attached to the `data-arena-region="arena"` element.
    - Measure with `const layoutBox = useArenaLayoutBox(arenaRef)`, then `const seatsModel = seats(props.view, layoutBox)`, computed once per render (A.2).
    - Replace L74-01's `data-zone="opponents"` placeholder with `<ArenaSeats model={seatsModel} arena={layoutBox.arena} tappedSeatId={null} />`. `ArenaSeats` now owns `data-zone="opponents"`, so exactly one remains. No `onOpenSeat` yet: a seat tap does nothing until L74-05 opens the seat sheet and derives `tappedSeatId` from it.

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 opponent seat anatomy in the 76×92 box (L74-04)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.2, §6.3, §6.4, §11, §14.3; Appendix A `opponent-seat`,
`active-persistent-thumbs`, `sentence-chips`, `global-connection-badge`; plan rulings A.8.5 and
A.8.9.

- **Box.** A seat is drawn at `SEAT_BOX.portrait` (76×92) in every mode until L75; the
  geometry still follows `seats()`. 76 px is the widest seat that fits four grid seats in a row
  on a 360 px phone with 16 px margins (§6.4), so the L73 values stay; the browser gate may
  still tune them (§14.3).
- **Rows.** The name in seat hue; the kit thumb or the initial disc beside the lives pill and the
  points / upgrade points / shield column; at most two status badges and "+n".
- **Compact statuses.** `BotSeatLabel` (about 95 px) and the connection chip (up to about
  150 px) cannot fit 76 px. The seat shows the `bot` and `disconnected` icons with their words
  for screen readers and in a `title`; the seat sheet (L74-05) shows both in full.
- **Actives and Sentences.** On the seat they are the `SeatModel` badges. Their 44 px thumbs,
  which open the read-only card sheet, live in the seat sheet (L74-05).
- **One control.** The seat is one button named by the nickname. The portrait is the
  non-clickable form beside it, so no control nests in another.
- **Grid positions.** Grid seats are placed by the pure `seatCenters`
  (`screens/arena/seat-geometry.ts`), which the arrows and drop zones reuse, so no seat is
  measured.
- **Classic CSS.** The landscape opponent kit-portrait width rule is scoped to `.table-shell`
  and the classic `[data-zone="opponents-dialog-row"]` (ruling A.8.5). The second selector is
  there because that V2 dialog renders outside `<main class="table-shell">`.

---

```

```
Seats render seats(view, box) in arc or grid at the 76x92 portrait
box. Bot and connection show as icons with their words for screen

```

**Acceptance map:**
- One component test per §6.3 tier:
  - Unspied shows `?` and no kit: `unspied: lives and resources read ?, no kit shows, …`.
  - Base Spy shows numbers but no kit and no hand: `base Spy: live numbers show, but no kit and no hand; …`.
  - Spy+ shows the kit portrait: `Spy+: the kit portrait shows, named by the kit (§11), …`.
  - Eliminated shows frozen values with the eliminated treatment: `eliminated: frozen values, the greyscale portrait …`.
  - Spectator overlay has full vision: `spectator overlay ($label): every seat shows its kit and numbers` (2 cases).
- A 7-opponent view renders the grid when `seats()` returns grid mode: `renders the grid in seat order when seats() returns grid mode (7 opponents at 360 px)`.
- The active seat has `data-active-seat`: `marks only the active seat with data-active-seat`.
- No unspied total in the DOM: `never prints an unspied total: …`.
- Review Focus 5: `a 24-character nickname is the full accessible name of the seat button, …`.
- Index.css watch point: Step 3 `index.css`, and the CSS-reading tests in Step 4 stay green.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **The seat keeps the 76×92 box.**
  - Width: 76 px is the most a four-seat grid row allows at 360 px with 16 px margins.
  - Height: the three rows (14 + 53 + 16 px) fit the 86 px inside the 3 px outline.
  - The price is that the bot difficulty and the connection words are icons on the seat (Designer question below); every word is in the seat sheet and in the seat's accessible description.
- **Opponent actives and Sentences are badges on the seat, and thumbs in the seat sheet.** This overrides the L74-04 brief sentence "Opponent active thumbs and Sentence chips open the read-only card sheet": a 44 px thumb does not fit a 76 px seat. §6.2's seat anatomy lists badges, not thumbs, and L74-05 makes the thumbs inspectable.
- **The kit portrait is `KitPortrait` at thumb size, not an `Avatar` image.** Kit art is a trimmed drawing that `Avatar`'s round `object-cover` crop would cut (parity: "never crop the kit portrait"). A hidden kit is `Avatar` at `sm` (32 px), centred in the same 40×53 slot.
- **One stretched button per seat**, named by the nickname. The details are a sibling, pointed to by `aria-describedby`, so a screen reader hears "p1, button" and then the seat's values.
- **Grid seats are absolutely positioned from `seatCenters`, not laid out by CSS.** One pure source places the seats, the arrows (L74-06) and the drop zones (L74-12), with no DOM measuring and with node tests.
- **`data-hint-anchor="hidden-kit"` follows `SeatModel.kitHidden`** (unspied and base-Spied, Q66). An eliminated or Spy+ seat never carries it.
- **Active highlight:** a 3 px outline in the seat's own hue (`v7SeatColor`). The top bar names the active player, so the highlight is not the only cue. **Tapped:** the `--cta` selection ring.
- **The `index.css` scope also keeps `[data-zone="opponents-dialog-row"]`**, so the V2 table's look is exactly unchanged (ruling A.8.5's intent).
- **Until L74-05, a seat tap does nothing.** `ArenaScreen` passes `tappedSeatId={null}` and no `onOpenSeat` (optional on `ArenaSeats`). L74-05 adds the sheet host with the seat sheet, the first real opener (Appendix A.5): opponent actives and Sentences are badges on the seat here, and their thumbs live in the L74-05 seat sheet.
- **New strings (§10.6):** `'more statuses'` (screen-reader words after "+n"), `'Absorber window'` (the eliminated seat's absorb-window badge).
- **Designer question:** should the 76 px seat print the bot difficulty and the connection state as words? The default shown is icons, with words in the title, for screen readers and in the seat sheet. A words line needs `SEAT_BOX.portrait` 76×104: it changes no L73 seat-layout result (33 arcs, the same grid switches at 360 and 375 px), and a 360×740 grid still leaves room for the plate (2×104 + 8 + 96 = 312 ≤ 320).

```ts
// arena-sheets.ts (additions)
export type ArenaSheet =
  | { kind: 'card'; source: CardSheetSource }
  | { kind: 'kit'; kitId: KitId }
  | { kind: 'seat'; playerId: string };          // new
// arenaSheetKey({ kind: 'seat', playerId }) === `seat:${playerId}`
/** The player of the newest open seat sheet, else null: that seat is "tapped" (spec v7 §6.5: its arrows light up). */
export function openSeatId(state: ArenaSheetsState): string | null;

// threat-copy.ts
export interface PendingEffectCopy {
  /** `formatCardLabel(cardId, isUpgraded)`, as V2 `pending-queue.tsx`. */
  label: string;
  /** `Inspect {label}` (the V2 chip's aria-label). */
  ariaLabel: string;
  /** V2 compact chip title: `{label} · {source} → {target} · queued #{n}[ · {d} damage]`, plus ` · {Mirror name}` when redirected. */
  title: string;
}
export function pendingEffectCopy(entry: ThreatEntry, nicknameOf: (playerId: string) => string): PendingEffectCopy;

// seat-sheet.tsx
export interface SeatSheetProps {
  /** False while the sheet plays its exit from `closing` (arena-sheets.ts, Appendix A.5). */
  open: boolean;
  seat: SeatModel;
  view: PlayingStateView;
  threatModel: ThreatsModel;
  onClose: () => void;
  onOpenKit: (kitId: KitId) => void;
  onOpenCard: (source: CardSheetSource) => void;
}
export function SeatSheet(props: SeatSheetProps): ReactElement;
```

```ts
describe('seat sheets (L74-05)', () => {
  it('keys a seat sheet by its player', () => {
    expect(arenaSheetKey({ kind: 'seat', playerId: 'p1' })).toBe('seat:p1');
  });
```
