```ts
/** Pending effect words: the V2 `pending-queue.tsx` chip copy, reused verbatim (L74-05). */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { makePending, makeTableView } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { threats, type ThreatEntry } from '../../view-model/threats';
import { pendingEffectCopy } from './threat-copy';

function entryOf(pending: Parameters<typeof makePending>[0]): ThreatEntry {
  const [entry] = threats(makeTableView(['me', 'p1', 'p2'], { pendingEffects: [makePending(pending)] }), null).entries;
  if (entry === undefined) throw new Error('no entry');
  return entry;
}
const NICKS: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = { me: 'Ana', p1: 'Bo', p2: 'Cy' };
const nick = (id: string): string => NICKS[id] ?? id;

describe('pendingEffectCopy (V2 chip words)', () => {
  it('names an attack, its route, its queue slot and its own damage', () => {
    expect(pendingEffectCopy(entryOf({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'strong-attack', queuedAt: 4 }), nick)).toEqual({
      label: 'Strong attack',
      ariaLabel: 'Inspect Strong attack',
      title: 'Strong attack · Bo → Ana · queued #4 · 2 damage',
    });
  });

  it('gives an effect no damage and marks an upgraded card with +', () => {
    expect(pendingEffectCopy(entryOf({ id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'spy', isUpgraded: true, queuedAt: 6 }), nick).title)
      .toBe('Spy + · Cy → Bo · queued #6');
  });

  it('appends the Mirror that redirected it', () => {
    expect(pendingEffectCopy(entryOf({ id: 'e3', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'basic-attack', redirectedBy: 'super-mirror' }), nick).title)
      .toBe('Basic attack · Bo → Cy · queued #1 · 1 damage · Super Mirror');
  });
});
```

`apps/client/src/screens/arena/seat-sheet.test.tsx` (12 tests):

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Seat sheet: technical spec v7 §6.3, §6.5 (tap a seat: kit if visible, statuses, pending effects,
 * Spy hand), §6.6.5 (long-press), §11 (Q46); Appendix A `opponent-reveal-dialog`, `opponent-seat`,
 * `kit-inspect-dialog` (L74-05).
 */
import { getKit, type PlayingStateView, type PublicPlayerView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { act, fireEvent, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { RESOURCE_CAPTIONS } from '../../ui/resource-captions';
import { inPlayThumbs } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import type { LayoutBox } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { seats, type SeatModel } from '../../view-model/seats';
import { makePending, makePersistent, makePlayer } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { threats } from '../../view-model/threats';
import { ArenaScreen } from './arena-screen';
import type { CardSheetSource } from './card-sheet-source';
import { SeatSheet } from './seat-sheet';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const BOX: LayoutBox = { viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 }, arena: { width: 390, height: 360 } };

function seatOf(view: PlayingStateView, playerId: string): SeatModel {
  const seat = seats(view, BOX).seats.find((entry) => entry.playerId === playerId);
  if (seat === undefined) throw new Error(`no seat ${playerId}`);
  return seat;
}

function renderSheet(view: PlayingStateView, playerId = 'p1', options: { advanceTimers?: (ms: number) => unknown } = {}) {
  const onClose = vi.fn<() => void>();
  const onOpenKit = vi.fn<(kitId: string) => void>();
  const onOpenCard = vi.fn<(source: CardSheetSource) => void>();
  const seat = seatOf(view, playerId);
  const result = renderUi(
    <SeatSheet open seat={seat} view={view} threatModel={threats(view, null)} onClose={onClose} onOpenKit={onOpenKit} onOpenCard={onOpenCard} />,
    options.advanceTimers === undefined ? {} : { advanceTimers: options.advanceTimers },
  );
  return { ...result, seat, onClose, onOpenKit, onOpenCard };
}

function section(name: string): HTMLElement {
  const el = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-seat-sheet-section="${name}"]`);
  if (el === null) throw new Error(`no ${name} section`);
  return el;
}

function replacePlayer(view: PlayingStateView, player: PublicPlayerView): PlayingStateView {
  return { ...view, players: view.players.map((entry) => (entry.id === player.id ? player : entry)) };
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe('SeatSheet (§6.3, §6.5)', () => {
  it('base Spy: shows the numbers, and no kit control and no hand', () => {
    const { seat } = renderSheet(makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'base-spy' } }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'p1' });
    expect(dialog.getAttribute('data-arena-sheet')).toBe('seat:p1');
    for (const resource of seat.resources) {
      expect(typeof resource.value).toBe('number');
      expect(within(section('resources')).getByText(`${RESOURCE_CAPTIONS[resource.kind]} ${String(resource.value)}`)).toBeDefined();
    }
    expect(dialog.querySelector('[data-zone="kit-portrait"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('heading', { name: 'Hand' })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('unspied: no kit control, and every value reads unknown', () => {
    renderSheet(makeArenaView());
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'p1' });
    expect(dialog.querySelector('[data-zone="kit-portrait"]')).toBeNull();
    for (const label of ['Lives unknown', 'Points unknown', 'Upgrade points unknown', 'Shield unknown']) {
      expect(within(dialog).getByText(label)).toBeDefined();
    }
  });

  it('Spy+: the kit opens the kit sheet, and the hand thumbs open the card sheet as a Spy reveal', async () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'spy-plus' } });
    const { user, seat, onOpenKit, onOpenCard } = renderSheet(view);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: getKit('tactician').name }));
    expect(onOpenKit).toHaveBeenCalledWith('tactician');
    const thumbs = within(section('hand')).getAllByRole('button');
    expect(thumbs).toHaveLength(seat.reveal?.hand.length ?? -1);
    const [first] = thumbs;
    if (first === undefined) throw new Error('no hand thumb');
    await user.click(first);
    expect(onOpenCard).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ kind: 'spy-reveal', instance: seat.reveal?.hand[0], ownerPlayerId: 'p1' });
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-seat-sheet-section="specials"]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('a long-press on a hand thumb opens the same read-only card sheet (§6.6.5)', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const { seat, onOpenCard } = renderSheet(makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'spy-plus' } }), 'p1', { advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime });
    const [first] = within(section('hand')).getAllByRole('button');
    if (first === undefined) throw new Error('no hand thumb');
    // The press `use-long-press.test.tsx` (L74-02) fires: a primary button, held still.
    fireEvent.pointerDown(first, { button: 0, pointerId: 1, clientX: 10, clientY: 10 });
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(450);
    });
    expect(onOpenCard).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ kind: 'spy-reveal', instance: seat.reveal?.hand[0], ownerPlayerId: 'p1' });
  });

  it('an eliminated seat is titled "{nick} (eliminated)" with frozen values and its kit', async () => {
    const { user, seat, onOpenKit } = renderSheet(makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'eliminated' } }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'p1 (eliminated)' });
    expect(dialog.querySelector('[data-zone="kit-portrait"]')?.getAttribute('data-eliminated')).toBe('true');
    for (const resource of seat.resources) {
      expect(within(section('resources')).getByText(`${RESOURCE_CAPTIONS[resource.kind]} ${String(resource.value)}`)).toBeDefined();
    }
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: /, eliminated$/ }));
    expect(onOpenKit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(within(section('hand')).getByText('Empty')).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('lists every status badge, past the two the seat shows', () => {
    const p1 = makePlayer('p1', {
      spyingOnYou: true,
      blockTurnsRemaining: 2,
      activeShield: { isUpgraded: false },
      activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'poi', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 })],
    });
    renderSheet(replacePlayer(makeArenaView(), p1));
    const statuses = section('statuses');
    for (const label of ['Spying on you', 'Block · 2 left', 'Shield', 'Poison, 3 card lives']) {
      expect(within(statuses).getByText(label)).toBeDefined();
    }
  });

  it('shows the bot label and the connection state in full', () => {
    let view = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 4 });
    view = replacePlayer(view, makePlayer('p1', { isBot: true, botDifficulty: 'hard' }));
    view = replacePlayer(view, makePlayer('p2', { connection: { status: 'absent', disconnectedAt: 0, automaticTurnsTaken: 2, consecutiveTimeouts: 0 } }));
    renderSheet(view, 'p1');
    expect(section('identity').textContent).toContain('Bot·Hard');
    renderSheet(view, 'p2');
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'p2' }).textContent).toContain('absent 2/3');
  });

  it('lists its public pending effects in and out once each; each opens the queue inspect', async () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({
      seatCount: 3,
      overrides: {
        pendingEffects: [
          makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'strong-attack', queuedAt: 3 }),
          makePending({ id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'spy', queuedAt: 4 }),
          makePending({ id: 'e3', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'suicide', queuedAt: 5 }),
        ],
      },
    });
    const { user, onOpenCard } = renderSheet(view);
    const names = within(section('pending')).getAllByRole('button').map((button) => button.getAttribute('aria-label'));
    expect(names).toEqual(['Inspect Spy', 'Inspect Suicide', 'Inspect Strong attack']);
    await user.click(within(section('pending')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Inspect Strong attack' }));
    expect(onOpenCard).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ kind: 'queue', effectId: 'e1', cardId: 'strong-attack', isUpgraded: false });
  });

  it('active thumbs and Sentence chips open the in-play card sheet; Sentence turns are a line, not a card counter', async () => {
    const base = makeArenaView({ overrides: { pendingSentences: [{ id: 's1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', remainingOwnerTurns: 2, isUpgraded: false }] } });
    const view = replacePlayer(base, makePlayer('p1', {
      activeShield: { isUpgraded: false },
      activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'poi', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 })],
    }));
    const { user, onOpenCard } = renderSheet(view);
    const inPlay = section('in-play');
    expect(within(inPlay).getAllByRole('button').map((button) => button.getAttribute('aria-label'))).toEqual(['Shield', 'Poison, 3 card lives', 'Sentence']);
    expect(inPlay.querySelectorAll('[data-card-counter]')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(within(inPlay).getByText('2 turns remaining')).toBeDefined();
    await user.click(within(inPlay).getByRole('button', { name: 'Poison, 3 card lives' }));
    expect(onOpenCard).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ kind: 'in-play', thumb: inPlayThumbs(view, 'p1')[1] });
  });
});

describe('the seat sheet in the arena (§6.5, §6.6.4)', () => {
  it('a seat tap opens its sheet and marks the seat; Esc closes it; nothing is sent', async () => {
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'base-spy' } }) });
    const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    const seat = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="p1"]');
    if (seat === null) throw new Error('no p1 seat');
    await user.click(within(seat).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'p1' }).getAttribute('data-arena-sheet')).toBe('seat:p1');
    expect(seat.getAttribute('data-tapped-seat')).toBe('true');
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'p1' })).toBeNull();
    });
    expect(seat.hasAttribute('data-tapped-seat')).toBe(false);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a Spy+ hand thumb opens the read-only card sheet above the seat sheet; nothing is sent', async () => {
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'spy-plus' } }) });
    const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    const seat = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="p1"]');
    if (seat === null) throw new Error('no p1 seat');
    await user.click(within(seat).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1' }));
    const [thumb] = within(section('hand')).getAllByRole('button');
    if (thumb === undefined) throw new Error('no hand thumb');
    await user.click(thumb);
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-arena-sheet^="card:"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-arena-sheet="seat:p1"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a Spy+ seat kit opens the kit sheet above the seat sheet; nothing is sent', async () => {
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: makeArenaView({ tiers: { p1: 'spy-plus' } }) });
    const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    const seat = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="p1"]');
    if (seat === null) throw new Error('no p1 seat');
    await user.click(within(seat).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1' }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'p1' })).getByRole('button', { name: getKit('tactician').name }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: getKit('tactician').name }).getAttribute('data-arena-sheet')).toBe('kit:tactician');
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-arena-sheet="seat:p1"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});
```

The hook's `onClickCapture` swallows the click that ends a long press, so a long-press opens one sheet; opening an open sheet would only move it to the top anyway (A.5).

```tsx
const [sheets, setSheets] = useState<ArenaSheetsState>(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS);
const openSheet = useCallback((sheet: ArenaSheet): void => { setSheets((current) => openArenaSheet(current, sheet)); }, []);
const closeSheet = useCallback((sheet: ArenaSheet): void => { setSheets((current) => closeArenaSheet(current, sheet)); }, []);
const tappedSeatId = openSeatId(sheets);
// … the last children of <main>, after the six regions, never inside a clickable element (portals bubble clicks):
{arenaSheetEntries(sheets).map(({ sheet, key, open }) => {
  switch (sheet.kind) {
    case 'card':
      return <CardSheet key={key} open={open} source={sheet.source} view={props.view} ctx={ctx} onClose={() => { closeSheet(sheet); }} />;
    case 'kit':
      return <KitSheet key={key} open={open} kitId={sheet.kitId} onClose={() => { closeSheet(sheet); }} />;
    case 'seat': {
      const seat = seatsModel.seats.find((entry) => entry.playerId === sheet.playerId);
      return seat === undefined ? null : (
        <SeatSheet key={key} open={open} seat={seat} view={props.view} threatModel={threatModel}
          onClose={() => { closeSheet(sheet); }}
          onOpenKit={(kitId) => { openSheet({ kind: 'kit', kitId }); }}
          onOpenCard={(source) => { openSheet({ kind: 'card', source }); }} />
      );
    }
  }
})}
```

      The switch has no `default`, so a new `ArenaSheet` variant fails `noImplicitReturns` until it gets a case. `setSheets` stays in scope for L74-10 (targeting).
    - `ArenaSeats` now gets `tappedSeatId={tappedSeatId}` (L74-04 passed `null`) and `onOpenSeat={(playerId) => { openSheet({ kind: 'seat', playerId }); }}`.
    - Opening a seat, a thumb or a kit sends nothing.

**Acceptance map:**
- A base-Spied sheet shows numbers and no kit or hand: `base Spy: shows the numbers, and no kit control and no hand`.
- A Spy+ sheet lists hand thumbs that open the card sheet:
  - `Spy+: the kit opens the kit sheet, and the hand thumbs open the card sheet as a Spy reveal`
  - `a long-press on a hand thumb opens the same read-only card sheet (§6.6.5)`
  - `a Spy+ hand thumb opens the read-only card sheet above the seat sheet; nothing is sent`
- An eliminated seat shows the '{nick} (eliminated)' title: `an eliminated seat is titled "{nick} (eliminated)" with frozen values and its kit`.
- An unspied seat has no kit control: `unspied: no kit control, and every value reads unknown`.
- Opening the sheet sends no intent: `a seat tap opens its sheet and marks the seat; Esc closes it; nothing is sent`.
- The sheet host (Appendix A.5), red before this task: the seat tap test above, `a Spy+ hand thumb opens the read-only card sheet above the seat sheet; nothing is sent` and `a Spy+ seat kit opens the kit sheet above the seat sheet; nothing is sent`.
- Brief items beyond the Acceptance line:
  - every status badge: `lists every status badge, past the two the seat shows`;
  - public pending in and out: `lists its public pending effects in and out once each; …`;
  - thumbs and Sentence chips: `active thumbs and Sentence chips open the in-play card sheet; …`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **The kit control is shown exactly when `SeatModel.avatar.kind === 'kit'`.** That is Spy+, a death reveal, or the spectator overlay (the server fills `spied.kitId` there; Q46). The sheet never re-derives it.
- **The kit button's name is the kit name** (`KitPortrait`'s default, ", eliminated" added on a dead seat), and the visible kit name sits beside it. No new string.
- **The sheet host lands here** (Appendix A.5, controller ruling): the seat sheet is the first surface that opens a sheet, so its tests drive the host red to green. L74-04 has none.
- **`SeatSheet` takes `open`** like `CardSheet` and `KitSheet`: a closed seat sheet stays mounted with `open={false}` through its exit, so the Sheet press shield absorbs a double tap (Appendix A.5).
- **The pending list is one "Pending effects" list:**
  - incoming first, then outgoing, with each entry's route printed, so in and out read from the route;
  - a self-queued Suicide appears once, as incoming (ruling A.8.9);
  - the sheet's entries carry no `data-pending-id`, which stays on arena elements for the FX anchor.
- **The `ConnectionBadge` gets the real `PublicPlayerView`, rendered only when `SeatModel.connectionLabel !== null`.** That covers grace, absent and idle; the model already hides a dead seat's label.
- **Long-press is wired on the seat sheet's thumbs only**, which the brief names. Pending entries and plate badges open on tap. The press-and-release of a long-press opens one sheet: `useLongPress` swallows the trailing click, and opening is idempotent by key.
- **Section headings reuse V2 words:** 'Resources', 'Hand', 'Specials' (`OpponentRevealDialog`), 'Active cards' (the V2 thumbs' title), 'Pending effects' and 'None' (`PendingQueue`), 'Empty' (reveal hand).
- **New string (§10.6):** `'Statuses'` (the statuses heading).

```ts
// threat-geometry.ts
export const ARROW_SEAT_TRIM_PX = 40;
export const ARROW_PLATE_TRIM_PX = 44;
export const ARROW_FAN_SPACING_PX = 12;
export const ARROW_HEAD_PX = 8;
export const ARROW_HEAD_HALF_PX = 5;
export const FAN_BADGE_INSET_PX = 10;
export interface ArrowGeometry {
  /** The shaft, `M x y L x y`, ending where the head starts. */
  d: string;
  /** The head triangle, `M tip L corner L corner Z`. */
  head: string;
  /** The label's centre: the trimmed segment's midpoint. */
  label: Point;
}
export function arrowPath(from: Point, to: Point, options: { trimStart: number; trimEnd: number; offset: number }): ArrowGeometry | null;
export interface ThreatGeometryInput { model: ThreatsModel; layout: SeatLayout; arena: Size; povId: string; tappedSeatId: string | null }
export interface ThreatArrow {
  entry: ThreatEntry;
  /** Untrimmed anchors: a seat centre, or the plate centre for the POV. */
  from: Point;
  to: Point;
  geometry: ArrowGeometry;
  /** null while no seat is tapped; else whether the tapped seat sends or receives it. */
  highlighted: boolean | null;
}
export function threatArrows(input: ThreatGeometryInput): readonly ThreatArrow[];
export interface FanOutMark {
  group: FanOutGroup;
  /** The source seat's centre, or the plate for the POV. */
  burst: Point;
  /** One badge per copy aimed at a seat other than the POV's (the plate shows the POV's), at that seat's top-right corner. */
  targets: readonly { playerId: string; effectId: string; label: ThreatLabel; at: Point }[];
}
export function fanOutMarks(input: Omit<ThreatGeometryInput, 'tappedSeatId'>): readonly FanOutMark[];
/** Stroke hex: incoming = tone (`--threat-attack` / `--threat-other`), outgoing = `--mine`, third party = the source seat hue. */
export function arrowStroke(entry: ThreatEntry): string;

// plate-model.ts
/** The newest public play (spec v7 §6.1 item 2), else null. */
export function lastPlayedCard(actionLog: readonly ActionLogEntryView[]): CardInstance | null;
/** The in-play thumb a recurring chip ticks from (`inPlayThumbs` of its owner), else null. */
export function recurringChipThumb(view: PlayingStateView, chip: RecurringChip): InPlayThumb | null;
/** The catalog rate as a glyph (never a prediction, §6.5). */
export function recurringAmountGlyph(amount: RecurringAmount): { amount: GlyphAmount; signed?: CostSign };

// threat-copy.ts (additions)
export const HITS_ALL_LABEL = 'Hits all';
export const RECURRING_STATE_COPY: Readonly<Record<Exclude<RecurringChipState, 'live'>, string>> = { paused: 'Paused', ending: 'Ending' };

// threat-layer.tsx
export interface ThreatLayerProps { model: ThreatsModel; layout: SeatLayout; arena: Size; povId: string; tappedSeatId: string | null }
export function ThreatLayer(props: ThreatLayerProps): ReactElement;

// central-plate.tsx
export interface CentralPlateProps {
  view: PlayingStateView;
  model: ThreatsModel;
  /** `SeatsModel.layout.mode`: picks `plateCenterFor` and `PLATE_BOX`. */
  mode: LayoutMode;
  arena: Size;
  onOpenCard: (source: CardSheetSource) => void;
  // No `ref`: L74-12 measures the `data-zone="pending"` root in the DOM (`measureDropZones`).
  // L74-12 adds `dropHighlight?`.
}
export function CentralPlate(props: CentralPlateProps): ReactElement;
```

`apps/client/src/screens/arena/threat-geometry.test.ts` (12 tests). It uses a hand-built arc, so the expected paths are exact:

```ts
/** Threat geometry: spec v7 §6.5 (one arrow per effect, fan side by side, hits-all burst, grid shows POV arrows), L74-06. */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { V7_COLORS, v7SeatColor } from '../../ui/palette';
import type { SeatLayout } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { makePending, makeTableView } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { threats, type ThreatsModel } from '../../view-model/threats';
import { arrowPath, arrowStroke, fanOutMarks, threatArrows } from './threat-geometry';

const ARENA = { width: 400, height: 300 }; // portrait plate centre: (200, 252)
const ARC: SeatLayout = {
  kind: 'arc',
  mode: 'portrait',
  placements: [
    { playerId: 'p1', centerX: 100, centerY: 100 },
    { playerId: 'p2', centerX: 300, centerY: 100 },
    { playerId: 'p3', centerX: 200, centerY: 40 },
  ],
};
const GRID: SeatLayout = { kind: 'grid', mode: 'portrait', rows: [['p1', 'p2', 'p3']] };

function modelOf(pending: Parameters<typeof makePending>[0][]): ThreatsModel {
  return threats(makeTableView(['me', 'p1', 'p2', 'p3'], { pendingEffects: pending.map(makePending) }), null);
}
function arrowsOf(model: ThreatsModel, layout: SeatLayout = ARC, tappedSeatId: string | null = null) {
  return threatArrows({ model, layout, arena: ARENA, povId: 'me', tappedSeatId });
}

describe('arrowPath', () => {
  it('trims both ends and leaves room for the head', () => {
    expect(arrowPath({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 100, y: 0 }, { trimStart: 10, trimEnd: 10, offset: 0 })).toEqual({
      d: 'M 10 0 L 82 0',
      head: 'M 90 0 L 82 -5 L 82 5 Z',
      label: { x: 50, y: 0 },
    });
  });

  it('shifts a positive offset to the left of travel (up, for a rightward arrow)', () => {
    expect(arrowPath({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 100, y: 0 }, { trimStart: 10, trimEnd: 10, offset: 6 })?.d).toBe('M 10 -6 L 82 -6');
  });

  it('gives null when the trims and the head leave no shaft, or both ends are one point', () => {
    expect(arrowPath({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 15, y: 0 }, { trimStart: 10, trimEnd: 10, offset: 0 })).toBeNull();
    // Two adjacent grid seats: 84 px apart, 2 × 40 px of trims, no room for the 8 px head.
    expect(arrowPath({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 84, y: 0 }, { trimStart: 40, trimEnd: 40, offset: 0 })).toBeNull();
    expect(arrowPath({ x: 5, y: 5 }, { x: 5, y: 5 }, { trimStart: 0, trimEnd: 0, offset: 0 })).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('threatArrows (§6.5)', () => {
  it('fans two effects between one pair side by side, one label each (never a sum)', () => {
    const arrows = arrowsOf(modelOf([
      { id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'strong-attack', queuedAt: 7 },
      { id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'basic-attack', queuedAt: 7 },
    ]));
    expect(arrows.map((a) => [a.entry.effectId, a.geometry.d, a.entry.label])).toEqual([
      ['e1', 'M 140 106 L 252 106', { kind: 'damage', amount: 2 }],
      ['e2', 'M 140 94 L 252 94', { kind: 'damage', amount: 1 }],
    ]);
  });

  it('separates a mutual pair instead of drawing both on one line', () => {
    const arrows = arrowsOf(modelOf([
      { id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'strong-attack' },
      { id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'strong-attack' },
    ]));
    expect(arrows.map((a) => a.geometry.d)).toEqual(['M 140 106 L 252 106', 'M 260 94 L 148 94']);
  });

  it('ends incoming arrows at the plate and starts outgoing ones there', () => {
    const [incoming, outgoing] = arrowsOf(modelOf([
      { id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'spy' },
      { id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'me', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'spy' },
    ]));
    expect(incoming?.to).toEqual({ x: 200, y: 252 });
    expect(outgoing?.from).toEqual({ x: 200, y: 252 });
  });

  it('draws no arrow for fan-out members or a self-queued effect', () => {
    const model = modelOf([
      { id: 'm1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'mega-attack', queuedAt: 9 },
      { id: 'm2', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'mega-attack', queuedAt: 9 },
      { id: 'm3', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p3', cardId: 'mega-attack', queuedAt: 9 },
      { id: 's1', sourcePlayerId: 'me', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'suicide' },
    ]);
    expect(arrowsOf(model)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('in grid mode draws only arrows to and from the POV; a tapped seat adds its own', () => {
    const model = modelOf([
      { id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'spy' },
      { id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p3', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'spy' },
    ]);
    expect(arrowsOf(model, GRID).map((a) => a.entry.effectId)).toEqual(['e1']);
    expect(arrowsOf(model, GRID, 'p3').map((a) => a.entry.effectId)).toEqual(['e1', 'e2']);
  });

  it('highlights the tapped seat in and out arrows, and nothing while no seat is tapped', () => {
    const model = modelOf([
      { id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'spy' },
      { id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p3', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'spy' },
      { id: 'e3', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'p3', cardId: 'spy' },
    ]);
    expect(arrowsOf(model).map((a) => a.highlighted)).toEqual([null, null, null]);
    expect(arrowsOf(model, ARC, 'p1').map((a) => a.highlighted)).toEqual([true, true, false]);
  });
});

describe('fanOutMarks (§6.5)', () => {
  it('bursts the POV play on the plate with one badge per target seat corner', () => {
    const [mark] = fanOutMarks({
      model: modelOf(['p1', 'p2', 'p3'].map((t, i) => ({ id: `m${String(i)}`, sourcePlayerId: 'me', targetPlayerId: t, cardId: 'mega-attack' as const, queuedAt: 9 }))),
      layout: ARC, arena: ARENA, povId: 'me',
    });
    expect(mark?.burst).toEqual({ x: 200, y: 252 });
    expect(mark?.targets.map((t) => [t.playerId, t.at])).toEqual([
      ['p1', { x: 128, y: 64 }],
      ['p2', { x: 328, y: 64 }],
      ['p3', { x: 228, y: 4 }],
    ]);
  });

  it('bursts a third-party play on its seat and leaves the POV copy to the plate', () => {
    const [mark] = fanOutMarks({
      model: modelOf(['me', 'p2', 'p3'].map((t, i) => ({ id: `m${String(i)}`, sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: t, cardId: 'mega-attack' as const, queuedAt: 9 }))),
      layout: ARC, arena: ARENA, povId: 'me',
    });
    expect(mark?.burst).toEqual({ x: 100, y: 100 });
    expect(mark?.targets.map((t) => t.playerId)).toEqual(['p2', 'p3']);
  });
});

describe('arrowStroke (§6.5 ends and colours)', () => {
  it('tones incoming by threat, outgoing as mine, third party by the source seat hue', () => {
    const model = modelOf([
      { id: 'a', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'strong-attack' },
      { id: 'b', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'spy' },
      { id: 'c', sourcePlayerId: 'me', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'spy' },
      { id: 'd', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'p3', cardId: 'strong-attack' },
    ]);
    expect(model.entries.map(arrowStroke)).toEqual([
      V7_COLORS['threat-attack'],
      V7_COLORS['threat-other'],
      V7_COLORS.mine,
      v7SeatColor(model.entries[3]?.sourceSeatColorIndex ?? -1),
    ]);
  });
});
```

`apps/client/src/screens/arena/plate-model.test.ts` (5 tests):

```ts
/** Plate model: spec v7 §6.1 item 2 (landing spot), §6.5 (recurring chips at the catalog rate), L74-06. */
import type { ActionPlayedLogEntry } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { inPlayThumbs } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import { recurringChips } from '../../view-model/recurring-chips';
import { makePersistent, makePlayer, makeSelf, makeTableView } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { lastPlayedCard, recurringAmountGlyph, recurringChipThumb } from './plate-model';

// `ActionPlayedLogEntry`, not the `ActionLogEntryView` union, so `{ ...PLAY, … }` spreads one shape.
const DRAW: ActionPlayedLogEntry = { kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId: 'p1', action: 'draw', turnSequence: 1 };
const PLAY: ActionPlayedLogEntry = { kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId: 'p1', action: 'playCard', cardId: 'strong-attack', isUpgraded: true, targetPlayerId: 'me', turnSequence: 2 };

describe('lastPlayedCard (§6.1 item 2)', () => {
  it('is null for an empty log and for a log of draws only', () => {
    expect(lastPlayedCard([])).toBeNull();
    expect(lastPlayedCard([DRAW])).toBeNull();
  });

  it('takes the newest playCard, keyed by its log index', () => {
    const older: ActionPlayedLogEntry = { ...PLAY, cardId: 'spy', isUpgraded: false, turnSequence: 1 };
    expect(lastPlayedCard([older, PLAY, DRAW])).toEqual({ instanceId: 'landing-1', cardId: 'strong-attack', isUpgraded: true });
  });

  it("shows a multi-attack's first attack, and skips a play with no public card", () => {
    const multi: ActionPlayedLogEntry = {
      kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId: 'p2', action: 'playMultipleAttacks', turnSequence: 3,
      attacks: [{ cardId: 'basic-attack', targetPlayerId: 'p1', isUpgraded: false }, { cardId: 'strong-attack', targetPlayerId: 'me', isUpgraded: false }],
    };
    const blind: ActionPlayedLogEntry = { kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId: 'p1', action: 'playCard', turnSequence: 4 };
    expect(lastPlayedCard([PLAY, multi, blind])).toEqual({ instanceId: 'landing-1', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false });
  });
});

describe('recurring chips on the plate (§6.5)', () => {
  const view = makeTableView(['me', 'p1'], {
    players: [makePlayer('me', { isYou: true }), makePlayer('p1', { activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'poi', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 })] })],
    self: makeSelf({ activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'cur', cardId: 'curse' })] }),
  });

  it("finds the opponent's Poison thumb and the POV's own Curse thumb", () => {
    const [curse, poison] = recurringChips(view);
    if (curse === undefined || poison === undefined) throw new Error('expected two chips');
    expect(recurringChipThumb(view, curse)).toEqual(inPlayThumbs(view, 'me').find((thumb) => thumb.instance.instanceId === 'cur'));
    expect(recurringChipThumb(view, poison)).toEqual(inPlayThumbs(view, 'p1').find((thumb) => thumb.instance.instanceId === 'poi'));
  });

  it('draws the catalog rate: lives per turn, points taken, points per life', () => {
    expect(recurringAmountGlyph({ kind: 'lives-per-turn', lives: 1 })).toEqual({ amount: { kind: 'lives', amount: 1 }, signed: 'cost' });
    expect(recurringAmountGlyph({ kind: 'points-above-floor', maxPoints: 2, floor: 7 })).toEqual({ amount: { kind: 'points', amount: 2 }, signed: 'cost' });
    expect(recurringAmountGlyph({ kind: 'life-per-points-spent', pointsPerLife: 3 })).toEqual({ amount: { kind: 'pointsPerLife', amount: 3 } });
  });
});
```

(`recurringChips` lists chips in `view.players` order, so the POV's Curse comes before p1's Poison.)

`apps/client/src/screens/arena/threat-layer.test.tsx` (16 tests):

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Threat arrows, central plate and recurring chips: technical spec v7 §6.1 item 2, §6.5, §8.2, §11
 * (Q35); Appendix A `incoming-pending`, `waiting-on-others-strip`, `table-subchoice-mirror`,
 * `table-action-log-inspect` (L74-06). The arrow layer is aria-hidden, so it is read by attributes.
 */
import { formatCardLabel, type ActionLogEntryView, type PlayingStateView, type PublicPlayerView, type SubChoiceRequiredPayload } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { spokenTexts, visibleText } from '../../test/glyph-queries';
import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { inPlayThumbs } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import type { LayoutBox } from '../../view-model/seat-layout';
import { seats } from '../../view-model/seats';
import { makePending, makePersistent, makePlayer, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { threats } from '../../view-model/threats';
import { ArenaScreen } from './arena-screen';
import type { CardSheetSource } from './card-sheet-source';
import { CentralPlate } from './central-plate';
import { ThreatLayer } from './threat-layer';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const ARC_BOX: LayoutBox = { viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 }, arena: { width: 390, height: 360 } };
const PHONE_360: LayoutBox = { viewport: { width: 360, height: 740 }, arena: { width: 360, height: 320 } };
const MIRROR = (ids: string[]): SubChoiceRequiredPayload => ({ kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: ids, deadlineMs: 1 });

function renderThreats(view: PlayingStateView, options: { box?: LayoutBox; subChoice?: SubChoiceRequiredPayload; tappedSeatId?: string } = {}) {
  const box = options.box ?? ARC_BOX;
  const model = threats(view, options.subChoice ?? null);
  const { layout } = seats(view, box);
  const onOpenCard = vi.fn<(source: CardSheetSource) => void>();
  const result = renderUi(
    <div className="relative">
      <ThreatLayer model={model} layout={layout} arena={box.arena} povId={view.you} tappedSeatId={options.tappedSeatId ?? null} />
      <CentralPlate view={view} model={model} mode={layout.mode} arena={box.arena} onOpenCard={onOpenCard} />
    </div>,
  );
  return { ...result, model, onOpenCard };
}

const arrowIds = (): (string | null)[] => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-threat-arrow]'), (el) => el.getAttribute('data-threat-arrow'));
function arrow(id: string): Element {
  const el = document.querySelector(`[data-threat-arrow="${id}"]`);
  if (el === null) throw new Error(`no arrow ${id}`);
  return el;
}
function label(id: string): Element {
  const el = document.querySelector(`[data-threat-label][data-pending-id="${id}"]`);
  if (el === null) throw new Error(`no label ${id}`);
  return el;
}
function plate(): HTMLElement {
  const el = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="pending"]');
  if (el === null) throw new Error('no plate');
  return el;
}
function withPlayer(view: PlayingStateView, player: PublicPlayerView): PlayingStateView {
  return { ...view, players: view.players.map((entry) => (entry.id === player.id ? player : entry)) };
}

describe('threat arrows (§6.5)', () => {
  it('two attacks from one Assassin action on the same seat draw two labels with their own damage, never a sum', () => {
    renderThreats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 4, overrides: { pendingEffects: [
      makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'strong-attack', queuedAt: 7 }),
      makePending({ id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'basic-attack', queuedAt: 7 }),
    ] } }));
    expect(arrowIds()).toEqual(['e1', 'e2']);
    expect(visibleText(label('e1'))).toBe('2');
    expect(visibleText(label('e2'))).toBe('1');
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="threats"]')?.textContent).not.toContain('3');
  });

  it('MEGA ATTACK against 7 opponents renders one burst plus 7 target badges and no arrow', () => {
    const targets = ['p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4', 'p5', 'p6', 'p7'];
    renderThreats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8, overrides: { pendingEffects: targets.map((t, i) =>
      makePending({ id: `m${String(i)}`, sourcePlayerId: 'me', targetPlayerId: t, cardId: 'mega-attack', queuedAt: 9 })) } }), { box: PHONE_360 });
    const bursts = document.querySelectorAll('[data-threat-burst]');
    expect(bursts).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(bursts[0]?.textContent).toContain('Hits all');
    const badges = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-fan-out-target]'));
    expect(badges.map((badge) => badge.getAttribute('data-fan-out-target'))).toEqual(targets);
    expect(badges.map((badge) => visibleText(badge))).toEqual(targets.map(() => '20'));
    expect(arrowIds()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('a third-party hits-all play bursts on its seat; the POV copy lands on the plate', () => {
    const targets = ['me', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4', 'p5', 'p6', 'p7'];
    renderThreats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8, overrides: { pendingEffects: targets.map((t, i) =>
      makePending({ id: `m${String(i)}`, sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: t, cardId: 'mega-attack', queuedAt: 9 })) } }), { box: PHONE_360 });
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-threat-burst]')?.getAttribute('data-source-player-id')).toBe('p1');
    expect(document.querySelectorAll('[data-fan-out-target]')).toHaveLength(6);
    const badge = within(plate()).getByRole('button', { name: `Inspect ${formatCardLabel('mega-attack', false)}` });
    expect(visibleText(badge)).toBe('20');
  });

  it("a Mirror-redirected effect shows its badge on the redirector's arrow", () => {
    renderThreats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 4, overrides: { pendingEffects: [
      makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'p3', cardId: 'strong-attack', redirectedBy: 'mirror' }),
    ] } }));
    expect(arrow('e1').getAttribute('data-source-player-id')).toBe('p2');
    expect(label('e1').querySelector('[data-mirror-badge="mirror"]')?.textContent).toBe(formatCardLabel('mirror', false));
  });

  it('highlights eligible ids on their arrows and plate badges while a Mirror prompt is open', () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 4, overrides: { pendingEffects: [
      makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'strong-attack' }),
      makePending({ id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'basic-attack' }),
    ] } });
    const { unmount } = renderThreats(view, { subChoice: MIRROR(['e1']) });
    expect(plate().querySelector('[data-pending-id="e1"]')?.getAttribute('data-mirror-eligible')).toBe('true');
    expect(plate().querySelector('[data-pending-id="e2"]')?.hasAttribute('data-mirror-eligible')).toBe(false);
    expect(arrow('e1').getAttribute('data-mirror-eligible')).toBe('true');
    unmount();
    renderThreats(view);
    expect(document.querySelectorAll('[data-mirror-eligible]')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('in grid mode draws only POV arrows; a tapped seat adds its own', () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({ seatCount: 8, overrides: { pendingEffects: [
      makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'spy' }),
      makePending({ id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'me', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'spy' }),
      makePending({ id: 'e3', sourcePlayerId: 'p3', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'spy' }),
    ] } });
    const { unmount } = renderThreats(view, { box: PHONE_360 });
    expect(arrowIds()).toEqual(['e1', 'e2']);
    unmount();
    renderThreats(view, { box: PHONE_360, tappedSeatId: 'p3' });
    expect(arrowIds()).toEqual(['e1', 'e2', 'e3']);
  });

  it('a tapped seat lights its in and out arrows and dims the rest', () => {
    renderThreats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 4, overrides: { pendingEffects: [
      makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'p2', cardId: 'spy' }),
      makePending({ id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p3', targetPlayerId: 'p1', cardId: 'spy' }),
      makePending({ id: 'e3', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'p3', cardId: 'spy' }),
    ] } }), { tappedSeatId: 'p1' });
    expect(['e1', 'e2', 'e3'].map((id) => arrow(id).getAttribute('data-highlighted'))).toEqual(['true', 'true', 'false']);
  });

  it('hides the arrow layer from assistive tech and never takes a press', () => {
    renderThreats(makeArenaView());
    const layer = document.querySelector('[data-zone="threats"]');
    expect(layer?.getAttribute('aria-hidden')).toBe('true');
    expect(layer?.querySelector('button, a, [tabindex]')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('central plate (§6.1 item 2)', () => {
  it('keeps data-zone="pending" and the incoming hint anchor even when empty', () => {
    renderThreats(makeArenaView());
    expect(plate().getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('incoming');
    expect(plate().querySelector('[data-pending-id], [data-recurring-chip], [data-card-face]')).toBeNull();
    expect(plate().querySelector('[data-plate-landing]')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('gives each incoming effect its own badge button: two attacks never show a sum', () => {
    renderThreats(makeArenaView({ seatCount: 3, overrides: { pendingEffects: [
      makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'strong-attack', queuedAt: 7 }),
      makePending({ id: 'e2', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'basic-attack', queuedAt: 7 }),
    ] } }));
    const badges = within(plate()).getAllByRole('button', { name: /^Inspect / });
    expect(badges.map((badge) => badge.getAttribute('data-pending-id'))).toEqual(['e1', 'e2']);
    expect(badges.map((badge) => visibleText(badge))).toEqual(['2', '1']);
    expect(visibleText(plate())).not.toContain('3');
  });

  it('a plate badge opens the queue inspect', async () => {
    const { user, onOpenCard } = renderThreats(makeArenaView({ overrides: { pendingEffects: [
      makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'strong-attack' }),
    ] } }));
    await user.click(within(plate()).getByRole('button', { name: `Inspect ${formatCardLabel('strong-attack', false)}` }));
    expect(onOpenCard).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ kind: 'queue', effectId: 'e1', cardId: 'strong-attack', isUpgraded: false });
  });

  it('shows a Curse-on-you chip as paused under own Invisibility, in words', () => {
    renderThreats(makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ lives: 6, activePersistentEffects: [
      makePersistent({ id: 'cur', cardId: 'curse' }),
      makePersistent({ id: 'inv', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 2 }),
    ] }) } }));
    const chip = plate().querySelector('[data-recurring-chip="cur"]');
    expect(chip?.getAttribute('data-recurring-state')).toBe('paused');
    expect(chip === null ? '' : visibleText(chip)).toContain('Paused');
  });

  it('shows a Curse at 1 life as ending, in words', () => {
    renderThreats(makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ lives: 1, activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'cur', cardId: 'curse' })] }) } }));
    const chip = plate().querySelector('[data-recurring-chip="cur"]');
    expect(chip?.getAttribute('data-recurring-state')).toBe('ending');
    expect(chip === null ? '' : visibleText(chip)).toContain('Ending');
  });

  it("an opponent's Poison chip names its owner and rate and opens the in-play card sheet", async () => {
    const view = withPlayer(makeArenaView(), makePlayer('p1', { activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'poi', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 })] }));
    const { user, onOpenCard } = renderThreats(view);
    const chip = plate().querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-recurring-chip="poi"]');
    if (chip === null) throw new Error('no Poison chip');
    expect(chip.getAttribute('data-recurring-state')).toBe('live');
    expect(visibleText(chip)).toContain('p1');
    expect(spokenTexts(chip)).toContain('minus 1 life');
    await user.click(chip);
    expect(onOpenCard).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ kind: 'in-play', thumb: inPlayThumbs(view, 'p1').find((thumb) => thumb.instance.instanceId === 'poi') });
  });

  it('holds the last publicly played card on its landing spot, and opens it as a log inspect', async () => {
    const log: ActionLogEntryView[] = [
      { kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId: 'p1', action: 'playCard', cardId: 'strong-attack', isUpgraded: true, targetPlayerId: 'me', turnSequence: 2 },
      { kind: 'actionPlayed', actorPlayerId: 'p2', action: 'draw', turnSequence: 3 },
    ];
    const { user, onOpenCard } = renderThreats(makeArenaView({ overrides: { actionLog: log } }));
    const landing = plate().querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-plate-landing] [data-card-id="strong-attack"]');
    if (landing === null) throw new Error('no landing card');
    expect(landing.tagName).toBe('BUTTON');
    await user.click(landing);
    expect(onOpenCard).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ kind: 'log', cardId: 'strong-attack', isUpgraded: true });
  });
});

describe('the plate in the arena (§6.6.4)', () => {
  it('a plate badge opens the read-only card sheet; nothing is sent', async () => {
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: makeArenaView({ overrides: { pendingEffects: [
      makePending({ id: 'e1', sourcePlayerId: 'p1', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'strong-attack' }),
    ] } }) });
    const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Inspect ${formatCardLabel('strong-attack', false)}` }));
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-arena-sheet="card:queue-e1"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});
```

That is 12 + 5 + 16 = 33 new tests.

  - **`threat-geometry.ts`** (pure, no React):
    - **`arrowPath`:**
      - With `len = hypot(dx, dy)`, return null when `len <= trimStart + trimEnd + ARROW_HEAD_PX`, which also covers `len === 0`: the shaft must have room for the head, or `shaftEnd` would land before `start` and draw a reversed stub (two adjacent grid seats are 84 px apart, 80 px of trims).
      - Vectors: `u = (dx, dy) / len` and `n = (u.y, -u.x)`, the left of travel on a y-down screen.
      - Points:
        - `start = from + u * trimStart + n * offset`;
        - `end = to - u * trimEnd + n * offset`;
        - `shaftEnd = end - u * ARROW_HEAD_PX`;
        - corners `shaftEnd + n * ARROW_HEAD_HALF_PX` then `shaftEnd - n * ARROW_HEAD_HALF_PX`.
      - Output: `d = M start L shaftEnd`, `head = M end L c1 L c2 Z`, `label = midpoint(start, end)`.
      - Numbers print through `f(v) = String(Math.round(v * 100) / 100)`, so -0 prints `0`.

      - **Fanning:** group the visible set by the **unordered** pair (`[source, target].sort().join('|')`). Index `i` of `n` in wire order gives the canonical offset `(i - (n - 1) / 2) * ARROW_FAN_SPACING_PX`. Pass it as is when `source < target`, else negated, so both directions keep one screen side.
      - **Trims:** `ARROW_PLATE_TRIM_PX` at the POV end, `ARROW_SEAT_TRIM_PX` at a seat end. Drop the arrow when `arrowPath` returns null.

```
- `screens/arena/threat-layer.tsx`, `central-plate.tsx`, `threat-geometry.ts`,

    arena's elements come first in the DOM, so they anchor the resolution flash.
  - **Reachability:** every public pending effect stays reachable, on the plate or in a seat
```

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 threat arrows and central plate (L74-06)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.1 item 2, §6.5, §8.2, §11, §13 Q35; Appendix A `incoming-pending`,
`waiting-on-others-strip`, `table-fx-threat`, `table-subchoice-mirror`, `table-action-log-inspect`.

- **Dead state removed.** `TableFxContext.mirrorHighlightIds` and `setMirrorHighlightIds`
  (`fx/table-fx-hooks.ts`, `fx/table-fx-context.tsx`) are deleted. No product code set or read
  them: the V2 table derives its Mirror highlight from `subChoice`, and the arena from
  `ThreatEntry.mirrorEligible` (spec v7 §11). `screens/table/persistent-incoming.ts` was already
  deleted in L73-05.
- **Reachability.** The arrow layer (paths, labels, hits-all bursts and badges) is `aria-hidden`
  and never takes a press, so overlapping 44 px hit areas never pile up on a phone. The POV's
  incoming effects are buttons on the central plate. Every seat's public pending effects, in
  and out, are buttons in its seat sheet (L74-05). Grid mode draws only the POV's arrows plus a
  tapped seat's.
- **Fanning.** Arrows fan per seat pair, in both directions, not only per `groupKey`. A mixed
  multi-attack and a mutual pair therefore never overlap, and every arrow keeps its own label
  (golden rule 1).
- **Landing spot.** The plate shows the newest public play: the last `actionPlayed` `playCard`
  with a `cardId`, or a multi-attack's first attack. It is a face button that opens the read-only
  card sheet with the `log` source (catalog face, no footer, L56-05), so every card face on the
  plate is inspectable (spec v7 §6.6.5).
- **Recurring chips.** A chip is a button that opens the in-play card sheet of the persistent it
  ticks from. The amount is the catalog rate (Imposition shows its cap, not the floor). Paused
  and ending say so in words; live has no word.

---
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Two attacks from one Assassin action on the same seat render two labels, each with its own damage, and no sum:
  - `two attacks from one Assassin action on the same seat draw two labels …`
  - `gives each incoming effect its own badge button: two attacks never show a sum`
  - the geometry test `fans two effects between one pair side by side, one label each (never a sum)`.
- MEGA ATTACK against 7 opponents renders one burst plus 7 target badges:
  - `MEGA ATTACK against 7 opponents renders one burst plus 7 target badges and no arrow`
  - `a third-party hits-all play bursts on its seat; the POV copy lands on the plate`.
- A Mirror-redirected effect shows its badge on the redirector's arrow: `a Mirror-redirected effect shows its badge on the redirector's arrow`.
- Eligible ids are highlighted while a Mirror prompt is open: `highlights eligible ids on their arrows and plate badges while a Mirror prompt is open`.
- A Curse-on-you chip shows paused under own Invisibility: `shows a Curse-on-you chip as paused under own Invisibility, in words`.
- A plate badge opens the queue inspect:
  - `a plate badge opens the queue inspect`
  - `a plate badge opens the read-only card sheet; nothing is sent`.
- Brief items beyond the Acceptance line:
  - grid mode: `in grid mode draws only POV arrows; a tapped seat adds its own`;
  - tapped seat: `a tapped seat lights its in and out arrows and dims the rest`;
  - plate anchors: `keeps data-zone="pending" and the incoming hint anchor even when empty`;
  - landing spot: `holds the last publicly played card on its landing spot, and opens it as a log inspect`.
- The dead-context removal: Step 3 grep and Step 4 (`fx/` tests green).
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **The arrow layer is visual only** (`aria-hidden`, press-through). The brief makes only plate badges buttons. A 44 px label button per arrow would overlap seats and other labels on a 360 px phone. The seat sheet (L74-05) already lists every seat's pending effects as buttons, so every public pending effect stays reachable.
- **Arrows fan per seat pair, not per `groupKey` alone.** One Assassin action with two different cards on one seat (different `cardId`, so different `groupKey`) and a mutual pair would otherwise lie on one line with stacked labels. Fanning is presentation: the model's `groupKey` groups are a subset.
- **Straight arrows in both modes**, trimmed to the seat edge (40 px) and the plate (44 px), with a 12 px fan step. The browser gate tunes the numbers (§14.3).
- **No arrow when the trims and the head leave no shaft** (`len <= trimStart + trimEnd + ARROW_HEAD_PX`). Two adjacent grid seats (84 px apart) therefore draw no arrow between them; their effect stays reachable in either seat's sheet (L74-05). The grid tests use non-adjacent seats (p3 → p1) for that reason.
- **Grid-mode geometry is not measured.** Seats are placed by the same pure `seatCenters` as the arrows (L74-04), so the controller's suggested measured seat rects are not needed and the geometry is node-tested.
- **The POV's anchor is the plate centre**, for both incoming ends and outgoing starts. The POV has no seat in the arena; it is the bottom anchor below the plate.
- **A hits-all play** gets one burst on the source seat, or on the plate for the POV. Each target seat gets a badge at its top-right corner. The POV's own copy is the plate badge, never a seat badge.
- **The landing spot holds the newest public play:**
  - the last `actionPlayed` with `action: 'playCard'` and a `cardId`, or `'playMultipleAttacks'` showing `attacks[0]`;
  - draws, buys, sells, upgrades and deactivations are not plays;
  - it opens the read-only card sheet with L74-02's `log` source; L74-15's log reuses the same source.
- **The plate is bottom-anchored** at `PLATE_BOX[mode]` width with `PLATE_BOX` minimum height. Its order is landing spot, incoming badges, recurring chips; extra content grows upward into the space between the arc ends.
- **Mirror eligible** uses a thicker arrow and a 3 px `--cta-2` outline on labels and badges. The words live in the Mirror sheet (L74-18), which lists exactly those effects.
- **Recurring chip words:**
  - the amount is the catalog rate (`recurringAmountGlyph`); Imposition shows its cap (`−2 pts`), not "above 7", and the card sheet carries the full effect text;
  - `live` has no word;
  - the owner's nickname shows for Poison and Imposition; the POV's Curse needs none.
- **The plate's accessible name is `'Incoming'`** (`INCOMING_OPEN_LABEL`, V2).
- **New strings (§10.6):** `'Hits all'` (the fan-out burst), `'Paused'` and `'Ending'` (recurring chip states).
- **Designer question:** should arrow labels be pressable (opening the queue inspect) instead of the seat-sheet path? The default shown is not pressable.

```ts
// hand-fan-model.ts
export type HandCardSource = Extract<CardSheetSource, { kind: 'hand' | 'special' }>;

export interface HandFanCard {
  readonly source: HandCardSource;
  /** `cardActions(view, { zone, instance }, ctx)`; L74-12 reads `canDrag` and `drop`. */
  readonly actions: CardActionsModel;
  /** Use disabled for a card-specific reason (spec v7 §5.4): never off-turn or while locked. */
  readonly unplayable: boolean;
  /** `DISABLED_REASON_COPY[reason]` when `unplayable`, else null. */
  readonly description: string | null;
}

export interface HandFanModel {
  /** Attacks, then actions, each in `view.self.hand` order (§6.1 item 4). Empty for a walk-in. */
  readonly hand: readonly HandFanCard[];
  /** `view.self.specialCards` order. Empty for a walk-in. */
  readonly specials: readonly HandFanCard[];
}

/** V2 `card-band.tsx` captions and empty texts, verbatim. */
export const HAND_GROUP_COPY = {
  hand: { label: 'Hand', empty: 'Empty' },
  specials: { label: 'Specials', empty: 'None' },
} as const;

/** True for every `DisabledReason` except the lock reasons `not-your-turn` and `actions-locked`. */
export function isCardSpecificUseReason(reason: DisabledReason): boolean;
export function handFanModel(view: PlayingStateView, ctx: ActionContext): HandFanModel;
/** The newest open `card` sheet whose source is `hand` or `special`, else null. */
export function selectedHandInstanceId(state: ArenaSheetsState): string | null;

// hand-fan.tsx
export interface HandFanProps {
  readonly view: PlayingStateView;
  readonly ctx: ActionContext;
  /** `selectedHandInstanceId(sheets)`, derived by ArenaScreen: the card whose sheet is open. */
  readonly selectedInstanceId: string | null;
  /** A tap selects the card by opening its sheet. It never sends anything (§6.6.4). */
  readonly onOpenCard: (source: HandCardSource) => void;
}
export function HandFan(props: HandFanProps): ReactElement;   // L74-12 adds `drag?: HandFanDragProps`

// ui/card/card.tsx: CardCommonProps gains
description?: string;
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/hand-fan-model.test.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * handFanModel and selectedHandInstanceId: technical spec v7 §5.4 (unplayable), §6.1 item 4
 * (hand order); Appendix A `card-band` (L74-07).
 */
import type { SubChoiceRequiredPayload } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { ActionContext } from '../../view-model/action-lock';
import { DISABLED_REASONS } from '../../view-model/action-reasons';
import { makeCard, makePlayingView, makeSelf, makeTableView } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, openArenaSheet } from './arena-sheets';
import { handFanModel, isCardSpecificUseReason, selectedHandInstanceId } from './hand-fan-model';

const LIVE: ActionContext = { subChoice: null, readOnly: false };
const MIRROR_OPEN: SubChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 1 };

describe('handFanModel (L74-07)', () => {
  it('orders attacks then actions, each in hand order, and keeps the specials order (§6.1 item 4)', () => {
    const view = makePlayingView({
      self: makeSelf({
        hand: [makeCard('spy'), makeCard('basic-attack'), makeCard('shield'), makeCard('strong-attack')],
        specialCards: [makeCard('poison'), makeCard('block')],
      }),
    });
    const model = handFanModel(view, LIVE);
    expect(model.hand.map((card) => card.source)).toEqual([
      { kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('basic-attack') },
      { kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('strong-attack') },
      { kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy') },
      { kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('shield') },
    ]);
    expect(model.specials.map((card) => card.source)).toEqual([
      { kind: 'special', instance: makeCard('poison') },
      { kind: 'special', instance: makeCard('block') },
    ]);
  });

  it.each(DISABLED_REASONS)('treats %s as card-specific unless it is a lock reason', (reason) => {
    expect(isCardSpecificUseReason(reason)).toBe(reason !== 'not-your-turn' && reason !== 'actions-locked');
  });

  it('marks an unaffordable card unplayable on the POV turn only, with the reason copy', () => {
    const self = makeSelf({ points: 1, hand: [makeCard('basic-attack'), makeCard('strong-attack')] });
    expect(handFanModel(makePlayingView({ self }), LIVE).hand.map((card) => [card.unplayable, card.description]))
      .toEqual([[false, null], [true, 'Not enough points']]);
    const offTurn = handFanModel(makePlayingView({ self, currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }), LIVE);
    expect(offTurn.hand.every((card) => !card.unplayable && card.description === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('never marks a card unplayable while actions are locked (sub-choice, finished board)', () => {
    const view = makePlayingView({ self: makeSelf({ points: 0, hand: [makeCard('super-attack')] }) });
    for (const ctx of [{ subChoice: MIRROR_OPEN, readOnly: false }, { subChoice: null, readOnly: true }]) {
      expect(handFanModel(view, ctx).hand.map((card) => card.unplayable)).toEqual([false]);
    }
  });

  it('shows no card for a walk-in, whatever the filler self holds (Q38)', () => {
    const view = makeTableView(['me', 'p1', 'p2'], {
      you: 'watcher',
      isSpectator: true,
      self: makeSelf({ hand: [makeCard('spy')], specialCards: [makeCard('poison')] }),
    });
    expect(handFanModel(view, LIVE)).toEqual({ hand: [], specials: [] });
  });
});

describe('selectedHandInstanceId (L74-07)', () => {
  it('is the newest open hand or special card sheet, else null', () => {
    const kit = { kind: 'kit', kitId: 'kamikaze' } as const;
    expect(selectedHandInstanceId(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS)).toBeNull();
    expect(selectedHandInstanceId(openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, kit))).toBeNull();
    const both = openArenaSheet(
      openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, { kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'special', instance: makeCard('poison') } }),
      { kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy') } },
    );
    expect(selectedHandInstanceId(both)).toBe('spy-1');
    expect(selectedHandInstanceId(openArenaSheet(both, kit))).toBe('spy-1');
  });
});
```

That makes 18 test cases (the `it.each` over the 13 `DISABLED_REASONS` counts as 13).

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/hand-fan.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Hand fan: technical spec v7 §5.4, §6.1 item 4, §6.6.1, §6.6.4, §6.6.5; Appendix A `card-band`,
 * `global-card-face` (L74-07).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView, PrivateSelfView, SubChoiceRequiredPayload } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { fireEvent, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import type { ActionContext } from '../../view-model/action-lock';
import { DISABLED_REASON_COPY } from '../../view-model/action-reasons';
import { makeCard, makePersistent, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen } from './arena-screen';
import { HandFan, type HandFanProps } from './hand-fan';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const LIVE: ActionContext = { subChoice: null, readOnly: false };
const MIRROR_OPEN: SubChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 1 };

function viewWith(self: Partial<PrivateSelfView>, overrides: Partial<PlayingStateView> = {}): PlayingStateView {
  return makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf(self), ...overrides } });
}

function fan(view: PlayingStateView, props: Partial<HandFanProps> = {}): ReactElement {
  return <HandFan view={view} ctx={LIVE} selectedInstanceId={null} onOpenCard={vi.fn()} {...props} />;
}

function group(name: 'Hand' | 'Specials'): HTMLElement {
  return screen.getByRole('list', { name });
}

function labels(list: HTMLElement): (string | null)[] {
  return within(list).getAllByRole('button').map((card) => card.getAttribute('aria-label'));
}

function flipOf(container: HTMLElement, instanceId: string): Element | null {
  return container.querySelector(`[data-instance-id="${instanceId}"]`)?.closest('[data-upgrade-flip]') ?? null;
}

describe('HandFan (L74-07)', () => {
  it('renders attacks, then actions, then the specials group with gold rims (§6.1 item 4)', () => {
    renderUi(fan(viewWith({
      hand: [makeCard('spy'), makeCard('basic-attack'), makeCard('shield'), makeCard('strong-attack')],
      specialCards: [makeCard('poison'), makeCard('block')],
    })));
    const hand = group('Hand');
    const specials = group('Specials');
    expect(labels(hand)).toEqual(['Basic attack, 1 damage', 'Strong attack, 2 damage', 'Spy', 'Shield']);
    expect(labels(specials)).toEqual(['Poison', 'Block']);
    expect(within(hand).getAllByRole('button').map((card) => card.getAttribute('data-instance-id')))
      .toEqual(['basic-attack-1', 'strong-attack-1', 'spy-1', 'shield-1']);
    expect(within(hand).getAllByRole('button').every((card) => card.getAttribute('data-card-rim') === 'holo')).toBe(true);
    expect(within(specials).getAllByRole('button').every((card) => card.getAttribute('data-card-rim') === 'gold')).toBe(true);
    expect(hand.compareDocumentPosition(specials)).toBe(Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING);
    expect(hand.closest('[data-zone="hand"]')?.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('hand');
    expect(specials.closest('[data-zone="specials"]')?.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('specials');
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-card-row]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('desaturates no card off-turn, even an unaffordable one (§5.4)', () => {
    const { container } = renderUi(fan(viewWith(
      { points: 0, hand: [makeCard('super-attack'), makeCard('spy')], specialCards: [makeCard('poison')] },
      { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' },
    )));
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-unplayable]')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('on the POV turn desaturates only a card whose Use fails for a card-specific reason, described by it', () => {
    renderUi(fan(viewWith({ points: 1, hand: [makeCard('basic-attack'), makeCard('strong-attack')] })));
    const hand = group('Hand');
    const strong = within(hand).getByRole('button', {
      name: 'Strong attack, 2 damage',
      description: DISABLED_REASON_COPY['not-enough-points'],
    });
    expect(strong.getAttribute('data-unplayable')).toBe('true');
    const basic = within(hand).getByRole('button', { name: 'Basic attack, 1 damage' });
    expect(basic.hasAttribute('data-unplayable')).toBe(false);
    expect(basic.hasAttribute('aria-describedby')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('desaturates a hostile card under own Invisibility and leaves a self card playable (L58-06)', () => {
    renderUi(fan(viewWith({
      points: 20,
      hand: [makeCard('basic-attack'), makeCard('shield')],
      activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'eff-invisibility', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 3 })],
    })));
    const hand = group('Hand');
    expect(within(hand).getByRole('button', {
      name: 'Basic attack, 1 damage',
      description: DISABLED_REASON_COPY.invisibility,
    }).getAttribute('data-unplayable')).toBe('true');
    expect(within(hand).getByRole('button', { name: 'Shield' }).hasAttribute('data-unplayable')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('never desaturates while actions are locked on the POV turn (open sub-choice)', () => {
    const { container } = renderUi(fan(
      viewWith({ points: 0, hand: [makeCard('super-attack')] }),
      { ctx: { subChoice: MIRROR_OPEN, readOnly: false } },
    ));
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-unplayable]')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('asks to open the sheet on a tap, off-turn too, with the hand or special source', async () => {
    const onOpenCard = vi.fn();
    const { user } = renderUi(fan(
      viewWith({ hand: [makeCard('spy')], specialCards: [makeCard('poison')] }, { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }),
      { onOpenCard },
    ));
    await user.click(within(group('Hand')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }));
    await user.click(within(group('Specials')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Poison' }));
    expect(onOpenCard.mock.calls).toEqual([
      [{ kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy') }],
      [{ kind: 'special', instance: makeCard('poison') }],
    ]);
  });

  it('marks only the selected card pressed (§5.4 selected)', () => {
    renderUi(fan(viewWith({ hand: [makeCard('basic-attack'), makeCard('spy')] }), { selectedInstanceId: 'spy-1' }));
    const hand = group('Hand');
    expect(within(hand).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expect(within(hand).getByRole('button', { name: 'Basic attack, 1 damage' }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false');
  });

  it('renders the empty hand and empty specials states with their hint anchors (§6.1, Q60)', () => {
    const { container } = renderUi(fan(viewWith({ hand: [], specialCards: [] })));
    const hand = container.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="hand"]');
    const specials = container.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-zone="specials"]');
    if (hand === null || specials === null) throw new Error('missing hand or specials zone');
    expect(hand.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('hand');
    expect(specials.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('specials');
    expect(within(hand).getByText('Hand')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(within(hand).getByText('Empty')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(within(specials).getByText('Specials')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(within(specials).getByText('None')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryAllByRole('button')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('shows only the empty states for a walk-in, whatever the filler self holds (Q38)', () => {
    renderUi(fan(makeArenaView({
      pov: 'walk-in',
      overrides: { self: makeSelf({ hand: [makeCard('spy')], specialCards: [makeCard('poison')] }) },
    })));
    expect(screen.getByText('Empty')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText('None')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryAllByRole('button')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('plays the upgrade flip when a held card upgrades, never on mount (L50-05)', () => {
    const { container, rerender } = renderUi(fan(viewWith({
      hand: [makeCard('spy'), makeCard('shield', { isUpgraded: true })],
    })));
    expect(flipOf(container, 'spy-1')?.getAttribute('data-upgrade-flip')).toBe('0');
    expect(flipOf(container, 'shield-1')?.getAttribute('data-upgrade-flip')).toBe('0');
    rerender(fan(viewWith({
      hand: [makeCard('spy', { isUpgraded: true }), makeCard('shield', { isUpgraded: true })],
    })));
    expect(flipOf(container, 'spy-1')?.getAttribute('data-upgrade-flip')).toBe('1');
    expect(flipOf(container, 'spy-1')?.getAttribute('data-motion')).toBe('full');
    expect(flipOf(container, 'shield-1')?.getAttribute('data-upgrade-flip')).toBe('0');
  });

  it('keeps the upgrade cue under reduced motion, as a fade (§8.3)', () => {
    const { container, rerender } = renderUi(fan(viewWith({ hand: [makeCard('spy')] })), { reducedMotion: true });
    rerender(fan(viewWith({ hand: [makeCard('spy', { isUpgraded: true })] })));
    expect(flipOf(container, 'spy-1')?.getAttribute('data-upgrade-flip')).toBe('1');
    expect(flipOf(container, 'spy-1')?.getAttribute('data-motion')).toBe('reduced');
  });

  it('blocks the context menu on a face (long-press is ours, §6.6.5)', () => {
    renderUi(fan(viewWith({ hand: [makeCard('spy')] })));
    expect(fireEvent.contextMenu(within(group('Hand')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('hand fan in the arena (L74-07)', () => {
  it('opens the card sheet on a tap, off-turn too, lifts the card and sends no intent (§6.6.4)', async () => {
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: viewWith({ hand: [makeCard('spy')] }, { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }) });
    const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    const hand = group('Hand');
    expect(hand.closest('[data-arena-region="hand"]')?.getAttribute('data-zone')).toBe('card-band');
    await user.click(within(hand).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).not.toBeNull();
    expect(within(hand).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).toBeNull();
    expect(within(hand).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false');
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('still opens the sheet of an unplayable card on the POV turn', async () => {
    const props = makeArenaProps({ view: viewWith({ points: 0, hand: [makeCard('strong-attack')] }) });
    const { user } = renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    const strong = within(group('Hand')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Strong attack, 2 damage' });
    expect(strong.getAttribute('data-unplayable')).toBe('true');
    await user.click(strong);
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Strong attack' })).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});
```

    - Update the `CardRoot` doc line to: "a toggle-style button when the caller passes `selected`, a plain button otherwise, an article without `onActivate`".
  - **`hand-fan-model.ts` (pure).**
    - `handFanModel` returns `{ hand: [], specials: [] }` when `view.isSpectator === true`. A walk-in's `self` is filler (Q38).
    - Otherwise, `hand` holds `view.self.hand` cards with `isSharedAttackCardId(cardId)` (in hand order), then the other hand cards (in hand order). `specials` is `view.self.specialCards` as given. The server routes every special to `specialCards` (`transferCardInstance`), so the hand holds only shared attacks and actions.
    - Each card:

```ts
const actions = cardActions(view, { zone, instance }, ctx);      // zone 'hand' | 'special'
const use = actions.actions.find((action) => action.kind === 'use');
const reason = use?.reason ?? null;
const unplayable = reason !== null && isCardSpecificUseReason(reason);
// description = unplayable ? DISABLED_REASON_COPY[reason] : null; source = { kind: zone === 'hand' ? 'hand' : 'special', instance }
```

    - `isCardSpecificUseReason(reason)` is `reason !== 'not-your-turn' && reason !== 'actions-locked'`. `cardActions` puts the lock first, so a card-specific reason only shows on the POV's own turn with nothing locked.
    - `selectedHandInstanceId` walks `state.open` from the newest entry, with `findLast` (ES2023). It returns the first `card` sheet whose `source.kind` is `hand` or `special`, as `source.instance.instanceId`, else null.
  - **`hand-fan.tsx`.** It exports only `HandFan` and its props type. The private pieces `HandGroup`, `HandSlot` and `HandCard` are not exported. Structure:

```tsx
type HandFanStyle = CSSProperties & { readonly '--hand-strips': string; readonly '--hand-fulls': string };
// Every card but the last of its group sits in a narrower slot, so the next card overlaps it from the
// right (DOM order paints later cards on top) and its left-edge chips stay readable (spec v7 §5.4).
// The slot is at least 44 px and at most a card plus an 8 px gap. Below 44 px the hand scrolls inside
// itself (§6.1 item 4). 44px = the scroller's 2 × 16 px padding + the 12 px group gap.
const STRIP_WIDTH =
  'max(44px, min(var(--hand-card-w) + 8px, (100cqi - 44px - var(--hand-fulls) * var(--hand-card-w)) / max(var(--hand-strips), 1)))';

export function HandFan({ view, ctx, selectedInstanceId, onOpenCard }: HandFanProps): ReactElement {
  const model = handFanModel(view, ctx);
  const groups = [model.hand, model.specials];
  const style: HandFanStyle = {
    '--hand-strips': String(groups.reduce((sum, cards) => sum + Math.max(0, cards.length - 1), 0)),
    '--hand-fulls': String(groups.filter((cards) => cards.length > 0).length),
  };
  return (
    // The container for `cqi`. `--hand-card-w` restates the Card `hand` width (spec v7 §5.4); each card
    // takes it through `[--card-w:var(--hand-card-w)]`, the Card's only resize path.
    <div data-hand-fan="" className="@container h-full w-full [--hand-card-w:clamp(64px,19vw,120px)]" style={style}>
      <ScrollArea axis="x" className="h-full px-4 pt-4 pb-3">
        <div className="mx-auto flex w-max items-end gap-3">
          <HandGroup zone="hand" … />
          <HandGroup zone="specials" … />
        </div>
      </ScrollArea>
    </div>
  );
}
```

  - **`HandGroup`.**
    - Root: `<div data-zone={zone} data-hint-anchor={zone} className="flex items-end">`.
    - Empty: `<p className="flex min-h-11 flex-col justify-center px-1 font-v7-body text-v7-caption font-bold text-v7-text-2"><span>{copy.label}</span><span>{copy.empty}</span></p>`, with `copy = HAND_GROUP_COPY[zone]`.
    - Otherwise: `<ul aria-label={copy.label} className="flex items-end">`, holding one `HandSlot` per card. The slot is keyed by instance id, gets `last` for the final card, and gets `selected={instanceId === selectedInstanceId}`.
  - **`HandSlot`.**
    - Renders `<li className={`relative shrink-0 focus-within:z-10 ${selected ? 'z-10' : ''}`} style={last ? undefined : { width: STRIP_WIDTH }}>`.
    - The `li` is not clipped: the card overflows its slot to the right, and a selected or focused card rises above its neighbour.
  - **`HandCard` (the upgrade flip, V2 `AnimatedCard`).** V2 `card-band.tsx` always passes `skipEntrance`. V2 replays a 90° `rotateY` flip when `isUpgraded` changes, and does nothing under reduced motion.
    - Track flips with the adjust-state-during-render pattern (as in `ui/primitives/sheet.tsx`). Never use `useEffect` + `setState`, which the React Compiler lint rejects:

```tsx
const reduced = usePrefersReducedMotion();
const [shownUpgraded, setShownUpgraded] = useState(instance.isUpgraded);
const [flips, setFlips] = useState(0);
if (shownUpgraded !== instance.isUpgraded) {
  setShownUpgraded(instance.isUpgraded);
  setFlips(flips + 1);
}
// <motion.div key={flips} data-upgrade-flip={String(flips)} data-motion={motionAttribute(reduced)} …>
//   flips === 0 → initial={false} (no entrance, ever)
//   full motion → initial={{ rotateY: 90 }} animate={{ rotateY: 0 }}
//                 transition={{ duration: durationS('base'), ease: EASE_ENTRANCE }} style={{ transformPerspective: 600 }}
//   reduced     → initial={{ opacity: 0.4 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} transition={{ duration: durationS('fast') }}
```

    - Raw `motion` props are used here because `V7MotionTarget` (`ui/motion/reduced-motion.ts`) has no `rotateY`. The reduced path is the §8.3 fade.
    - Inside it:

```tsx
<Card size="hand" instance={instance} selected={selected} unplayable={card.unplayable}
  {...(card.description !== null ? { description: card.description } : {})}
  className="[--card-w:var(--hand-card-w)]" onActivate={() => { onOpen(card.source); }} />
```

    - The Card root carries `data-instance-id`. The wrapper never repeats it, and nothing uses `data-card-row`.
  - **`arena-screen.tsx`.**
    - Inside the `hand` region element (L74-01's `data-arena-region="hand"` `data-zone="card-band"`, kept as is), replace L74-01's placeholder children with:

```tsx
<HandFan view={props.view} ctx={ctx} selectedInstanceId={selectedHandInstanceId(sheets)}
  onOpenCard={(source) => { openSheet({ kind: 'card', source }); }} />
```

    - `sheets` and `openSheet` belong to L74-05's sheet host; its `card` case already renders the opened `CardSheet` (Appendix A.5). This task adds no host.
