```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 Card: aria-pressed only for a selection, and a description passthrough (L74-07)

Source: technical spec v7 §5.4 (unplayable), §11 (Q39); `docs/backlog_v7.md` L74-07 Watch point;
Appendix A `global-card-face`, `card-band`.

- `ui/card/card.tsx` emits `aria-pressed` only when the caller passes `selected`. The hand fan
  passes it (a tap selects the card and opens its sheet). Faces that only open a sheet (thumbs,
  log, queue, pool) pass none, so they no longer read as toggles. This supersedes L72's
  "aria-pressed always equals selected".
- The new optional `description` becomes the face's accessible description (`aria-describedby`
  to a hidden node). The hand passes `DISABLED_REASON_COPY[reason]` for an unplayable card, so
  the reason behind the dimming reaches assistive tech. The face stays activatable.
- The hand dims a card when Use is disabled for a card-specific reason: every `DisabledReason`
  except `not-your-turn` and `actions-locked`. `cardActions` ranks the lock above every other
  reason, so this happens only on the POV's own turn with no sub-choice open, and never dims the
  whole hand (§5.4).
- Lot 74 plan Appendix A.8 ruling 8 lists `ui/card/card.tsx` and its test for this backlog Watch
  point. `DESIGN.md` is updated in the same commit (spec v7 §5.8).

---
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Cards render in the order attacks, actions, then the specials group:
  - `renders attacks, then actions, then the specials group with gold rims (§6.1 item 4)`;
  - `orders attacks then actions, each in hand order, and keeps the specials order (§6.1 item 4)`.
- Off-turn no card is desaturated: `desaturates no card off-turn, even an unaffordable one (§5.4)` and `marks an unaffordable card unplayable on the POV turn only, with the reason copy`.
- On the POV's turn an unaffordable card is desaturated and still opens the sheet with its reason:
  - `on the POV turn desaturates only a card whose Use fails for a card-specific reason, described by it` (the reason as the card's description);
  - `still opens the sheet of an unplayable card on the POV turn`;
  - the sheet's reason line is L74-08 (`disables Card Transformer Use with nothing to consume, with its reason (§11)`).
- A tap opens the sheet and sends no intent: `opens the card sheet on a tap, off-turn too, lifts the card and sends no intent (§6.6.4)`.
- An empty hand and empty specials render their empty states:
  - `renders the empty hand and empty specials states with their hint anchors (§6.1, Q60)`;
  - `shows only the empty states for a walk-in, whatever the filler self holds (Q38)`.
- Watch point:
  - `aria-pressed` only with `selected`: the edited `card.test.tsx` test and `marks only the selected card pressed (§5.4 selected)`;
  - the description passthrough: `links a caller description to the face on both root forms (L74-07)`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Which reasons dim a card.** Every `DisabledReason` except `not-your-turn` and `actions-locked`.
  - The ones Use can actually produce are `temporarily-unavailable`, `invisibility`, `attack-ban`, `no-valid-target`, `pool-empty`, `reanimation-armed`, `nothing-to-consume` and `not-enough-points` (`card-actions.ts` `reasonForUse`).
  - The lock reasons are excluded because the lock outranks them in the model. That is what keeps dimming on the POV's own turn only, and never during a sub-choice or on the finished board.
- **Selection is derived** from the open sheets (`selectedHandInstanceId`), not held as separate state. It ends when the sheet closes. During L74-10 targeting the collapsed sheet stays open, so the card stays lifted.
- **Hand order** sorts by `isSharedAttackCardId`, which is stable within each family. The hand holds only shared cards, because `transferCardInstance` sends specials to `specialCards`.
- **Empty states and group names** reuse V2's `Hand` / `Empty` and `Specials` / `None`.
  - The caption is visible only when the group is empty; a filled group is named by its `aria-label`.
  - The gold rim and the gap separate the specials (§6.1 item 4).
- **The overlap is CSS.**
  - The strip slots use `max(44px, min(card + 8px, fitted))` on a container (`cqi`), with the counts passed as `--hand-strips` / `--hand-fulls`.
  - The values are starting points the L74-26 browser gate checks at 360, 375 and 390 px. happy-dom has no layout, so no test pins widths.
- **`ScrollArea` without a `label`.** The cards are focusable, so the scroller needs no name or Tab stop of its own, and no new string.
- **No entrance motion.** The upgrade flip is V2's 90° `rotateY`. Under reduced motion it becomes a 120 ms opacity fade (§8.3 keeps fades), where V2 showed nothing.
- **New strings:** none.

```ts
// arena-commit.ts: verbatim Appendix A.6
export type ArenaIntent =
  | CardIntent
  | { kind: 'upgradeCard'; payload: UpgradeCardPayload }
  | { kind: 'playMultipleAttacks'; payload: PlayMultipleAttacksPayload }
  | { kind: 'deactivatePersistent'; payload: DeactivatePersistentPayload }
  | { kind: 'clearSpy'; payload: ClearSpyPayload }
  | { kind: 'drawCard' }
  | { kind: 'activateDuplication' };
export type ArenaCommit = (intent: ArenaIntent) => void;
export type ArenaIntentHandlers = Pick<ArenaScreenProps,
  'onPlayCard' | 'onSellCard' | 'onUpgradeCard' | 'onPlayMultipleAttacks' | 'onDraw' | 'onClearSpy'
  | 'onDeactivatePersistent' | 'onActivateDuplication'>;
export function createArenaCommit(handlers: ArenaIntentHandlers): ArenaCommit;

// card-sheet-actions.ts
/** V2 `screens/table/card-actions.tsx` label, verbatim. */
export const USE_ACTION_LABEL = 'Use';
export const UPGRADE_ACTION_COST: StructuredCost = { kind: 'upgradePoint', amount: 1 };
/** The catalog `sellYield`, as V2 shows it (an upgraded copy's refunded upgrade point is not shown). */
export function sellYieldOf(instance: CardInstance): StructuredCost | null;

// card-sheet-source.ts: an added export (A.5 is unchanged)
/**
 * The ref `cardActions` reads against the latest view (Review Focus 1). A held hand or special
 * card takes its live instance (an upgrade since the sheet opened). One that left `view.self`
 * becomes `{ zone: 'read-only', instance }`. Every other source is `cardRefFor(source)`.
 */
export function liveCardRefFor(source: CardSheetSource, view: PlayingStateView): CardRef;

// card-sheet.tsx: CardSheetProps gains (L74-02's props unchanged; Appendix A.7)
/** The single intent exit (L74-08). Without it the sheet renders no action row (a pure inspect). */
commit?: ArenaCommit;
/** Use routed `target`: the sheet hands the card and its seats over. L74-10 passes it from ArenaScreen and collapses the sheet into targeting. */
onBeginTargeting?: (request: { instanceId: string; seatIds: readonly string[] }) => void;
```

```ts
/**
 * createArenaCommit: technical spec v7 §6.6.4; Lot 74 plan Appendix A.6 (L74-08).
 */
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { createArenaCommit, type ArenaIntentHandlers } from './arena-commit';

function makeHandlers() {
  return {
    onPlayCard: vi.fn<ArenaIntentHandlers['onPlayCard']>(),
    onSellCard: vi.fn<ArenaIntentHandlers['onSellCard']>(),
    onUpgradeCard: vi.fn<ArenaIntentHandlers['onUpgradeCard']>(),
    onPlayMultipleAttacks: vi.fn<ArenaIntentHandlers['onPlayMultipleAttacks']>(),
    onDraw: vi.fn<ArenaIntentHandlers['onDraw']>(),
    onClearSpy: vi.fn<ArenaIntentHandlers['onClearSpy']>(),
    onDeactivatePersistent: vi.fn<(effectId: string) => void>(),
    onActivateDuplication: vi.fn<() => void>(),
  };
}
type Handlers = ReturnType<typeof makeHandlers>;
const NAMES = [
  'onPlayCard', 'onSellCard', 'onUpgradeCard', 'onPlayMultipleAttacks', 'onDraw', 'onClearSpy',
  'onDeactivatePersistent', 'onActivateDuplication',
] as const satisfies readonly (keyof Handlers)[];

function expectOnly(handlers: Handlers, name: keyof Handlers): void {
  for (const other of NAMES) {
    if (other !== name) expect(handlers[other]).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  }
}

describe('createArenaCommit (L74-08, Appendix A.6)', () => {
  it('maps a playCard with no option key to onPlayCard(instanceId) alone', () => {
    const handlers = makeHandlers();
    createArenaCommit(handlers)({ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: 'card-1' } });
    expect(handlers.onPlayCard.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['card-1']]);
    expectOnly(handlers, 'onPlayCard');
  });

  it.each([
    { targetPlayerId: 'p1' },
    { quantity: 3 },
    { consumeInstanceId: 'card-2' },
    { targetPlayerId: 'p1', quantity: 2, consumeInstanceId: 'card-2' },
  ])('passes only the defined option keys: %o', (options) => {
    const handlers = makeHandlers();
    createArenaCommit(handlers)({ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: 'card-1', ...options } });
    expect(handlers.onPlayCard.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['card-1', options]]);
    expectOnly(handlers, 'onPlayCard');
  });

  it('maps sellCard to onSellCard', () => {
    const handlers = makeHandlers();
    createArenaCommit(handlers)({ kind: 'sellCard', payload: { instanceId: 'card-1' } });
    expect(handlers.onSellCard.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['card-1']]);
    expectOnly(handlers, 'onSellCard');
  });

  it('maps upgradeCard to onUpgradeCard, an in-play id included', () => {
    const handlers = makeHandlers();
    createArenaCommit(handlers)({ kind: 'upgradeCard', payload: { instanceId: 'active-shield' } });
    expect(handlers.onUpgradeCard.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['active-shield']]);
    expectOnly(handlers, 'onUpgradeCard');
  });

  it('maps playMultipleAttacks to onPlayMultipleAttacks with the same attacks', () => {
    const handlers = makeHandlers();
    const attacks = [{ instanceId: 'a-1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' }, { instanceId: 'a-2', targetPlayerId: 'p1' }];
    createArenaCommit(handlers)({ kind: 'playMultipleAttacks', payload: { attacks } });
    expect(handlers.onPlayMultipleAttacks.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toBe(attacks);
    expectOnly(handlers, 'onPlayMultipleAttacks');
  });

  it('maps deactivatePersistent, clearSpy, drawCard and activateDuplication', () => {
    const handlers = makeHandlers();
    const commit = createArenaCommit(handlers);
    commit({ kind: 'deactivatePersistent', payload: { effectId: 'eff-1' } });
    commit({ kind: 'clearSpy', payload: { targetPlayerId: 'p2' } });
    commit({ kind: 'drawCard' });
    commit({ kind: 'activateDuplication' });
    expect(handlers.onDeactivatePersistent.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['eff-1']]);
    expect(handlers.onClearSpy.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['p2']]);
    expect(handlers.onDraw.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([[]]);
    expect(handlers.onActivateDuplication.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([[]]);
    for (const name of ['onPlayCard', 'onSellCard', 'onUpgradeCard', 'onPlayMultipleAttacks'] as const) {
      expect(handlers[name]).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    }
  });

  it('makes an intent without its optional handler a no-op', () => {
    const all = makeHandlers();
    // Built by hand: a rest-destructure would leave two unused bindings that
    // @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars rejects (ignoreRestSiblings is off).
    const required: ArenaIntentHandlers = {
      onPlayCard: all.onPlayCard,
      onSellCard: all.onSellCard,
      onUpgradeCard: all.onUpgradeCard,
      onPlayMultipleAttacks: all.onPlayMultipleAttacks,
      onDraw: all.onDraw,
      onClearSpy: all.onClearSpy,
    };
    const commit = createArenaCommit(required);
    expect(() => {
      commit({ kind: 'deactivatePersistent', payload: { effectId: 'eff-1' } });
      commit({ kind: 'activateDuplication' });
    }).not.toThrow();
    for (const name of NAMES) expect(all[name]).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

That makes 10 test cases (the `it.each` counts as 4).

Append to `apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet-source.test.ts`, merging the imports into the file's own (`makeCard`, `makePlayingView`, `makeSelf` from `../../view-model/test-fixtures`; `inPlayThumbs` from `../../view-model/card-actions`; `cardRefFor`, `liveCardRefFor`, `type CardSheetSource` from `./card-sheet-source`):

```ts
describe('liveCardRefFor (L74-08, Review Focus 1)', () => {
  const spy = makeCard('spy');
  const poison = makeCard('poison');

  it('reads a held hand card from the latest view, upgrade included', () => {
    const upgraded = makeCard('spy', { isUpgraded: true });
    expect(liveCardRefFor({ kind: 'hand', instance: spy }, makePlayingView({ self: makeSelf({ hand: [upgraded] }) })))
      .toEqual({ zone: 'hand', instance: upgraded });
  });

  it('turns a hand card that left the hand into a read-only ref', () => {
    expect(liveCardRefFor({ kind: 'hand', instance: spy }, makePlayingView()))
      .toEqual({ zone: 'read-only', instance: spy });
  });

  it('does the same for a special', () => {
    expect(liveCardRefFor({ kind: 'special', instance: poison }, makePlayingView({ self: makeSelf({ specialCards: [poison] }) })))
      .toEqual({ zone: 'special', instance: poison });
    expect(liveCardRefFor({ kind: 'special', instance: poison }, makePlayingView()))
      .toEqual({ zone: 'read-only', instance: poison });
  });

  it('leaves every other source to cardRefFor', () => {
    const view = makePlayingView({ self: makeSelf({ shield: 4 }) });
    const [thumb] = inPlayThumbs(view, 'me');
    if (thumb === undefined) throw new Error('no own Shield thumb');
    const queue: CardSheetSource = { kind: 'queue', effectId: 'e-1', cardId: 'basic-attack', isUpgraded: false };
    expect(liveCardRefFor({ kind: 'in-play', thumb }, view)).toEqual(cardRefFor({ kind: 'in-play', thumb }));
    expect(liveCardRefFor(queue, view)).toEqual(cardRefFor(queue));
  });
});
```

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet-actions.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Card sheet actions: technical spec v7 §6.6.1, §6.6.2, §6.6.4, §11 (Q39); Appendix A
 * `card-actions-dialog`, `table-use-disabled-reasons`, `table-use-target-routing`; Lot 74 Review
 * Focus 1 and 3 (L74-08). Intents are read on the `makeArenaProps` spies (the mocked connection).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView, PrivateSelfView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { fireEvent, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { UserEvent } from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import type { ActionContext } from '../../view-model/action-lock';
import { DISABLED_REASON_COPY } from '../../view-model/action-reasons';
import { makeCard, makePersistent, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import type { ArenaCommit } from './arena-commit';
import { ArenaScreen } from './arena-screen';
import { CardSheet } from './card-sheet';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const LIVE: ActionContext = { subChoice: null, readOnly: false };

function viewWith(self: Partial<PrivateSelfView>, overrides: Partial<PlayingStateView> = {}): PlayingStateView {
  return makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf(self), ...overrides } });
}

function renderArena(view: PlayingStateView) {
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view });
  return { ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />), props };
}

async function openCard(user: UserEvent, group: 'Hand' | 'Specials', card: string, title: string | RegExp): Promise<HTMLElement> {
  await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('list', { name: group })).getByRole('button', { name: card }));
  return screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: title });
}

function expectDisabled(sheet: HTMLElement, name: string, reason: string): void {
  expect(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name, description: reason }).hasAttribute('disabled')).toBe(true);
}

describe('card sheet actions (L74-08)', () => {
  it('Use on Poison sends one playCard {instanceId} and closes; Upgrade shows its reason; no Sell on a special', async () => {
    const { user, props } = renderArena(viewWith({ points: 8, specialCards: [makeCard('poison')] }));
    const sheet = await openCard(user, 'Specials', 'Poison', 'Poison');
    expectDisabled(sheet, 'Upgrade minus 1 upgrade point', DISABLED_REASON_COPY['no-upgrade-point']);
    expect(within(sheet).queryByRole('button', { name: /^Sell/ })).toBeNull();
    const use = within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Use minus 8 pts' });
    expect(within(use).getByText('−8')).not.toBeNull();
    await user.click(use);
    expect(vi.mocked(props.onPlayCard).mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['poison-1']]);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Poison' })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('sends upgradeCard, then sellCard once the upgraded state arrives, each once (§6.6.1)', async () => {
    const { user, props, rerender } = renderArena(viewWith({ upgradePoints: 1, hand: [makeCard('spy')] }));
    let sheet = await openCard(user, 'Hand', 'Spy', 'Spy');
    const use = within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Use minus 2 pts' });
    const upgrade = within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Upgrade minus 1 upgrade point' });
    const sell = within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Sell plus 2 pts' });
    expect(within(sheet).getAllByRole('button', { name: /^(Use|Upgrade|Sell)\b/ })).toEqual([use, upgrade, sell]);
    await user.click(upgrade);
    expect(vi.mocked(props.onUpgradeCard).mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['spy-1']]);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).toBeNull();
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} view={viewWith({ hand: [makeCard('spy', { isUpgraded: true })] })} />);
    sheet = await openCard(user, 'Hand', 'Spy upgraded', 'Spy');
    expect(within(sheet).queryByRole('button', { name: /^Upgrade/ })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Sell plus 2 pts' }));
    expect(vi.mocked(props.onSellCard).mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['spy-1']]);
    expect(props.onPlayCard).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('disables a hostile Use under own Invisibility and keeps Upgrade and Sell (L58-06)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = renderArena(viewWith({
      points: 5,
      upgradePoints: 1,
      hand: [makeCard('basic-attack')],
      activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'eff-invisibility', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 3 })],
    }));
    const sheet = await openCard(user, 'Hand', 'Basic attack, 1 damage', 'Basic attack');
    expectDisabled(sheet, 'Use minus 1 pt', DISABLED_REASON_COPY.invisibility);
    expect(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Upgrade minus 1 upgrade point' }).hasAttribute('disabled')).toBe(false);
    await user.click(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Sell plus 1 pt' }));
    expect(vi.mocked(props.onSellCard).mock.calls).toStrictEqual([['basic-attack-1']]);
    expect(props.onPlayCard).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('disables Card Transformer Use with nothing to consume, with its reason (§11)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = renderArena(viewWith({ points: 2, specialCards: [makeCard('card-transformer')] }));
    const sheet = await openCard(user, 'Specials', 'Card Transformer', 'Card Transformer');
    expectDisabled(sheet, 'Use minus 2 pts', DISABLED_REASON_COPY['nothing-to-consume']);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('disables every action off-turn with Not your turn', async () => {
    const { user, props } = renderArena(viewWith(
      { points: 10, upgradePoints: 1, hand: [makeCard('spy')] },
      { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' },
    ));
    const sheet = await openCard(user, 'Hand', 'Spy', 'Spy');
    for (const name of ['Use minus 2 pts', 'Upgrade minus 1 upgrade point', 'Sell plus 2 pts']) {
      expectDisabled(sheet, name, DISABLED_REASON_COPY['not-your-turn']);
    }
    expect(within(sheet).getAllByText(DISABLED_REASON_COPY['not-your-turn'])).toHaveLength(3);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('sends nothing when the sheet opens and closes (§6.6.4)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = renderArena(viewWith({ points: 10, hand: [makeCard('spy')] }));
    await openCard(user, 'Hand', 'Spy', 'Spy');
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('sends nothing on Use for a targeted card until a target is chosen (§6.6.2)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = renderArena(viewWith({ points: 2, hand: [makeCard('spy')] }));
    const sheet = await openCard(user, 'Hand', 'Spy', 'Spy');
    const use = within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Use minus 2 pts' });
    expect(use.hasAttribute('disabled')).toBe(false);
    await user.click(use);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('sends one playCard for a double tap on Use (Review Focus 3)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = renderArena(viewWith({ points: 8, specialCards: [makeCard('poison')] }));
    const sheet = await openCard(user, 'Specials', 'Poison', 'Poison');
    await user.dblClick(within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Use minus 8 pts' }));
    expect(props.onPlayCard).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('turns the sheet read-only when its card leaves the hand, so no intent names it (Review Focus 1)', async () => {
    const { user, props, rerender } = renderArena(viewWith({ points: 8, specialCards: [makeCard('poison')] }));
    const sheet = await openCard(user, 'Specials', 'Poison', 'Poison');
    const staleUse = within(sheet).getByRole('button', { name: 'Use minus 8 pts' });
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} view={viewWith({ points: 8, specialCards: [] })} />);
    const after = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Poison' });
    expect(within(after).queryByRole('button', { name: /^(Use|Upgrade|Sell)\b/ })).toBeNull();
    // Belt-and-braces: after the rerender `staleUse` is detached, so this click cannot reach React.
    // The proof is the `queryByRole(...)` line above: the live panel has no action left.
    fireEvent.click(staleUse);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('hands a targeted Use to onBeginTargeting and commits nothing (the L74-10 seam)', async () => {
    const commit = vi.fn<ArenaCommit>();
    const onBeginTargeting = vi.fn<(request: { instanceId: string; seatIds: readonly string[] }) => void>();
    const onClose = vi.fn();
    const { user } = renderUi(
      <CardSheet
        open
        source={{ kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy') }}
        view={viewWith({ points: 2, hand: [makeCard('spy')] })}
        ctx={LIVE}
        onClose={onClose}
        commit={commit}
        onBeginTargeting={onBeginTargeting}
      />,
    );
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).getByRole('button', { name: 'Use minus 2 pts' }));
    expect(onBeginTargeting.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([[{ instanceId: 'spy-1', seatIds: ['p1', 'p2'] }]]);
    expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(onClose).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- Use on Regeneration or Card Transformer is rendered with its model state but is not tested here: its click does nothing until L74-10 adds the in-sheet pre-send step.

```ts
const { instanceId, targetPlayerId, quantity, consumeInstanceId } = intent.payload;
const options: PlayCardOptions = {
  ...(targetPlayerId !== undefined ? { targetPlayerId } : {}),
  ...(quantity !== undefined ? { quantity } : {}),
  ...(consumeInstanceId !== undefined ? { consumeInstanceId } : {}),
};
if (Object.keys(options).length === 0) handlers.onPlayCard(instanceId);
else handlers.onPlayCard(instanceId, options);
```

    - `deactivatePersistent` → `handlers.onDeactivatePersistent?.(effectId)`; `activateDuplication` → `handlers.onActivateDuplication?.()`; every other case calls its required handler with the payload field.
    - It never checks legality (golden rule 8).

    - The Use cost is `cardCostChip(cardId, isUpgraded)` (`ui/card/card-face.ts`): the same catalog play cost the card's cost chip prints, with the per-life rate by tier for Regeneration.
  - **`card-sheet-source.ts`.** Add `liveCardRefFor(source, view)`:
    - `hand`: `view.self.hand.find((card) => card.instanceId === source.instance.instanceId)`. If found, `{ zone: 'hand', instance: found }`, else `{ zone: 'read-only', instance: source.instance }`.
    - `special`: the same against `view.self.specialCards`.
    - Every other kind: `cardRefFor(source)`. In-play refs need no check, because `cardActions` already gives an in-play card it no longer finds as the POV's own `actions: []`.
  - **`card-sheet.tsx`.** Add `commit?` and `onBeginTargeting?` to `CardSheetProps` (Appendix A.7).
    - In the body, `source` is L74-02's `liveCardSheetSource(props.source, props.view)` (in-play cards re-read); destructure `open`, `view`, `ctx`, `commit`, `onBeginTargeting` and `onClose` from the props now:

```tsx
const ref = liveCardRefFor(source, view);
const model = cardActions(view, ref, ctx);
// The face follows the live copy of a held card; any other source keeps L74-02's sheetInstanceFor.
const face = ref.zone === 'hand' || ref.zone === 'special' ? ref.instance : sheetInstanceFor(source);
// One commit per view (Review Focus 3): a second tap before the next state update sends nothing,
// even on the stale panel an exit animation keeps on screen.
const committedFor = useRef<PlayingStateView | null>(null);
// Per opening, not per mount: a closed sheet stays mounted through its exit (`closing`) and is reused
// when the same card opens again (same key), so the guard re-arms when `open` turns true. A ref is
// not written during render (React Compiler lint), hence the effect; L74-10 adds the state resets.
useEffect(() => {
  if (open) committedFor.current = null;
}, [open]);
function send(intent: ArenaIntent): void {
  if (commit === undefined || committedFor.current === view) return;
  committedFor.current = view;
  commit(intent);
  onClose();
}
function onUse(): void {
  const use = model.use;
  if (use === null) return;
  switch (use.kind) {
    case 'send':
      send({ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: model.instanceId } });
      return;
    case 'target':
      // L74-10 collapses this sheet into targeting; nothing is sent until a seat is tapped.
      onBeginTargeting?.({ instanceId: model.instanceId, seatIds: use.seatIds });
      return;
    case 'regeneration':
    case 'consume':
      // L74-10 adds the in-sheet pre-send step (quantity / consume tiles); until then Use does nothing.
      return;
  }
}
```

    - Regeneration and Card Transformer Use render with their model state (enabled, reason, cost) but their click is a no-op in this task, and no L74-08 test covers it.

    - The action row is the whole `<Sheet actions>` slot: L74-02's read-only sheet passes no `actions`. It renders only when `commit !== undefined && model.actions.length > 0`: `<div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-2">{model.actions.map(renderAction)}</div>`.
    - `renderAction(action: CardAction)` is a `switch (action.kind)` in model order. Every button carries `key={action.kind}`, `data-card-action={action.kind}` (the L75 tutorial anchor), `disabled={!action.enabled}` and `{...(action.reason !== null ? { disabledReason: DISABLED_REASON_COPY[action.reason] } : {})}`:
      - `use`: `variant="primary"`, `onClick={onUse}`, label `{USE_ACTION_LABEL}` followed by `{' '}` and `<ActionCost amount={cost} signed="cost" />` when `cardCostChip(face.cardId, face.isUpgraded)` is not null.
      - `upgrade`: `variant="secondary"`, `onClick` → `send({ kind: 'upgradeCard', payload: { instanceId: model.instanceId } })`, label `{CARD_UPGRADE_LABEL}{' '}<ActionCost amount={UPGRADE_ACTION_COST} signed="cost" />`. It also serves in-play cards: `model.instanceId` is the effect id, `active-shield` or the Sentence id.
      - `sell`: `variant="quiet"`, `onClick` → `send({ kind: 'sellCard', payload: { instanceId: model.instanceId } })`, label `{CARD_SELL_LABEL}` plus `{' '}<ActionCost amount={yield} signed="gain" />` when `sellYieldOf(face)` is not null.
      - `multi-attack`: `return null;` (L74-11 adds the entry).
      - `deactivate`: `return null;` (L74-09 adds it).
    - `ActionCost` is a private component. Icons sit in a `--bg-0` well on CTA fills (DESIGN.md › Cost glyphs):

```tsx
<span data-action-cost="" className={`${ICON_WELL_CLASS} gap-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 text-v7-text`}>
  <CostAmount amount={amount} signed={signed} title={null} />
</span>
```

    - The accessible names are then "Use minus 8 pts", "Upgrade minus 1 upgrade point" and "Sell plus 2 pts". These are `spokenCost` wording, as in V2 (ruling 4).

- [ ] **Step 5: Docs.**
  - **`docs/agent/frontend.md`, Arena section.** Append after the L74-07 bullet:

```
- `screens/arena/arena-commit.ts` (L74-08, spec v7 §6.6.4, Lot 74 Appendix A.6):
  `createArenaCommit(handlers)` is the only exit for every `ArenaIntent`. `ArenaScreen` builds it
  once per render from its props and passes `commit` down; no component calls an intent handler
  (`onPlayCard`, `onSellCard`, …) directly. Leave, forfeit and chat are App-level actions: the
  `TopBar` (and later the chat sheet) calls `onLeave` / `onForfeit` / `onSendChat` directly,
  outside the helper; L75 handles the tutorial Skip separately. `playCard` forwards only the defined option keys, and a missing optional handler
  (`onDeactivatePersistent`, `onActivateDuplication`) makes the intent a no-op. It never checks
  legality. L74-17 (own-intent FX), L74-25 (reject spring-back) and L75 (`allowTutorialSend`)
  plug in here.
- `screens/arena/card-sheet.tsx` actions + `card-sheet-actions.ts` (L74-08, spec v7 §6.6.1,
  §6.6.2, §11): the row comes from `cardActions(view, liveCardRefFor(source, view), ctx).actions`,
  in model order. Use (`primary`, "Use" plus the play cost as a `−` `CostAmount`), Upgrade
  (`secondary`, `−1` upgrade point) and Sell (`quiet`, `+` the catalog `sellYield`), each tagged
  `data-card-action`. A disabled action shows `DISABLED_REASON_COPY[reason]` under it (`Button
  disabledReason`); there is no shared "Actions locked" line. A Use routed `send` commits
  `playCard {instanceId}` and closes the sheet; a Use routed `target` calls
  `onBeginTargeting({ instanceId, seatIds })` (L74-10 collapses the sheet into targeting) and
  sends nothing; Regeneration and Card Transformer get their in-sheet pre-send step in L74-10.
  A sheet commits at most once per view, so a double tap sends one intent; the guard re-arms
  each time the sheet opens (a closed sheet stays mounted and is reused on reopen). A
  hand or special card that left `view.self` turns its open sheet read-only (`liveCardRefFor`),
  so no intent can name a stale instance.
```

  - **`docs/agent/decisions.md`.** Append at the end:

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 arena: one commit helper; stale and repeated taps send nothing (L74-08)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.6.1, §6.6.4 and §11 (Q39); Lot 74 plan Appendix A.6 and Review
Focus 1 and 3.

- `screens/arena/arena-commit.ts` `createArenaCommit(handlers)` is the only exit for every
  `ArenaIntent`. `ArenaScreen` builds it once per render from its props. Components receive
  `commit` and never call an intent handler directly. L74-17 (own-intent FX), L74-25 (reject
  spring-back) and L75 (`allowTutorialSend`) plug in there. It never checks legality (golden
  rule 8).
- Leave, forfeit and chat are App-level actions, not `ArenaIntent`s: the `TopBar` (and later the
  chat sheet) calls `onLeave` / `onForfeit` / `onSendChat` directly from an explicit confirm or
  send button, outside the helper. L75 handles the tutorial Skip separately.
- The card sheet reads `cardActions` against the latest view through `liveCardRefFor`. A hand
  or special card that left `view.self` (played, stolen, sold, transformed, wiped) turns the open
  sheet read-only, with no action, instead of closing it under the player's finger.
- A card sheet commits at most once per view. A second tap on any action before the next state
  update sends nothing, whatever the exit animation keeps on screen. The guard re-arms when the
  sheet opens again: a closed sheet stays mounted and is reused on reopen, so a rejected intent
  (an `actionReject` with no state update) can be retried by reopening the card.
- V2's "Actions locked — you can still read the card." line is not carried over. Each disabled
  action shows its own reason (§11, Q39), `Not your turn` and `Actions locked` included.

---
```

  - **`docs/backlog_v7.md`.**

  1. `pnpm verify` green.
  2. `git add apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-commit.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-commit.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet-actions.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet-actions.test.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet-source.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/card-sheet-source.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-screen.tsx docs/agent/frontend.md docs/agent/decisions.md docs/backlog_v7.md`
  3. `git commit` with this message and no attribution trailer:

```
feat(arena): add card sheet actions (L74-08)

Every arena intent leaves through createArenaCommit. A sheet commits
once per view, and a card that left the hand turns its sheet
read-only (Review Focus 1 and 3).
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Use on Poison sends one `playCard {instanceId}`: `Use on Poison sends one playCard {instanceId} and closes; Upgrade shows its reason; no Sell on a special`.
- Sell sends `sellCard` and Upgrade sends `upgradeCard`: `sends upgradeCard, then sellCard once the upgraded state arrives, each once (§6.6.1)`.
- Under own Invisibility, Use on a hostile card is disabled with the Invisibility reason while Upgrade and Sell stay enabled: `disables a hostile Use under own Invisibility and keeps Upgrade and Sell (L58-06)`.
- Card Transformer with nothing to consume shows its reason: `disables Card Transformer Use with nothing to consume, with its reason (§11)`.
- Off-turn every action is disabled with 'not your turn': `disables every action off-turn with Not your turn`.
- Opening and closing the sheet sends nothing: `sends nothing when the sheet opens and closes (§6.6.4)` and `sends nothing on Use for a targeted card until a target is chosen (§6.6.2)`.
- Review Focus 1: `turns the sheet read-only when its card leaves the hand, so no intent names it (Review Focus 1)` and the `liveCardRefFor` tests.
- Review Focus 3 (Use): `sends one playCard for a double tap on Use (Review Focus 3)`.
- Every intent leaves through one helper:
  - the 10 `arena-commit.test.ts` cases;
  - `hands a targeted Use to onBeginTargeting and commits nothing (the L74-10 seam)`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Button variants.** Use `primary` (`--cta`), Upgrade `secondary`, Sell `quiet`, and later Deactivate `danger` (L74-09). V7 has no green Button, so V2's green Sell cannot carry over: **Designer question** (default `quiet`).
- **Reasons sit under each button.** No V2 "Actions locked — you can still read the card." line. Off-turn this shows three "Not your turn" lines: **Designer question**, whether to collapse an identical lock reason into one line (default: one per button, §11).
- **Costs come from the catalog**, never from game state.
  - Use: `cardCostChip`, the same source as the card's cost chip, Regeneration's per-life rate included.
  - Sell: the catalog `sellYield`. As in V2, the extra upgrade point an upgraded copy refunds is not shown.
  - Upgrade: one upgrade point.
  - The amounts sit in a `--bg-0` well because `point` / `life` icons are banned directly on the CTA fills (DESIGN.md › Icons).
- **No Cancel button in the action row.** The sheet closes by Esc, backdrop and swipe, and L74-02 owns any Close button.
- **A stale card leaves the sheet open, read-only.** Review Focus 1 allows either option; this one avoids closing a sheet under the player's finger.
- **The double-tap guard is per view** (`committedFor`, a ref holding the `view` at the last commit). L74-02 keeps a closing sheet mounted through its exit, and the guard still holds on that stale panel; it lets a deliberate second action through once the next state arrives. Every later in-sheet commit (L74-09 Deactivate, L74-10 pre-send Use, L74-11 Play) goes through `send`.
- **The guard re-arms on every opening, which covers a retry after a reject.** `send` always closes the sheet, and a closed sheet stays mounted (`closing`) and is reused when the same card opens again. If the server rejects the intent with only an `actionReject` (no state update), `view` is unchanged, so a guard that only compared views would block every later tap on that card. Clearing `committedFor.current` when `open` turns true means the player retries by reopening the card. L74-10 and L74-11 reset their per-opening state on the same transition.
- **`commit` is optional on `CardSheet`.** Without it there is no action row, which keeps L74-02's read-only tests valid. ArenaScreen always passes it.
- **The L74-10 seam is `onBeginTargeting({ instanceId, seatIds })`** (Appendix A.7). Only `target` leaves the sheet; pre-send choices live inside it (§6.6.2).
  - Until L74-10 passes it, Use on a targeted card sends nothing; Use on Regeneration or Card Transformer does nothing until L74-10 adds its in-sheet step. No intent can leave without its target or choice.
  - The Use-on-Spy test stays true after L74-10.
- **`data-card-action`** on each action button is a new anchor for the L75 tutorial mapping of `use` / `upgrade` / `sell`.
- **New strings:** none. `Use` is the V2 `card-actions.tsx` literal; `Upgrade` and `Sell` come from `chrome-labels.ts`; the reasons come from `DISABLED_REASON_COPY`.

```ts
// self-strip.tsx
export interface SelfStripProps {
  /** `selfStrip(view, ctx)`, computed once in ArenaScreen (Appendix A.2); L74-13 reads `.dock` from the same value. */
  readonly model: SelfStripModel;
  /** The own kit portrait opens the kit sheet (§6.1 item 3). */
  readonly onOpenKit: (kitId: KitId) => void;
  /** A thumb opens the card sheet with source `{ kind: 'in-play', thumb }` (§6.6.1). */
  readonly onOpenCard: (source: CardSheetSource) => void;
}
/** Null for a walk-in (no kit, no resources): L74-23 owns the Watching strip. */
export function SelfStrip(props: SelfStripProps): ReactElement | null;

// card-sheet-actions.ts additions (V2 `card-actions.tsx` labels, verbatim)
export const DEACTIVATE_ACTION_LABEL = 'Deactivate';
export const CANCEL_ACTION_LABEL = 'Cancel';
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/self-strip.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Self strip and own in-play card actions: technical spec v7 §6.1 item 3, §6.6.1, §11 (Q37,
 * Q40); Appendix A `private-zone-identity`, `active-persistent-thumbs`, `sentence-chips`,
 * `table-inspect-active-upgrade`, `deactivate-confirm-dialog`,
 * `table-deactivate-confirm-escape-layer`, `global-resource-captions`,
 * `global-activated-art-guard`; Lot 74 Appendix A.8 rulings 1 and 9 (L74-09).
 */
import type {
  PendingSentenceView,
  PlayingStateView,
  PrivateSelfView,
  SubChoiceRequiredPayload,
} from '@card-battle/shared';
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { visibleText } from '../../test/glyph-queries';
import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import type { ActionContext } from '../../view-model/action-lock';
import { DISABLED_REASON_COPY } from '../../view-model/action-reasons';
import { inPlayThumbs } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import { selfStrip } from '../../view-model/self-strip';
import { makePersistent, makePlayer, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import type { ArenaCommit } from './arena-commit';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { CardSheet } from './card-sheet';
import type { CardSheetSource } from './card-sheet-source';
import { SelfStrip } from './self-strip';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const LIVE: ActionContext = { subChoice: null, readOnly: false };
const MIRROR_OPEN: SubChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 1 };
const POISON = makePersistent({ id: 'eff-poison', cardId: 'poison', counter: 3 });
const INVISIBILITY = makePersistent({ id: 'eff-invisibility', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 3 });
const OWN_SENTENCE: PendingSentenceView = { id: 'sentence:3:me:1', sourcePlayerId: 'me', remainingOwnerTurns: 2, isUpgraded: false };
const UPGRADE = 'Upgrade minus 1 upgrade point';

function viewWith(self: Partial<PrivateSelfView>, overrides: Partial<PlayingStateView> = {}): PlayingStateView {
  return makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf(self), ...overrides } });
}

function renderStrip(view: PlayingStateView) {
  const onOpenKit = vi.fn();
  const onOpenCard = vi.fn();
  const result = renderUi(<SelfStrip model={selfStrip(view, LIVE)} onOpenKit={onOpenKit} onOpenCard={onOpenCard} />);
  return { ...result, onOpenKit, onOpenCard };
}

function renderArena(view: PlayingStateView, extra: Partial<ArenaScreenProps> = {}) {
  const onDeactivatePersistent = vi.fn<(effectId: string) => void>();
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view, onDeactivatePersistent, ...extra });
  return { ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />), props, onDeactivatePersistent };
}

function stripOf(container: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {
  const strip = container.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-arena-region="self-strip"]');
  if (strip === null) throw new Error('no self-strip region');
  return strip;
}

function renderCardSheet(source: CardSheetSource, view: PlayingStateView, commit: ArenaCommit) {
  return renderUi(<CardSheet open source={source} view={view} ctx={LIVE} onClose={vi.fn()} commit={commit} />);
}

describe('SelfStrip (L74-09)', () => {
  it('shows the four own resources under data-zone resources, with visible captions (L43-01)', () => {
    const { container } = renderStrip(viewWith({ lives: 9, shield: 4, points: 12, upgradePoints: 1 }));
    const resources = container.querySelector('[data-zone="resources"]');
    if (resources === null) throw new Error('no resources zone');
    expect(resources.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('resources');
    const pills = Array.from(resources.querySelectorAll('[data-resource-kind]'));
    expect(pills.map((pill) => pill.getAttribute('data-resource-kind'))).toEqual(['life', 'shield', 'point', 'upgradePoint']);
    expect(pills.map((pill) => visibleText(pill))).toEqual(['9Lives', '4Shield', '12Points', '1Upgrade points']);
    expect(pills[0]?.getAttribute('data-size')).toBe('lg');
    expect(screen.queryByText('Shield ↑')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('marks an upgraded own shield with "Shield ↑"', () => {
    renderStrip(viewWith({ shield: 7, shieldIsUpgraded: true }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Shield ↑')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('opens the own kit from the portrait button', async () => {
    const { user, onOpenKit } = renderStrip(viewWith({ kitId: 'tactician' }));
    const portrait = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Inspect your kit' });
    expect(portrait.getAttribute('data-zone')).toBe('kit-portrait');
    await user.click(portrait);
    expect(onOpenKit.mock.calls).toEqual([['tactician']]);
  });

  it('shows own flow badges, the connection label and Duplication active, in model order', () => {
    renderStrip(makeArenaView({
      overrides: {
        players: [
          makePlayer('me', {
            isYou: true,
            blockTurnsRemaining: 2,
            duplicationActive: true,
            connection: { status: 'connected', disconnectedAt: null, automaticTurnsTaken: 0, consecutiveTimeouts: 2 },
          }),
          makePlayer('p1'),
          makePlayer('p2'),
        ],
        self: makeSelf({ kitId: 'duplicator', activePersistentEffects: [INVISIBILITY] }),
      },
    }));
    const block = screen.getByText('Block · 2 left');
    const invisible = screen.getByText('Invisible');
    expect(block.compareDocumentPosition(invisible)).toBe(Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING);
    expect(invisible.parentElement?.textContent).toBe('Invisible3');
    expect(screen.getByText('idle timeouts 2/5')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByText('Duplication active')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Reanimating')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('shows the attack ban when no Block turn is left', () => {
    renderStrip(makeArenaView({
      overrides: { players: [makePlayer('me', { isYou: true, blockAttacksForbidden: true }), makePlayer('p1'), makePlayer('p2')] },
    }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Block · attacks banned')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('shows Reanimating for an eliminated POV with Reanimation armed', () => {
    renderStrip(makeArenaView({ pov: 'reanimating' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Reanimating')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('lists own thumbs Shield, actives, then Sentences, with counters only for card lives and turns (ruling 1)', async () => {
    const { user, onOpenCard } = renderStrip(viewWith(
      { shield: 4, activePersistentEffects: [POISON, INVISIBILITY] },
      { pendingSentences: [OWN_SENTENCE] },
    ));
    const list = screen.getByRole('list', { name: 'Active cards' });
    const thumbs = within(list).getAllByRole('button');
    expect(thumbs.map((thumb) => thumb.getAttribute('aria-label')))
      .toEqual(['Shield', 'Poison, 3 card lives', 'Invisibility, 3 turns remaining', 'Sentence']);
    expect(thumbs.map((thumb) => thumb.querySelector('[data-card-counter]')?.getAttribute('data-card-counter') ?? null))
      .toEqual([null, 'card-lives', 'turns', null]);
    expect(thumbs.every((thumb) => !thumb.hasAttribute('aria-pressed'))).toBe(true);
    const sentence = thumbs[3]?.closest('li') ?? null;
    if (sentence === null) throw new Error('no Sentence thumb item');
    expect(within(sentence).getByText('Turns left')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(visibleText(sentence)).toBe('2');
    await user.click(within(list).getByRole('button', { name: 'Shield' }));
    expect(onOpenCard.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'in-play',
      thumb: { instance: { instanceId: 'active-shield', cardId: 'shield' } },
    });
  });

  it('renders nothing for a walk-in (L74-23 owns the Watching strip)', () => {
    const { container } = renderStrip(makeArenaView({ pov: 'walk-in' }));
    expect(container.innerHTML).toBe('');
  });
});

describe('self strip and in-play card sheet in the arena (L74-09)', () => {

  it('offers no Upgrade on an opponent base Shield while the own base Shield offers it (Q37 regression)', async () => {

    });
    const [opponentShield] = inPlayThumbs(view, 'p1');
    const [ownShield] = inPlayThumbs(view, 'me');
    if (opponentShield === undefined || ownShield === undefined) throw new Error('missing Shield thumbs');
    const commit = vi.fn<ArenaCommit>();
    const first = renderCardSheet({ kind: 'in-play', thumb: opponentShield }, view, commit);
    expect(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Shield' })).queryByRole('button', { name: /^Upgrade/ })).toBeNull();
    first.unmount();
    const { user } = renderCardSheet({ kind: 'in-play', thumb: ownShield }, view, commit);
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Shield' })).getByRole('button', { name: UPGRADE }));
    expect(commit.mock.calls).toStrictEqual([[{ kind: 'upgradeCard', payload: { instanceId: 'active-shield' } }]]);
  });
});
```

That makes 18 test cases (the `it.each` counts as 3). `renderCardSheet` passes exactly `CardSheet`'s props: L74-02's `{ open, source, view, ctx, onClose }` plus L74-08's `commit`.

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 self strip, in-play sheet actions and the private-zone CSS scope (L74-09)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.1 item 3, §6.6.1, §11 (Q37, Q40); Appendix A `private-zone-identity`,
`deactivate-confirm-dialog`, `table-deactivate-confirm-escape-layer`, `global-resource-captions`;
Lot 74 plan Appendix A.8 rulings 1, 5 and 9.

- The own resources keep visible captions (L43-01, Appendix A keep) through `Pill
  captionVisible`. The lives pill is `lg` (22 px, the Vital rule).
- In-play Upgrade and Deactivate come only from `cardActions` on the thumb's ref. So an
  opponent's Shield sheet has no Upgrade (Q37), Deactivate is disabled during a sub-choice
  (Q40), and an eliminated POV's leftover Shield shows Upgrade disabled with `actions-locked`.
- The Lot 69 confirm is a `Dialog` stacked over the card sheet. The sheet stack gives Esc to the
  top sheet only (Q49), so one Esc closes the confirm and keeps the card sheet; V2 needed a
  dedicated guard for this. The confirm closes when its persistent leaves the model, and its
  Deactivate is disabled with the reason if the turn passes while it is open.
- Shield and Sentence thumbs never pass `counter`. A Sentence's remaining owner turns are a
  separate "Turns left" badge (ruling 1).
- `index.css`'s landscape `[data-zone="private-zone"] [data-zone="kit-portrait"]` width rule is
  scoped to `.table-shell`. It keeps the V2 table's look and no longer shrinks the arena's
  `KitPortrait` (ruling 5).

---
```

```
feat(arena): add self strip (L74-09)

In-play Upgrade and Deactivate follow cardActions, so an opponent's
Shield offers no Upgrade (Q37). The landscape private-zone portrait
rule in index.css is scoped to .table-shell (ruling 5).
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Upgrade sends `upgradeCard {instanceId}` with the effect id, with `active-shield` and with the Sentence id: `Upgrade on $label sends one upgradeCard` (3 cases).
- Inspecting an opponent's base Shield offers no Upgrade even when the POV has a base shield and 1 upgrade point: `offers no Upgrade on an opponent base Shield while the own base Shield offers it (Q37 regression)`.
- Deactivate sends nothing until confirmed, then `deactivatePersistent {effectId}`: `sends deactivatePersistent only from the confirm, once for a double tap, and closes both sheets (Lot 69)`.
- Deactivate is disabled while a sub-choice is open: `disables Deactivate with its reason while a sub-choice is open (Q40)`.
- Esc on the confirm keeps the card sheet open: `closes only the confirm on Esc or Cancel and keeps the card sheet open, sending nothing (Lot 69)`.
- Captions are visible: `shows the four own resources under data-zone resources, with visible captions (L43-01)`.
- Brief items:
  - kit portrait → kit sheet: `opens the own kit from the portrait button`, `opens the kit sheet from the strip inside the private zone, sending nothing`;
  - flow, connection and Duplication: the three badge tests;
  - thumbs and ruling 1: `lists own thumbs Shield, actives, then Sentences, …`;
  - ruling 9: `shows Upgrade disabled (Actions locked) on the leftover Shield of an eliminated Watching POV (ruling 9)`;
  - off-turn, and no Deactivate on Shield and Sentence: `disables in-play actions off-turn and never offers Deactivate on Shield or Sentence`.
- Watch point (`index.css`): Step 3, plus the `grep` check and the CSS-reading suites in Step 4.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Kit portrait `size="tile"`** (72 px). It keeps the Appendix A rule that the own portrait is one step larger than the seats' (decisions 2026-09-28). **Designer question:** with visible captions the strip runs about three rows on a 375 px phone. The default is `tile`; `thumb` (40 px, 44 px tap target) is the fallback if the L74-26 gate finds it too tall.
- **"Shield ↑"** is V2's upgraded-shield pill text, verbatim, as a `Badge` with the `shield` tone.
- **Flow badges** use V2's `FlowStatusBadges` strings. Invisibility's turns ride in the `Badge` `value` instead of a new composite string.
- **Connection.** The strip renders the model's `connectionLabel` in a `Badge` with the `disconnected` icon. `ConnectionBadge` would need the player record, and the model already says when to show it: null when connected, eliminated, or a walk-in.
- **Sentence turns** use the V2 label "Turns left" (screen-reader only) with the number visible and the `sentence-ticking` icon. The tone ring is `threat-attack`, the V2 red.
- **The confirm is the `Dialog` primitive** (a read-only Sheet, `placement="auto"`).
  - Cancel is `quiet` and takes the initial focus; Deactivate is `danger` ("This card will be lost").
  - `data-arena-sheet="deactivate:<effectId>"` marks it, even though it is not an `ArenaSheet`.
- **SelfStrip returns null for a walk-in**, and ArenaScreen keeps L74-01's empty state. The nickname, `povLabel` and Leave are not rendered: §6.1 item 3 lists none of them, and L74-23 owns Watching.
- **No Activate duplication in the strip.** `DockModel.duplication` owns that button (L74-13, §6.1 item 5); the strip keeps only the 'Duplication active' badge. `ArenaScreen` computes `strip` once, and L74-13 reads `strip.dock` and `strip.isActive` from it.
- **New strings:** none. All are V2 verbatim: `Inspect your kit`, `Active cards`, `Turns left`, `Shield ↑`, `Duplication active`, `Block · N left`, `Block · attacks banned`, `Invisible`, `Reanimating`, `Deactivate`, `Cancel`, and `Are you sure?` / `This card will be lost. This does not cost a turn.` (`table-copy.ts`).

```ts
// targeting-model.ts
export const CHOOSE_TARGET_LABEL = 'Choose a target';                       // §10.6 new string
export function targetSeatLabel(nickname: string): string;                  // `${nickname}, target` (§6.6.5)
/**
 * `sheets.targeting` with the CURRENT model's seats, or null when nothing is targeted, the targeted
 * card sheet is gone, its card left the hand (`liveCardRefFor` gives `read-only`), Use is no longer
 * enabled, or Use no longer routes `target`.
 */
export function liveTargeting(view: PlayingStateView, ctx: ActionContext, sheets: ArenaSheetsState): TargetingState | null;
export interface SeatTargeting {
  /** The targeted sheet's key: a new key moves focus to the first valid seat. */
  readonly key: string;
  readonly seatIds: readonly string[];
  readonly onTarget: (playerId: string) => void;
}
export type SeatTarget = { readonly kind: 'valid'; readonly onTarget: () => void } | { readonly kind: 'invalid' };
export function seatTargetFor(targeting: SeatTargeting | undefined, playerId: string): SeatTarget | null;

// arena-sheets.ts (additions; TargetingState is Appendix A.5 verbatim)
export interface TargetingState { readonly sheetKey: string; readonly instanceId: string; readonly seatIds: readonly string[] }
// ArenaSheetsState gains `readonly targeting: TargetingState | null`;
// EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS becomes { open: [], closing: [], targeting: null }.
export function startTargeting(state: ArenaSheetsState, targeting: TargetingState): ArenaSheetsState;
export function endTargeting(state: ArenaSheetsState): ArenaSheetsState;

// targeting.tsx
export interface TargetingBarProps { onCancel: () => void }
export function TargetingBar({ onCancel }: TargetingBarProps): ReactElement;

// pre-send-model.ts
export const REGENERATION_GROUP_LABEL = 'Regeneration quantity';            // V2 dialog title
export const CONSUME_GROUP_LABEL = 'Choose a card to transform';            // V2 dialog title
export const DEFAULT_REGENERATION_QUANTITY: RegenerationQuantity = 1;
export function regenerationTileLabel(tile: RegenerationTile): string;      // '3 lives, minus 9 pts'
export function resolveConsumeId(options: readonly CardInstance[], picked: string | null): string | null;

// pre-send-choice.tsx
export interface RegenerationChoiceProps {
  tiles: readonly RegenerationTile[];
  selected: RegenerationQuantity;
  onSelect: (quantity: RegenerationQuantity) => void;
  /** Attached to the checked tile; the card sheet passes it as `initialFocusRef`. */
  checkedRef: RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>;
}
export function RegenerationChoice(props: RegenerationChoiceProps): ReactElement;
export interface ConsumeChoiceProps {
  /** Non-empty: the sheet renders no group when there is nothing to consume. */
  options: readonly CardInstance[];
  selectedId: string;
  /** Use is disabled: the faces show, without a press action. */
  disabled: boolean;
  onSelect: (instanceId: string) => void;
}
export function ConsumeChoice(props: ConsumeChoiceProps): ReactElement;

// use-tap-guard.ts
export const TAP_GUARD_MS = DUR_BASE_MS;
export interface TapGuard { readonly guarded: boolean; readonly raise: () => void }
export function useTapGuard(): TapGuard;

// card-sheet.tsx: new props (CardSheetProps). `onBeginTargeting?` is L74-08's (Appendix A.7).
collapsed?: boolean;
collapsedBar?: ReactNode;
// arena-seats.tsx: ArenaSeatsProps gains `targeting?: SeatTargeting` (L74-04 declares no placeholder).
// opponent-seat.tsx: OpponentSeatProps gains `target?: SeatTarget`.
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Tap-tap targeting and pre-send choices: technical spec v7 §6.6.2, §6.6.4, §6.6.5; Appendix A
 * `card-target-picker`, `table-target-picker-eligibility`, `table-use-target-routing`,
 * `regeneration-quantity-picker`, `card-transformer-consume-picker` (L74-10).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView, PublicPlayerView, SubChoiceRequiredPayload } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { act, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { UserEvent } from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi, type RenderUiOptions } from '../../test/render';
import { DUR_BASE_MS } from '../../ui/motion/motion-tokens';
import { DISABLED_REASON_COPY } from '../../view-model/action-reasons';
import { makeCard, makePending, makePersistent, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const MIRROR: SubChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 1 };

function withSeat(view: PlayingStateView, playerId: string, patch: Partial<PublicPlayerView>): PlayingStateView {
  return { ...view, players: view.players.map((player) => (player.id === playerId ? { ...player, ...patch } : player)) };
}
const SPY_AND_ATTACK = [makeCard('spy', { instanceId: 'spy-1' }), makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' })];
/** Me, p1, p2; 10 points; Spy and Basic attack in hand. */
const playView = (): PlayingStateView => makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: SPY_AND_ATTACK }) } });
/** p1 living Invisible, p2 living, p3 a corpse with nothing pending, p4 a corpse whose attack is pending on me. */
function eligibilityView(): PlayingStateView {
  const base = makeArenaView({
    seatCount: 5,
    tiers: { p3: 'eliminated', p4: 'eliminated' },
    overrides: {
      self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: SPY_AND_ATTACK }),
      pendingEffects: [makePending({ id: 'atk-p4', sourcePlayerId: 'p4', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'basic-attack' })],
    },
  });
  return withSeat(base, 'p1', { activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'inv-p1', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 3 })] });
}
function setup(view: PlayingStateView, overrides: Partial<ArenaScreenProps> = {}, options: RenderUiOptions = {}) {
  const onPlayCard = vi.fn<ArenaScreenProps['onPlayCard']>();
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view, onPlayCard, ...overrides });
  return { onPlayCard, props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />, options) };
}
function region(name: string): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
  if (element === null) throw new Error(`no ${name} region`);
  return element;
}
const handCard = (name: string): HTMLElement => within(region('hand')).getByRole('button', { name });
const sheet = (name: string | RegExp): HTMLElement => screen.getByRole('dialog', { name });
/** A valid seat while targeting (§6.6.5). */
const target = (nickname: string): HTMLElement => within(region('arena')).getByRole('button', { name: `${nickname}, target` });
/** A seat's control outside targeting: its accessible name is the nickname (L74-04 Review Focus 5). */
const seatButton = (nickname: string): HTMLElement => within(region('arena')).getByRole('button', { name: nickname });
/** The seat's `<button>` (L74-04), which L74-10 marks while targeting, valid or not. */
function marked(playerId: string): HTMLElement {
  const seat = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="${playerId}"]`);
  if (seat === null) throw new Error(`no seat ${playerId}`);
  const control = seat.querySelector<HTMLElement>('button[data-target-seat]');
  if (control === null) throw new Error(`seat ${playerId} is not marked for targeting`);
  return control;
}
const barShown = (): boolean => screen.queryByText('Choose a target') !== null;
const anyMarked = (): boolean => document.querySelector('[data-target-seat]') !== null;
async function useCard(user: UserEvent, cardName: string, sheetName: string | RegExp): Promise<void> {
  await user.click(handCard(cardName));
  await user.click(within(sheet(sheetName)).getByRole('button', { name: /^Use/ }));
}

describe('tap-tap targeting (§6.6.2, §6.6.5)', () => {
  it('Use on Spy collapses the sheet into the bar, offers the seats and sends nothing', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(playView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    expect(screen.getByText('Choose a target').closest('[data-zone="sheet-bar"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).toBeNull();
    expect([target('p1').getAttribute('data-target-seat'), target('p2').getAttribute('data-target-seat')]).toEqual(['valid', 'valid']);
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(target('p1'));
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a valid seat tap sends one playCard with targetPlayerId and ends targeting', async () => {
    const { user, onPlayCard } = setup(playView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    await user.click(target('p2'));
    expect(onPlayCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['spy-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p2' }]]);
    expect([barShown(), anyMarked()]).toEqual([false, false]);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('a double tap on a target sends one intent and opens no seat sheet (Review Focus 3)', async () => {
    const { user, onPlayCard } = setup(playView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    await user.dblClick(target('p2'));
    expect(onPlayCard).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'p2' })).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each(['Cancel', 'Escape'] as const)('%s ends targeting, sends nothing and restores the hand', async (how) => {
    const { user, props } = setup(playView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    expect(barShown()).toBe(true);
    if (how === 'Cancel') {
      await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    } else {
      await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    }
    expect([barShown(), anyMarked()]).toEqual([false, false]);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).toBeNull();
    expect(handCard('Spy').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false');
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('opening another card ends targeting', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(playView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    expect(barShown()).toBe(true);
    await user.click(handCard('Basic attack, 1 damage'));
    expect(sheet('Basic attack')).not.toBeNull();
    expect([barShown(), anyMarked()]).toEqual([false, false]);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('tapping the targeted hand card again ends targeting and shows its sheet expanded, not as a bar', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(playView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    expect(barShown()).toBe(true);
    await user.click(handCard('Spy'));
    expect(sheet('Spy')).not.toBeNull();
    expect([barShown(), anyMarked()]).toEqual([false, false]);
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-zone="sheet-bar"]')).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('ignores seat taps for DUR_BASE_MS after targeting ends (frontend.md "L74 carry-overs")', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const { user, props } = setup(playView(), {}, { advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime });
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    await user.click(seatButton('p1'));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'p1' })).toBeNull();
    act(() => { vi.advanceTimersByTime(DUR_BASE_MS); });
    await user.click(seatButton('p1'));
    expect(sheet('p1')).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});

describe('target eligibility comes from the model (Appendix A table-target-picker-eligibility)', () => {
  it('Spy: the living Invisible seat and both corpses are aria-disabled and inert', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(eligibilityView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(target('p2'));
    for (const playerId of ['p1', 'p3', 'p4']) {
      const seat = marked(playerId);
      expect([seat.getAttribute('data-target-seat'), seat.getAttribute('aria-disabled')]).toEqual(['invalid', 'true']);
      await user.click(seat);
    }
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    expect(barShown()).toBe(true);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('Basic attack: the corpse with an attack pending on you is valid, the other corpse is not (L68-07)', async () => {
    const { user, onPlayCard } = setup(eligibilityView());
    await useCard(user, 'Basic attack, 1 damage', 'Basic attack');
    expect([marked('p2').getAttribute('data-target-seat'), marked('p4').getAttribute('data-target-seat')]).toEqual(['valid', 'valid']);
    expect([marked('p1').getAttribute('aria-disabled'), marked('p3').getAttribute('aria-disabled')]).toEqual(['true', 'true']);
    await user.click(target('p4'));
    expect(onPlayCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['atk-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p4' }]]);
  });

  it('upgraded Card Thief plays at once with no target and no bar (L66-01)', async () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({
      overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 10, specialCards: [makeCard('card-thief', { instanceId: 'thief-1', isUpgraded: true })] }) },
    });
    const { user, onPlayCard } = setup(view);
    await useCard(user, 'Card Thief upgraded', /^Card Thief/);
    expect(onPlayCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['thief-1']]);
    expect([barShown(), anyMarked()]).toEqual([false, false]);
  });
});

describe('targeting ends when its model goes stale (Review Focus 2)', () => {
  it('a sub-choice opening ends targeting for good; a later seat tap sends nothing', async () => {
    const { user, props, rerender } = setup(playView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} subChoice={MIRROR} />);
    expect([barShown(), anyMarked()]).toEqual([false, false]);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(seatButton('p2'));
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    expect(barShown()).toBe(false);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('the turn passing ends targeting for good', async () => {
    const { user, props, rerender } = setup(playView());
    await useCard(user, 'Spy', 'Spy');
    expect(barShown()).toBe(true);
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} view={{ ...props.view, currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }} />);
    expect([barShown(), anyMarked()]).toEqual([false, false]);
    await user.click(seatButton('p2'));
    rerender(<ArenaScreen {...props} />);
    expect(barShown()).toBe(false);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });
});

describe('pre-send choices in the sheet (§6.6.2)', () => {
  const regenView = (points: number): PlayingStateView =>
    makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points, hand: [makeCard('regeneration', { instanceId: 'regen-1' })] }) } });

  it('Regeneration preselects 1 life; picking 3 lives only selects until Use sends quantity 3', async () => {
    const { user, onPlayCard, props } = setup(regenView(12));
    await user.click(handCard('Regeneration'));
    const group = within(sheet('Regeneration')).getByRole('group', { name: 'Regeneration quantity' });
    const one = within(group).getByRole('button', { name: '1 life, minus 3 pts' });
    expect(one.getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(one);
    const three = within(group).getByRole('button', { name: '3 lives, minus 9 pts' });
    await user.click(three);
    expect([one.getAttribute('aria-pressed'), three.getAttribute('aria-pressed')]).toEqual(['false', 'true']);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(within(sheet('Regeneration')).getByRole('button', { name: /^Use/ }));
    expect(onPlayCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['regen-1', { quantity: 3 }]]);
  });

  it('Regeneration disables the quantities the POV cannot pay for', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(regenView(7));
    await user.click(handCard('Regeneration'));
    const group = within(sheet('Regeneration')).getByRole('group', { name: 'Regeneration quantity' });
    const names = ['1 life, minus 3 pts', '2 lives, minus 6 pts', '3 lives, minus 9 pts', '4 lives, minus 12 pts'];
    expect(names.map((name) => within(group).getByRole('button', { name }).hasAttribute('disabled'))).toEqual([false, false, true, true]);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('Card Transformer preselects the first shared card and Use sends the chosen consumeInstanceId', async () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({
      overrides: {
        self: makeSelf({
          points: 5,
          hand: [makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' }), makeCard('tax', { instanceId: 'tax-1' })],
          specialCards: [makeCard('card-transformer', { instanceId: 'tf-1' })],
        }),
      },
    });
    const { user, onPlayCard, props } = setup(view);
    await user.click(handCard('Card Transformer'));
    const group = within(sheet('Card Transformer')).getByRole('group', { name: 'Choose a card to transform' });
    expect(within(group).getByRole('button', { name: 'Basic attack, 1 damage' }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    await user.click(within(group).getByRole('button', { name: 'Tax' }));
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(within(sheet('Card Transformer')).getByRole('button', { name: /^Use/ }));
    expect(onPlayCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['tf-1', { consumeInstanceId: 'tax-1' }]]);
  });

  it('Card Transformer with nothing to consume shows its reason once and no tiles', async () => {
    const view = makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 5, specialCards: [makeCard('card-transformer', { instanceId: 'tf-1' })] }) } });
    const { user } = setup(view);
    await user.click(handCard('Card Transformer'));
    const dialog = sheet('Card Transformer');
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('group', { name: 'Choose a card to transform' })).toBeNull();
    expect(within(dialog).getAllByText(DISABLED_REASON_COPY['nothing-to-consume'])).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});
```

```ts
/**
 * Targeting model: technical spec v7 §6.6.2, §6.6.5 (L74-10).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import type { ActionContext } from '../../view-model/action-lock';
import { makeCard, makePersistent, makePlayer, makePlayingView, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, type ArenaSheetsState, type TargetingState } from './arena-sheets';
import { liveTargeting, seatTargetFor, targetSeatLabel } from './targeting-model';

const LIVE: ActionContext = { subChoice: null, readOnly: false };
const SPY = makeCard('spy', { instanceId: 'spy-1' });
const TARGETING: TargetingState = { sheetKey: 'card:spy-1', instanceId: 'spy-1', seatIds: ['p1', 'p2'] };
/** The Spy card sheet, collapsed into targeting. */
const TARGETED: ArenaSheetsState = { open: [{ kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'hand', instance: SPY } }], closing: [], targeting: TARGETING };
const INVISIBLE = makePersistent({ id: 'inv', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 2 });
const view = (overrides: Partial<PlayingStateView> = {}): PlayingStateView =>
  makePlayingView({ self: makeSelf({ points: 10, hand: [SPY] }), ...overrides });

describe('liveTargeting (§6.6.2)', () => {
  it('re-reads the seat set from the current model', () => {
    const p1Invisible = view({ players: [makePlayer('me', { isYou: true }), makePlayer('p1', { activePersistentEffects: [INVISIBLE] }), makePlayer('p2')] });
    expect(liveTargeting(view(), LIVE, TARGETED)).toEqual(TARGETING);
    expect(liveTargeting(p1Invisible, LIVE, TARGETED)).toEqual({ ...TARGETING, seatIds: ['p2'] });
  });

  it('ends when the card left the hand', () => {
    expect(liveTargeting(view({ self: makeSelf({ points: 10 }) }), LIVE, TARGETED)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('ends off-turn, while a sub-choice is open, and once no seat is left', () => {
    const allInvisible = view({
      players: [makePlayer('me', { isYou: true }), makePlayer('p1', { activePersistentEffects: [INVISIBLE] }), makePlayer('p2', { activePersistentEffects: [INVISIBLE] })],
    });
    expect(liveTargeting(view({ currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }), LIVE, TARGETED)).toBeNull();
    expect(liveTargeting(view(), { subChoice: { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 1 }, readOnly: false }, TARGETED)).toBeNull();
    expect(liveTargeting(allInvisible, LIVE, TARGETED)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('is null without targeting', () => {
    expect(liveTargeting(view(), LIVE, EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('seat targets (§6.6.5)', () => {
  it('names a valid seat "{nickname}, target" and marks every other seat invalid', () => {
    const onTarget = vi.fn<(playerId: string) => void>();
    const targeting = { key: 'card:spy-1', seatIds: ['p2'], onTarget };
    expect(targetSeatLabel('Ana')).toBe('Ana, target');
    expect(seatTargetFor(undefined, 'p2')).toBeNull();
    expect(seatTargetFor(targeting, 'p1')).toEqual({ kind: 'invalid' });
    const valid = seatTargetFor(targeting, 'p2');
    if (valid?.kind !== 'valid') throw new Error('p2 should be a valid target');
    valid.onTarget();
    expect(onTarget.mock.calls).toEqual([['p2']]);
  });
});
```

```ts
/**
 * Pre-send choice model: technical spec v7 §6.6.2; Appendix A `regeneration-quantity-picker`,
 * `card-transformer-consume-picker` (L74-10).
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { makeCard } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { regenerationTileLabel, resolveConsumeId } from './pre-send-model';

describe('pre-send model', () => {
  it('names a Regeneration tile with its spoken price', () => {
    expect(regenerationTileLabel({ quantity: 1, label: '1 life', totalCost: 3, enabled: true, reason: null })).toBe('1 life, minus 3 pts');
    expect(regenerationTileLabel({ quantity: 4, label: '4 lives', totalCost: 8, enabled: false, reason: 'not-enough-points' })).toBe('4 lives, minus 8 pts');
  });

  it('keeps the picked consume card while it is offered, else falls back to the first (V2)', () => {
    const attack = makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' });
    const tax = makeCard('tax', { instanceId: 'tax-1' });
    expect(resolveConsumeId([attack, tax], null)).toBe('atk-1');
    expect(resolveConsumeId([attack, tax], 'tax-1')).toBe('tax-1');
    expect(resolveConsumeId([attack], 'tax-1')).toBe('atk-1');
    expect(resolveConsumeId([], 'tax-1')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

That is 2 tests.

In `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-sheets.test.ts`, change L74-02's `expect(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS).toEqual({ open: [], closing: [] });` to `expect(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS).toEqual({ open: [], closing: [], targeting: null });`. Then append this `describe` and add `startTargeting`, `endTargeting` and `type TargetingState` to its `./arena-sheets` import (`makeCard` is already imported there):

```ts
describe('targeting (L74-10, §6.6.2)', () => {
  const card = (instanceId: string): ArenaSheet => ({ kind: 'card', source: { kind: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy', { instanceId }) } });
  const kit: ArenaSheet = { kind: 'kit', kitId: 'tactician' };
  const targetingOf = (sheet: ArenaSheet): TargetingState => ({ sheetKey: arenaSheetKey(sheet), instanceId: 'spy-1', seatIds: ['p1'] });

  it('keeps only the targeted card sheet open, closes the others through their exit, and records the target set', () => {
    const spy = card('spy-1');
    const state = openArenaSheet(openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, kit), spy);
    expect(startTargeting(state, targetingOf(spy))).toEqual({ open: [spy], closing: [kit], targeting: targetingOf(spy) });
  });

  it('ignores a target request for a sheet that is not open', () => {
    const state = openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, kit);
    expect(startTargeting(state, targetingOf(card('spy-1')))).toBe(state);
  });

  it('ends targeting by closing the targeted sheet', () => {
    const spy = card('spy-1');
    const state = startTargeting(openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, spy), targetingOf(spy));
    expect(endTargeting(state)).toEqual({ open: [], closing: [spy], targeting: null });
  });

  it('returns the same state when nothing is targeted', () => {
    const state = openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, kit);
    expect(endTargeting(state)).toBe(state);
  });

  it('opening any sheet ends targeting first: targeting is exclusive', () => {
    const spy = card('spy-1');
    const state = startTargeting(openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, spy), targetingOf(spy));
    expect(openArenaSheet(state, kit)).toEqual({ open: [kit], closing: [spy], targeting: null });
  });

  it('closing the targeted sheet clears targeting', () => {
    const spy = card('spy-1');
    const state = startTargeting(openArenaSheet(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS, spy), targetingOf(spy));
    expect(closeArenaSheet(state, spy)).toEqual({ open: [], closing: [spy], targeting: null });
  });
});
```
