- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**
  - **`arena-sheets.ts`.**
    - `TargetingState` is Appendix A.5 verbatim. `ArenaSheetsState` gains `readonly targeting: TargetingState | null`, and `EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS` becomes `{ open: [], closing: [], targeting: null }`. L74-02's reducers spread `...state`, so `targeting` passes through them.
    - Every sheet these functions close goes through L74-02's close path: it moves to `closing` (capped at `MAX_CLOSING_SHEETS`) and stays mounted with `open={false}` through its exit.
    - `startTargeting(state, t)`:
      - If no entry of `state.open` has `arenaSheetKey(sheet) === t.sheetKey`, return `state` itself.
      - If `state.targeting?.sheetKey === t.sheetKey` and that sheet is the only one open, return `state` (a double Use is idempotent).
      - Otherwise close every other open sheet, oldest first, as `closeArenaSheet` does, and return `{ ...closed, open: [thatSheet], targeting: t }`. Every other read-only sheet closes, so targeting is exclusive.
    - `endTargeting(state)`:
      - With no targeting, return `state`.
      - Otherwise return `closeArenaSheet(state, targetedSheet)` (`targetedSheet` = the open entry keyed `state.targeting.sheetKey`), which also clears `targeting` (below); return `{ ...state, targeting: null }` if that entry is missing.
    - `openArenaSheet(state, sheet)` starts from `endTargeting(state)` when `state.targeting !== null`.
    - `closeArenaSheet` sets `targeting: null` when the closed sheet's key is `state.targeting?.sheetKey`.
    - Doc-comment the exclusivity rule and cite §6.6.2.
  - **`targeting-model.ts`.**
    - `liveTargeting(view, ctx, sheets)`:
      - It returns null when `sheets.targeting` is null, or when no `card` entry of `sheets.open` has the key `sheets.targeting.sheetKey`.
      - Otherwise `const ref = liveCardRefFor(sheet.source, view)` (L74-08); it returns null when `ref.zone === 'read-only'` (the card left the hand).
      - Otherwise it computes `const model = cardActions(view, ref, ctx)`.
      - It returns null unless the `use` action is present and `enabled` and `model.use?.kind === 'target'`.
      - Otherwise it returns `{ ...targeting, seatIds: model.use.seatIds }`.
      - The bar and the seats always read this live set, never the stored one.
    - `seatTargetFor` returns:
      - null when there is no targeting;
      - `{ kind: 'valid', onTarget: () => { targeting.onTarget(playerId); } }` for a listed seat;
      - `{ kind: 'invalid' }` otherwise.
  - **`use-tap-guard.ts`.** Copy the shape of the Sheet transition shield (`ui/primitives/sheet.tsx`, lines 254-271):
    - state `guarded` and a `count`;
    - `raise()` sets `guarded = true` and bumps `count`;
    - an effect keyed on `[guarded, count]` starts `window.setTimeout(() => { setGuarded(false); }, TAP_GUARD_MS)` and clears it on cleanup.
    - It stays active under reduced motion: it guards double taps, not animation.
    - Doc comment: closing a collapsed sheet unmounts its bar at once with no shield (frontend.md "L74 carry-overs").
  - **`targeting.tsx`, `TargetingBar`.** Render `<div data-zone="targeting-bar" className="flex w-full items-center gap-2">` holding:
    - a `<p>` with classes `${CHROME_SHAPE_SM_CLASS} min-w-0 flex-1 truncate bg-v7-panel px-3 py-2 font-v7-display text-v7-body text-v7-text` and the text `CHOOSE_TARGET_LABEL`;
    - `<Button variant="quiet" icon="close" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</Button>`.
    - The bar slot is 44 px tall, and Button `md` is 44 px.
  - **`pre-send-model.ts`.**
    - `regenerationTileLabel(tile)` returns `` `${tile.label}, ${costAriaLabel({ kind: 'points', amount: tile.totalCost }, 'cost')}` ``.
    - `resolveConsumeId(options, picked)` returns `picked` when it is among the options, else `options[0]?.instanceId ?? null`.
  - **`pre-send-choice.tsx`.**
    - `RegenerationChoice` renders `<div role="group" aria-label={REGENERATION_GROUP_LABEL} className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">`. It holds one `<button type="button">` per tile, with:
      - `aria-pressed={tile.quantity === selected}` and `aria-label={regenerationTileLabel(tile)}`;
      - `disabled={!tile.enabled}` (a tile carries Use's reason first, then its own price);
      - `ref={tile.quantity === selected ? checkedRef : undefined}`;
      - content: the visible `tile.label` and `<CostAmount amount={{ kind: 'points', amount: tile.totalCost }} signed="cost" title={null} />`;
      - classes: `${CHROME_SHAPE_SM_CLASS} ${CHROME_FOCUS_CLASS} ${CHROME_DISABLED_CLASS} min-h-11 bg-v7-panel-2 px-3 text-v7-text`, `ring-4 ring-v7-cta` when selected (never a `--cta` fill, which would put the point icon on its own colour), and `chromePressClass(reduced)` with `data-motion`.
    - `ConsumeChoice` renders `<div role="group" aria-label={CONSUME_GROUP_LABEL} className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">`. It holds one `<Card size="tile" instance={option} selected={option.instanceId === selectedId} />` per option, with `onActivate={onSelect}` spread only when `!disabled`. The Card button gives `aria-pressed` and the face label as its name.
  - **`card-sheet.tsx`.**
    - L74-08's Use handler already routes `send` (through `send`) and `target` (`onBeginTargeting?.(…)`); this task fills its empty `regeneration` / `consume` branches.
    - New state: `quantity` (initially `DEFAULT_REGENERATION_QUANTITY`), `consumePick: string | null` (initially `null`), and `checkedRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)`.
    - Reset the per-opening state when `open` turns true. A closed sheet stays mounted through its exit (`closing`) and is reused when the same card opens again (same key), so "pick 3 lives, Esc, reopen" must show '1 life' again. Use the adjust-state-during-render pattern (as `ui/primitives/sheet.tsx` does; never an effect with `setState`); L74-08's effect already re-arms `committedFor`:

```tsx
const [wasOpen, setWasOpen] = useState(open);
if (open !== wasOpen) {
  setWasOpen(open);
  if (open) {
    setQuantity(DEFAULT_REGENERATION_QUANTITY);
    setConsumePick(null);
  }
}
```

    - From `model.use`:
      - `regeneration`: render `RegenerationChoice` in the sheet body, after the effect copy. The Use button's `disabled` and reason come from the selected tile, `use.tiles.find((tile) => tile.quantity === quantity)`, instead of the `use` action. Pass `initialFocusRef={checkedRef}` to the Sheet.
      - `consume` with at least one option: render `ConsumeChoice` (`selectedId = resolveConsumeId(use.options, consumePick)`, `disabled = !useAction.enabled`) in the body, after the effect copy.
      - `consume` with no option: no group. Use's own reason, `nothing-to-consume`, is the same sentence, so it shows once.
    - Use's click handler (the `send` and `target` branches are L74-08's and stay; `send` is L74-08's private helper: one commit per view, then `onClose()`):
      ```ts
      switch (use.kind) {
        case 'send':          /* L74-08: send({ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId } }) */ return;
        case 'target':        /* L74-08: onBeginTargeting?.({ instanceId: model.instanceId, seatIds: use.seatIds }) */ return;
        case 'regeneration':  send({ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: model.instanceId, quantity } }); return;
        case 'consume': {
          const consumeInstanceId = resolveConsumeId(use.options, consumePick);
          if (consumeInstanceId === null) return;
          send({ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: model.instanceId, consumeInstanceId } }); return;
        }
      }
      ```
    - Pass `collapsed` and `collapsedBar` to `<Sheet>` only when defined (`{...(collapsed !== undefined ? { collapsed } : {})}`, the same for the bar).
  - **`opponent-seat.tsx`.** The seat's main control is L74-04's stretched `<button>` (named by the nickname; from L74-05 its click opens the seat sheet). With `target` set:
    - `data-target-seat={target.kind}` on that button.
    - `valid`: `aria-label={targetSeatLabel(nickname)}`; the click calls `target.onTarget()`; classes `ring-4 ring-v7-mine` plus `animate-pulse` only when `!reduced`.
    - `invalid`: `aria-disabled="true"`; the click does nothing; `opacity-50`. Keep its usual accessible name.
    - Without `target`, the control behaves exactly as before.
  - **`arena-seats.tsx`.**
    - Pass `target={seatTargetFor(targeting, seat.playerId) ?? undefined}` to each `OpponentSeat`, conditionally spread (`exactOptionalPropertyTypes`).
    - Hold a ref on the `data-zone="opponents"` container. Add `useEffect(() => { if (focusKey === null) return; rootRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-target-seat="valid"]')?.focus(); }, [focusKey])` with `focusKey = targeting?.key ?? null`, so focus lands on the first valid seat in DOM order (§6.6.5).
  - **`arena-screen.tsx`.** Use L74-05's sheet host state, `const [sheets, setSheets] = useState<ArenaSheetsState>(…)`.
    ```ts
    const tapGuard = useTapGuard();
    const live = liveTargeting(props.view, ctx, sheets);
    if (sheets.targeting !== null && live === null) {
      // Card left the hand, turn passed or a sub-choice opened (Review Focus 2). React's
      // "adjust state while rendering" pattern, as ui/primitives/sheet.tsx does; never an effect.
      setSheets(endTargeting(sheets));
    }
    const finishTargeting = (): void => { setSheets((current) => endTargeting(current)); tapGuard.raise(); };
    const onTarget = (playerId: string): void => {
      if (live === null || !live.seatIds.includes(playerId)) return;
      commit({ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: live.instanceId, targetPlayerId: playerId } });
      finishTargeting();
    };
    ```
    - Esc: `const store = useSheetStackStore();`. Then `const onTargetingKey = useEffectEvent((event: KeyboardEvent) => { … })`:
      - ignore `event.key !== 'Escape'`, `event.defaultPrevented` and `event.isComposing`;
      - return when `liveSheetId(store.getSnapshot()) !== null` (an expanded sheet above takes it);
      - otherwise call `event.preventDefault()` and `finishTargeting()`.
      - `useEffect` keyed on `live !== null` adds and removes it as a `document` `keydown` listener, like Sheet's own listener.
    - `ArenaSeats`:
      - add `{...(live === null ? {} : { targeting: { key: live.sheetKey, seatIds: live.seatIds, onTarget } })}`;
      - its `onOpenSeat` returns early while `tapGuard.guarded`.
    - The `card` case of L74-05's host switch (each entry already carries `key = arenaSheetKey(sheet)` and `open`) gains:
      - `onBeginTargeting={(request) => { setSheets((current) => startTargeting(current, { sheetKey: key, ...request })); }}`;
      - `collapsed={live?.sheetKey === key}`, always a boolean, and `{...(live?.sheetKey === key ? { collapsedBar: <TargetingBar onCancel={finishTargeting} /> } : {})}`. The boolean matters: tapping the targeted hand card again ends targeting and reopens the same sheet in one reducer step, so React never sees `open={false}`. Sheet syncs minimization only `if (open && collapsed !== undefined)` (`ui/primitives/sheet.tsx`), so an `undefined` would leave the sheet minimized as a generic restore bar; `false` expands it.
    - `TargetingState`, `startTargeting` and `endTargeting` come from `./arena-sheets`, and `liveTargeting` from `./targeting-model`. `commit` is L74-08's `createArenaCommit(props)`.

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 targeting and pre-send choices (L74-10)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.6.2, §6.6.4, §6.6.5; Appendix A `card-target-picker`,
`regeneration-quantity-picker`, `card-transformer-consume-picker`; frontend.md "L74 carry-overs".

- **Regeneration is select then Use.** §6.6.2 ("chosen in the sheet before Use sends") replaces
  L44-06's click-to-commit quantity picker: a tile only selects (`aria-pressed`), '1 life' is
  preselected, and Use sends `playCard { instanceId, quantity }`. The V2 table keeps
  click-to-commit until L75 deletes it.
- **Card Transformer consume tiles live in the sheet.** The first shared card is preselected, as
  in V2, and Use sends `consumeInstanceId`. With nothing to consume no group is drawn: Use's
  disabled reason is the same sentence, so it shows once.
- **Targeting is exclusive.** Entering targeting closes every other read-only sheet and opening
  any sheet ends it, so no expanded sheet sits under the bar (the B2 carry-over) and Esc always
  reaches the arena's handler, which stands down while an expanded sheet is live.
- **A stale target set ends targeting.** The seats are re-read from `cardActions` every render;
  when the card leaves the hand, the turn passes or a sub-choice opens, targeting ends and the
  card sheet closes, so a later seat tap cannot send.
- **Exit guard.** Closing a collapsed sheet unmounts its bar with no shield, so for
  `DUR_BASE_MS` after the player ends targeting the arena ignores seat taps and dock commits.

---
```

target" bar; valid seats commit playCard at once, others are
aria-disabled. Regeneration becomes select then Use, replacing

```

**Acceptance map:**
- Spy opens the bar, and tapping a target sends one `playCard` with `targetPlayerId`:
  - `Use on Spy collapses the sheet into the bar, offers the seats and sends nothing`;
  - `a valid seat tap sends one playCard with targetPlayerId and ends targeting`.
- Cancel and Esc send nothing and restore the hand: `%s ends targeting, sends nothing and restores the hand` (Cancel, Escape).
- A living Invisible seat and a non-qualifying corpse are `aria-disabled`, and tapping them sends nothing:
  - `Spy: the living Invisible seat and both corpses are aria-disabled and inert`;
  - `Basic attack: the corpse with an attack pending on you is valid, the other corpse is not (L68-07)`.
- A corpse with a pending attack on you is a valid attack target but not a Spy target: the same two tests (p4).
- Upgraded Card Thief sends with no target: `upgraded Card Thief plays at once with no target and no bar (L66-01)`.
- Regeneration quantity 3 sends `quantity: 3`: `Regeneration preselects 1 life; picking 3 lives only selects until Use sends quantity 3`.
- Transformer sends `consumeInstanceId`: `Card Transformer preselects the first shared card and Use sends the chosen consumeInstanceId`.
- Review Focus 2: `a sub-choice opening ends targeting for good; a later seat tap sends nothing` and `the turn passing ends targeting for good`.
- Review Focus 3: `a double tap on a target sends one intent and opens no seat sheet (Review Focus 3)`.
- Focus moves to the first valid seat (§6.6.5): the `document.activeElement` assertions in the first Spy tests.
- The decisions.md entry for the L44-06 replacement: Step 5. `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Targeting is exclusive.** Entering targeting closes every other read-only sheet, and opening any sheet (another hand card, a plate badge, a kit) ends it.
  - This settles the B2 carry-over: no expanded sheet ever sits under the bar.
  - So Esc always reaches the arena handler. The handler still stands down whenever `liveSheetId` is not null, so a sheet that a later task opens above the bar (the L74-13 Unspy sheet) takes Esc first.
- **The seats are read live.** `TargetingState.seatIds` is stored as A.5 says, but the bar and the seats use `liveTargeting`'s fresh set every render.
- **The host always passes a boolean `collapsed` to a card sheet** (`live?.sheetKey === key`), and `collapsedBar` only when it is true. Re-tapping the lifted hand card ends targeting and reopens the same sheet without an `open={false}` in between; `collapsed={false}` is what un-minimizes it. Test: `tapping the targeted hand card again ends targeting and shows its sheet expanded, not as a bar`.
- **A stale model ends targeting and closes the card sheet.** That happens when the card left the hand, the turn passed or a sub-choice opened. The sheet is not re-expanded. **Designer question:** re-expand the sheet read-only instead, to show why? Default: close it.
- **Exit guard: `TAP_GUARD_MS = DUR_BASE_MS` (220 ms) after the player ends targeting** (target tap, Cancel, Esc). Seat taps are ignored; from L74-13, dock commits are too.
  - Not raised for a stale end, because Draw is locked then.
  - Kept under reduced motion: it guards double taps, not an animation.
- **Valid seats:** a `--mine` ring (the POV's own outgoing effect, §5.1) plus `animate-pulse` at full motion only. Invalid seats: `opacity-50` plus `aria-disabled`, and they keep their normal name.
- **After Cancel, focus returns to the hand card.** The Sheet restores focus to its opener. After a target tap, focus stays on the seat.
- **The Esc handler** follows the Sheet's guards (`defaultPrevented`, `isComposing`) and runs only while no expanded sheet is live.
- **Regeneration preselects '1 life'.** It mirrors Card Transformer's "first preselected" and gives the radio-like group a checked tile. **Designer question:** no preselection (Use disabled until a pick)? Default: '1 life'.
- **The quantity and the consume pick reset on every opening,** not on mount: a closed card sheet stays mounted and is reused when the same card opens again, so the `wasOpen` block resets them when `open` turns true (L74-11 adds the multi-attack selection to it).
- **The Regeneration tile's name is `'{n} lives, minus {cost}'`** (`regenerationTileLabel`), with the visible label plus a `CostAmount`.
  - A tile the POV cannot pay for is natively `disabled`. Use takes the selected tile's availability, which carries Use's own reason first.
  - Use keeps L74-08's per-life cost label.
- **The group labels reuse the V2 dialog titles** 'Regeneration quantity' and 'Choose a card to transform'.
- **Card Transformer with nothing to consume:** no group, because the reason under Use is the same sentence (it would show twice).
- **The bar's Cancel is `variant="quiet"` with the `close` icon,** not `danger`: `--danger` is for leave and destroy (DESIGN.md).
- **The bar text does not name the card;** the lifted hand card shows which one.
- **New strings (§10.6):**
  - 'Choose a target' (listed in §10.6);
  - '{nickname}, target' (the §6.6.5 wording).
  - Reused: 'Cancel', 'Regeneration quantity', 'Choose a card to transform', '1 life' / 'N lives', `costAriaLabel` output.

// multi-attack-selection.ts
export const MULTI_ATTACK_ENTRY_LABEL = 'Multi-attack';                                  // V2
export const MULTI_ATTACK_TITLE = 'Assassin multi-attack';                               // V2 dialog title
export const MULTI_ATTACK_HINT = 'Select at least two attack cards and a target each.';  // V2

export interface MultiAttackPick { readonly instanceId: string; readonly targetPlayerId: string | null }
/** Toggled rows in toggle order; the payload lists them in hand (row) order. */

export function pickMultiAttackTarget(selection: MultiAttackSelection, instanceId: string, targetPlayerId: string): MultiAttackSelection;

/**
 * Multi-attack selection: technical spec v7 §6.6.2, §13 Q44; Appendix A
 * `table-multi-attack-target-eligibility` (L74-11).
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { MULTI_ATTACK_MIN_ATTACKS, type MultiAttackModel } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import { makeCard } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import {
  EMPTY_MULTI_ATTACK_SELECTION, multiAttackPayload, pickMultiAttackTarget, playAttacksLabel,
  selectedMultiAttackRows, toggleMultiAttackRow,
} from './multi-attack-selection';

const BASIC = makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' });
const STRONG = makeCard('strong-attack', { instanceId: 'str-1' });
const MODEL: MultiAttackModel = {
  rows: [{ instance: BASIC, seatIds: ['p1', 'p2'] }, { instance: STRONG, seatIds: ['p1', 'p2'] }],
  minAttacks: MULTI_ATTACK_MIN_ATTACKS,
};

describe('multi-attack selection (Q44)', () => {
  it('toggles a row on with no target, and off together with its target', () => {
    const on = toggleMultiAttackRow(EMPTY_MULTI_ATTACK_SELECTION, 'atk-1');
    expect(on).toEqual([{ instanceId: 'atk-1', targetPlayerId: null }]);
    expect(toggleMultiAttackRow(pickMultiAttackTarget(on, 'atk-1', 'p1'), 'atk-1')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('picks a target only for a toggled row', () => {
    expect(pickMultiAttackTarget(EMPTY_MULTI_ATTACK_SELECTION, 'atk-1', 'p1')).toBe(EMPTY_MULTI_ATTACK_SELECTION);
    expect(pickMultiAttackTarget(toggleMultiAttackRow(EMPTY_MULTI_ATTACK_SELECTION, 'atk-1'), 'atk-1', 'p2'))
      .toEqual([{ instanceId: 'atk-1', targetPlayerId: 'p2' }]);
  });

  it('builds no payload below two rows or while a toggled row has no target', () => {
    const one = pickMultiAttackTarget(toggleMultiAttackRow(EMPTY_MULTI_ATTACK_SELECTION, 'atk-1'), 'atk-1', 'p1');
    expect(multiAttackPayload(MODEL, one)).toBeNull();
    expect(multiAttackPayload(MODEL, toggleMultiAttackRow(one, 'str-1'))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('sends two rows on the same seat in hand order, whatever the toggle order', () => {
    let selection = toggleMultiAttackRow(EMPTY_MULTI_ATTACK_SELECTION, 'str-1');
    selection = pickMultiAttackTarget(selection, 'str-1', 'p1');
    selection = pickMultiAttackTarget(toggleMultiAttackRow(selection, 'atk-1'), 'atk-1', 'p1');
    expect(multiAttackPayload(MODEL, selection)).toEqual({
      attacks: [{ instanceId: 'atk-1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' }, { instanceId: 'str-1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' }],
    });
  });

  it('drops rows that left the model and targets that left a row', () => {
    const shrunk: MultiAttackModel = { rows: [{ instance: BASIC, seatIds: ['p2'] }], minAttacks: MULTI_ATTACK_MIN_ATTACKS };
    let selection = pickMultiAttackTarget(toggleMultiAttackRow(EMPTY_MULTI_ATTACK_SELECTION, 'atk-1'), 'atk-1', 'p1');
    selection = pickMultiAttackTarget(toggleMultiAttackRow(selection, 'str-1'), 'str-1', 'p1');
    expect(selectedMultiAttackRows(shrunk, selection)).toEqual([{ instanceId: 'atk-1', targetPlayerId: null }]);
    expect(multiAttackPayload(shrunk, selection)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('labels Play with the selected count (V2 copy)', () => {
    expect(playAttacksLabel(2)).toBe('Play 2 attacks');
  });
});
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Assassin multi-attack in the card sheet: technical spec v7 §6.6.1, §6.6.2, §13 Q44; Appendix A
 * `assassin-multi-attack`, `table-multi-attack-target-eligibility` (L74-11).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView, PublicPlayerView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { UserEvent } from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { DISABLED_REASON_COPY } from '../../view-model/action-reasons';
import { makeCard, makePending, makePersistent, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const BASIC = 'Basic attack, 1 damage';
const STRONG = 'Strong attack, 2 damage';
const assassinSelf = (kitId: 'assassin' | 'kamikaze' = 'assassin') => makeSelf({
  kitId,
  points: 10,
  hand: [makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' }), makeCard('strong-attack', { instanceId: 'str-1' })],
  specialCards: [makeCard('mega-attack', { instanceId: 'mega-1' })],
});
const assassinView = (overrides: Partial<PlayingStateView> = {}): PlayingStateView =>
  makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: assassinSelf(), ...overrides } });
function withSeat(view: PlayingStateView, playerId: string, patch: Partial<PublicPlayerView>): PlayingStateView {
  return { ...view, players: view.players.map((player) => (player.id === playerId ? { ...player, ...patch } : player)) };
}
function setup(view: PlayingStateView) {
  const onPlayMultipleAttacks = vi.fn<ArenaScreenProps['onPlayMultipleAttacks']>();
  const onPlayCard = vi.fn<ArenaScreenProps['onPlayCard']>();
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view, onPlayMultipleAttacks, onPlayCard });
  return { onPlayMultipleAttacks, onPlayCard, props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />) };
}
function region(name: string): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
  if (element === null) throw new Error(`no ${name} region`);
  return element;
}
const handCard = (name: string): HTMLElement => within(region('hand')).getByRole('button', { name });
const sheet = (name: string): HTMLElement => screen.getByRole('dialog', { name });
const isDisabled = (button: HTMLElement): boolean => button.hasAttribute('disabled') || button.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true';
function reasonOf(button: HTMLElement): string {
  const ids = button.getAttribute('aria-describedby') ?? '';
  return ids.split(' ').map((id) => document.getElementById(id)?.textContent ?? '').join(' ').trim();
}
async function openMultiAttack(user: UserEvent): Promise<HTMLElement> {
  await user.click(handCard(BASIC));
  await user.click(within(sheet('Basic attack')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Multi-attack' }));
  return sheet('Assassin multi-attack');
}
/** A toggled row's seat group is named by its card (aria-labelledby is not available on Card). */
const seatIn = (dialog: HTMLElement, card: string, seat: string): HTMLElement =>
  within(within(dialog).getByRole('group', { name: card })).getByRole('button', { name: seat });

describe('Assassin multi-attack (§6.6.2)', () => {
  it('two rows on the same seat send one playMultipleAttacks with both, in hand order', async () => {
    const { user, onPlayMultipleAttacks, onPlayCard, props } = setup(assassinView());
    const dialog = await openMultiAttack(user);
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Select at least two attack cards and a target each.')).not.toBeNull();
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: STRONG }));
    await user.click(seatIn(dialog, STRONG, 'p1'));
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: BASIC }));
    await user.click(seatIn(dialog, BASIC, 'p1'));
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Play 2 attacks' }));
    expect(onPlayMultipleAttacks.mock.calls).toEqual([[[
      { instanceId: 'atk-1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' },
      { instanceId: 'str-1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' },
    ]]]);
    expect(onPlayCard).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Assassin multi-attack' })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('Play is disabled with one row, and with two rows until each has a target', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(assassinView());
    const dialog = await openMultiAttack(user);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: BASIC }));
    await user.click(seatIn(dialog, BASIC, 'p1'));
    expect(isDisabled(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Play 1 attacks' }))).toBe(true);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: STRONG }));
    expect(isDisabled(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Play 2 attacks' }))).toBe(true);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('Cancel returns to the card and drops the selection: reopening shows none (Q44)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(assassinView());
    let dialog = await openMultiAttack(user);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: BASIC }));
    await user.click(seatIn(dialog, BASIC, 'p1'));
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    expect(sheet('Basic attack')).not.toBeNull();
    await user.click(within(sheet('Basic attack')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Multi-attack' }));
    dialog = sheet('Assassin multi-attack');
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: BASIC }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false');
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('group', { name: BASIC })).toBeNull();
    expect(isDisabled(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Play 0 attacks' }))).toBe(true);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('Esc in the multi-attack step closes the sheet; reopening the card shows the card step with nothing selected (Q44)', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(assassinView());
    let dialog = await openMultiAttack(user);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: BASIC }));
    await user.click(seatIn(dialog, BASIC, 'p1'));
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Assassin multi-attack' })).toBeNull();
    });
    // The closed sheet stays mounted (`closing`) and is reused: the open transition must reset it.
    await user.click(handCard(BASIC));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Assassin multi-attack' })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(within(sheet('Basic attack')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Multi-attack' }));
    dialog = sheet('Assassin multi-attack');
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: BASIC }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false');
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('group', { name: BASIC })).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('never lists MEGA ATTACK, and its own sheet has no Multi-attack', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(assassinView());
    const dialog = await openMultiAttack(user);
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('button', { name: /MEGA ATTACK/ })).toBeNull();
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await user.click(handCard('MEGA ATTACK, 20 damage'));
    expect(within(sheet('MEGA ATTACK')).queryByRole('button', { name: 'Multi-attack' })).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a kit without multiple attacks per turn shows no Multi-attack (Kamikaze)', async () => {
    const { user } = setup(makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: assassinSelf('kamikaze') } }));
    await user.click(handCard(BASIC));
    expect(within(sheet('Basic attack')).queryByRole('button', { name: 'Multi-attack' })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("rows offer the model's attack seats: a riposte corpse, never a living Invisible seat", async () => {
    const base = makeArenaView({
      seatCount: 4,
      tiers: { p2: 'eliminated' },
      overrides: {
        self: assassinSelf(),
        pendingEffects: [makePending({ id: 'atk-p2', sourcePlayerId: 'p2', targetPlayerId: 'me', cardId: 'basic-attack' })],
      },
    });
    const view = withSeat(base, 'p3', { activePersistentEffects: [makePersistent({ id: 'inv-p3', cardId: 'invisibility', counter: 2 })] });
    const { user, props } = setup(view);
    const dialog = await openMultiAttack(user);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: BASIC }));
    const group = within(dialog).getByRole('group', { name: BASIC });
    expect(within(group).getAllByRole('button').map((button) => button.getAttribute('aria-label'))).toEqual(['p1', 'p2, eliminated']);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('off-turn the Multi-attack entry is disabled with its reason', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(assassinView({ currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }));
    await user.click(handCard(BASIC));
    const entry = within(sheet('Basic attack')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Multi-attack' });
    expect(isDisabled(entry)).toBe(true);
    expect(reasonOf(entry)).toBe(DISABLED_REASON_COPY['not-your-turn']);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a double tap on Play sends one intent', async () => {
    const { user, onPlayMultipleAttacks } = setup(assassinView());
    const dialog = await openMultiAttack(user);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: BASIC }));
    await user.click(seatIn(dialog, BASIC, 'p2'));
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: STRONG }));
    await user.click(seatIn(dialog, STRONG, 'p1'));
    await user.dblClick(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Play 2 attacks' }));
    expect(onPlayMultipleAttacks).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

- `screens/arena/multi-attack.tsx`, `multi-attack-selection.ts`, `seat-choice-button.tsx`

  at least two in hand; never MEGA ATTACK). It turns the sheet into the 'Assassin multi-attack'
  step: one row per model row in hand order, each a card toggle (`aria-pressed`) and, once
  toggled, a group of the row's attack seats (`SeatChoiceButton`; a riposte corpse reads
  '{nick}, eliminated'). No row has a default target, and several rows may pick one seat. 'Play
  N attacks' needs two toggled rows that each have a target; it sends one `playMultipleAttacks`
  in hand order through the commit and closes the sheet. Cancel returns to the card and drops
  the selection (Q44); after Esc, the backdrop or any close, reopening the card starts on the card
  step with nothing selected (the reused, still-mounted sheet resets when it opens again).

  - Append to `docs/agent/decisions.md`:

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 Assassin multi-attack resets on Cancel (L74-11)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.6.2, §13 Q44; Appendix A `assassin-multi-attack`,
`table-multi-attack-target-eligibility`.

- **Cancel resets the selection (Q44).** V2 kept `multiIds` / `multiTargets` after Cancel; the
  arena drops them on Cancel, and the card sheet resets them when it opens again after Esc, the
  backdrop or any close. A closed sheet stays mounted through its exit and is reused on reopen, so
  the reset rides on the open transition, not on an unmount; reopening shows nothing selected.
- **No default target per row.** V2 seeded each toggled row with the first living opponent. The
  arena asks for an explicit seat per row, like tap-tap targeting (§6.6.2), so two toggles never
  send an attack the player did not aim. Several rows may still pick the same seat (golden
  rule 1).
- **Rows and order.** Rows are `cardActions(...).multiAttack` rows (hand shared attacks, never
  MEGA ATTACK); the intent lists the picked rows in hand order, whatever order they were
  toggled. The server pairs the volley (L68-05).
- **A step of the card sheet.** The panel replaces the card sheet's body and actions, so Cancel
  returns to the card; Esc and the backdrop close the sheet.

---

     ```

```

**Acceptance map:**
- Two rows on the same seat send one `playMultipleAttacks` containing both: `two rows on the same seat send one playMultipleAttacks with both, in hand order`.
- The button is disabled with one row: `Play is disabled with one row, and with two rows until each has a target`.
- Cancelling and reopening shows no selection: `Cancel returns to the card and drops the selection: reopening shows none (Q44)`.
- MEGA ATTACK is never listed: `never lists MEGA ATTACK, and its own sheet has no Multi-attack`.
- A non-Assassin kit shows no Multi-attack: `a kit without multiple attacks per turn shows no Multi-attack (Kamikaze)`.
- Corpse riposte per row, living Invisible seats never offered: `rows offer the model's attack seats: a riposte corpse, never a living Invisible seat`.
- Commit rule (toggling and picking send nothing): the `expectNoArenaIntent(props)` checks before Play. Double tap: `a double tap on Play sends one intent`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Multi-attack is a step inside the card sheet, not a fourth sheet kind.** The `ArenaSheet` union is fixed (A.5).
  - The Sheet title switches to 'Assassin multi-attack'.
  - Cancel returns to the card step. **Designer question:** should Cancel close the whole sheet, as V2's dialog did? Default: back to the card.
- **No default seat per row.** **Designer question:** keep V2's default (the first living opponent)? Default: no default.
- **The payload follows hand order**, the model row order, not toggle order.
- **'Play N attacks' keeps the V2 string verbatim,** including 'Play 1 attacks' and 'Play 0 attacks', which only appear on a disabled button.
  - Play shows a disabled reason only when the multi-attack action itself is disabled. An incomplete selection is explained by the hint line.
- **Rows show a `thumb` card toggle plus its V2 caption** (`formatCardLabel`, `aria-hidden` because the toggle speaks the face label).
  - The seat group is named by the card's face label, because `Card` takes no `id` for `aria-labelledby`.
- **`SeatChoiceButton` draws the seat-hue initial, never a kit portrait.** It needs no visibility logic and leaks nothing. It is shared with L74-13's Unspy picker.
- **The row stagger animation is dropped** in Part 1; L78 owns motion.
- **New strings:** none. Reused: 'Multi-attack', 'Assassin multi-attack', 'Select at least two attack cards and a target each.', 'Play {n} attacks', 'Cancel', `eliminatedPortraitLabel`.

  - `apps/client/src/screens/arena/hand-drag-layer.tsx`: the `HandDragLayer` component (ghost and Sell zone).

  - `apps/client/src/screens/arena/hand-drag-layer.test.tsx` (new, happy-dom, 10)
- Modify:

  - `apps/client/src/ui/primitives/sheet-stack.test.ts`: the `Z_INDEX` literal in `'keeps z-indexes inside their Z_INDEX layers'` (`{ sheet: 50, sheetBar: 80, banner: 90, toast: 95 }`) gains `dragGhost: 40`; the assertion's meaning is unchanged.

- Existing tests that must stay green unchanged:
  - every arena test file from L74-01 … L74-11;
  - `apps/client/src/view-model/drop-target.test.ts` and `card-actions.test.ts`;

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `cardActions`, `CardActionsModel`, `DropZones` (`view-model/card-actions`).
  - `dropTarget`, `DropResult`, `DropZoneRects`, `Point`, `Rect` (`view-model/drop-target`).
  - `ActionContext`.
  - `ArenaCommit` and ArenaScreen's `commit` (L74-08); `liveCardRefFor` (`./card-sheet-source`, L74-08): the single "is this card still held" helper.
  - `HandCardSource` (`./hand-fan-model`, L74-07): `Extract<CardSheetSource, { kind: 'hand' | 'special' }>`, the source a hand card opens.
  - ArenaScreen's `openSheet` (the sheet host's, L74-05) and `live` (L74-10's `liveTargeting` result).
  - `HandFan` (L74-07; each card is a `HandSlot` `<li>` holding the flip wrapper and the `Card`), `ArenaSeats` and `OpponentSeat` (L74-04), `CentralPlate` (L74-06, root `data-zone="pending"`).
  - `Z_INDEX` (`ui/layers.ts`), which this task extends with `dragGhost`.
  - From `ui/`:
    - `Card`, `CostAmount`, `structuredCostFromCardCost`;
    - `CARD_SPRING`, `usePrefersReducedMotion`, `readVisualViewportBox` (`ui/visual-viewport`).
  - From shared: `getCard`.
  - From `motion/react`: `animate`, `motion`, `useMotionValue`, `type MotionValue`.
  - From React: `useEffectEvent` (React 19.2, as `ui/primitives/sheet.tsx`).
  - Tests: `makeArenaView`, `makeArenaProps` and `expectNoArenaIntent` (`./test-view`, L74-01); "nothing sent" is `expectNoArenaIntent(props)`, "exactly one intent" reads that spy's calls.
- Produces:

```ts
// ui/layers.ts (Appendix A.8 ruling 10)
export const Z_INDEX = { dragGhost: 40, sheet: 50, sheetBar: 80, banner: 90, toast: 95 } as const;

// hand-drag.ts
export const HAND_DRAG_LIFT_PX = 8;                                   // §6.6.5 "~8 px"
export const SELL_ZONE_SIZE = { width: 96, height: 72 } as const;
export const SELL_ZONE_GAP_PX = 8;
export const SELL_ZONE_INSET_PX = 16;
export type HandGesture = 'pending' | 'scroll' | 'lift';
/** lift: |dy| > 8 and |dy| > |dx|; scroll: |dx| > 8 and |dx| >= |dy|; else pending. */
export function classifyHandGesture(delta: Point): HandGesture;
/** §6.6.3: never off-turn or locked (canDrag), never while targeting (L74-10). L75 adds the tour and Look gate. */
export function canStartHandDrag(actions: CardActionsModel, targetingActive: boolean): boolean;
/** The card's model on the latest view (`liveCardRefFor`), or null once it left the hand. */
export function latestHeldActions(view: PlayingStateView, ctx: ActionContext, source: HandCardSource): CardActionsModel | null;
export function resolveDrop(pointer: Point, rects: DropZoneRects, latest: CardActionsModel | null): DropResult | null;
export type DropChoice = Extract<DropResult, { kind: 'open-choice' }>['choice'];
export type DropOutcome = 'sent' | 'choice' | 'returned';
export function dispatchDrop(result: DropResult | null, commit: ArenaCommit, openChoice: (choice: DropChoice) => void): DropOutcome;
export type HoveredDropZone = { readonly kind: 'seat'; readonly playerId: string } | { readonly kind: 'plate' } | { readonly kind: 'sell' };
export function hoveredZoneOf(result: DropResult | null): HoveredDropZone | null;
export function sameHoveredZone(a: HoveredDropZone | null, b: HoveredDropZone | null): boolean;
export interface SeatDropHighlight { readonly seatIds: readonly string[]; readonly overId: string | null }
export type PlateDropHighlight = 'valid' | 'over';
export function seatDropHighlight(zones: DropZones, hover: HoveredDropZone | null): SeatDropHighlight;
export function plateDropHighlight(zones: DropZones, hover: HoveredDropZone | null): PlateDropHighlight | null;
/** Above the hand, on the side opposite the drag origin (§6.6.3). */
export function sellZoneRect(hand: Rect, originX: number, viewportWidth: number): Rect;
export function rectOf(box: { left: number; top: number; width: number; height: number }): Rect;
/** Seats by `[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id]` (DOM order), plate by `[data-zone="pending"]`. */
export function measureDropZones(root: ParentNode, sell: Rect | null): DropZoneRects;
export interface HandFanDragProps {
  readonly onCardPointerDown: (source: HandCardSource, event: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => void;
  readonly onCardClickCapture: (event: ReactMouseEvent<HTMLElement>) => void;
  readonly liftedInstanceId: string | null;
}

// use-hand-drag.ts
export interface HandDragInput {
  view: PlayingStateView;
  ctx: ActionContext;
  targetingActive: boolean;
  commit: ArenaCommit;
  /** Opens this card's sheet: its pre-send choice step (§6.6.3). */
  openChoice: (source: HandCardSource) => void;
}
export interface HandDragView {
  /** The dragged hand or special card. */
  readonly card: HandCardSource;
  readonly phase: 'dragging' | 'returning';
  /** The latest model's zones; empty once the card left the hand. */
  readonly zones: DropZones;
  readonly hover: HoveredDropZone | null;
  readonly sellRect: Rect | null;
  readonly ghostX: MotionValue<number>;
  readonly ghostY: MotionValue<number>;
}
export function useHandDrag(input: HandDragInput): { readonly fanProps: HandFanDragProps; readonly view: HandDragView | null };

// hand-drag-layer.tsx
export interface HandDragLayerProps { drag: HandDragView | null }
export function HandDragLayer({ drag }: HandDragLayerProps): ReactElement | null;

// New optional props
// HandFan: drag?: HandFanDragProps
// ArenaSeats: dropHighlight?: SeatDropHighlight     (each OpponentSeat gets drop?: 'valid' | 'over')
// CentralPlate: dropHighlight?: PlateDropHighlight | null
```

```ts
/**
 * Drag from the hand, pure helpers: technical spec v7 §6.6.3–§6.6.5, §12.2 (L74-12).
 */
import type { CardInstance, PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import type { ActionContext } from '../../view-model/action-lock';
import { cardActions } from '../../view-model/card-actions';
import { dropTarget, type DropZoneRects, type Rect } from '../../view-model/drop-target';
import { makeCard, makePlayingView, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import type { ArenaCommit } from './arena-commit';
import {
  canStartHandDrag, classifyHandGesture, dispatchDrop, hoveredZoneOf, latestHeldActions,
  plateDropHighlight, resolveDrop, seatDropHighlight, sellZoneRect, SELL_ZONE_SIZE, type DropChoice,
} from './hand-drag';

const LIVE: ActionContext = { subChoice: null, readOnly: false };
const ATTACK = makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' });
const TAX = makeCard('tax', { instanceId: 'tax-1' });
const REGEN = makeCard('regeneration', { instanceId: 'regen-1' });
const TRANSFORMER = makeCard('card-transformer', { instanceId: 'tf-1' });
const VIEW: PlayingStateView = makePlayingView({ self: makeSelf({ points: 30, hand: [ATTACK, TAX, REGEN], specialCards: [TRANSFORMER] }) });
const box = (left: number, top: number): Rect => ({ left, top, width: 100, height: 100 });
const RECTS: DropZoneRects = {
  seats: [{ playerId: 'p1', rect: box(0, 0) }, { playerId: 'p2', rect: box(200, 0) }],
  plate: box(100, 200),
  sell: box(300, 400),
};
const SEAT_P1 = { x: 50, y: 50 };
const PLATE = { x: 150, y: 250 };
const SELL = { x: 350, y: 450 };
const OUTSIDE = { x: 999, y: 999 };
const held = (instance: CardInstance, zone: 'hand' | 'special' = 'hand') => cardActions(VIEW, { zone, instance }, LIVE);
function spies() {
  return { commit: vi.fn<ArenaCommit>(), openChoice: vi.fn<(choice: DropChoice) => void>() };
}

describe('gesture and drag start guard (§6.6.5)', () => {
  it.each([
    [{ x: 0, y: 7 }, 'pending'],
    [{ x: 5, y: -9 }, 'lift'],
    [{ x: 3, y: 12 }, 'lift'],
    [{ x: 12, y: 9 }, 'scroll'],
    [{ x: 9, y: 9 }, 'scroll'],
  ] as const)('reads %o as %s', (delta, gesture) => {
    expect(classifyHandGesture(delta)).toBe(gesture);
  });

  it('starts only from a model that can drag, and never while targeting', () => {
    expect(canStartHandDrag(held(ATTACK), false)).toBe(true);
    expect(canStartHandDrag(held(ATTACK), true)).toBe(false);
    const spyNoPoints = makePlayingView({ self: makeSelf({ points: 0, hand: [makeCard('spy', { instanceId: 'spy-1' })] }) });
    // Use is unaffordable, but Sell is open: the Sell zone still takes the card.
    expect(canStartHandDrag(cardActions(spyNoPoints, { zone: 'hand', instance: makeCard('spy', { instanceId: 'spy-1' }) }, LIVE), false)).toBe(true);
  });

  it.each<{ label: string; view: PlayingStateView; ctx: ActionContext }>([
    { label: 'off-turn', view: { ...VIEW, currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }, ctx: LIVE },
    { label: 'a sub-choice open', view: VIEW, ctx: { subChoice: { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 1 }, readOnly: false } },
    { label: 'the finished board', view: VIEW, ctx: { subChoice: null, readOnly: true } },
  ])('never starts a drag $label (actions locked)', ({ view, ctx }) => {
    expect(canStartHandDrag(cardActions(view, { zone: 'hand', instance: ATTACK }, ctx), false)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('dispatchDrop maps each dropTarget result (§6.6.3, §6.6.4)', () => {
  it('a valid seat commits playCard with that target', () => {
    const { commit, openChoice } = spies();
    expect(dispatchDrop(dropTarget(SEAT_P1, RECTS, held(ATTACK)), commit, openChoice)).toBe('sent');
    expect(commit.mock.calls).toEqual([[{ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: 'atk-1', targetPlayerId: 'p1' } }]]);
    expect(openChoice).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('the plate commits an untargeted card with no targetPlayerId key', () => {
    const { commit, openChoice } = spies();
    expect(dispatchDrop(dropTarget(PLATE, RECTS, held(TAX)), commit, openChoice)).toBe('sent');
    expect(commit.mock.calls).toEqual([[{ kind: 'playCard', payload: { instanceId: 'tax-1' } }]]);
    expect(openChoice).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('Regeneration on the plate opens its choice and sends nothing', () => {
    const { commit, openChoice } = spies();
    expect(dispatchDrop(dropTarget(PLATE, RECTS, held(REGEN)), commit, openChoice)).toBe('choice');
    expect(openChoice.mock.calls).toEqual([['regeneration']]);
    expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('Card Transformer on the plate opens its consume choice and sends nothing', () => {
    const { commit, openChoice } = spies();
    expect(dispatchDrop(dropTarget(PLATE, RECTS, held(TRANSFORMER, 'special')), commit, openChoice)).toBe('choice');
    expect(openChoice.mock.calls).toEqual([['consume']]);
    expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('the Sell zone commits sellCard', () => {
    const { commit } = spies();
    expect(dispatchDrop(dropTarget(SELL, RECTS, held(ATTACK)), commit, vi.fn())).toBe('sent');
    expect(commit.mock.calls).toEqual([[{ kind: 'sellCard', payload: { instanceId: 'atk-1' } }]]);
  });

  it('null sends nothing and returns the card', () => {
    const { commit, openChoice } = spies();
    expect(dropTarget(OUTSIDE, RECTS, held(ATTACK))).toBeNull();
    expect(dispatchDrop(null, commit, openChoice)).toBe('returned');
    expect([commit.mock.calls.length, openChoice.mock.calls.length]).toEqual([0, 0]);
  });
});

describe('a drop resolves against the latest view (Review Focus 4)', () => {
  it('sends nothing once the card left the hand', () => {
    const { commit, openChoice } = spies();
    const latest = latestHeldActions({ ...VIEW, self: makeSelf({ points: 30, hand: [TAX] }) }, LIVE, { kind: 'hand', instance: ATTACK });
    expect(latest).toBeNull();
    expect(dispatchDrop(resolveDrop(SEAT_P1, RECTS, latest), commit, openChoice)).toBe('returned');
    expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('sends nothing when the turn passed mid-drag: the latest model cannot drag', () => {
    const { commit, openChoice } = spies();
    const latest = latestHeldActions({ ...VIEW, currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }, LIVE, { kind: 'hand', instance: ATTACK });
    expect(latest?.canDrag).toBe(false);
    expect(dispatchDrop(resolveDrop(SEAT_P1, RECTS, latest), commit, openChoice)).toBe('returned');
    expect(commit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('highlights and the Sell zone (§6.6.3)', () => {
  it('names the zone under the pointer', () => {
    expect(hoveredZoneOf(dropTarget(SEAT_P1, RECTS, held(ATTACK)))).toEqual({ kind: 'seat', playerId: 'p1' });
    expect(hoveredZoneOf(dropTarget(PLATE, RECTS, held(TAX)))).toEqual({ kind: 'plate' });
    expect(hoveredZoneOf(dropTarget(PLATE, RECTS, held(REGEN)))).toEqual({ kind: 'plate' });
    expect(hoveredZoneOf(dropTarget(SELL, RECTS, held(ATTACK)))).toEqual({ kind: 'sell' });
    expect(hoveredZoneOf(null)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('lights seats for a targeted card and only the plate for an untargeted one', () => {
    expect(seatDropHighlight(held(ATTACK).drop, { kind: 'seat', playerId: 'p2' })).toEqual({ seatIds: ['p1', 'p2'], overId: 'p2' });
    expect(plateDropHighlight(held(ATTACK).drop, null)).toBeNull();
    expect(seatDropHighlight(held(TAX).drop, null)).toEqual({ seatIds: [], overId: null });
    expect([plateDropHighlight(held(TAX).drop, null), plateDropHighlight(held(TAX).drop, { kind: 'plate' })]).toEqual(['valid', 'over']);
  });

  it('puts the Sell zone above the hand on the side opposite the drag origin', () => {
    const hand: Rect = { left: 0, top: 600, width: 375, height: 140 };
    const top = 600 - SELL_ZONE_SIZE.height - 8;
    expect(sellZoneRect(hand, 60, 375)).toEqual({ left: 375 - 96 - 16, top, width: 96, height: 72 });
    expect(sellZoneRect(hand, 300, 375)).toEqual({ left: 16, top, width: 96, height: 72 });
  });
});
```

That is 20 tests: the gesture and start-guard describe holds 9 (the gesture table counts as 5, the lock table as 3), then 6 dispatch, 2 latest-view and 3 highlight tests.

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/hand-drag-layer.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Drag from the hand in the arena: technical spec v7 §6.6.3–§6.6.5 (L74-12). happy-dom has no
 * layout: every measured rect is empty unless a test stubs it, so a drop lands nowhere by default.
 */
import type { PlayingStateView } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { fireEvent, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { renderUi } from '../../test/render';
import { readVisualViewportBox } from '../../ui/visual-viewport';
import { makeCard, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { measureDropZones, sellZoneRect } from './hand-drag';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const PRIMARY = { pointerId: 1, isPrimary: true, button: 0 } as const;
const HAND = [
  makeCard('basic-attack', { instanceId: 'atk-1' }),
  makeCard('tax', { instanceId: 'tax-1' }),
  makeCard('regeneration', { instanceId: 'regen-1' }),
];
const playView = (overrides: Partial<PlayingStateView> = {}): PlayingStateView => makeArenaView({
  overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 30, hand: HAND, specialCards: [makeCard('mega-attack', { instanceId: 'mega-1' })] }), ...overrides },
});
function setup(view: PlayingStateView) {
  const onPlayCard = vi.fn<ArenaScreenProps['onPlayCard']>();
  const onSellCard = vi.fn<ArenaScreenProps['onSellCard']>();
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view, onPlayCard, onSellCard });
  return { onPlayCard, onSellCard, props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />, { reducedMotion: true }) };
}
function region(name: string): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-arena-region="${name}"]`);
  if (element === null) throw new Error(`no ${name} region`);
  return element;
}
function anchor(selector: string): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector);
  if (element === null) throw new Error(`no ${selector}`);
  return element;
}
const handCard = (name: string): HTMLElement => within(region('hand')).getByRole('button', { name });
const seat = (playerId: string): HTMLElement => anchor(`[data-zone="opponent-seat"][data-player-id="${playerId}"]`);
const plate = (): HTMLElement => anchor('[data-zone="pending"]');
const ghost = (): Element | null => document.querySelector('[data-zone="hand-drag-ghost"]');
const sellZone = (): Element | null => document.querySelector('[data-zone="sell-drop"]');
const press = (element: HTMLElement, x: number, y: number): void => { fireEvent.pointerDown(element, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: x, clientY: y }); };
const move = (x: number, y: number): void => { fireEvent.pointerMove(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: x, clientY: y }); };
const release = (x: number, y: number): void => { fireEvent.pointerUp(window, { ...PRIMARY, clientX: x, clientY: y }); };
/** Press at (100, 700) and move 12 px up: past the threshold and mostly vertical. */
function lift(card: HTMLElement): void { press(card, 100, 700); move(103, 688); }

describe('drag from the hand (§6.6.3, §6.6.5)', () => {
  it('lifts a ghost only past 8 px vertical, lights the valid seats and sends nothing', () => {
    const { props } = setup(playView());
    const card = handCard('Basic attack, 1 damage');
    press(card, 100, 700);
    move(103, 694);
    expect(ghost()).toBeNull();
    move(103, 688);
    expect(ghost()).not.toBeNull();
    expect(card.closest('[data-lifted]')?.getAttribute('data-lifted')).toBe('true');
    expect([seat('p1').getAttribute('data-drop'), seat('p2').getAttribute('data-drop')]).toEqual(['valid', 'valid']);
    expect(plate().hasAttribute('data-drop')).toBe(false);
    expect(sellZone()?.textContent).toContain('Sell');
    release(103, 650);
    expect([ghost(), sellZone()]).toEqual([null, null]);
    expect(seat('p1').hasAttribute('data-drop')).toBe(false);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a mostly horizontal move is the hand scroll and never lifts', () => {
    const { props } = setup(playView());
    press(handCard('Basic attack, 1 damage'), 100, 700);
    move(115, 697);
    move(130, 680);
    expect(ghost()).toBeNull();
    release(130, 680);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('never lifts off-turn, and the tap still opens the sheet', async () => {
    const { user, props } = setup(playView({ currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }));
    lift(handCard('Basic attack, 1 damage'));
    expect(ghost()).toBeNull();
    release(103, 650);
    await user.click(handCard('Basic attack, 1 damage'));
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Basic attack' })).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a drop on a valid seat sends playCard with that target once', () => {
    const { onPlayCard } = setup(playView());
    vi.spyOn(seat('p1'), 'getBoundingClientRect').mockReturnValue(new DOMRect(0, 0, 100, 100));
    lift(handCard('Basic attack, 1 damage'));
    move(50, 50);
    expect(seat('p1').getAttribute('data-drop')).toBe('over');
    release(50, 50);
    expect(onPlayCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['atk-1', { targetPlayerId: 'p1' }]]);
    expect(ghost()).toBeNull();
  });

  it('a drop on the plate sends an untargeted card', () => {
    const { onPlayCard } = setup(playView());
    vi.spyOn(plate(), 'getBoundingClientRect').mockReturnValue(new DOMRect(100, 300, 120, 96));
    lift(handCard('Tax'));
    expect(plate().getAttribute('data-drop')).toBe('valid');
    expect(seat('p1').hasAttribute('data-drop')).toBe(false);
    release(150, 340);
    expect(onPlayCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['tax-1']]);
  });

  it('Regeneration dropped on the plate opens its choice in the sheet and sends nothing', () => {
    const { props } = setup(playView());
    vi.spyOn(plate(), 'getBoundingClientRect').mockReturnValue(new DOMRect(100, 300, 120, 96));
    lift(handCard('Regeneration'));
    release(150, 340);
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Regeneration' });
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('group', { name: 'Regeneration quantity' })).not.toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('a drop on the Sell zone sells the card', () => {
    const { onSellCard } = setup(playView());
    lift(handCard('Basic attack, 1 damage'));
    const sell = sellZoneRect({ left: 0, top: 0, width: 0, height: 0 }, 100, readVisualViewportBox().width);
    release(sell.left + sell.width / 2, sell.top + sell.height / 2);
    expect(onSellCard.mock.calls).toEqual([['atk-1']]);
  });

  it('a special shows no Sell zone and lights the plate (MEGA ATTACK)', () => {
    const { props } = setup(playView());
    lift(handCard('MEGA ATTACK, 20 damage'));
    expect(ghost()).not.toBeNull();
    expect(sellZone()).toBeNull();
    expect(plate().getAttribute('data-drop')).toBe('valid');
    release(103, 650);
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('the click that ends a drag does not open the card sheet; the next tap does', async () => {
    const { user } = setup(playView());
    const card = handCard('Basic attack, 1 damage');
    lift(card);
    release(103, 650);
    fireEvent.click(card);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Basic attack' })).toBeNull();
    await user.click(card);
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Basic attack' })).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('measures seats and the plate from their arena anchors', () => {
    renderUi(<>
      <div data-zone="opponent-seat" data-player-id="p1" />
      <div data-zone="opponent-seat" data-player-id="p2" />
      <div data-zone="pending" />
    </>);
    const sell = { left: 1, top: 2, width: 3, height: 4 };
    const rects = measureDropZones(document, sell);
    expect(rects.seats.map((entry) => entry.playerId)).toEqual(['p1', 'p2']);
    expect(rects.plate).toEqual({ left: 0, top: 0, width: 0, height: 0 });
    expect(rects.sell).toBe(sell);
  });
});
```

That is 10 tests.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**
  - **`hand-drag.ts` (pure, no DOM except `measureDropZones`).**
    - `canStartHandDrag(actions, targetingActive)` is `actions.canDrag && !targetingActive`.
    - `latestHeldActions(view, ctx, source)`: `const ref = liveCardRefFor(source, view);` then `ref.zone === 'read-only' ? null : cardActions(view, ref, ctx)`.
    - `resolveDrop` returns `latest === null ? null : dropTarget(pointer, rects, latest)`.
    - `dispatchDrop`:
      - `null` returns `'returned'`;
      - `{ kind: 'intent' }` calls `commit(result.intent)` (a `CardIntent` is an `ArenaIntent`) and returns `'sent'`;
      - `{ kind: 'open-choice' }` calls `openChoice(result.choice)` and returns `'choice'`.
    - `hoveredZoneOf`:
      - open-choice gives plate;
      - `playCard` with `payload.targetPlayerId !== undefined` gives that seat;
      - other `playCard` gives plate;
      - `sellCard` gives sell.
    - `seatDropHighlight`: `{ seatIds: zones.seatIds, overId: hover?.kind === 'seat' ? hover.playerId : null }`.
    - `plateDropHighlight`: null when `zones.plate === null`, else `'over'` when hover is the plate, else `'valid'`.
    - `sellZoneRect`: top = `hand.top - SELL_ZONE_SIZE.height - SELL_ZONE_GAP_PX`. Left = `viewportWidth - width - SELL_ZONE_INSET_PX` when `originX < viewportWidth / 2`, else `SELL_ZONE_INSET_PX`.
    - `measureDropZones`:
      - reads `getBoundingClientRect()` once per element;
      - takes seat ids with `getAttribute('data-player-id')` (`dataset` is an index signature under `noPropertyAccessFromIndexSignature`) and skips an empty id;
      - `plate` is the first `[data-zone="pending"]` or null.
  - **`use-hand-drag.ts`.** The phase lives in state:
    ```ts
    type Phase =
      | { kind: 'pressed'; card: HandCardSource; pointerId: number; start: Point; origin: Rect }
      | { kind: 'dragging'; card: HandCardSource; pointerId: number; origin: Rect; grab: Point; rects: DropZoneRects; sellRect: Rect | null; hover: HoveredDropZone | null }
      | { kind: 'returning'; card: HandCardSource; origin: Rect };
    ```
    - `onCardPointerDown(source, event)` (a React handler):
      1. clear `suppressClick.current`;
      2. ignore unless `event.isPrimary && event.button === 0`;
      3. ignore unless `canStartHandDrag(cardActions(input.view, liveCardRefFor(source, input.view), input.ctx), input.targetingActive)`;
      4. `setPhase({ kind: 'pressed', … start: client point, origin: rectOf(event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect()) })`;
      5. never `preventDefault`, so the tap still clicks.
    - Window listeners: `useEffect` keyed on the tracked pointer id (`pressed` or `dragging`) adds `pointermove`, `pointerup` and `pointercancel` on `window`. Each calls a `useEffectEvent` handler that reads the latest phase and props, the same pattern as Sheet's keydown. Ignore other pointer ids.
    - **Move, `pressed`:**
      - `classifyHandGesture(point − start)`;
      - `scroll` gives `setPhase(null)`;
      - `lift`:
        - `grab = start − origin top-left`;
        - `sellRect = sellZoneRect(rectOf(handRegion.getBoundingClientRect()), start.x, readVisualViewportBox().width)`, where `handRegion = document.querySelector('[data-arena-region="hand"]')` (an empty rect when missing);
        - `latest = latestHeldActions(input.view, input.ctx, phase.card)`;
        - `rects = measureDropZones(document, latest?.drop.sell === true ? sellRect : null)`;
        - `ghostX.set(point.x − grab.x)` and `ghostY.set(point.y − grab.y)`;
        - `suppressClick.current = true`;
        - `setPhase({ kind: 'dragging', …, hover: hoveredZoneOf(resolveDrop(point, rects, latest)) })`.
    - **Move, `dragging`:** set the ghost values. Then `const hover = hoveredZoneOf(resolveDrop(point, phase.rects, latestHeldActions(input.view, input.ctx, phase.card)))`, and set the phase only when `!sameHoveredZone(hover, phase.hover)`. Ghost moves never re-render the arena.
    - **Up:**
      - `pressed` gives `setPhase(null)`; the click then opens the sheet as usual.
      - `dragging`: `const outcome = dispatchDrop(resolveDrop(point, phase.rects, latestHeldActions(input.view, input.ctx, phase.card)), input.commit, () => { input.openChoice(phase.card); })`.
        - `'returned'` springs back.
        - Otherwise `setPhase(null)`; the flight is L74-17 / L78.
    - **Cancel** (`pointercancel`): `pressed` gives null; `dragging` springs back.
    - **Spring back:**
      - under `usePrefersReducedMotion()`, `setPhase(null)` at once;
      - otherwise `setPhase({ kind: 'returning', card, origin })`, then `animate(ghostX, origin.left, CARD_SPRING)` and `animate(ghostY, origin.top, { ...CARD_SPRING, onComplete: () => { setPhase(null); } })`.
    - `fanProps.onCardClickCapture`: when `suppressClick.current`, reset it and call `event.preventDefault()` and `event.stopPropagation()`. That swallows the click that ends a drag.
    - `liftedInstanceId` is `phase.card.instance.instanceId` while dragging or returning.
    - `view`: null unless the phase is dragging or returning. Then `zones = latestHeldActions(input.view, input.ctx, phase.card)?.drop ?? { seatIds: [], plate: null, sell: false }`, recomputed every render, and `sellRect`, `hover` and the two motion values.
  - **`ui/layers.ts`** (Appendix A.8 rulings 8 and 10): `export const Z_INDEX = { dragGhost: 40, sheet: 50, sheetBar: 80, banner: 90, toast: 95 } as const;`. Add to the doc comment that `dragGhost` is the hand-drag ghost and Sell zone, a `pointer-events: none` portal mounted only during a drag, below every sheet. Update the `sheet-stack.test.ts` literal to match.
  - **`hand-drag-layer.tsx`.** It returns null when `drag === null`. Otherwise it renders `createPortal(…, document.body)` with:
    - the ghost: `<motion.div data-zone="hand-drag-ghost" aria-hidden="true" style={{ position: 'fixed', left: 0, top: 0, x: drag.ghostX, y: drag.ghostY, zIndex: Z_INDEX.dragGhost, pointerEvents: 'none' }} data-motion={motionAttribute(reduced)}>` holding `<Card size="hand" instance={{ ...drag.card.instance, instanceId: `ghost-${drag.card.instance.instanceId}` }} className={reduced ? '' : 'scale-105'} />`. The synthetic id keeps the ghost out of `[data-instance-id="…"]` queries (L74-17 FX, the L75 tutorial highlight), which must find the real card in its slot;
    - when `drag.phase === 'dragging' && drag.zones.sell && drag.sellRect !== null`, the Sell zone: `<div data-zone="sell-drop" aria-hidden="true" data-drop={drag.hover?.kind === 'sell' ? 'over' : 'valid'}>`.
      - Fixed at `sellRect`, the same `Z_INDEX.dragGhost`, `pointerEvents: 'none'`.
      - Classes `${CHROME_SHAPE_SM_CLASS} flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-v7-panel font-v7-display text-v7-body text-v7-text`, plus `ring-4 ring-v7-mine` when over.
      - Content `CARD_SELL_LABEL` and `<CostAmount amount={sellYield} signed="gain" title={null} />`, where `sellYield = structuredCostFromCardCost(getCard(cardId)?.sellYield)` (no CostAmount when null).
  - **`hand-fan.tsx`.** New optional prop `drag?: HandFanDragProps`, passed down to `HandSlot`. Each `HandSlot` `<li>` (L74-07: one per hand and special card, around the flip wrapper and the `Card`) gets:

    - the class `touch-pan-x`, so a horizontal pan stays the browser's native scroll;

  - **`opponent-seat.tsx` / `arena-seats.tsx`.** ArenaSeats `dropHighlight?: SeatDropHighlight` gives each seat `drop` = `'over'` when `overId === playerId`, `'valid'` when listed, else nothing.   - **`central-plate.tsx`.** `dropHighlight?: PlateDropHighlight | null` sets `data-drop` on the `data-zone="pending"` root, with the same rings.
  - **`arena-screen.tsx`.**

  - Append to `docs/agent/decisions.md`:

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 hand drag: pointer events, portal ghost, drop on the latest view (L74-12)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.6.3–§6.6.5, §12.2; Appendix A `table-use-target-routing`,
`global-input-modalities`.

- **Pointer events and a threshold, not motion `drag`.** Hand cards carry `touch-action: pan-x`,
  so a horizontal swipe stays the browser's native scroll of the hand (the browser cancels the
  pointer), and a move more vertical than horizontal past 8 px (`classifyHandGesture`) lifts the
  card. motion's element `drag` moves the element inside the clipped scroller and sets
  `touch-action: none`, which would kill that native scroll; §6.6.5 allows "an equivalent
  threshold".
- **Portal ghost.** The ghost and the Sell zone are a `pointer-events: none` layer portaled to
  `document.body` on `Z_INDEX.dragGhost` (40, `ui/layers.ts`), under `Z_INDEX.sheet`, mounted only
  during a drag. They are the one fixed layer outside `Sheet` / `Dialog` / `Toast` (Lot 74 plan
  Appendix A.8 ruling 10), because §6.6.5 asks for a ghost out of the scroller. Hit testing stays
  `dropTarget` rect math on zones measured once at lift.
- **A drop resolves against the latest view.** The card's model is recomputed at release, so a
  card that left the hand, a passed turn or a lock sends nothing and springs back.
- **Sell zone.** It shows only while a sellable card is dragged, above the hand on the side
  opposite the drag origin, so neither a straight lift nor a release over the hand sells.
- **No drag while targeting.** Targeting is exclusive (L74-10). L75 adds the tutorial tour and
  Look gate to `canStartHandDrag`.

---
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Unit tests on the drop handler map each `dropTarget` result to the right commit or none: the `dispatchDrop maps each dropTarget result` describe (seat, plate, Regeneration, Card Transformer, Sell, null).
- A pre-send-choice result opens the choice step without sending:
  - `Regeneration on the plate opens its choice and sends nothing`;
  - `Card Transformer on the plate opens its consume choice and sends nothing`;
  - `Regeneration dropped on the plate opens its choice in the sheet and sends nothing`.
- `null` sends nothing: `null sends nothing and returns the card`.
- The drag start guard (no drag when actions are locked or off-turn):
  - `never starts a drag $label (actions locked)` (off-turn, sub-choice, finished board);
  - `starts only from a model that can drag, and never while targeting`;
  - `never lifts off-turn, and the tap still opens the sheet`.
- Review Focus 4: `sends nothing once the card left the hand` and `sends nothing when the turn passed mid-drag: the latest model cannot drag`.
- Seat, plate and Sell highlights:
  - `lifts a ghost only past 8 px vertical, lights the valid seats and sends nothing`;
  - `a drop on the plate sends an untargeted card`;
  - `a special shows no Sell zone and lights the plate (MEGA ATTACK)`;
  - `lights seats for a targeted card and only the plate for an untargeted one`.
- The browser check at 375×812 and 390×844 is recorded in frontend.md: Step 5. `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Gesture: pointer events plus `classifyHandGesture`, not motion's `dragDirectionLock`.**
  - motion's element drag moves the card inside the overflow-clipped scroller and sets `touch-action: none`, which would remove the native horizontal scroll.
  - `touch-action: pan-x` lets the browser own horizontal pans and leaves vertical moves to the hook.
- **Lift rule:** `|dy| > 8 && |dy| > |dx|`, in either direction; a tie is a scroll.
- **The ghost** is the same `hand`-size face, `scale-105` at full motion. It keeps the grab offset and is `aria-hidden` (the real card stays in its slot, dimmed `opacity-40`, `data-lifted="true"`). Its instance id is `ghost-<instanceId>`, so `data-instance-id` stays unique to the real card.
- **The z-index is `Z_INDEX.dragGhost` (40) in `ui/layers.ts`,** below `sheet` (50), so the ghost never covers a sheet (Appendix A.8 ruling 10). `DESIGN.md` Overlays records it, and the `sheet-stack.test.ts` literal that pins `Z_INDEX` gains the key.
- **Sell zone: 96×72, 8 px above the hand, 16 px from the edge opposite the pointer's starting x.** It shows 'Sell' and the catalog sell gain. **Designer question:** placement and size. Default: as stated.
- **Highlights are read from the latest model each render;** the rects are measured once, at lift.
- **Spring-back uses `CARD_SPRING`** and is instant under reduced motion. A sent drop or an opened choice removes the ghost at once; the card flight is L74-17 / L78.
- **A drop that opens a pre-send choice opens the card sheet.** The choice group is the sheet's first control, so it takes focus.
- **No drag while targeting** (exclusive, L74-10).
- **No Esc to cancel a drag;** `pointercancel` springs the card back.
- **The click that ends a drag is swallowed in the capture phase.** The flag resets on the next pointerdown, so it never eats a later tap.
- **The test interactions are mouse-shaped.** The browser check records touch-specific behaviour only through the computed `touch-action`. The built-in browser emulates touch points but delivers clicks as mouse events.
- **New strings:** none ('Sell' is `CARD_SELL_LABEL`).
