Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock-model.test.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Dock model: technical spec v7 §6.1 item 5; L58-09, L59-01, L64-05; A.8 ruling 4 (L74-13).
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { UnspyControl } from '../../view-model/self-strip';
import { dockHasControls, drawAriaLabel, drawPulseKey, resolvedSpyId, unspyAriaLabel } from './dock-model';

const control = (spyIds: readonly string[]): UnspyControl => ({
  cost: { kind: 'points', amount: 10 },
  spyIds,
  preselectedSpyId: spyIds.length === 1 ? (spyIds[0] ?? null) : null,
  enabled: true,
  reason: null,
});

describe('dock model', () => {
  it("keeps V2's spoken Draw and Unspy names (pt / pts)", () => {
    expect([drawAriaLabel(1), drawAriaLabel(37)]).toEqual(['Draw, plus 1 pt', 'Draw, plus 37 pts']);
    expect(unspyAriaLabel({ kind: 'points', amount: 10 })).toBe('Unspy, minus 10 pts');
  });

  it('pulses at every own turn start and payout change, never off-turn', () => {
    const turn5 = drawPulseKey({ isMyTurn: true, turnSequence: 5, gain: 10 });
    expect(drawPulseKey({ isMyTurn: false, turnSequence: 5, gain: 10 })).toBeNull();
    expect(turn5).not.toBeNull();
    expect(drawPulseKey({ isMyTurn: true, turnSequence: 5, gain: 37 })).not.toBe(turn5);
    expect(drawPulseKey({ isMyTurn: true, turnSequence: 6, gain: 10 })).not.toBe(turn5);
    expect(drawPulseKey({ isMyTurn: true, turnSequence: 5, gain: 10 })).toBe(turn5);
  });

  it('resolves the spy pick: a living pick, else the single spy, else none', () => {
    expect(resolvedSpyId(control(['p1']), null)).toBe('p1');
    expect(resolvedSpyId(control(['p1', 'p2']), null)).toBeNull();
    expect(resolvedSpyId(control(['p1', 'p2']), 'p2')).toBe('p2');
    expect(resolvedSpyId(control(['p1']), 'p2')).toBe('p1');
  });

  it('has controls unless every entry is null (walk-in, Q38)', () => {
    expect(dockHasControls({ draw: null, shop: null, unspy: null, duplication: null, stats: false })).toBe(false);
    expect(dockHasControls({ draw: null, shop: { browseOnly: true }, unspy: null, duplication: null, stats: true })).toBe(true);
  });

  it('never has controls for a walk-in on the finished board (stats belongs to L74-24)', () => {
    expect(dockHasControls({ draw: null, shop: null, unspy: null, duplication: null, stats: true })).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

That is 5 tests.

Create `apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * Dock: technical spec v7 §6.1 item 5, §6.12, §11, §12.2; Appendix A `economy-bar`,
 * `unspy-dialog`, `private-zone-identity`; A.8 rulings 4 and 7 (L74-13). The ArenaScreenProps
 * spies stand in for the connection (`onClearSpy` → `connection.clearSpy`).
 */
import type { PlayingStateView, PublicPlayerView, SubChoiceRequiredPayload } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { act, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { visibleText } from '../../test/glyph-queries';
import { renderUi, type RenderUiOptions } from '../../test/render';
import { DUR_BASE_MS } from '../../ui/motion/motion-tokens';
import { DISABLED_REASON_COPY, type DisabledReason } from '../../view-model/action-reasons';
import { UNSPY_PICKER_HINT } from '../../view-model/table-copy';
import { makeCard, makeSelf } from '../../view-model/test-fixtures';
import { ArenaScreen, type ArenaScreenProps } from './arena-screen';
import { expectNoArenaIntent, makeArenaProps, makeArenaView } from './test-view';

const MIRROR: SubChoiceRequiredPayload = { kind: 'mirror', eligibleEffectIds: [], deadlineMs: 1 };
const SPYING = { spyingOnYou: true } as const;
function withSeat(view: PlayingStateView, playerId: string, patch: Partial<PublicPlayerView>): PlayingStateView {
  return { ...view, players: view.players.map((player) => (player.id === playerId ? { ...player, ...patch } : player)) };
}
const viewWith = (self: Parameters<typeof makeSelf>[0], overrides: Partial<PlayingStateView> = {}): PlayingStateView =>
  makeArenaView({ overrides: { self: makeSelf(self), ...overrides } });
function setup(view: PlayingStateView, overrides: Partial<ArenaScreenProps> = {}, options: RenderUiOptions = {}) {
  const spies = {
    onDraw: vi.fn<ArenaScreenProps['onDraw']>(),
    onClearSpy: vi.fn<ArenaScreenProps['onClearSpy']>(),
    onActivateDuplication: vi.fn<() => void>(),
  };
  const props = makeArenaProps({ view, ...spies, ...overrides });
  return { ...spies, props, ...renderUi(<ArenaScreen {...props} />, options) };
}
function dock(): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-arena-region="dock"]');
  if (element === null) throw new Error('no dock region');
  return element;
}
const draw = (): HTMLElement => within(dock()).getByRole('button', { name: /^Draw, plus / });
const unspy = (): HTMLElement => within(dock()).getByRole('button', { name: 'Unspy, minus 10 pts' });
const unspySheet = (): HTMLElement => screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Unspy' });
const isDisabled = (button: HTMLElement): boolean => button.hasAttribute('disabled') || button.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true';
function reasonOf(button: HTMLElement): string {
  const ids = button.getAttribute('aria-describedby') ?? '';
  return ids.split(' ').map((id) => document.getElementById(id)?.textContent ?? '').join(' ').trim();
}

describe('Draw (§6.1 item 5, L59-01, L64-05)', () => {
  it('shows +N with the point icon and no word, keeps V2 names and sends drawCard once', async () => {
    const { user, onDraw } = setup(makeArenaView());
    const button = draw();
    expect([button.getAttribute('aria-label'), button.getAttribute('title')]).toEqual(['Draw, plus 1 pt', 'Draw, plus 1 pt']);
    expect(visibleText(button).trim()).toBe('+1');
    expect(button.querySelector('[data-icon="point"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(button.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('draw');
    await user.click(button);
    expect(onDraw).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("shows a Gambler's rolled payout and turns red only above 10", () => {
    const rolled = withSeat(viewWith({ kitId: 'gambler' }), 'me', { drawGain: 37 });
    const { unmount } = setup(rolled);
    expect([draw().getAttribute('aria-label'), draw().getAttribute('data-variant')]).toEqual(['Draw, plus 37 pts', 'danger']);
    unmount();
    setup(viewWith({ kitId: 'gambler' }));
    expect([draw().getAttribute('aria-label'), draw().getAttribute('data-variant')]).toEqual(['Draw, plus 10 pts', 'primary']);
  });
});

describe('Unspy (L58-07, L58-09)', () => {
  it.each<{ label: string; view: () => PlayingStateView; subChoice: SubChoiceRequiredPayload | null; reason: DisabledReason }>([
    { label: 'no living spy', view: () => viewWith({ points: 20 }), subChoice: null, reason: 'no-living-spy' },
    { label: 'under 10 points', view: () => withSeat(viewWith({ points: 9 }), 'p1', SPYING), subChoice: null, reason: 'not-enough-points' },
    { label: 'off-turn', view: () => withSeat(viewWith({ points: 20 }, { currentTurnPlayerId: 'p1' }), 'p1', SPYING), subChoice: null, reason: 'not-your-turn' },
    { label: 'a sub-choice open', view: () => withSeat(viewWith({ points: 20 }), 'p1', SPYING), subChoice: MIRROR, reason: 'actions-locked' },
  ])('is rendered but disabled with its reason: $label', async ({ view, subChoice, reason }) => {
    const { user, props } = setup(view(), { subChoice });
    const button = unspy();
    expect(isDisabled(button)).toBe(true);
    expect(reasonOf(button)).toBe(DISABLED_REASON_COPY[reason]);
    await user.click(button);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Unspy' })).toBeNull();
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('preselects a single spy, and Confirm sends clearSpy once', async () => {
    const { user, onClearSpy, props } = setup(withSeat(viewWith({ points: 10 }), 'p1', SPYING));
    expect(visibleText(unspy()).trim()).toBe('−10');
    expect(unspy().querySelector('[data-icon="unspy"]')).not.toBeNull();
    await user.click(unspy());
    const dialog = unspySheet();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText(UNSPY_PICKER_HINT)).not.toBeNull();
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1' }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm' }));
    expect(onClearSpy.mock.calls).toEqual([['p1']]);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Unspy' })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("never lists an eliminated spectator's overlay", async () => {
    const view = withSeat(withSeat(makeArenaView({ tiers: { p2: 'eliminated' }, overrides: { self: makeSelf({ points: 10 }) } }), 'p1', SPYING), 'p2', SPYING);
    const { user, props } = setup(view);
    await user.click(unspy());
    const dialog = unspySheet();
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('button', { name: /^p2/ })).toBeNull();
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'p1' }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
  });

  it('with two spies Confirm waits for a pick, and Cancel resets the pick', async () => {
    const view = withSeat(withSeat(viewWith({ points: 10 }), 'p1', SPYING), 'p2', SPYING);
    const { user, onClearSpy, props } = setup(view);
    await user.click(unspy());
    expect(isDisabled(within(unspySheet()).getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm' }))).toBe(true);
    await user.click(within(unspySheet()).getByRole('button', { name: 'p2' }));
    await user.click(within(unspySheet()).getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    await user.click(unspy());
    expect(within(unspySheet()).getByRole('button', { name: 'p2' }).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false');
    await user.click(within(unspySheet()).getByRole('button', { name: 'p2' }));
    await user.click(within(unspySheet()).getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm' }));
    expect(onClearSpy.mock.calls).toEqual([['p2']]);
  });
});

describe('Activate duplication and the other POVs', () => {
  it('shows Activate duplication only for a Duplicator, and it sends once', async () => {
    const { user, onActivateDuplication, unmount } = setup(viewWith({ kitId: 'duplicator' }));
    await user.click(within(dock()).getByRole('button', { name: 'Activate duplication' }));
    expect(onActivateDuplication).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    unmount();
    setup(viewWith({ kitId: 'kamikaze' }));
    expect(within(dock()).queryByRole('button', { name: 'Activate duplication' })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keeps the Shop word label and anchor; it opens nothing until L74-14', () => {
    setup(makeArenaView());
    const shop = within(dock()).getByRole('button', { name: 'Shop' });
    expect(shop.getAttribute('data-hint-anchor')).toBe('shop');
    expect(isDisabled(shop)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('an eliminated Watching POV keeps a disabled dock without Unspy (ruling 7)', () => {
    setup(makeArenaView({ pov: 'eliminated' }));
    expect(isDisabled(draw())).toBe(true);
    expect(reasonOf(draw())).toBe(DISABLED_REASON_COPY['actions-locked']);
    expect(within(dock()).queryByRole('button', { name: /^Unspy/ })).toBeNull();
    expect(within(dock()).getByRole('button', { name: 'Shop' })).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('a walk-in gets no dock controls, while the region stays (Q38)', () => {
    setup(makeArenaView({ pov: 'walk-in' }));
    expect(within(dock()).queryAllByRole('button')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('ignores Draw for DUR_BASE_MS after targeting is cancelled (bar double tap)', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const view = viewWith({ points: 10, hand: [makeCard('spy', { instanceId: 'spy-1' })] });
    const { user, onDraw, props } = setup(view, {}, { advanceTimers: vi.advanceTimersByTime });
    await user.click(within(document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-arena-region="hand"]') ?? document.body).getByRole('button', { name: 'Spy' }));
    await user.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Spy' })).getByRole('button', { name: /^Use/ }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    await user.click(draw());
    expectNoArenaIntent(props);
    act(() => { vi.advanceTimersByTime(DUR_BASE_MS); });
    await user.click(draw());
    expect(onDraw).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

That is 14 tests: the Unspy `it.each` counts as 4.

Create `apps/client/src/net/use-room-connection.clear-spy.behavior.test.ts`. It follows the pattern L74-03's `use-room-connection.forfeit.behavior.test.ts` establishes: `renderHook` in a happy-dom `.test.ts`, with a `vi.hoisted` fake room inside `vi.mock('@colyseus/sdk')`. Its fake is its own, inline (joining goes through `Client.joinById`):

```ts
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
/**
 * clearSpy over a mocked room (L58-07): the behavior half of the `use-room-connection.clear-spy`
 * product-behavior row (docs/backlog_v7_test_classification.md, Owner lot 74; spec v7 §12.1).
 * The hook is unchanged (§4.1), so the old source-text test stays beside this one.
 */
import { CLEAR_SPY } from '@card-battle/shared';
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import '../test/render'; // act environment and per-test cleanup (L72-06)
import { useRoomConnection } from './use-room-connection';

const fake = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  room: {
    roomId: 'ABCDEF',
    reconnectionToken: 'token',
    reconnection: { enabled: false, maxRetries: 0, maxDelay: 0, minDelay: 0 },
    send: vi.fn<(type: string, payload?: unknown) => void>(),
    leave: vi.fn<(consented?: boolean) => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.resolve()),
    onMessage: vi.fn(),
    onError: vi.fn(),
    onDrop: vi.fn(),
    onReconnect: vi.fn(),
    onLeave: vi.fn(),
  },
}));

vi.mock('@colyseus/sdk', () => ({
  Client: class {
    joinById(): Promise<typeof fake.room> {
      return Promise.resolve(fake.room);
    }
  },
}));

describe('clearSpy intent (L58-07)', () => {
  it('sends CLEAR_SPY once with the picked seat over the joined room', async () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useRoomConnection());
    await act(async () => {
      await result.current.joinGame('abcdef', 'Ana');
    });
    expect(result.current.status).toBe('connected');
    act(() => {
      result.current.clearSpy('p2');
    });
    expect(fake.room.send.mock.calls.filter(([type]) => type === CLEAR_SPY)).toEqual([[CLEAR_SPY, { targetPlayerId: 'p2' }]]);
  });
});
```

'danger' : 'primary'} icon="point" aria-label={drawAriaLabel(draw.gain)} title={drawAriaLabel(draw.gain)} data-hint-anchor="draw" disabled={!draw.enabled} focusableWhenDisabled reserveReasonSlot {...reason} onClick={() => { commit({ kind: 'drawCard' }); }}>{`+${String(draw.gain)}`}</Button>`.

Then `useEffect(() => { if (pulseKey === null || reduced || scope.current === null) return; void animate(scope.current, { scale: [1, 1.12, 1] }, { duration: durationS('slow'), ease: EASE_ENTRANCE }); }, [pulseKey, reduced, animate, scope])`.
      - It animates in place, never by remounting with a `key`, so focus stays on Draw.

    - **Unspy** (when `model.unspy !== null`): `<Button variant="quiet" icon="unspy" data-unspy="" aria-label={unspyAriaLabel(unspy.cost)} title={…same} disabled={!unspy.enabled} focusableWhenDisabled reserveReasonSlot {...reason} onClick={() => { setUnspyOpen(true); }}><CostAmount amount={unspy.cost} signed="cost" title={null} /></Button>`.

      - `<Button variant="quiet" onClick={close}>Cancel</Button>`.
  - **`arena-screen.tsx`.**

```
## 2026-10-28 · [P] V7 dock: Draw pulse per own turn, duplication in the dock (L74-13)

Source: technical spec v7 §6.1 item 5, §11; Appendix A `economy-bar`, `private-zone-identity`;
L59-01, L64-05.

- **Draw pulses at every own turn start and payout change.** The pulse key is
  `{turnSequence}:{gain}` on the POV's turn. V2 pulsed only when the turn came back from another
  seat, so the second turn of a Block chain did not pulse. The arena animates the button in
  place, so keyboard focus stays on Draw; no pulse under reduced motion.
- **Activate duplication lives in the dock** (§6.1 item 5, `DockModel.duplication`), not in the
  private zone as in V2. The self strip keeps the 'Duplication active' text.
- **Spoken names stay V2's** (A.8 ruling 4): `Draw, plus 1 pt`, `Unspy, minus 10 pts`.

---
```

    - Change `| **Total** | **134** | **46** |` to `| **Total** | **134** | **47** |`, or add one to the value it holds.
  - In `docs/backlog_v7_test_classification.md`, Owner lot 74, `net/use-room-connection.clear-spy.test.ts` row: at the end of its "Replacement / superseded by" cell, append ` L74-13 wrote both: (1) \`apps/client/src/net/use-room-connection.clear-spy.behavior.test.ts\` ('sends CLEAR_SPY once with the picked seat over the joined room'); (2) \`apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock.test.tsx\` ('is rendered but disabled with its reason: $label', 'preselects a single spy, and Confirm sends clearSpy once'). The source-text test stays: the hook does not change.`

- [ ] **Step 6: Verify and commit.**
  1. Run `pnpm verify` and wait for it to pass.
  2. Stage:

     ```
     git add apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock-model.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock-model.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/unspy-sheet.tsx apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock.test.tsx apps/client/src/net/use-room-connection.clear-spy.behavior.test.ts apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-screen.tsx docs/agent/frontend.md docs/agent/decisions.md docs/backlog_v7.md docs/backlog_v7_test_classification.md
     ```
  3. Commit:

```
feat(arena): add the economy dock (L74-13)

Draw, Shop entry, Unspy picker and Activate duplication from the
self-strip dock model. Adds the hook behavior test for the L74-owned
clear-spy source-text row (spec v7 §12.1).
```

**Acceptance map:**
- Draw shows `+N` with no word: `shows +N with the point icon and no word, keeps V2 names and sends drawCard once`.
- Draw is red above 10 for a Gambler: `shows a Gambler's rolled payout and turns red only above 10`.
- Draw sends `drawCard` once: the first Draw test.
- Unspy is disabled with each of its reasons: `is rendered but disabled with its reason: $label` (no living spy, under 10 points, off-turn, a sub-choice open).
- A single spy is preselected and Confirm sends `clearSpy`: `preselects a single spy, and Confirm sends clearSpy once`.
- An eliminated spectator's overlay is never listed: `never lists an eliminated spectator's overlay`.
- Activate duplication appears only for a Duplicator and sends once: `shows Activate duplication only for a Duplicator, and it sends once`.
- Ruling 7 and Q38: `an eliminated Watching POV keeps a disabled dock without Unspy (ruling 7)` and `a walk-in gets no dock controls, while the region stays (Q38)`.
- Cancel resets the pick: `with two spies Confirm waits for a pick, and Cancel resets the pick`.
- The pulse at turn start and on payout change: `pulses at every own turn start and payout change, never off-turn` (pure; the animation itself is checked in the L74-26 browser gate).
- The bar double tap carry-over: `ignores Draw for DUR_BASE_MS after targeting is cancelled (bar double tap)`.
- The classification row (hook half): `sends CLEAR_SPY once with the picked seat over the joined room`.
- `pnpm verify` green: Step 6.

**Decisions:**
- **Draw is `variant="primary"` (`--cta`) or `danger` when `hot`,** with `icon="point"` in Button's well and the visible text `+N`.
  - The spoken name is V2's `Draw, plus N pt(s)`; ruling 4 overrides the brief's "points".
  - `data-hint-anchor="draw"` sits on the button.
- **Pulse:** see the decisions.md entry.
  - It uses `durationS('slow')` (400 ms, V2 used 0.45 s) and `EASE_ENTRANCE` (V2's `MOTION_EASE` curve).
- **Shop in Part 1:** the button renders with its word label, `icon="shop"` and anchor, but it is disabled until L74-14 passes `onOpenShop`.
  - The anchor exists for the hint and tour code.
  - This state never ships: Part 2 lands before the PR.
- **Unspy is `variant="quiet"`** (Shop already uses `--cta-2`). It shows the crossed-eye `unspy` icon plus `CostAmount` `−10`, and keeps V2's `data-unspy` hook.
- **Activate duplication is `variant="quiet"`** with the `duplication-active` icon and V2's word label.
  - The dock row may wrap for a Duplicator; the L74-26 gate checks it at 360 px.
- **Every dock button uses `reserveReasonSlot`,** so the row height does not jump when a reason appears.
- **The Unspy sheet** is a read-only bottom Sheet titled 'Unspy' with V2's hint, `data-unspy-picker` and `SeatChoiceButton` options. It has no kit portrait and no hidden information.
  - Confirm is disabled without a pick, and shows the control's reason when Unspy itself became disabled while the sheet is open.
  - The sheet closes if Unspy disappears (the POV was eliminated).
- **A walk-in gets L74-01's empty dock region** (no controls, Q38). The finished board shows only Shop in Part 1; Stats is L74-24.
- **Dock sends use the tap-guarded commit** (L74-10 carry-over).
- **The hook test uses L74-03's pattern** (controller ruling): `renderHook` in a happy-dom `.test.ts` named `*.behavior.test.ts`, with its own `vi.hoisted` fake room inside `vi.mock('@colyseus/sdk')`. The name shares no base name with an existing `.ts` / `.tsx` test.
- **New strings:** none. Reused: 'Shop', 'Unspy', 'Confirm', 'Cancel', 'Activate duplication', `UNSPY_PICKER_HINT`, V2's spoken labels.

## Part 2 — Task briefs L74-14 … L74-26 (expanded after Part 1)

These briefs are expanded into TDD steps, in the format of Part 1, once L74-01 … L74-13 are committed on this branch, against the real interfaces. Appendix C carries the Part 1 notes each of these tasks must honor. A **Review** line overrides its brief.

### L74-14 — Shop sheet

**Cx / risk:** L / Medium · **Depends on:** L74-13, L74-02 · **Spec:** §6.8, §6.6.4, §13
**Parity (Appendix A):** `shop-dialog`, `table-shop-double-click-buy`, `economy-bar`
**Files:** `apps/client/src/screens/arena/shop-sheet.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/shop-sheet.test.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock.tsx`, `apps/client/src/view-model/`, `apps/client/src/screens/table/shop-dialog.tsx`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`

Build a full-height shop sheet (§6.8) from `shop(view)`, opened by a dock Shop button (`--cta-2`, word label). Tabs are Cards / Special / Pool / Upgrade points, with large tiles. It opens off-turn and on the finished board with actions disabled, since the pool is public. A tap selects a tile (`aria-pressed`) and the footer **Buy** commits. A double-tap buys at once (`touch-action: manipulation`, same guards as Buy). Long-press or the tile's info button opens the read-only card sheet. Prices: catalog costs (Spy play 2 / buy 4) and `alwaysUpgraded` faces. Upgrade-point Buy and Sell come from `upgradePointBuyCost` / `upgradePointSellYield` at render time. Buy special costs 20. Pool 'Buy random' uses the public `poolBuyCost` and has its empty copy. Every successful buy closes the sheet. Add one client constant for the special price (value 20) and import it in `shop()`, the token-chip module and the old `shop-dialog.tsx`, replacing both client copies. The server `SPECIAL_CARD_PURCHASE_COST` is untouched (Q50). Update the frontend.md Arena section.

**Acceptance:** Tests cover these cases. A single tap only selects, and Buy sends `buyCard {cardId}`. A double-tap sends one `buyCard` and closes. Off-turn every tile is disabled but the tabs stay browsable. An unaffordable tile's aria-label ends with ', cannot afford'. The Spy tile shows 2/4. Upgrade-point Buy and Sell follow the kit. Pool Buy random is disabled when the pool is empty. The client has exactly one special-price constant. The existing shop source-text tests stay green. `pnpm verify` green.

**Review:** Consumes `shop()`; does not edit `shop-dialog.tsx` or `view-model/`.

The What's new item and its pinned tests are updated. A browser check shows chips, ghosts and banners on 4- and 8-seat arenas. `pnpm verify` green.

### L74-18 — Sub-choice host, Mirror and elimination reward

**Cx / risk:** L / High · **Depends on:** L74-06, L72-09, L74-08 · **Spec:** §6.7, §13, §14.2
**Parity (Appendix A):** `table-subchoice-host`, `table-sub-choice-non-dismissible`, `table-sub-choice-timer-scale`, `table-subchoice-mirror`, `table-subchoice-reward`, `table-subchoice-wait-turn-strip`, `table-subchoice-wait-kill-rewards`, `table-subchoice-wait-active-seat-picks`, `table-subchoice-gate-reject-copy`
**Files:** `apps/client/src/screens/arena/sub-choice/sub-choice-sheet.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/sub-choice/mirror-choice.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/sub-choice/reward-choice.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/sub-choice/sub-choice-sheet.test.tsx`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`

Show sub-choices as blocking sheets on the stack (§6.7), with large tiles. The host gives each kind its title, its expiry hint, and a countdown with a 'Sub-choice timer' progressbar (40 s, `CLIENT_SUB_CHOICE_MS`). At 0 s it shows 'Time's up' and the controls stay live. The sheet closes only when `subChoice` clears or a resend replaces it, and a resend reopens it. Minimize turns it into a bar while intents stay locked (Q59). Every kind here is select then Confirm. **Mirror**: eligible pending attacks as tiles (damage × multiplier, '{source} → you'), and a new target from the living opponents, Invisible seats included. Both are preselected, and Confirm redirect sends `{kind:'mirror', pendingEffectId, newTargetPlayerId}`. Copy: 'Choose which pending attack to redirect and its new target.'. **Elimination reward** covers kills, lethal contributors, forfeits and simultaneous Lot 71 pickers. It shows two choice radiogroups (4 lives / 8 points / 1 upgrade point / A card, defaulting to lives and points) and a card grid when 'A card' is chosen. The client blocks the same card twice and greys cards that a refreshed prompt no longer offers (Q55). Confirm rewards sends `{kind:'elimination-reward', eliminationId, choices}`, and the sheet keeps `data-hint-anchor="reward"`. While any seat resolves, chat, the flag, How to play, Feedback and read-only sheets stay usable, and other seats see no 'choosing…' indicator (§14.2). The reward celebration is L78. Update the frontend.md Arena section.

**Acceptance:** Tests cover these cases. Esc and backdrop never close or resolve the sheet. Minimize keeps intents locked and keeps the bar above a later read-only sheet. At 0 s 'Time's up' shows and Confirm still sends. Clearing `subChoice` closes the sheet, and a resend reopens it. The Mirror payload is correct. A reward with two different cards sends both; the same card twice is blocked. A card missing from a refreshed prompt is greyed. `pnpm verify` green.

**Review:** Every sub-choice intent goes through `arena-commit.ts`.

### L74-21 — Turn clock and lobby-rules cache

**Cx / risk:** M / Medium · **Depends on:** L74-03 · **Spec:** §4.1, §6.10, §13, §14.3
**Parity (Appendix A):** `table-turn-timer-scale`, `turn-strip-timers`, `rules-turn-time-default-override`
**Files:** `apps/client/src/App.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/turn-clock.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/turn-clock.test.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/top-bar.tsx`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`

`App.tsx` keeps the last `LobbyStateView.lobbyRules` seen for the current room in memory: online and solo, from before `START_GAME`, cleared on leave or room change. It passes them, and `lastTurnStarted`, to the arena. The arena records the receive time and the view's `turnSequence` whenever `lastTurnStarted` changes identity (§4.1). The top-bar clock renders `turnClock()` (L73) in four modes. **Countdown**: a proportional bar scaled to the cached `turnTimeSeconds`; else to the first `TURN_STARTED` length for that `turnSequence` (a resume keeps the original length); else seconds only with no bar. The last 10 s show in `--danger`. **Paused**: 'Paused — reconnecting'. **Absent**: 'Absent — auto-draw'. **None**: '—' for the tutorial, the finished board, bot turns and a null deadline. Keep `data-zone` timers / timer-label, and `role="progressbar"` 'Turn timer' with `aria-valuenow` only when the ratio is known. Under reduced motion the bar jumps instead of animating. Display stays cosmetic and the server deadline rules (L12-07). Check the turn-length-on-reconnect watch point in the gate (§14.3). Update the frontend.md Arena section.

**Acceptance:** Tests cover these cases. A 180 s host turn shows a full bar at 180 s and a half bar at 90 s. A 5 s turn starts full. A resume with the same `turnSequence` keeps the original length. The paused, absent and none labels render. Seconds-only mode has no `aria-valuenow`. The rules cache survives lobby → playing and clears on leave. `pnpm verify` green.

**Review:** Owns the App-level cache of the last `LobbyStateView.lobbyRules` per room and the `lastTurnStarted` receive-time capture; pairs a payload with the view `turnSequence` at arrival (watch point if TURN_STARTED precedes its stateUpdate).

**Cx / risk:** M / Medium · **Depends on:** L74-14, L74-16, L74-17, L74-23 · **Spec:** §4.4, §6.12
**Parity (Appendix A):** `finished-board-readonly`, `end-finished-board-shell`, `end-finished-chat-readonly`, `end-chat-frozen-snapshot`, `table-leave-forfeit`
**Files:** `apps/client/src/screens/end.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-screen.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/dock.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/finished-board.test.tsx`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`

`EndScreen` renders the arena for `finalTable` when the switch is on, never in tutorial (§4.4, §6.12). Every intent is locked and the clock shows '—'. The dock is replaced by **Stats**, which reopens Game over and does nothing while Feedback is open. The flag becomes 'Return home?' with Stay, going through `requestLeave` (ask-once, L57-03). `!` opens EndScreen's manual Feedback only while stats are closed. The Shop is browse-only; inspect, log and read-only chat stay. On first paint a POV win shows 'You won!', and a POV elimination shows the POV elimination overlay in place of 'You are dead', both before Game over opens (~1.6 s). Past elimination beats do not replay (L74-18). The Game over dialog itself is unchanged until L77. Update the frontend.md Arena section.

**Acceptance:** Tests cover these cases. Stats reopens the stats and does nothing while Feedback is open. Confirming the flag's Return home calls `requestLeave`. Every intent callback is a no-op. No chat composer renders. A POV elimination shows the overlay, not the banner. The `end.tsx` and `feedback-surfaces` source-text tests stay green, or are classified per §12.1. `pnpm verify` green.

_Draft id L74-25._

### L74-25 — Reconnect banner and reject toast

**Cx / risk:** M / Medium · **Depends on:** L74-08, L74-17 · **Spec:** §6.6.2, §6.12, §8.3
**Parity (Appendix A):** `illegal-action-dialog`, `reject-state-carryover`, `global-room-connection-lifecycle`, `global-page-reload-recovery`, `table-subchoice-gate-reject-copy`
**Files:** `apps/client/src/screens/arena/reconnect-banner.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/reject-toast.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/reject-toast.test.tsx`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/arena-commit.ts`, `apps/client/src/App.tsx`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`

Show a non-blocking top banner while the connection status is reconnecting. The arena receives the raw `status`, and every input stays disabled until the connection is back (§6.12). Server rejects show a toast with the `ILLEGAL_ACTION_COPY` title and body (the wire message is the fallback) instead of the modal. Any dismissal, manual or by the L72 `Toast` timeout, calls `clearActionReject`. A state update never dismisses it, and it unmounts when cleared (L39-06). When the rejected intent was a card commit from the helper, the card springs back to its hand slot and shakes; under reduced motion it gets a color flash only (§6.6.2, §8.3). Update the frontend.md Arena section.

**Acceptance:** Tests cover these cases. Reconnecting renders the banner and disables Draw and Use. A `stateUpdate` with an open reject keeps the toast. Manual dismissal and timed dismissal each call `clearActionReject` once. The exhaustive `ILLEGAL_ACTION_COPY` test still passes. A rejected Spy play marks its hand card for spring-back, and reduced motion uses no shake. `pnpm verify` green.

### L74-26 — Lot 74 browser, parity and design gate

**Cx / risk:** L / Medium · **Depends on:** L74-01…L74-25 · **Spec:** §4.6, §10.6, §12.3, §12.4, §12.5
**Parity (Appendix A):** `table-shell-layout`, `turn-strip-timers`, `opponent-seat`, `private-zone-identity`, `card-band`, `card-actions-dialog`, `card-target-picker`, `incoming-pending`, `economy-bar`, `shop-dialog`, `table-subchoice-host`, `table-action-log-panel`, `table-chat`, `table-turn-timer-scale`, `walk-in-spectator-mode`, `finished-board-readonly`, `table-fx-layer`, `table-banners`, `illegal-action-dialog`
**Files:** `docs/agent/frontend.md`, `docs/backlog_v7.md`, `apps/client/src/screens/arena/`

Run the post-lot browser gate on the `?ui=next` arena (§12.3), leaving L75 surfaces out. Viewports: 360×740, 375×812 and 390×844, plus a landscape phone and 1440×900 to check only no page scroll and no crash before L75. Tables of 2, 4 and 8 seats. Scenarios: incoming attack, mutual cancel, Mirror, elimination with simultaneous reward pickers, slot-drop in both cases, base Spy and Spy+, walk-in spectator and claim, reconnect, house-rules recall with a non-60 s turn, chat side stream, tap-tap and drag commits, and the reject toast. Run the Appendix A parity sweep over every surface this lot owns. Run the Impeccable detector and `critique` on the arena files (§12.4). Fix and re-verify every defect. Record room codes under 'Lot 74 verified' in frontend.md. Open the single ready-for-review PR into `dev`, listing the new strings (§10.6) and the §11 defaults shipped (§4.6).

**Acceptance:** frontend.md has a 'Lot 74 verified' entry with room codes and the scenario list. The PR carries the parity sweep notes, the new-string list and the design-gate result, or 'design gate not run' for sessions without Impeccable. Every defect found is fixed. `pnpm verify` green.

## Appendix A — Interface contract, Part 1 (L74-01 … L74-13)

Every Part 1 task builds against these names and types. A task may add private helpers, but it never
renames or reshapes anything below. Part 2 (L74-14 … L74-26) extends these modules; it never breaks them.

Sources: the code on `claude/v7-lot-74` at `7407fa3`, `docs/technical_spec_v7.md` §6, the L73
view-model (`apps/client/src/view-model/`), the L72 `ui/` layer and the Lot 74 briefs. The shapes
below are final: the controller's consistency rulings of 2026-10-28 are folded in.

### A.1 Files (all under `apps/client/src/screens/arena/` unless a path says otherwise)

| File | Owner | Kind | Role |
|---|---|---|---|
| `arena-screen.tsx` | L74-01, extended by every Part 1 task | component | `ArenaScreen`: computes the view-model once per render, composes the six regions, hosts the read-only sheets (L74-05) |
| `arena-screen.test.tsx` | L74-01 (replaces `arena-screen.test.ts`, deleted first); L74-04 adds one test | test | six regions with empty states, 2- and 8-seat views; seats in the arena |
| `arena-regions.ts` | L74-01 | constants | `ARENA_REGIONS`, `ArenaRegion`, `arenaRegionAttributes` |
| `test-view.ts` + `test-view.test.ts` | L74-01 | test helper | `makeArenaView`, `makeArenaProps`, `arenaSpies`, `expectNoArenaIntent`, `ARENA_INTENT_PROPS`, `ARENA_SPIED_RESOURCES`, `ARENA_SPY_PLUS_KIT` (A.4) |
| `arena-sheets.ts` + `.test.ts` | L74-02 (L74-05 adds `seat` and `openSeatId`, L74-10 adds `targeting`, `startTargeting`, `endTargeting`) | pure reducer | which read-only sheets are open or closing, and targeting |
| `card-sheet.tsx` + `card-sheet.test.tsx` | L74-02 (read-only), L74-08 (actions, `commit`, `onBeginTargeting`), L74-09 (in-play Deactivate + confirm), L74-10 (pre-send choices, `collapsed` bar), L74-11 (Multi-attack step) | component | the card sheet |
| `card-sheet-source.ts` + `.test.ts` | L74-02 (L74-08 adds `liveCardRefFor`) | pure | `CardSheetSource` → `CardRef`, face, title, counter, context lines |
| `card-sheet-actions.ts` | L74-08 (L74-09 adds two labels) | pure | action labels, the Upgrade cost, the sell yield |
| `kit-sheet.tsx` + `.test.tsx` | L74-02 | component | the kit sheet |
| `../../ui/motion/use-long-press.ts` + `.test.tsx` | L74-02 | hook (ui/) | long-press (450 ms, > 8 px cancels) |
| `top-bar.tsx` + `top-bar.test.tsx`, `top-bar-model.ts` + `.test.ts`, `leave-confirm-sheet.tsx` | L74-03 | component + pure | round, turn banner, Block line, `!`, flag, menu |
| `../../net/use-room-connection.forfeit.behavior.test.ts` | L74-03 | test | FORFEIT hook behavior (classification row) |

| `seat-sheet.tsx` + `seat-sheet.test.tsx`, `threat-copy.ts` + `.test.ts` | L74-05 (L74-06 adds two copy constants) | component + pure | the seat sheet; V2 pending-effect words |
| `threat-layer.tsx`, `central-plate.tsx`, `threat-layer.test.tsx`, `threat-geometry.ts` + `.test.ts`, `plate-model.ts` + `.test.ts` | L74-06 | component + pure | arrows, bursts, plate badges, landing card, recurring chips |

| `multi-attack.tsx` + `multi-attack.test.tsx`, `multi-attack-selection.ts` + `.test.ts`, `seat-choice-button.tsx` | L74-11 (L74-13 reuses `SeatChoiceButton`) | component + pure | Assassin rows |
| `hand-drag.ts` + `hand-drag.test.ts`, `use-hand-drag.ts`, `hand-drag-layer.tsx` + `hand-drag-layer.test.tsx` | L74-12 | pure + hook + component | drag gesture and drop dispatch |

`.tsx` files export components only; constants, types and pure helpers go in sibling `.ts` files
(react-refresh rule, `map-ui.md` §0).

### A.2 Props and the arena context

```ts
// arena-screen.tsx — unchanged alias in Part 1. Part 2 (L74-21, L74-23, L74-25) widens it.
export type ArenaScreenProps = TableScreenProps;
export function ArenaScreen(props: ArenaScreenProps): ReactElement;
```

`ArenaScreen` computes, once per render and in this order:

```ts
const readOnly = props.readOnly ?? false;
const ctx: ActionContext = { subChoice: props.subChoice, readOnly };   // view-model/action-lock
const lock = actionLock(props.view, ctx);                              // LockReason | null; root `data-action-lock`
```

Later tasks add, each once per render: `layoutBox = useArenaLayoutBox(arenaRef)`, `seatsModel =
seats(view, layoutBox)` (L74-04); the sheets state, `openSheet` / `closeSheet`,
`threatModel = threats(view, subChoice)` and `tappedSeatId = openSeatId(sheets)` (L74-05); `commit = createArenaCommit(props)` (L74-08);
`strip = selfStrip(view, ctx)` (L74-09; L74-13 reads `strip.dock` and `strip.isActive`);
`tapGuard = useTapGuard()` and `live = liveTargeting(view, ctx, sheets)` (L74-10);
`handDrag = useHandDrag(…)` (L74-12).

ArenaScreen passes models and callbacks **down as props**. There is no React context for game data
in Part 1: every component receives the view-model output it renders plus the callbacks it may call.
Components never call `actionLock` themselves; they read `enabled` / `reason` from the models.

### A.3 Regions (L74-01)

```ts
// arena-regions.ts
export const ARENA_REGIONS = ['top-bar', 'ticker', 'arena', 'self-strip', 'hand', 'dock'] as const;
export type ArenaRegion = (typeof ARENA_REGIONS)[number];
/** Spread on a region root: a misspelt region fails typecheck. */
export function arenaRegionAttributes(region: ArenaRegion): { readonly 'data-arena-region': ArenaRegion };
```

DOM contract (top to bottom, §6.1). Every region always renders, with its empty state:

| Region | Element attributes | FX / hint anchors it must keep |
|---|---|---|
| root | `<main data-zone="table" data-arena="next" data-action-lock>` (`none` \| `not-your-turn` \| `actions-locked`), full-bleed `h-full w-full`, `overflow-hidden`, `.v7-ground` | none |
| `top-bar` | `data-arena-region="top-bar"` | `data-zone="timers"` (clock slot, rendered by `TopBar` from L74-03, filled by L74-21), `data-hint-anchor="your-turn"` on the clock slot |
| `ticker` | `data-arena-region="ticker"` | `data-zone="log-collapsed"` (a `<p>` in Part 1; L74-15 turns it into the ticker button) |
| `arena` | `data-arena-region="arena"` `data-zone="felt"`; a ref-able `relative min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden` element with **no padding** (L74-04 measures it and positions seats, arrows and the plate in its box) | `data-zone="opponents"` (seat container, `ArenaSeats` root from L74-04), `data-zone="pending"` + `data-hint-anchor="incoming"` (central plate, `CentralPlate` root from L74-06) |
| `self-strip` | `data-arena-region="self-strip"` `data-zone="private-zone"` `data-player-id={view.you}` | inner `data-zone="resources"` holding the four `data-resource-kind` values, `data-hint-anchor="resources"`; a `data-zone="kit-portrait"` descendant (from `KitPortrait`) |
| `hand` | `data-arena-region="hand"` `data-zone="card-band"` | `data-zone="hand"` + `data-hint-anchor="hand"`, `data-zone="specials"` + `data-hint-anchor="specials"`, `data-instance-id` on every hand card (the `Card` root emits it) |
| `dock` | `data-arena-region="dock"` `data-zone="dock"` | `data-hint-anchor="draw"` on Draw, `data-hint-anchor="shop"` on Shop (both from L74-13) |

- L74-01's region elements stay in `ArenaScreen` with their attributes; later components render
  **inside** them and keep every anchor.
- L74-01's region tests assert anchors (`data-arena-region`, `data-zone`, `data-hint-anchor`), never
  placeholder text a later task replaces. The hand placeholders are V2's `Hand` / `Empty` and
  `Specials` / `None`, the words `HandFan` (L74-07) keeps.
- The read-only sheets render as the last children of the arena root, never inside a clickable
  element (React portals bubble clicks; A.5).

Never use `data-card-row` on V7 markup: `index.css:150-154` forces `flex-wrap: nowrap !important` on it.
`[data-zone="card-band"]` gets `overflow: hidden` in landscape (`index.css:203-209`); the hand scroller is
a child of it, so that rule does not stop the hand's own horizontal scroll.

A walk-in has no seat: its `self-strip` region renders the Watching strip (L74-23) or, in Part 1, the
empty state, and its `private-zone` carries `data-player-id={view.you}` all the same.

### A.4 Test helpers (L74-01)

```ts
// test-view.ts — imported by tests only.
export type ArenaSeatTier = 'unspied' | 'base-spy' | 'spy-plus' | 'eliminated';
export type ArenaPov = 'seated' | 'walk-in' | 'eliminated' | 'reanimating';

export interface ArenaViewOptions {
  /** Total seats including the POV (2–8). Seat ids: 'me', then 'p1'…'p7'. Default 3. */
  seatCount?: number;
  /** Tier per opponent id; unlisted opponents are unspied. */
  tiers?: Readonly<Record<string, ArenaSeatTier>>;
  /** Default 'seated'. 'walk-in' makes every seat a non-POV seat (you = 'watcher'). */
  pov?: ArenaPov;
  /** Spread last (after the tiers and the POV are applied). */
  overrides?: Partial<PlayingStateView>;
}
/** Live resources every Spy relation sends (§6.3): base Spy and Spy+ seats carry exactly these. */
export const ARENA_SPIED_RESOURCES: { readonly lives: 7; readonly points: 12; readonly upgradePoints: 1; readonly shield: 2 };
/** The Spy+ kit. */
export const ARENA_SPY_PLUS_KIT: KitId; // 'tactician'
/** The 17 intent callbacks of `ArenaScreenProps`, `as const satisfies readonly (keyof ArenaScreenProps)[]`. */
export const ARENA_INTENT_PROPS: readonly [
  'onDraw', 'onPlayCard', 'onPlayMultipleAttacks', 'onResolveSubChoice', 'onBuyCard', 'onSellCard',
  'onUpgradeCard', 'onBuyUpgradePoint', 'onBuySpecialCard', 'onBuyPoolCard', 'onClearSpy',
  'onSellUpgradePoint', 'onLeave', 'onForfeit', 'onSendChat', 'onDeactivatePersistent', 'onActivateDuplication',
];
export function makeArenaView(options?: ArenaViewOptions): PlayingStateView;

/** Every `ArenaScreenProps` callback is a `vi.fn()`; `view` defaults to `makeArenaView()`. */
export function makeArenaProps(overrides?: Partial<ArenaScreenProps>): ArenaScreenProps;
/** The `vi.fn()` behind each `ARENA_INTENT_PROPS` callback of `props`. */
export function arenaSpies(props: ArenaScreenProps): readonly MockInstance[];
/** The commit rule (§6.6.4): asserts that every intent spy of `props` has zero calls. */
export function expectNoArenaIntent(props: ArenaScreenProps): void;
```

`makeArenaView` builds on `view-model/test-fixtures.ts` (`makeTableView`, `makePlayer`, `makeSelf`,
`makeCard`, `makeEliminationReveal`). Tier fields are exactly the §6.3 wire fields: base Spy = `spied`
with live `lives`/`points`/`upgradePoints`/`shield` and no `kitId`; Spy+ = `spied` with `kitId`, `hand`,
`specialCards`; eliminated = `isEliminated: true` + `eliminationReveal`. A spy+ seat gets kit
`'tactician'`, a hand of one `basic-attack` (`<id>-basic-attack`) and one `spy` (`<id>-spy`), and no
specials.

`expectNoArenaIntent` is the only "nothing sent" helper. In every test that renders `ArenaScreen`,
each "nothing was sent" assertion is `expectNoArenaIntent(props)` (the test's `setup` returns
`props`); an "exactly one intent" assertion keeps checking that spy's call count and arguments.
Below `ArenaScreen`, tests assert on the `commit` / callback `vi.fn()` they pass.

### A.5 Sheets (L74-02, L74-05, L74-10)

```ts
// card-sheet-source.ts (L74-02)
export type CardSheetSource =
  | { kind: 'hand'; instance: CardInstance }
  | { kind: 'special'; instance: CardInstance }
  /** An own or an opponent in-play card: Shield, active persistent, ticking Sentence (L73-07). */
  | { kind: 'in-play'; thumb: InPlayThumb }
  /** A Spy+ or death-frozen hand / special of another seat (§6.3). */
  | { kind: 'spy-reveal'; instance: CardInstance; ownerPlayerId: string }
  /** A plate badge or arrow label: catalog-only face, no footer (L56-05). */
  | { kind: 'queue'; effectId: string; cardId: CardId; isUpgraded: boolean }
  /** A log card: catalog-only face, no footer (L56-05). Opened by the plate's landing card (L74-06) and the log (L74-15). */
  | { kind: 'log'; cardId: CardId; isUpgraded: boolean };
// Part 2 adds 'pool', 'shop' and 'reward'. Every switch on `kind` ends in a `never` default, so Part 2
// gets a type error, not a silent fallthrough.

/** hand → zone 'hand', special → 'special', in-play → thumb.ref, others → 'read-only'. */
export function cardRefFor(source: CardSheetSource): CardRef;
/** The face at `sheet` size: queue `{ instanceId: `queue-${effectId}`, cardId, isUpgraded }`; log `log-${cardId}-${'up'|'base'}`. */
export function sheetInstanceFor(source: CardSheetSource): CardInstance;
// L74-02 also exports cardSheetTitle, cardSheetCounter, cardSheetContext, liveCardSheetSource (in-play
// re-read) and the V2 labels ACTIVE_LABEL, CARD_LIVES_LABEL, TURNS_LEFT_LABEL, SPY_REVEAL_LABEL.

// L74-08 adds:
/**
 * The ref `cardActions` reads against the latest view (Review Focus 1): a held hand or special card
 * takes its live instance; one that left `view.self` becomes `{ zone: 'read-only', instance }`;
 * every other source is `cardRefFor(source)`. The single "is this card still where the sheet
 * thinks" helper: L74-10 (targeting) and L74-12 (drag) use it too.
 */
export function liveCardRefFor(source: CardSheetSource, view: PlayingStateView): CardRef;

// arena-sheets.ts
export type ArenaSheet =
  | { kind: 'card'; source: CardSheetSource }
  | { kind: 'kit'; kitId: KitId }
  | { kind: 'seat'; playerId: string };          // L74-05

export interface ArenaSheetsState {
  /** Open read-only sheets, oldest first. The `Sheet` primitive paints the newest two (MAX_READ_ONLY_DEPTH). */
  readonly open: readonly ArenaSheet[];
  /** The last closed sheets, mounted with `open={false}` through their exit, so the Sheet press shield absorbs a double tap. */
  readonly closing: readonly ArenaSheet[];
  /** L74-10: the card being targeted, or null. While set, that card sheet is `collapsed` into the bar. */
  readonly targeting: TargetingState | null;
}
/** L74-02: `{ open: [], closing: [] }`; L74-10 adds `targeting: null`. */
export const EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS: ArenaSheetsState;
export const MAX_CLOSING_SHEETS = 2;
/** Opening a sheet already open (same key) moves it to the top and out of `closing`. L74-10: opening any sheet ends targeting first. */
export function openArenaSheet(state: ArenaSheetsState, sheet: ArenaSheet): ArenaSheetsState;
/** Moves an open sheet to `closing` (keeping the last MAX_CLOSING_SHEETS). L74-10: closing the targeted sheet clears targeting. */
export function closeArenaSheet(state: ArenaSheetsState, sheet: ArenaSheet): ArenaSheetsState;
/** `card:<sheetInstanceFor(source).instanceId>` (queue: `card:queue-<effectId>`, log: `card:log-<cardId>-<up|base>`), `kit:<kitId>`, `seat:<playerId>`. React key and `data-arena-sheet`. */
export function arenaSheetKey(sheet: ArenaSheet): string;
export interface ArenaSheetEntry { readonly sheet: ArenaSheet; readonly key: string; readonly open: boolean }
/** `closing` entries (open: false), then `open` entries (open: true). */
export function arenaSheetEntries(state: ArenaSheetsState): readonly ArenaSheetEntry[];
/** L74-05: the player of the newest open seat sheet, else null (the "tapped" seat, §6.5). */
export function openSeatId(state: ArenaSheetsState): string | null;

// L74-10 adds:
export interface TargetingState {
  /** The card sheet that collapsed into the bar. */
  readonly sheetKey: string;
  readonly instanceId: string;
  /** From `cardActions(...).use` routed `target`. */
  readonly seatIds: readonly string[];
}
/** Keeps only the targeted card sheet open (the others move to `closing`) and records `targeting`. */
export function startTargeting(state: ArenaSheetsState, targeting: TargetingState): ArenaSheetsState;
/** Closes the targeted sheet as `closeArenaSheet` does and clears `targeting`. */
export function endTargeting(state: ArenaSheetsState): ArenaSheetsState;
```

Sheet components take `open: boolean` (false while the sheet plays its exit from `closing`):
`CardSheet { open, source, view, ctx, onClose }` (L74-02; L74-08 adds `commit?` and
`onBeginTargeting?`, L74-10 adds `collapsed?` and `collapsedBar?`), `KitSheet { open, kitId, onClose }`
(L74-02), `SeatSheet { open, seat, view, threatModel, onClose, onOpenKit, onOpenCard }` (L74-05).
`CardSheet` has no `onOpenKit`: kit sheets open from `KitPortrait` buttons (the L74-05 seat sheet and
the L74-09 self strip).

**The sheet host (L74-05).** L74-05 is the first task with a real opener (a seat tap opens the seat
sheet), so it adds the whole host: `ArenaScreen` holds `useState<ArenaSheetsState>(EMPTY_ARENA_SHEETS)`
with `openSheet(sheet)` / `closeSheet(sheet)` callbacks, and renders an exhaustive switch over
`arenaSheetEntries(sheets)` with the `card`, `kit` and `seat` cases: one component per entry, keyed by
`key`, with `open={entry.open}`, as the last children of the arena root and never inside a clickable
element. L74-04 has no host: it leaves `ArenaSeats.onOpenSeat` unset and passes `tappedSeatId={null}`.
L74-08 passes `commit` and L74-10 `onBeginTargeting`, `collapsed` and `collapsedBar` to the `card`
case. No later task adds a second host. The Sheet primitive owns the stack, Esc, backdrop and focus.

### A.6 The commit helper (L74-08)

```ts
// arena-commit.ts
import type { CardIntent } from '../../view-model/drop-target';   // playCard / sellCard, already {kind, payload}

export type ArenaIntent =
  | CardIntent
  | { kind: 'upgradeCard'; payload: UpgradeCardPayload }
  | { kind: 'playMultipleAttacks'; payload: PlayMultipleAttacksPayload }
  | { kind: 'deactivatePersistent'; payload: DeactivatePersistentPayload }
  | { kind: 'clearSpy'; payload: ClearSpyPayload }
  | { kind: 'drawCard' }
  | { kind: 'activateDuplication' };
// Part 2 adds the shop and sub-choice intents (buyCard, buyUpgradePoint, sellUpgradePoint,
// buySpecialCard, buyPoolCard, resolveSubChoice).

export type ArenaCommit = (intent: ArenaIntent) => void;

/** The intent callbacks of `ArenaScreenProps` the helper routes to. */
export type ArenaIntentHandlers = Pick<
  ArenaScreenProps,
  'onPlayCard' | 'onSellCard' | 'onUpgradeCard' | 'onPlayMultipleAttacks' | 'onDraw' | 'onClearSpy'
  | 'onDeactivatePersistent' | 'onActivateDuplication'
>;

/**
 * The single exit for every arena intent (§6.6.4). L75 adds `allowTutorialSend` here, L74-17 the
 * own-intent FX and L74-25 the reject spring-back, so no component calls an intent handler directly.
 * Leave, forfeit and chat are App-level actions outside it (TopBar and the chat sheet call them).
 */
export function createArenaCommit(handlers: ArenaIntentHandlers): ArenaCommit;
```

`playCard` maps to `onPlayCard(instanceId)` when the payload has no option key, else
`onPlayCard(instanceId, options)` with only the defined keys (`exactOptionalPropertyTypes`).
`onDeactivatePersistent` / `onActivateDuplication` are optional props: a missing handler makes the
intent a no-op. The helper never checks legality (golden rule 8); callers only commit enabled actions.

### A.8 Rulings that bind every Part 1 task (controller, 2026-10-28)

1. **Activated art.** The L72 `Card` has no activated-art prop: art always comes from `artFor(cardId)`.
   The "activated" look is the `counter` glow. So: a persistent with a counter passes `counter`
   (`card-lives` or, for Invisibility, `turns`); **Shield and Sentence never pass `counter`** (the
   L72 guard, Appendix A `global-activated-art-guard`). A Sentence's remaining turns show as a
   separate badge or line, never as the card counter. `InPlayThumb.activatedArt` is not read in V7.
2. **Round** in the top bar = `tableRound(view.turnSequence, view.players.length)` from
   `@card-battle/shared` (the log's and the chat's count). V2 shows no round.
3. **Flag action.** Derive Forfeit / Leave / Return home from `tableFlagIntent(...)` with `isSpectator`.
   Never call `tableFlagLeaveAction` (it has no `isSpectator` input, map-view-model.md).
4. **Spoken costs keep the V2 words.** Use `CostAmount` / `spokenGlyphAmount` output as is (`pts`);
   the Draw aria-label is whatever V2's `Draw, plus N pts` pattern produces, not "points".
5. **Landscape kit-portrait CSS.** `index.css:195-201` (inside `@media (orientation: landscape)`, unlayered)
   shrink any `KitPortrait` under `data-zone="opponent-seat"` / `"private-zone"`.
   - L74-04 scopes the opponent rule to `.table-shell` (the V2 root class,
     `screens/table/table-shell.tsx:64`) **and** `[data-zone="opponents-dialog-row"]`: the V2
     Opponents dialog renders its seats beside `TableShell`, outside `<main class="table-shell">`
     (`screens/table.tsx:1607-1659`).
   - L74-09 scopes the private-zone rule to `.table-shell` (`PrivateZone` renders inside `TableShell`).
   - The V2 table keeps its look and the arena is unaffected. No test pins those two rules; the
     CSS-reading tests listed in `map-table.md` §5 stay green.
6. **No FX in Part 1.** Part 1 components never call `useTableFx()` (it throws without a provider).
   L74-17 wraps the arena in `TableFxProvider`. Resource values keep their `data-resource-kind`
   anchors now so L74-17 needs no markup change.
7. **Eliminated POV dock (designer 2026-10-28).** An eliminated POV without `pendingReanimation`
   (Watching) keeps a **visible, disabled** dock, exactly as `selfStrip(view, ctx).dock` models it
   (Draw and Duplication locked, Shop browse-only, Unspy absent). Only a walk-in has no dock (Q38).
   The L74-23 Review line "economy dock hidden for every spectator-overlay POV" is superseded.
8. **Old table untouched; the shared files Part 1 may edit.** No Part 1 task edits `screens/table.tsx`,
   `screens/table/**` or `design/**`. The only shared files Part 1 edits are:
   - `index.css` (ruling 5: L74-04, L74-09); `App.tsx` only if a task's own brief requires it (none
     does: L72-20 already routes);
   - `DESIGN.md`, `docs/agent/frontend.md`, `docs/agent/testing.md` (L74-01), `docs/agent/decisions.md`
     and `docs/backlog_v7.md`;
   - `ui/motion/use-long-press.ts` + its test (new, L74-02);
   - `ui/kit/kit-details.tsx` + `kit-details.test.tsx` (L74-02 Watch point: the sections lose their
     region landmarks), with `DESIGN.md`;
   - `ui/card/card.tsx` + `card.test.tsx` (L74-07 Watch point: `aria-pressed` only when `selected` is
     passed; a `description` passthrough for the unplayable reason), with `DESIGN.md` and a
     `decisions.md` entry;
   - `fx/table-fx-context.tsx` + `fx/table-fx-hooks.ts` (L74-06 removes the dead
     `mirrorHighlightIds` / `setMirrorHighlightIds` fields only);
   - `ui/layers.ts` (L74-12 adds `Z_INDEX.dragGhost`, ruling 10) and the `Z_INDEX` literal in
     `ui/primitives/sheet-stack.test.ts` that pins the layer object, with `DESIGN.md`;
   - `docs/backlog_v7_test_classification.md` (the L74-01 re-check line; the L74-02, L74-03 and
     L74-13 product-behavior rows);
   - new behavior test files under `net/`: L74-03 `use-room-connection.forfeit.behavior.test.ts`
     and L74-13 `use-room-connection.clear-spy.behavior.test.ts`. Both use one style: `renderHook`
     from `@testing-library/react` in a happy-dom `.test.ts`, with a `vi.hoisted` fake room inside
     `vi.mock('@colyseus/sdk')`; L74-03 establishes it and L74-13 inlines its own fake.
     `net/use-room-connection.ts` itself is not edited.
   - No new test file shares a base name with another `.ts` / `.tsx` test in its directory (tsc /
     lint skip one of such a pair). L74-01 therefore deletes `arena-screen.test.ts` before it
     creates `arena-screen.test.tsx`.
9. **Lot 73 carry-forward.**
   - Seat colors: always go through `v7SeatColor(index)` (`ui/palette.ts`), which clamps; never index
     `V7_SEAT_COLORS` directly, because `seatColorIndexOf` can return ≥ 8 (L74-04, L74-06).
   - Spied seats: `kitHidden` / tier key on `spied.kitId`, the avatar on the model's `avatar`; render
     both as the model says, never re-derive (L74-04).
   - A self-queued base Suicide appears in both `seatThreatIds` lists of the POV: the tap highlight
     shows it once, as incoming (L74-06).
   - An eliminated Watching POV with a leftover `self.shield` sees Upgrade disabled `actions-locked`
     in its thumbs' sheet (L73-07 model, V2 parity); render it as the model says (L74-09).
10. **Drag ghost layer (L74-12).** The hand-drag ghost and the Sell zone are the one exception to
    "overlays use `Sheet` / `Dialog` / `Toast` only, never a custom fixed layer": a
    `pointer-events: none` portal to `document.body`, mounted only during a drag, below the sheet
    layer. Its z-index is `Z_INDEX.dragGhost` (40) in `ui/layers.ts`, below `sheet` (50), recorded by
    one `DESIGN.md` Overlays line.

## Appendix B — Designer questions and new strings (Part 1)

Each question has a default the plan implements and tests. The designer settled the eliminated-POV dock
on 2026-10-28 (Appendix A.8 ruling 7) and answered Q1, Q2, Q10 and Q17 the same day, each confirming the
plan default (Q2 after weighing seat thumbs against a 76×140 seat). The other sixteen keep their defaults:
the designer was shown them and did not override any. A later override changes only the owning task's code
and tests.

| # | Question | Plan default | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| Q1 | Seat text on a 76 px seat: bot difficulty and connection state as words, or icons? | **Designer 2026-10-28:** Icons on the seat, with the words for screen readers, in the `title`, and in the seat sheet. Words on the seat need a 76×104 seat box (`SEAT_BOX.portrait`, a view-model change). | L74-04 |
| Q2 | Opponent actives and ticking Sentences: thumbs on the seat, or badges on the seat with thumbs in the seat sheet? | **Designer 2026-10-28:** Badges on the seat (≤ 2 + "+n", §6.2); thumbs that open the card sheet in the seat sheet. | L74-04, L74-05 |
| Q3 | Arrow labels pressable? | No: inspect goes through the plate badges (incoming) and the seat sheet (everything else). | L74-06 |
| Q4 | Recurring chip wording | `live` shows no word; `Paused` / `Ending` are written. Imposition shows its cap (`−2 pts` / `−4 pts`), not its 7-point floor. | L74-06 |
| Q5 | Plate landing card for a multi-attack | The first attack of the volley. | L74-06 |
| Q6 | Top-bar menu control name | `Menu`. | L74-03 |
| Q7 | Turn banner with no active seat | `—` alone (V2 rendered "—'s turn"). | L74-03 |
| Q8 | Leave confirm button fills | Stay `primary`, the confirm (Forfeit / Leave / Return home) `danger`. | L74-03 |
| Q9 | Game-code copy feedback | An inline status in the menu reusing the lobby's `Code copied` / `Copy failed`. | L74-03 |
| Q10 | Card sheet button colors | **Designer 2026-10-28:** Use `primary`, Upgrade `secondary`, Sell `quiet` (V2's green Sell has no V7 Button), Deactivate `danger`. | L74-08, L74-09 |
| Q11 | Repeated lock reason | One reason line under each disabled button (e.g. "Not your turn" up to three times). Alternative: one shared line. | L74-08 |
| Q12 | Own kit portrait size in the self strip | `tile` (72 px); `thumb` is the fallback if the strip runs to three rows on a 375 px phone. | L74-09 |
| Q13 | Upgrade cue under reduced motion | A 120 ms opacity fade (V2 showed nothing; §8.3 allows fades). | L74-07 |
| Q14 | A card sheet whose card left the hand | Stays open, read-only, with no action. Alternative: close it. | L74-08 |
| Q15 | Targeting when the turn passes or a sub-choice opens | The card sheet closes. Alternative: re-expand it read-only. | L74-10 |
| Q16 | Regeneration quantity default | `1 life` preselected. Alternative: nothing preselected, Use disabled until a pick. | L74-10 |
| Q17 | Multi-attack row target default | **Designer 2026-10-28:** No default target (V2 preselected the first living opponent). | L74-11 |
| Q18 | Multi-attack Cancel | Back to the card step with the selection reset. Alternative: close the sheet, as V2's dialog did. | L74-11 |
| Q19 | Sell drop zone | 96×72 px, 8 px above the hand, 16 px from the screen edge opposite the drag origin. | L74-12 |
| Q20 | Arrows between two adjacent grid seats | None drawn: two neighbours in the grid (84 px apart) leave no room for an arrow, so the effect shows only in either seat's sheet. | L74-06 |

#### From domain D (L74-10 … L74-13)

  - (1) a hook behavior test: send `FORFEIT` once, no `room.leave`;
  - (2) the arena flag test: Forfeit calls `onForfeit`, Leave calls `onLeave`.
  - Resolved by the controller's follow-up ruling: both `net/` hook tests use `renderHook` in a happy-dom `.test.ts` with a `vi.hoisted` fake room inside `vi.mock('@colyseus/sdk')`: `use-room-connection.forfeit.behavior.test.ts` (L74-03, establishes the pattern) and `use-room-connection.clear-spy.behavior.test.ts` (L74-13, its own inline fake). Neither creates a shared helper.
- Resolved by C9: L74-04's seat control is one stretched `<button>` named exactly by the nickname outside targeting, on a `data-zone="opponent-seat"` root that keeps `data-player-id`; seats sit in DOM order inside `data-zone="opponents"`.
- **L74-06** (`CentralPlate`): its root carries `data-zone="pending"`. L74-12 measures it and sets `data-drop` on it.
- **L74-07** (`HandFan`): hand and special cards sit under `[data-arena-region="hand"]` as `Card` buttons named by their face label, each in a `HandSlot` `<li>` that L74-12 gives its pointer handlers. A card's `aria-pressed` follows `selectedInstanceId` (the newest open hand / special card sheet, including a collapsed targeting sheet); L74-10's "restores the hand" test asserts it.
- Resolved by C7 and C8: Use's accessible name starts with 'Use'; `send` commits `playCard { instanceId }` and closes the sheet; `target` calls `onBeginTargeting`; `liveCardRefFor` is the one held-card helper.
- Resolved by C12: L74-09 renders no Activate duplication and keeps 'Duplication active'; ArenaScreen computes `strip = selfStrip(view, ctx)` once, and L74-13 reads `.dock` and `.isActive`.
- **L74-14:** passes `onOpenShop` to `Dock` (until then Shop renders disabled) and reads `dock.shop.browseOnly`.
- **L74-17:**
  - Own-intent FX hook into `createArenaCommit`. Tap-tap, multi-attack, drag drops and dock sends all go through it.
  - The drag ghost already exists during a drag, so the play flight can start from the drop point.
- **L74-18:** a sub-choice opening ends targeting by itself (`liveTargeting`), so the sub-choice host needs nothing for it.
- **L74-23:** `Dock` already renders nothing for a walk-in and the disabled Watching dock for an eliminated POV (ruling 7). L74-23 must not hide the dock for an eliminated POV.
- **L74-24:** add Stats to `Dock` from `dock.stats`. Today `dockHasControls` ignores `stats`; update it with Stats.
- **L74-25:** a server reject after a drag drop should spring the card back. `useHandDrag` knows the dropped card only until release, so L74-25 can key the spring-back on the rejected intent's `instanceId`, as for tap-tap.
- **L75:**
  - Add the tour / Look gate input to `canStartHandDrag`.
  - Add `allowTutorialSend` in the commit; it covers target taps, drops and dock sends.
  - Map `data-tutorial-highlight` `use` / `upgrade` / `sell` on the card sheet buttons.
- **L74-26** (gate): its browser scenarios "tap-tap and drag commits" should repeat the L74-12 check list plus targeting:
  - focus on the first valid seat;
  - Esc while the Unspy sheet sits above the bar closes only the Unspy sheet;
  - a double tap on the bar's Cancel does not draw.

#### From the critic (2026-10-28)

- **L74-23** needs a Watching-strip Leave control that opens the flag confirm, but the confirm state
  is private to `TopBar` (L74-03: `confirmOpen` and the `confirmed` ref latch). Plan for it as an
  extension of Appendix A.7's `TopBar` contract: a controlled confirm request prop (for example
  `confirmRequest`), or the confirm state lifted into `ArenaScreen`. Keep the latch semantics: one
  press sends one leave or forfeit.
- **L74-25** must lock every input while reconnecting, but Appendix A.2 fixes
  `ctx = { subChoice, readOnly }`. Do not reuse `readOnly` for it: `readOnly` also turns the dock into
  the finished-board Stats dock (`selfStrip` `dockFor`) and the flag into Return home. Plan a separate
  reconnect lock input, as an extension of A.2's action context.

### Draft questions and their dispositions (2026-10-21 backlog review)

Resolved in the 2026-10-21 backlog review unless listed under "Designer questions still open" in `docs/backlog_v7.md`; dispositions are recorded in the **Review** lines above and in the amended spec.

- What's new for an opt-in arena: the lot intro assumes arena-only surfaces add nothing, because default players cannot see them until the L75 flip. The flip would then write the Before/After items. Only L74-18's Q63 seeding, which also changes the default table, would add `Fixed some bugs.`. §10.4 names only L73 and L80 as lots with nothing to add. Should each player-facing L74 task add its items now instead?
- Sheet-stack home: L76/L77 §7.7 dialogs must follow the §6.7 contract without importing arena code. Does L72's `ui/primitives` Sheet already ship that contract? If not, can L74-02 add it to `ui/`, with L76 adopting it after L74 merges?
- Self-strip captions: §6.1 lists the self-strip resources without captions, but Appendix A `global-resource-captions` keeps visible Lives / Shield / Points / Upgrade points captions on the own dock (L43-01). L74-08 assumes they stay visible. Confirm, or retire them with a decisions.md entry?
- POV elimination during a live game: §6.12 says the overlay replaces the 'You are dead' banner on game end. L74-24/L74-25 assume the overlay replaces that cue on every POV elimination edge, live or final, so the two never show together. Confirm?
- Eliminated seated POV: §6.12 / Q38 hide the economy dock only for walk-in spectators. L74-24 assumes an eliminated POV keeps a rendered, disabled dock, with Unspy never enabled. Should it be hidden for every spectator-overlay POV instead?
- `TurnStartedPayload` carries no `turnSequence`. L74-22 pairs each new payload with the view's `turnSequence` at the moment it arrives. If TURN_STARTED can arrive before its matching stateUpdate, the §6.10 rule that a resume with the same turnSequence keeps the original length could misfire. Is that pairing acceptable, or should L73's `turnClock()` key the record differently, for example on `(activePlayerId, deadlineMs)`?
- Overlap with L73: this draft has L74-07 remove the dead `TableFxContext.mirrorHighlightIds` and `screens/table/persistent-incoming.ts`, and has L74-15 add the single special-price constant. If the L73 tasks already do any of these, drop them from L74.
- The elimination-reward 'short celebration' (§6.7 points to §8) is assumed to be L78. L74 only re-hosts the existing reward pulse. Confirm?
